<script>
(function(){
  var p = (location.pathname||'/');
  var seg = p.split('/').filter(Boolean)[0]||'';
  var qs = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('school')||'';
  var hs = (location.hash||'').replace('#','').split('/')[0]||'';
  var candidate = seg||qs||hs;
  var SLUG_RE = /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{2,19}$/;
  var isSuperAdmin = candidate === 'super_admin';
  var isSchool = candidate && candidate!=='index.html' && candidate!=='super_admin' && SLUG_RE.test(candidate);
  if(isSuperAdmin){
    window.__resolvedSchoolSlug = '__super_admin__';
    document.documentElement.style.background = '#070d1a';
    // Hide app chrome instantly so nothing flashes before renderSuperAdminPortal() runs
    var _saHideStyle = document.createElement('style');
    _saHideStyle.textContent = '#mainNav,#loginOverlay,.container,#jiibyFab{display:none!important;}';
    document.head.appendChild(_saHideStyle);
  } else if(isSchool){
    window.__resolvedSchoolSlug = candidate;
    document.documentElement.style.background = '#0f1b2d';
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function(){
      var ov = document.getElementById('loginOverlay');
      if(ov){ ov.style.cssText += ';display:flex!important;opacity:1!important;'; }
    });
  } else {
    // Root URL — show marketing page
    window.__resolvedSchoolSlug = '';
    document.documentElement.style.background = '#ffffff';
    document.documentElement.style.minHeight = '100vh';
    // Hide app chrome instantly so nothing flashes before renderMarketingPage() runs
    var _mktHideStyle = document.createElement('style');
    _mktHideStyle.textContent = '#mainNav,#loginOverlay,.container,#jiibyFab{display:none!important;}';
    document.head.appendChild(_mktHideStyle);
  }
})();
</script>

<script>
// ── Mobile drawer helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────
function toggleMobileDrawer(){
  const d=document.getElementById('mobileNavDrawer');
  const ov=document.getElementById('mobileDrawerOverlay');
  const isOpen=d.classList.contains('open');
  if(isOpen){closeMobileDrawer();}else{
    d.classList.add('open');ov.classList.add('open');
    updateMobDrawerInfo();updateMobDrawerActiveLink();
  }
}
function closeMobileDrawer(){
  document.getElementById('mobileNavDrawer').classList.remove('open');
  document.getElementById('mobileDrawerOverlay').classList.remove('open');
}
function mobNavGo(pageId){
  closeMobileDrawer();
  setTimeout(()=>navigateTo(pageId),60);
}
function updateMobDrawerInfo(){
  // School name
  const sn=window._schoolDisplayName||window.currentSchool?.name||'JIIB$CORE';
  const sEl=document.getElementById('mobDrawerSchoolName');
  if(sEl)sEl.textContent='⚡ '+sn;
  // Brand in top bar
  const bn=document.getElementById('mobBrandName');
  if(bn)bn.textContent='⚡ '+sn;
  // User
  const unEl=document.getElementById('mobDrawerUserName');
  const riEl=document.getElementById('mobDrawerRoleIcon');
  const role=window._userRole||'admin';
  if(unEl)unEl.textContent=window._userDisplayName||window._authedEmail||(window._parentPhone?'Parent':'User');
  if(riEl)riEl.textContent=role==='parent'?'👨‍👩‍👧':role==='teacher'?'🧑‍🏫':'👑';
  // Term
  const curr=typeof td==='function'?td():null;
  const termEl=document.getElementById('mobDrawerTerm');
  const brandTerm=document.getElementById('mobBrandTerm');
  if(termEl&&curr)termEl.textContent='📅 '+(curr.label||'');
  if(brandTerm&&curr)brandTerm.textContent=curr.label||'';
  // Role visibility in drawer
  const isAdmin=role==='admin';
  const isTeacher=role==='teacher';
  const isParent=role==='parent';
  document.querySelectorAll('.mob-admin-only').forEach(el=>el.style.display=isAdmin?'':'none');
  document.querySelectorAll('.mob-teacher-only').forEach(el=>el.style.display=isTeacher?'':'none');
  document.querySelectorAll('.mob-parent-only').forEach(el=>el.style.display=isParent?'':'none');
  document.querySelectorAll('.mob-not-parent').forEach(el=>el.style.display=isParent?'none':'');
  // Online pill
  const odot=document.getElementById('mobOnlineDot');
  if(odot)odot.style.display=isAdmin?'flex':'none';
  // Sync JIIBY toggle
  const ms=document.getElementById('jiibyMobSwitch');
  const msl=document.getElementById('jiibyMobSlider');
  const enabled=typeof appPrefs!=='undefined'&&appPrefs.jiibyEnabled!==false;
  if(ms)ms.checked=enabled;
  if(msl)msl.style.background=enabled?'var(--jiiby,#7c3aed)':'#ccc';
}
function updateMobDrawerActiveLink(){
  const active=document.querySelector('.page.active');
  const pid=active?active.id:'dashboard';
  document.querySelectorAll('.mob-nav-item[data-page]').forEach(btn=>{
    btn.classList.toggle('active',btn.getAttribute('data-page')===pid);
  });
}
function updateMobDarkIcon(){
  const dark=typeof appPrefs!=='undefined'?appPrefs.darkMode:false;
  const icon=dark?'☀️':'🌙';
  ['mobDarkBtn','mobDrawerDarkIcon'].forEach(id=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.textContent=icon;});
  const lbl=document.getElementById('mobDrawerDarkLabel');
  if(lbl)lbl.textContent=dark?'Light':'Dark';
}
// Update mobile clock every second
setInterval(()=>{
  const el=document.getElementById('mobClock');
  if(!el)return;
  const now=new Date();
  el.textContent=String(now.getHours()).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0');
},10000);
// Sync online teachers count to mobile pill
setInterval(()=>{
  const cnt=document.getElementById('onlineTeachersCount');
  const mob=document.getElementById('mobOnlineCount');
  if(cnt&&mob)mob.textContent=cnt.textContent;
},3000);
// Sync net status dot to mobile
const _origSetNetStatus=typeof setNetStatus==='function'?setNetStatus:null;
if(_origSetNetStatus){
  setNetStatus=function(state,text){
    _origSetNetStatus(state,text);
    const dot=document.getElementById('mobNetDot');
    if(dot){dot.style.background=state==='online'?'#22c55e':state==='syncing'?'#fbbf24':'#ef4444';dot.style.boxShadow=state==='online'?'0 0 6px #22c55e':'';}
  };
}
// Run once drawer info on login
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(updateMobDrawerInfo,800));
</script>

const AudioCtx=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;let audioCtx=null;
function getAudioCtx(){if(!audioCtx)audioCtx=new AudioCtx();return audioCtx;}
function playTone(freq,type='sine',dur=0.15,vol=0.3,delay=0){try{const ctx=getAudioCtx();const osc=ctx.createOscillator();const gain=ctx.createGain();osc.connect(gain);gain.connect(ctx.destination);osc.type=type;osc.frequency.value=freq;gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0,ctx.currentTime+delay);gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(vol,ctx.currentTime+delay+0.02);gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001,ctx.currentTime+delay+dur);osc.start(ctx.currentTime+delay);osc.stop(ctx.currentTime+delay+dur+0.05);}catch(e){}}
const SFX={
  success:()=>{playTone(523,'sine',0.12,0.25,0);playTone(659,'sine',0.12,0.25,0.1);playTone(784,'sine',0.15,0.25,0.2);},
  error:()=>{playTone(200,'sawtooth',0.18,0.3,0);playTone(150,'sawtooth',0.2,0.3,0.1);},
  notify:()=>{playTone(880,'sine',0.08,0.2,0);playTone(1046,'sine',0.08,0.2,0.08);},

  lock:()=>{playTone(330,'square',0.08,0.2,0);playTone(260,'square',0.1,0.2,0.12);},
  unlock:()=>{playTone(440,'sine',0.08,0.2,0);playTone(550,'sine',0.1,0.2,0.1);},

function deepMergeSchoolData(cloud, local){
  const merged=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(cloud));
  // Merge deletion tombstones first, so a student deleted on either side
  // within the last 14 days stays deleted through the merge.
  const _tombMap={};
  (merged.deletedStudentIds||[]).forEach(d=>{_tombMap[d.id]=d;});
  (local.deletedStudentIds||[]).forEach(d=>{if(!_tombMap[d.id]||d.ts>_tombMap[d.id].ts)_tombMap[d.id]=d;});
  const _cutoff=Date.now()-14*24*60*60*1000;
  merged.deletedStudentIds=Object.values(_tombMap).filter(d=>d.ts>_cutoff);
  const _deletedIdSet=new Set(merged.deletedStudentIds.map(d=>d.id));
  Object.entries(local.terms||{}).forEach(([termKey,localTerm])=>{
    if(!merged.terms) merged.terms={};
    if(!merged.terms[termKey]){merged.terms[termKey]=localTerm;return;}
    const cloudTerm=merged.terms[termKey];
    // Merge students by id, excluding anyone recently deleted
    const studentMap={};
    (cloudTerm.students||[]).forEach(s=>{studentMap[s.id]=s;});
    (localTerm.students||[]).forEach(s=>{
      if(!studentMap[s.id]) studentMap[s.id]=s;
      else if(Object.keys(s).length>Object.keys(studentMap[s.id]).length) studentMap[s.id]=s;
    });
    cloudTerm.students=Object.values(studentMap).filter(s=>!_deletedIdSet.has(s.id));
    // Merge marks by (studentId, subject)
    const mk=m=>`${m.studentId}::${m.subject}`;
    const markMap={};
    (cloudTerm.marks||[]).forEach(m=>{markMap[mk(m)]=m;});
    (localTerm.marks||[]).forEach(localMark=>{
      const k=mk(localMark);
      if(!markMap[k]){markMap[k]=localMark;}
      else{
        const out={...markMap[k]};
        const _mFields=Object.keys(localMark).filter(f=>/^c\d+$/.test(f));
        _mFields.forEach(f=>{
          if(localMark[f]!=null){
            out[f]=markMap[k][f]==null ? localMark[f] : Math.max(localMark[f],markMap[k][f]);
          }
        });
        markMap[k]=out;
      }
    });
    cloudTerm.marks=Object.values(markMap);
    if(!localTerm.locked) cloudTerm.locked=false;
  });
  if(local.subjects){
    Object.entries(local.subjects).forEach(([cls,subs])=>{
      if(!merged.subjects) merged.subjects={};
      if(!merged.subjects[cls]){merged.subjects[cls]=subs;return;}
      const nameSet=new Set(merged.subjects[cls].map(s=>s.name));
      subs.forEach(s=>{if(!nameSet.has(s.name)) merged.subjects[cls].push(s);});
    });
  }
  if(local.activeTerm&&merged.terms&&merged.terms[local.activeTerm])
    merged.activeTerm=local.activeTerm;
  if(local.schoolNews&&local.schoolNews.updatedAt){
    const lTs=new Date(local.schoolNews.updatedAt).getTime();
    const cTs=merged.schoolNews&&merged.schoolNews.updatedAt ? new Date(merged.schoolNews.updatedAt).getTime():0;
    if(lTs>cTs) merged.schoolNews=local.schoolNews;
  }
  // Events: union merge by id — never let one device's events overwrite another's
  if(Array.isArray(local.events)){
    const evMap={};
    (merged.events||[]).forEach(e=>{evMap[e.id]=e;});
    local.events.forEach(e=>{evMap[e.id]=e;});
    merged.events=Object.values(evMap);
  }
  // Lists that get toggled independently — union merge instead of overwrite
  ['blockedParents','blockedTeachers'].forEach(key=>{
    if(Array.isArray(local[key])){
      merged[key]=[...new Set([...(merged[key]||[]),...local[key]])];
    }
  });
  // Grading scale, marks deadline, lock password, houses/settings: this function
  // only runs when the local device has pending unsaved edits, so trust local
  // over the (older-known) cloud copy for these rarely-conflicting fields.
  if(local.grading) merged.grading=local.grading;
  if(local.marksDeadline!==undefined) merged.marksDeadline=local.marksDeadline;
  if(local.lockPassword) merged.lockPassword=local.lockPassword;
  if(local.settings){
    merged.settings={...(merged.settings||{}),...local.settings};
    if(Array.isArray(local.settings.houses)){
      merged.settings.houses=[...new Set([...(merged.settings?.houses||[]),...local.settings.houses])];
    }
  }
  return merged;
}

// ─── OFFLINE QUEUE (IndexedDB) ───────────────────────────────────────────────
const IDB_NAME='jiibcore_offline',IDB_VER=1,IDB_STORE='mark_queue';
let _idb=null;
function openIDB(){return new Promise((res,rej)=>{if(_idb)return res(_idb);const req=indexedDB.open(IDB_NAME,IDB_VER);req.onupgradeneeded=e=>{const db=e.target.result;if(!db.objectStoreNames.contains(IDB_STORE))db.createObjectStore(IDB_STORE,{keyPath:'id',autoIncrement:true});};req.onsuccess=e=>{_idb=e.target.result;res(_idb);};req.onerror=e=>rej(e);});}
async function idbPush(item){try{const db=await openIDB();return new Promise((res,rej)=>{const tx=db.transaction(IDB_STORE,'readwrite');const req=tx.objectStore(IDB_STORE).add({...item,ts:Date.now()});req.onsuccess=()=>res(req.result);req.onerror=e=>rej(e);});}catch(e){}}
async function idbGetAll(){try{const db=await openIDB();return new Promise((res,rej)=>{const tx=db.transaction(IDB_STORE,'readonly');const req=tx.objectStore(IDB_STORE).getAll();req.onsuccess=()=>res(req.result||[]);req.onerror=e=>rej(e);});}catch(e){return[];}}
async function idbClear(){try{const db=await openIDB();return new Promise((res,rej)=>{const tx=db.transaction(IDB_STORE,'readwrite');const req=tx.objectStore(IDB_STORE).clear();req.onsuccess=()=>res();req.onerror=e=>rej(e);});}catch(e){}}
async function flushOfflineQueue(){
  if(!navigator.onLine||!sb||!window.currentSchool)return;
  const items=await idbGetAll();
  if(!items.length)return;
  const validItems=items.filter(item=>
    item.type==='mark' &&
    item.termKey &&
    item.studentId && item.subject && item.fields
  );
  if(!validItems.length){ await idbClear(); return; }
  setSyncStatus('saving','↳ Syncing '+validItems.length+' offline mark'+(validItems.length>1?'s':'')+'…');

  // Group by student+subject so we upsert once per row
  const grouped={};
  validItems.forEach(item=>{
    const key=item.termKey+'::'+item.studentId+'::'+item.subject;
    if(!grouped[key]) grouped[key]={...item,fields:{...item.fields}};
    else Object.assign(grouped[key].fields, item.fields);
  });

  let failed=false;
  for(const key of Object.keys(grouped)){
    const item=grouped[key];
    try{
      // Discard any queued item that's stale (older than 6 hours). A queue
      // entry sitting this long almost certainly predates a bug fix or was
      // superseded by a real, more recent save — replaying it would
      // silently overwrite correct current data with old garbage. This is
      // exactly what was making freshly-typed marks revert to old values.
      const itemAge=Date.now()-(item.ts||0);
      if(itemAge > 6*60*60*1000){
        console.warn('[IDB flush] discarding stale queued item (>6h old):',key);
        continue;
      }

      // Find class from local blob
      const t=data.terms[item.termKey];
      const s=t?(t.students||[]).find(x=>x.id===item.studentId):null;
      const cls=s?s.class:(item.class||'');

      // Check whether the table has been updated MORE RECENTLY than this
      // queued item was created. If so, someone/something already saved a
      // newer value — do NOT let this stale queued write clobber it.
      const {data:existingRow}=await sb.from('marks')
        .select('updated_at')
        .eq('school_id',window.currentSchool.id)
        .eq('term_key',item.termKey)
        .eq('student_id',item.studentId)
        .eq('subject',item.subject)
        .maybeSingle();

      if(existingRow && existingRow.updated_at){
        const rowUpdatedMs = new Date(existingRow.updated_at).getTime();
        if(rowUpdatedMs > (item.ts||0)){
          console.warn('[IDB flush] skipping — table already has a newer value for',key);
          continue;
        }
      }

      const row={
        school_id:window.currentSchool.id,
        term_key:item.termKey,
        student_id:item.studentId,
        subject:item.subject,
        class:cls,
        updated_at:new Date().toISOString(),
        updated_by:window._authedEmail||'teacher',
        ...item.fields
      };
      const {error}=await sb.from('marks').upsert(row,{
        onConflict:'school_id,term_key,student_id,subject'
      });
      if(error) throw error;
      // Also update local blob so UI stays current
      if(t){
        let lm=(t.marks||[]).find(m=>m.studentId===item.studentId&&m.subject===item.subject);
        if(!lm){lm={studentId:item.studentId,subject:item.subject,class:cls};if(!t.marks)t.marks=[];t.marks.push(lm);}
        Object.assign(lm,item.fields);
      }
    }catch(e){ console.warn('[IDB flush] failed for',key,e.message); failed=true; }
  }
  
  await idbClear();
  if(!failed){
    try{localStorage.setItem(_schoolKey,JSON.stringify(data));}catch(e){}
    setSyncStatus('saved','✓ Offline marks synced');
  } else {
    setSyncStatus('saved','⚠ Some offline marks failed to sync');
  }
}

window.addEventListener('online',()=>{flushOfflineQueue();setSyncStatus('online','');});

// ─── STUDENTS TABLE HELPERS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function sbUpsertStudent(student, termKey){
  if(!sb||!window.currentSchool||!navigator.onLine) return;
  try{
    await sb.from('students').upsert({
      id: student.id,
      school_id: window.currentSchool.id,
      term_key: termKey||data.activeTerm,
      name: student.name,
      class: student.class,
      house: student.house||'',
      parent_phone: student.parentPhone||'',
      subjects: student.subjects||null,
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
    },{onConflict:'id,school_id,term_key'});
  }catch(e){ console.warn('[Students] upsert failed:',e.message); }
}

async function sbDeleteStudent(studentId, termKey){
  if(!sb||!window.currentSchool||!navigator.onLine) return;
  try{
    await sb.from('students').delete()
      .eq('id', studentId)
      .eq('school_id', window.currentSchool.id)
      .eq('term_key', termKey||data.activeTerm);
  }catch(e){ console.warn('[Students] delete failed:',e.message); }
}

async function sbLoadStudentsIntoBlob(termKey){
  if(!sb||!window.currentSchool||!navigator.onLine) return false;
  try{
    const {data:rows, error} = await sb.from('students').select('*')
      .eq('school_id', window.currentSchool.id)
      .eq('term_key', termKey||data.activeTerm);
    if(error||!rows||!rows.length) return false;
    const curr = data.terms[termKey||data.activeTerm];
    if(!curr) return false;
    const existingIds = new Set((curr.students||[]).map(s=>s.id));
    let added = 0;
    rows.forEach(r=>{
      if(existingIds.has(r.id)){
        // Update existing blob entry — but only overwrite a field if the
        // table actually has a value for it. Never let a null/empty DB
        // column blank out a value the blob already has (this was silently
        // wiping student.subjects whenever the table's subjects column
        // lagged behind — the cause of optional subjects randomly
        // disappearing for individual students).
        const idx = curr.students.findIndex(s=>s.id===r.id);
        if(idx>=0){
          const existing = curr.students[idx];
          curr.students[idx] = {
            id: r.id,
            name: r.name ?? existing.name,
            class: r.class ?? existing.class,
            house: (r.house!=null && r.house!=='') ? r.house : existing.house,
            parentPhone: (r.parent_phone!=null && r.parent_phone!=='') ? r.parent_phone : existing.parentPhone,
            subjects: (Array.isArray(r.subjects) && r.subjects.length) ? r.subjects : existing.subjects
          };
        }
      } else {
        curr.students.push({
          id: r.id,
          name: r.name,
          class: r.class,
          house: r.house||'',
          parentPhone: r.parent_phone||'',
          subjects: r.subjects||undefined
        });
        added++;
      }
    });
    if(added>0){
      try{ localStorage.setItem(_schoolKey, JSON.stringify(data)); }catch(e){}
    }
    return true;
  }catch(e){ console.warn('[Students] load failed:',e.message); return false; }
}

async function migrateExistingStudentsToTable(){
  if(!sb||!window.currentSchool||!navigator.onLine) return;
  // Only run if we haven't migrated this school yet
  const migKey = 'students_migrated_'+window.currentSchool.id;
  if(localStorage.getItem(migKey)) return;
  try{
    const termKeys = Object.keys(data.terms||{});
    let totalMigrated = 0;
    for(const termKey of termKeys){
      const term = data.terms[termKey];
      const students = term.students||[];
      if(!students.length) continue;
      // Check how many already exist in the table
      const {data:existing} = await sb.from('students').select('id')
        .eq('school_id', window.currentSchool.id)
        .eq('term_key', termKey);
      const existingIds = new Set((existing||[]).map(r=>r.id));
      const toInsert = students.filter(s=>!existingIds.has(s.id));
      if(!toInsert.length) continue;
      // Batch upsert in chunks of 50
      for(let i=0;i<toInsert.length;i+=50){
        const chunk = toInsert.slice(i,i+50).map(s=>({
          id: s.id,
          school_id: window.currentSchool.id,
          term_key: termKey,
          name: s.name,
          class: s.class,
          house: s.house||'',
          parent_phone: s.parentPhone||'',
          subjects: s.subjects||null,
          updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
        }));
        await sb.from('students').upsert(chunk,{onConflict:'id,school_id,term_key'});
        totalMigrated += chunk.length;
      }
    }
    if(totalMigrated>0){
      console.log('[Migration] Migrated',totalMigrated,'students to Supabase table');
      showAppleNotif('✅ Students Synced', totalMigrated+' student records backed up to cloud','green',5000);
    }
    localStorage.setItem(migKey,'1');
  }catch(e){ console.warn('[Migration] Failed:',e.message); }
}

// Purge any stale IDB entries left from previous sessions on startup
(async()=>{
  try{
    const stale=await idbGetAll();
    if(stale.length){
      // If we already have a cloud connection, stale entries are safe to drop
      if(navigator.onLine) await idbClear();
    }
  }catch(e){}
})();


// ─── SYNC STATUS UI ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
let _sqHideTimer=null;
function setSyncStatus(state,msg){
  const el=document.getElementById('syncQueueBadge');if(!el)return;
  el.className='sq-'+state;
  const icons={saving:'\u21b3 ',saved:'\u2713 ',error:'\u26a0 ',offline:'\u26a1 '};
  el.textContent=(icons[state]||'')+(msg||'');
  el.style.display=(state==='online'||!msg)?'none':'flex';
  clearTimeout(_sqHideTimer);
  if(state==='saved')_sqHideTimer=setTimeout(()=>{el.style.display='none';},3000);
}

let _sessionExpiredBarShown=false;
function showSessionExpiredBar(){
  if(_sessionExpiredBarShown)return;
  _sessionExpiredBarShown=true;
  const bar=document.createElement('div');
  bar.id='sessionExpiredBar';
  bar.style.cssText='position:fixed;bottom:80px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);z-index:9990;background:rgba(15,23,42,0.97);border:1.5px solid rgba(239,68,68,0.5);border-radius:14px;padding:12px 20px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;box-shadow:0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,0.4);backdrop-filter:blur(12px);min-width:300px;max-width:90vw;';
  bar.innerHTML=`<span style="font-size:.9rem;color:#fca5a5;font-weight:600;flex:1;">⚠ Session expired — marks saved locally</span>
    <button id="sessionRefreshBtn" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#3b82f6,#1d4ed8);color:white;border:none;border-radius:9px;padding:8px 16px;font-weight:700;font-size:.85rem;cursor:pointer;flex-shrink:0;white-space:nowrap;transition:opacity .1s;">↻ Refresh</button>`;
  document.body.appendChild(bar);

  document.getElementById('sessionRefreshBtn').addEventListener('click',function(){
    const btn=this;
    btn.textContent='↻ Refreshing…';
    btn.style.opacity='0.7';
    btn.disabled=true;

    // Race: try refreshSession with a hard 3-second timeout.
    // If it wins → restore silently. If it times out or fails → reload page instantly.
    let _done=false;
    const _timeout=setTimeout(()=>{
      if(_done)return;
      _done=true;
      // Save any pending local state before reload
      try{if(data)localStorage.setItem(_schoolKey,JSON.stringify(data));}catch(e){}
      location.reload();
    },3000);

    sb.auth.refreshSession().then(({data:rd,error:re})=>{
      if(_done)return;
      _done=true;
      clearTimeout(_timeout);
      if(!re&&rd&&rd.session){
        // Restored — no reload needed
        _sessionExpiredBarShown=false;
        bar.remove();
        setSyncStatus('saved','✓ Session restored');
        // Flush pending marks immediately, no await so UI doesn't hang
        if(_pendingDirty){guardedPush();}
        else{flushOfflineQueue();}
        showAppleNotif('✅ Session Restored','Marks synced','green',3000);
      }else{
        // Refresh returned but with error — reload is the safest option
        try{if(data)localStorage.setItem(_schoolKey,JSON.stringify(data));}catch(e){}
        location.reload();
      }
    }).catch(()=>{
      if(_done)return;
      _done=true;
      clearTimeout(_timeout);
      try{if(data)localStorage.setItem(_schoolKey,JSON.stringify(data));}catch(e){}
      location.reload();
    });
  });
}

function checkBlobSize(){
  try{
    const raw=localStorage.getItem('schoolData')||'{}';
    const kb=Math.round(raw.length*2/1024);
    if(kb>1800){const el=document.getElementById('conflictWarning');if(el){el.innerHTML='\u26a0 School data is '+(kb>1024?(kb/1024).toFixed(1)+' MB':kb+' KB')+'. Archive old terms in Settings to free space.';el.style.display='block';setTimeout(()=>{el.style.display='none';},12000);}}
  }catch(e){}
}

// ─── OPTIMISTIC LOCKING ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
let _lastCloudUpdatedAt=null;
async function checkWriteConflict(){
  if(!sb||!window.currentSchool)return false;
  try{
    const {data:row}=await sb.from('school_data').select('updated_at').eq('school_id',window.currentSchool.id).maybeSingle();
    if(!row||!row.updated_at)return false;
    const cloudTs=new Date(row.updated_at).getTime();
    if(_lastCloudUpdatedAt&&cloudTs>_lastCloudUpdatedAt+5000){showConflictWarning('\u26a0 Another user saved while you were editing. Refreshing\u2026');await syncPullFromCloud();return true;}
    _lastCloudUpdatedAt=cloudTs;return false;
  }catch(e){return false;}
}
function showConflictWarning(msg){const el=document.getElementById('conflictWarning');if(!el)return;el.innerHTML=msg;el.style.display='block';setTimeout(()=>{el.style.display='none';},9000);}
function showPresenceConflictIfNeeded(cls,sub){
  try{
    const others=Object.values(_onlineUsers||{}).flat().filter(u=>u.editingClass===cls&&u.editingSubject===sub&&u.email!==(window._authedEmail||''));
    if(others.length){const names=others.map(u=>u.name||u.email).join(', ');showConflictWarning('\u26a1 '+names+' is also editing '+cls+' \u2022 '+sub+'. Changes may conflict.');}
    else{const el=document.getElementById('conflictWarning');if(el)el.style.display='none';}
  }catch(e){}
}

// ─── DOUBLE-SAVE GUARD ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
let _isSaving=false;
let _retryCount=0, _retryTimer=null;

function scheduleRetry(){
  clearTimeout(_retryTimer);
  if(_retryCount>=3){_retryCount=0;return;}
  const delay=Math.pow(2,_retryCount)*5000;
  _retryCount++;
  _retryTimer=setTimeout(()=>{if(_pendingDirty)guardedPush();},delay);
}

async function guardedPush(){
  if(!sb||!window._authedEmail||!window.currentSchool) return;
  if(!navigator.onLine){setSyncStatus('offline','⚡ Offline — will sync when online');return;}
  if(_isSaving){
    // A push is already running — retry shortly instead of dropping this
    // update on the floor.
    setSyncStatus('saving','↳ Save in progress…');
    clearTimeout(_pushTimer);
    _pushTimer=setTimeout(()=>{if(_pendingDirty)guardedPush();},1200);
    return;
  }
  // Refresh session silently before pushing — prevents sync errors after idle
  try{
    const {data:sessCheck}=await sb.auth.getSession();
    if(!sessCheck||!sessCheck.session){
      const {error:refreshErr}=await sb.auth.refreshSession();
      if(refreshErr){
        setSyncStatus('saved','⚠ Session expired — please refresh');
        showSessionExpiredBar();
        return;
      }
    }
  }catch(e){console.warn('[SYNC] Session check failed:',e.message);}
  _isSaving=true;
  setSyncStatus('saving','↳ Saving…');
  try{
    // Pull latest cloud snapshot first
    const {data:cloudRow,error:fetchErr}=await sb
      .from('school_data').select('data, updated_at')
      .eq('school_id',window.currentSchool.id).maybeSingle();
    if(fetchErr) throw fetchErr;

    let payload=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(data));

    if(cloudRow&&cloudRow.data){
      const cloudTs=new Date(cloudRow.updated_at||0).getTime();
      const localTs=new Date(localStorage.getItem(_schoolKey+'_localUpdatedAt')||'0').getTime();
      if(cloudTs>localTs+500){
        payload=deepMergeSchoolData(cloudRow.data, data);
      }
    }

    const nowIso=new Date().toISOString();
    const {error:pushErr}=await sb.from('school_data').upsert(
      {school_id:window.currentSchool.id, data:payload, updated_at:nowIso, updated_by:window._authedEmail},
      {onConflict:'school_id'}
    );
    if(pushErr) throw pushErr;

    data=payload;
    localStorage.setItem(_schoolKey,JSON.stringify(data));
    localStorage.setItem(_schoolKey+'_localUpdatedAt',nowIso);
    localStorage.setItem('schoolDataCloudUpdatedAt',nowIso);
    _lastCloudUpdatedAt=new Date(nowIso).getTime();
    _pendingDirty=false;
    _retryCount=0;
    setSyncStatus('saved','✓ Saved');
  }catch(e){
    console.warn('[SYNC] Push failed:',e.message);
    setSyncStatus('saved','⚠ Sync error — retrying');
    scheduleRetry();
  }finally{
    _isSaving=false;
  }
}


// =============== MULTI-TENANT BOOTSTRAP ===============
// Read school slug from URL: jiibscoresync.workers.dev/<slug>
window.currentSchool = null;          // {id, slug, name, logo_url, primary_color}
window.isSuperAdminMode = false;
function getSchoolSlugFromURL(){
  if (window.__resolvedSchoolSlug !== undefined && window.__resolvedSchoolSlug !== null) 
    return window.__resolvedSchoolSlug;
  const seg = (location.pathname || '/').split('/').filter(Boolean)[0] || '';
  const qs = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('school') || '';
  const hs = (location.hash || '').replace('#','').split('/')[0] || '';
  const candidate = seg || qs || hs;
  if (!candidate || candidate === 'index.html') return '';
  // Super admin has its own route — not a school slug
  if (candidate === 'super_admin') return '__super_admin__';
  if (!/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{2,19}$/.test(candidate)) return null;
  return candidate;
}

async function resolveSchoolFromURL(){
  const slug = getSchoolSlugFromURL();
  if (slug === '') { window.isSuperAdminMode = true; return null; }
  // Show login overlay immediately so page is never blank
  const _ov = document.getElementById('loginOverlay');
  const _errEl = document.getElementById('loginError');
  if (_ov) { _ov.style.display = 'flex'; _ov.style.opacity = '1'; }
  if (slug === null) {
    if(_errEl){_errEl.style.color='#fbbf24';_errEl.textContent='⚠️ Invalid school URL. Check the link with your administrator.';}
    return null;
  }
  // Fast load: apply cached branding immediately before the network request
  try{
    const cached = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('school_cache:'+slug)||'null');
    if(cached && cached.active !== false){
      window.currentSchool = cached;
      applySchoolBranding(cached);
    }
  }catch(e){}
  if (!sb) {
    if(_errEl){_errEl.style.color='#fbbf24';_errEl.textContent='⚠️ Could not connect to server. Check your internet and refresh.';}
    return null;
  }
  try{
    const {data:school, error} = await sb.from('schools').select('*').eq('slug', slug).maybeSingle();
    if (error) {
      if(_errEl){_errEl.style.color='#fbbf24';_errEl.textContent='⚠️ Server error: ' + (error.message||'unknown error') + '. Try refreshing.';}
      return null;
    }
    if (!school) {
      try{localStorage.removeItem('school_cache:'+slug);}catch(e){}
      document.body.innerHTML = '<div style="min-height:100vh;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#0f1b2d,#1a2540);color:white;font-family:system-ui,sans-serif;text-align:center;padding:24px;"><div><div style="font-size:64px;margin-bottom:16px;">🔗</div><h1 style="margin:0 0 8px;font-size:1.8rem;font-weight:800;">Invalid School URL</h1><div style="background:rgba(239,68,68,0.15);border:1px solid rgba(239,68,68,0.3);border-radius:12px;padding:16px 24px;margin:20px auto;max-width:460px;"><p style="margin:0;font-size:1rem;color:#fca5a5;font-weight:700;">⚠️ This school link does not exist or has been removed.</p></div><p style="opacity:.65;font-size:.92rem;">The address <code style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.1);padding:2px 8px;border-radius:4px;">/'+slug+'</code> is not registered in this system.</p><p style="opacity:.45;font-size:.82rem;margin-top:6px;">If you believe this is an error, contact your system administrator.</p></div></div>';
      return null;
    }
    if (!school.active) {
      document.body.innerHTML = `
        <div style="min-height:100vh;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#0f1b2d,#1a2540);color:white;font-family:system-ui;text-align:center;padding:24px;">
          <div>
            <div style="font-size:64px;margin-bottom:16px;">⚠️</div>
            <h1 style="margin:0 0 8px;font-size:1.8rem;">${school.name}</h1>
            <div style="background:rgba(239,68,68,0.15);border:1px solid rgba(239,68,68,0.3);border-radius:12px;padding:16px 24px;margin:20px auto;max-width:400px;">
              <p style="margin:0;font-size:1.05rem;color:#fca5a5;font-weight:700;">🔒 This school has been suspended</p>
            </div>
            <p style="opacity:.65;font-size:.95rem;">Please contact your system administrator to restore access.</p>
            <a href="/" style="display:inline-block;margin-top:24px;padding:11px 24px;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.1);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.2);color:white;border-radius:8px;text-decoration:none;font-size:.88rem;">← Back to main portal</a>
          </div>
        </div>`;
      return null;
    }
    window.currentSchool = school;
    applySchoolBranding(school);
    // Cache school for fast next-load branding
    try{localStorage.setItem('school_cache:'+slug, JSON.stringify(school));}catch(e){}
    return school;
  }catch(e){
    if(_errEl){_errEl.style.color='#fbbf24';_errEl.textContent='⚠️ Connection failed. Check your internet and refresh.';}
    return null;
  }
}
function applySchoolBranding(s){
  const effectiveName = (data?.settings?.schoolName) || s.name;
  const effectiveLogo = (data?.settings?.logoUrl) || s.logo_url;
  if (!s) return;
  document.title = s.name + ' — JIIB$CORE SYNC';
  if (s.primary_color) {
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--primary', s.primary_color);
  }
  // Update theme-color meta for installed PWA
    let themeMeta = document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]');
    if(!themeMeta){ themeMeta = document.createElement('meta'); themeMeta.name='theme-color'; document.head.appendChild(themeMeta); }
    themeMeta.content = s.primary_color || '#1e3a8a';

    // Update apple PWA title
    let appleTitle = document.querySelector('meta[name="apple-mobile-web-app-title"]');
    if(appleTitle) appleTitle.content = s.name;

    // Update page title
    document.title = s.name + ' — JIIB$CORE SYNC';
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-school-name]').forEach(el => el.textContent = s.name);
  const lt = document.querySelector('.login-title'); if (lt) lt.textContent = s.name;
  const ls = document.getElementById('loginSchoolSub'); if (ls) ls.textContent = s.name;
  const dt = document.getElementById('dashboardTitle'); if (dt) dt.textContent = s.name + ' 🗣️';
  const navBrand = document.querySelector('.nav-brand'); if (navBrand) navBrand.innerHTML = '⚡ ' + s.name;
  // Apply school badge/logo to every img[src*="badge.png"] on the page
  if (s.logo_url) {
    document.querySelectorAll('img[src="badge.png"], img[src*="badge.png"]').forEach(img => {
      img.src = s.logo_url;
    });
    window._schoolBadgeUrl = s.logo_url;
  }
  // Update report school info from school settings if available
  window._schoolDisplayName = s.name;
}

      <div>
        <div style="font-size:64px;">🏫</div>
        <h1 style="margin:16px 0 8px;">${msg}</h1>
        <p style="opacity:.7;">Check the URL with your school administrator.</p>
        <a href="/" style="display:inline-block;margin-top:24px;padding:12px 24px;background:#3b82f6;color:white;border-radius:8px;text-decoration:none;font-weight:600;">Go to main portal</a>
      </div>
    </div>`;
}
// School-scoped role helpers (replaces old ADMIN_EMAILS check)
window._schoolRoles = [];   // ['admin','teacher'] for the CURRENT school only
function isSchoolAdmin(){ return window._schoolRoles.includes('admin') || isSuperAdmin(); }
function isSchoolTeacher(){ return window._schoolRoles.includes('teacher'); }
function isSuperAdmin(){ return window._isSuperAdmin === true; }
async function loadUserRolesForSchool(userId){
  window._schoolRoles = [];
  window._isSuperAdmin = false;
  if (!sb || !userId) return;
  const {data} = await sb.from('user_roles').select('role,school_id,subjects,display_name').eq('user_id', userId);
  if (!data) return;
  data.forEach(r => {
    if (r.role === 'super_admin') window._isSuperAdmin = true;
    if (window.currentSchool && r.school_id === window.currentSchool.id) {
      window._schoolRoles.push(r.role);
      if (r.subjects) window._userSubjects = r.subjects;
      if (r.display_name) window._userDisplayName = r.display_name;
    }
  });
}
