function updateUserChip(){
  // Legacy chip (still in DOM but hidden by default in role layout)
  const chip=document.getElementById('userChip'),emailEl=document.getElementById('userChipEmail');
  if(chip&&emailEl){if(window._authedEmail){chip.style.display='none';emailEl.textContent=window._authedEmail;}else chip.style.display='none';}
  // New role-aware name chip
  const nc=document.getElementById('userNameChip'),nt=document.getElementById('userNameChipText'),rb=document.getElementById('userRoleBadge'),ic=document.getElementById('userNameChipIcon');
  if(!nc||!nt||!rb)return;
  if(!window._authedEmail&&!window._parentPhone){nc.style.display='none';return;}
  nc.style.display='inline-flex';
  const role=window._userRole||'admin';
  const name=window._userDisplayName||window._authedEmail||(window._parentPhone?('Parent · '+window._parentPhone):'User');
  nt.textContent=name;
  rb.className='role-badge '+role;
  rb.textContent=role;
  if(ic)ic.textContent=role==='parent'?'👨‍👩‍👧':role==='teacher'?'🧑‍🏫':'👑';
}

// ===== ROLE VISIBILITY =====
function isTeacher(){return window._userRole==='teacher';}
function isParent(){return window._userRole==='parent';}
function canTeachSubject(cls,subject){
  if(!isTeacher())return true;
  const subs=window._userSubjects||[];
  return subs.some(s=>s.class===cls&&s.subject===subject);
}
function applyRoleVisibility(){
  const role = window._userRole || (isAdmin() ? 'admin' : 'teacher');
  // Refresh mobile drawer role-visibility whenever roles are applied
  setTimeout(()=>{if(typeof updateMobDrawerInfo==='function')updateMobDrawerInfo();},200);

  // Toggle nav buttons
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-roles]').forEach(el => {
    const allowed = el.getAttribute('data-roles').split(',').map(s => s.trim());
    el.style.display = allowed.includes(role) ? '' : 'none';
  });

  // === TERM NAV CONTROL ===
  const selector = document.getElementById('termNavSelector');
  const display = document.getElementById('termNavDisplay');

  if (role === 'admin') {
    selector.style.display = '';
    display.style.display = 'none';
  } else {
    // Teachers & Parents
    selector.style.display = 'none';
    display.style.display = 'flex';
    updateTermDisplay();
  }
  // Online teachers pill — only admin
  const pill=document.getElementById('onlineTeachersPill');
  if(pill)pill.style.display=role==='admin'?'inline-flex':'none';

  // Marks nav button: visible for teachers only, auto-hides after 8 seconds
  const marksBtn=document.getElementById('marksNavBtn');
  if(marksBtn){
    if(role==='teacher'){
      marksBtn.style.display='';
      marksBtn.style.transition='opacity 1.5s ease';
      setTimeout(()=>{
        marksBtn.style.opacity='0';
        marksBtn.style.pointerEvents='none';
      },8000);
    } else {
      marksBtn.style.display='none';
    }
  }
  // Hide Jiiby FAB for parent
  const jb = document.getElementById('jiibyFab');
  if (jb) jb.style.display = (role === 'parent' || appPrefs.jiibyEnabled === false) ? 'none' : 'flex';

  // JIIBY nav toggle — teachers only
  const jnt = document.getElementById('jiibyNavToggle');
  if (jnt) jnt.style.display = (role === 'teacher') ? 'flex' : 'none';
  // Stat grid hidden for parent (admin/teacher numerical dashboard)
  const sg=document.getElementById('statGrid');if(sg)sg.style.display=role==='parent'?'none':'';
  // Parent always lands on dashboard
  const defaults={admin:'dashboard',teacher:'dashboard',parent:'dashboard'};
  navigateTo(defaults[role]||'dashboard');
}

// ===== LOGIN MODE TOGGLE =====
let _loginMode='staff';
function setLoginMode(mode){
  _loginMode=mode;
  document.getElementById('loginModeStaffBtn').classList.toggle('active',mode==='staff');
  document.getElementById('loginModeParentBtn').classList.toggle('active',mode==='parent');
  document.getElementById('loginStaffFields').style.display=mode==='staff'?'':'none';
  document.getElementById('loginParentFields').style.display=mode==='parent'?'':'none';
  const btn=document.getElementById('loginActionBtn');
  if(mode==='parent'){btn.textContent='👨‍👩‍👧 Parent Login';btn.setAttribute('onclick','attemptParentLogin()');}
  else{btn.textContent='🔐 LOGIN';btn.setAttribute('onclick','attemptLogin()');}
  document.getElementById('loginError').textContent='';
}

// ===== PARENT LOGIN =====
function normalizePhone(p){return (p||'').replace(/[^0-9]/g,'').replace(/^0+/,'').slice(-9);}
async function attemptParentLogin(){
  const errEl=document.getElementById('loginError');
  try{ if(typeof clearAllNotifications==='function')clearAllNotifications(); }catch(e){}
  const raw=document.getElementById('loginParentPhone').value.trim();
  const phone=normalizePhone(raw);
  if(phone.length<7){errEl.style.color='#f87171';errEl.textContent='⚠️ Enter a valid phone number';return;}
  errEl.style.color='#facc15';errEl.textContent='Checking...';
  // Rate-limit: 5 attempts per 15 minutes — stored locally on THIS device only
  const _rlKey='jiib_parent_rl_'+normalizePhone(raw);
  const _rlRaw=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(_rlKey)||'{"count":0,"since":0}');
  const _rlNow=Date.now();
  if(_rlNow-_rlRaw.since>15*60*1000){_rlRaw.count=0;_rlRaw.since=_rlNow;}
  if(_rlRaw.count>=5){
    const _wait=Math.ceil((15*60*1000-(_rlNow-_rlRaw.since))/60000);
    errEl.style.color='#f87171';errEl.textContent='⏳ Too many attempts on this device. Wait '+_wait+' min.';SFX.error();return;
  }
  _rlRaw.count++;localStorage.setItem(_rlKey,JSON.stringify(_rlRaw));
  // Pull latest data from cloud
  if(navigator.onLine && sb){
    try{
      const {data:row}=await sb.from('school_data').select('data').eq('school_id',(window.currentSchool&&window.currentSchool.id)).maybeSingle();
      if(row&&row.data){data=row.data;localStorage.setItem('schoolData',JSON.stringify(data));}
    }catch(e){}
  }
  const matches=[];
  Object.entries(data.terms||{}).forEach(([tk,t])=>{
    (t.students||[]).forEach(s=>{
      if(s.parentPhone&&normalizePhone(s.parentPhone)===phone){
        if(!matches.some(m=>m.id===s.id))matches.push({id:s.id,name:s.name,class:s.class,termKey:tk});
      }
    });
  });
  if(!matches.length){errEl.style.color='#f87171';errEl.textContent='✗ No child found with that phone number. Contact the school.';return;}
  // Check if parent is blocked
  const _blockedParents=data.blockedParents||JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('blockedParents_'+(window.currentSchool?.id||''))||'[]');
  if(_blockedParents.includes(phone)){errEl.style.color='#f87171';errEl.textContent='🚫 Your portal access has been restricted. Contact the school.';SFX.error();return;}
  window._parentPhone=phone;window._parentChildren=matches;window._userRole='parent';
  window._userDisplayName='Parent of '+matches[0].name.split(' ')[0];window._authedEmail=null;
  updateUserChip();applyRoleVisibility();
  const ov=document.getElementById('loginOverlay');ov.style.transition='opacity .5s';ov.style.opacity='0';
  setTimeout(()=>{ov.style.display='none';setLoginLock(false);},500);
  document.getElementById('loginParentPhone').value='';errEl.textContent='';
  reloadPrefsForCurrentUser();setupParentChildSelector();
  try{renderParentDashboard();}catch(e){}
  try{renderParentReport();}catch(e){}
  setTimeout(()=>showWelcomeMessage(),600);
  checkAndNotifyNewSchoolNews();
}

function setupParentChildSelector(){
  const sel=document.getElementById('parentChildSelect'),wrap=document.getElementById('parentChildSelector');
  if(!sel||!wrap)return;
  const kids=window._parentChildren||[];
  if(kids.length<=1){wrap.style.display='none';return;}
  wrap.style.display='';sel.innerHTML='';
  kids.forEach((k,i)=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=i;o.textContent=k.name.toUpperCase()+' ('+k.class+')';sel.appendChild(o);});
}
function listParentTermsForKid(kidId){
  const out=[];
  Object.entries(data.terms||{}).forEach(([tk,t])=>{
    if((t.students||[]).some(s=>s.id===kidId))out.push({key:tk,label:t.label||tk});
  });
  return out;
}
function setupParentTermSelector(kid){
  const sel=document.getElementById('parentTermSelect');if(!sel||!kid)return;
  const terms=listParentTermsForKid(kid.id);
  const prev=sel.value;
  sel.innerHTML='';
  if(!terms.length){const o=document.createElement('option');o.value='';o.textContent='No terms';sel.appendChild(o);return;}
  terms.forEach(t=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=t.key;o.textContent=t.label;sel.appendChild(o);});
  sel.value=terms.some(t=>t.key===prev)?prev:(terms.some(t=>t.key===kid.termKey)?kid.termKey:terms[terms.length-1].key);
}
function buildParentSummaryHTML(student,term){
  const subs=(data.subjects[student.class]||[]).filter(sub=>!student.subjects||student.subjects.includes(sub.name));
  const mks=term.marks||[];
  let totals=[],rows='';
  subs.forEach(sub=>{
    const m=mks.find(x=>x.studentId===student.id&&x.subject===sub.name);
    const tot=m?((m.aoi||0)+(m.midterm||0)+(m.end||0)):null;
    if(tot!=null)totals.push(tot);
    const g=tot!=null?getGrade(tot):{grade:'—',descriptor:'No marks recorded yet'};
    rows+=`<div class="parent-subj-row"><div><div style="font-weight:700;">${sub.name}</div><div class="descriptor">${g.descriptor}</div></div><div class="grade-pill">${g.grade}${tot!=null?' · '+tot+'/100':''}</div></div>`;
  });
  // Overall category (auto write-up)
  let category='Pending',catCls='warn',writeup='No marks have been recorded yet for this term.';
  if(totals.length){
    const avg=totals.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/totals.length;
    if(avg>=75){category='Excellent';catCls='';writeup=`${student.name.split(' ')[0]} is performing exceptionally well, demonstrating mastery across most subjects. Keep encouraging this consistency at home.`;}
    else if(avg>=60){category='Good';catCls='';writeup=`${student.name.split(' ')[0]} is doing well overall and shows a strong understanding in most areas. A little extra revision in weaker subjects will lift the average even higher.`;}
    else if(avg>=50){category='Fair';catCls='warn';writeup=`${student.name.split(' ')[0]} is coping but the performance is just average. Daily revision and supervised homework would make a noticeable difference.`;}
    else if(avg>=40){category='Needs Support';catCls='warn';writeup=`${student.name.split(' ')[0]} is struggling in several subjects. Please arrange a meeting with the class teacher and add structured study time at home.`;}
    else{category='Critical';catCls='danger';writeup=`${student.name.split(' ')[0]} is failing in most subjects. Urgent attention from both school and home is needed to recover this term.`;}
  }
  const badgeImg=window._schoolBadgeUrl||'badge.png';
  const className=(term.label||td().label||'');
  return `<div class="parent-summary-badge-row">
    <img src="${badgeImg}" alt="Badge">
    <div style="flex:1;">
      <div style="font-size:.78rem;color:#92400e;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:1.5px;">${(window._schoolDisplayName||window.currentSchool?.name||'').toUpperCase()}</div>
      <div style="font-size:1.05rem;font-weight:800;color:#78350f;">${student.name.toUpperCase()}</div>
      <div style="font-size:.82rem;color:#92400e;">${student.class} · ${student.house?student.house.toUpperCase():''} · ${className}</div>
    </div>
    <div style="text-align:right;"><div class="parent-category-pill ${catCls}">${category}</div></div>
  </div>
  <div class="card" style="margin-bottom:14px;padding:18px;">
    <h3 style="color:var(--primary);margin-bottom:8px;">📝 Class Teacher's Note</h3>
    <p style="font-size:.92rem;line-height:1.55;color:var(--text);">${writeup}</p>
  </div>
  <div class="card" style="margin-bottom:0;padding:0;">
    <div style="padding:14px 18px;border-bottom:2px solid var(--border);"><strong style="color:var(--primary);">📚 Subject Performance</strong></div>
    ${rows||'<div style="padding:24px;text-align:center;color:var(--text-muted);">No subjects recorded.</div>'}
  </div>`;
}
function renderParentReport(){
  const out=document.getElementById('parentReportContent');if(!out)return;
  const kids=window._parentChildren||[];if(!kids.length){out.innerHTML='<p style="color:var(--text-muted);text-align:center;padding:30px;">No child found.</p>';return;}
  const idx=parseInt(document.getElementById('parentChildSelect')?.value||'0')||0;
  const kid=kids[idx]||kids[0];
  setupParentTermSelector(kid);
  const tk=document.getElementById('parentTermSelect')?.value||kid.termKey;
  const term=data.terms[tk]||td();
  const student=(term.students||[]).find(s=>s.id===kid.id);
  if(!student){out.innerHTML='<p style="color:var(--text-muted);text-align:center;padding:30px;">No record for this term yet.</p>';return;}
  try{out.innerHTML=buildParentSummaryHTML(student,term);}
  catch(e){out.innerHTML='<p style="color:var(--danger);">Could not render summary: '+e.message+'</p>';}
}
// Open a specific child from parent dashboard list
function parentOpenChild(idx){
  const sel=document.getElementById('parentChildSelect');
  if(sel&&sel.options.length>idx)sel.value=idx;
  navigateTo('parentView');
  setTimeout(()=>renderParentReport(),50);
}
// Build the parent-only dashboard content (school news + class size + child cards)
function renderParentDashboard() {
  const wrap = document.getElementById('parentDashContent');
  if (!wrap) return;
  const kids = window._parentChildren || [];
  const news = data.schoolNews || null;
  const parentLabel = window._userDisplayName || 'Parent';

  // School Notice Board — distinct design
  let newsHtml = '';
  if (news && news.title) {
    const postedDate = news.updatedAt ? new Date(news.updatedAt).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {
      weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric'
    }) : '';
    newsHtml = `
      <div style="margin-bottom:22px;">
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-bottom:10px;">
          <div style="width:4px;height:28px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#e67e22,#f39c12);border-radius:2px;"></div>
          <span style="font-weight:800;font-size:1rem;color:var(--primary);letter-spacing:.5px;">📌 NOTICE BOARD</span>
          <span style="font-size:.72rem;color:var(--text-muted);margin-left:auto;">${postedDate}</span>
        </div>
        <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fffbeb,#fef3c7);border:2px solid #f59e0b;border-left:6px solid #e67e22;border-radius:12px;padding:20px 22px;box-shadow:0 4px 16px rgba(245,158,11,0.15);">
          <div style="font-size:.68rem;letter-spacing:2.5px;font-weight:800;color:#92400e;text-transform:uppercase;margin-bottom:6px;">${(window._schoolDisplayName||window.currentSchool?.name||'School').toUpperCase()} · OFFICIAL NOTICE</div>
          <div style="font-size:1.08rem;font-weight:800;color:#78350f;margin-bottom:10px;">${news.title}</div>
          <div style="height:1px;background:rgba(180,120,0,0.2);margin-bottom:12px;"></div>
          <div style="font-size:.93rem;line-height:1.65;color:#451a03;white-space:pre-wrap;">Dear ${parentLabel},\n\n${news.body || ''}</div>
          ${news.updatedBy ? `<div style="margin-top:14px;font-size:.75rem;color:#92400e;font-style:italic;">— Posted by ${news.updatedBy}</div>` : ''}
        </div>
      </div>`;
  } else {
    newsHtml = `
      <div style="background:var(--bg);border:2px dashed var(--border);border-radius:12px;padding:18px;text-align:center;margin-bottom:18px;color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.88rem;">
        📌 No school notices at the moment. Check back soon.
      </div>`;
  }

  // Children cards
  let childCards = '';
  if (kids.length) {
    childCards = `<div style="font-weight:800;color:var(--primary);font-size:1rem;margin:18px 0 10px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;">
      <span>👶</span> ${kids.length > 1 ? 'Your Children' : 'Your Child'}
    </div>`;
    kids.forEach((k, i) => {
      const term = data.terms[k.termKey] || td();
      const classmates = (term.students || []).filter(s => s.class === k.class).length;
      const initials = (k.name.split(' ').map(p => p[0]).join('').slice(0, 2) || 'S').toUpperCase();
      childCards += `<div class="parent-child-card" onclick="parentOpenChild(${i})">
        <div class="child-avatar">${initials}</div>
        <div class="child-info">
          <div class="name">${k.name.toUpperCase()}</div>
          <div class="meta">${k.class} · class size: <strong>${classmates}</strong></div>
        </div>
        <div class="open-arrow">›</div>
      </div>`;
    });
  } else {
    childCards = `<p style="color:var(--text-muted);padding:18px;text-align:center;font-size:.88rem;">No child registered to your phone number yet. Please contact the school.</p>`;
  }

  // Chat button
  const chatBtn = `<div style="margin-top:16px;">
    <button onclick="openParentChat()" style="width:100%;padding:14px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#25d366,#128c7e);color:white;border:none;border-radius:12px;font-size:.95rem;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:8px;box-shadow:0 4px 16px rgba(37,211,102,0.3);">
      💬 Message the School Admin
    </button>
  </div>`;

  wrap.innerHTML = newsHtml + childCards + chatBtn;
}

// ===== ONLINE PRESENCE (Realtime) =====
let _presenceChannel=null;let _onlineUsers={};let _onlineNotifiedKeys=new Set();
function startPresence(){
  if(!sb||!window._authedEmail)return;
  if(_presenceChannel){try{_presenceChannel.unsubscribe();}catch(e){}_presenceChannel=null;}
  _onlineNotifiedKeys=new Set();
  const _presChName='staff-online:'+(window.currentSchool&&window.currentSchool.id||'global');
  const ch=sb.channel(_presChName,{config:{presence:{key:window._authedEmail}}});
  ch.on('presence',{event:'sync'},()=>{
    _onlineUsers=ch.presenceState();
    renderOnlineTeachers();
  });
  ch.on('presence',{event:'join'},({key,newPresences})=>{
    if(!isAdmin())return;
    if(key.toLowerCase()===(window._authedEmail||'').toLowerCase())return;
    if(_onlineNotifiedKeys.has(key))return; // already notified this session — avoid spam on every mark-entry presence update
    _onlineNotifiedKeys.add(key);
    const np=newPresences&&newPresences[0];
    const nm=(np&&(np.name||np.email))||key;
    const role=(np&&np.role)||'teacher';
    if(role==='admin')return;
    if(typeof showAppleNotif==='function')showAppleNotif('🔔 Teacher Online',nm+' just signed in','blue',6000);
    if(typeof addNotif==='function')addNotif('Teacher Online',nm+' signed in','green');
  });
  ch.subscribe(async(status)=>{
    if(status==='SUBSCRIBED'){
      await ch.track({email:window._authedEmail,name:window._userDisplayName||window._authedEmail,role:window._userRole||'teacher',ts:Date.now()});
    }
  });
  _presenceChannel=ch;
}

// Real-time listener: when another device saves data, pull it immediately
let _realtimeDataChannel=null;
function startRealtimeDataSync(){
  if(!sb||!window.currentSchool||_realtimeDataChannel)return;
  _realtimeDataChannel=sb.channel('school-data-sync-'+window.currentSchool.id)
    .on('postgres_changes',{
      event:'UPDATE',
      schema:'public',
      table:'school_data',
      filter:'school_id=eq.'+window.currentSchool.id
    },async(payload)=>{
      // Never pull while teacher has unsaved marks in progress
      if(_pendingDirty) return;
      // Never pull while teacher is actively on marks page
      if(isTeacher()&&document.getElementById('marks')?.classList.contains('active')) return;
      const cloudTs=new Date(payload.new?.updated_at||0).getTime();
      const localUpdStr=localStorage.getItem(_schoolKey+'_localUpdatedAt')||'0';
      const localTs=new Date(localUpdStr).getTime();
      if(cloudTs>localTs+1000){
        await syncPullFromCloud();
        // Refresh the current view silently
        try{
          const activePg=document.querySelector('.page.active');
          if(activePg){
            const id=activePg.id;
            if(id==='dashboard')loadDashboard();
            else if(id==='students')loadStudents();
            else if(id==='marks')loadMarksEntry();
            else if(id==='performance')loadPerformance();
          }
        }catch(e){}
        showAppleNotif('🔄 Data Updated','Another device saved changes — refreshed','blue',3000);
      }
    })
    .subscribe();
}
function stopRealtimeDataSync(){
  if(_realtimeDataChannel){
    try{_realtimeDataChannel.unsubscribe();}catch(e){}
    _realtimeDataChannel=null;
  }
}

function stopPresence(){if(_presenceChannel){try{_presenceChannel.untrack();_presenceChannel.unsubscribe();}catch(e){}_presenceChannel=null;}_onlineUsers={};_onlineNotifiedKeys=new Set();renderOnlineTeachers();}
function renderOnlineTeachers(){
  // Deduplicate by email — same teacher on multiple tabs/devices counts once
  const seen=new Set();
  const list=Object.values(_onlineUsers).flat().filter(u=>{
    if(u.role==='admin')return false;
    const key=(u.email||'').toLowerCase();
    if(seen.has(key))return false;
    seen.add(key);return true;
  });
  const c=document.getElementById('onlineTeachersCount');if(c)c.textContent=list.length;
  const box=document.getElementById('onlineTeachersList');if(!box)return;
  if(!list.length){box.innerHTML='<p style="color:var(--text-muted);text-align:center;padding:20px;font-size:.85rem;">No teachers online right now.</p>';return;}
  box.innerHTML=list.map(u=>'<div style="padding:10px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;"><span style="width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:#22c55e;display:inline-block;"></span><div style="flex:1;"><div style="font-weight:600;font-size:.88rem;">'+(u.name||u.email)+'</div><div style="font-size:.72rem;color:var(--text-muted);">'+u.email+'</div></div></div>').join('');
}
function toggleOnlineTeachersPanel(){const p=document.getElementById('onlineTeachersPanel');p.style.display=p.style.display==='none'||!p.style.display?'block':'none';if(p.style.display==='block')renderOnlineTeachers();}

// ===== TEACHER SUBJECTS PICKER (Manage Teachers form) =====
// Returns a map of "class::subject" → owner display name (first teacher who has it)
function _existingSubjectAssignments(){
  const owners={};
  const list=getCachedAllowedUsers();
  list.forEach(u=>{
    if(!u||(u.role&&u.role==='admin'))return;
    (u.subjects||[]).forEach(s=>{
      const k=s.class+'::'+s.subject;
      if(!owners[k])owners[k]=u.display_name||u.email||'another teacher';
    });
  });
  return owners;
}
function renderTeacherSubjectsPicker(){
  const grid=document.getElementById('teacherSubjectsPicker');if(!grid)return;
  const owners=_existingSubjectAssignments();
  let html='';
  ['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4'].forEach(cls=>{
    const subs=(data.subjects&&data.subjects[cls])||[];
    if(!subs.length)return;
    const total=subs.length;
    const taken=subs.filter(s=>owners[cls+'::'+s.name]).length;
    const free=total-taken;
    html+=`<div class="subj-picker-class" data-class="${cls}">
      <div class="subj-picker-class-head" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('collapsed')">
        <span>${cls} <span class="pick-count">${free} free / ${total}</span></span>
        <span><button type="button" class="select-all-btn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();pickAllAvailable('${cls}')">Tick all free</button> <span style="margin-left:6px;font-size:.75rem;opacity:.6;">▼</span></span>
      </div>
      <div class="subj-picker-class-body">`;
    subs.forEach(s=>{
      const k=cls+'::'+s.name;
      const owner=owners[k];
      const isTaken=!!owner;
      html+=`<label class="subj-pill ${isTaken?'disabled checked':''}" title="${isTaken?('Already assigned to '+owner):'Available — tick to assign'}">
        <input type="checkbox" class="ts-cb" value="${k}" ${isTaken?'checked disabled':''} onchange="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('checked',this.checked)">
        <span>${s.name}</span>${isTaken?`<span class="owner-tag">${owner}</span>`:''}
      </label>`;
    });
    html+='</div></div>';
  });
  grid.innerHTML=html||'<p style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.85rem;text-align:center;padding:14px;">No subjects configured yet. Add subjects under 📚 Manage Subjects first.</p>';
}
function pickAllAvailable(cls){
  document.querySelectorAll(`.subj-picker-class[data-class="${cls}"] .ts-cb:not(:disabled)`).forEach(cb=>{
    cb.checked=true;cb.parentElement.classList.add('checked');
  });
}
function getPickedTeacherSubjects(){
  // Only NEW (not-disabled) ticks count — disabled ones are already assigned to other teachers
  return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#teacherSubjectsPicker .ts-cb:checked:not(:disabled)')).map(cb=>{const [c,s]=cb.value.split('::');return{class:c,subject:s};});
}

// ===== AUTO-SAVE MARKS (teacher mode) =====
const _autoSaveTimers={};
const _inputDebounceTimers={};
function setMarkInputState(input){
  const v=input.value.trim();
  input.classList.remove('mark-empty','mark-filled','mark-invalid');
  if(v==='')input.classList.add('mark-empty');
  else input.classList.add('mark-filled');
}

// Fires on every keystroke, but only gives instant visual feedback — it
// does NOT validate or save yet. That happens in commitMarkInput() once
// the teacher pauses typing. This is what stops "16" from getting silently
// saved as "1": previously, every keystroke triggered an immediate
// validate+save, so typing "1" then "6" saved "1" first, and when "16"
// turned out to exceed the max, the box was cleared but the earlier saved
// "1" was never removed.
function autoSaveMark(input){
  const sid=input.dataset.id,f=input.dataset.field;
  const cls=document.getElementById('marksClass').value,sub=document.getElementById('marksSubject').value;
  if(!sid||!f||!cls||!sub)return;

  input.classList.remove('mark-empty','mark-filled','mark-invalid');
  input.classList.add(input.value.trim()===''?'mark-empty':'mark-saving');

  const debKey=sid+'::'+f+'::'+sub;
  clearTimeout(_inputDebounceTimers[debKey]);
  _inputDebounceTimers[debKey]=setTimeout(()=>commitMarkInput(input,sid,f,cls,sub), 450);
}

async function commitMarkInput(input,sid,f,cls,sub){
  // ── Validation runs once, on the FINAL typed value ──────────────────────
  const v=input.value.trim();
  const n=v===''?null:parseFloat(v);
  const schema=getMarksSchema();
  const schemaField=schema.find(c=>c.field===f);
  const max=schemaField?schemaField.max:(f==='c3'?80:10);
  if(v!==''&&(isNaN(n)||n<0||n>max)){
    input.value='';
    input.classList.remove('mark-filled','mark-invalid','mark-saving');
    input.classList.add('mark-empty');
    if(typeof showAppleNotif==='function')showAppleNotif('❌ Invalid Mark',f.toUpperCase()+' must be 0–'+max+'. Cleared.','red',3200);
    if(typeof SFX!=='undefined'&&SFX.error)SFX.error();
    // Also clear any stale value already sitting in the data for this
    // field, so a rejected entry can never leave a stale number behind.
    const currInvalid=td();
    const mkInvalid=(currInvalid.marks||[]).find(m=>m.studentId===sid&&m.subject===sub);
    if(mkInvalid && mkInvalid[f]!=null){
      mkInvalid[f]=null;
      try{localStorage.setItem(_schoolKey,JSON.stringify(data));}catch(e){}
      _pendingDirty=true;
      localStorage.setItem(_schoolKey+'_localUpdatedAt', new Date().toISOString());
      clearTimeout(_pushTimer);
      _pushTimer=setTimeout(guardedPush, 600);
    }
    return;
  }
  // ── End validation ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  setMarkInputState(input);
  broadcastMarksPresence(cls,sub);

  const curr=td();
  let mk=(curr.marks||[]).find(m=>m.studentId===sid&&m.subject===sub);
  if(!mk){mk={studentId:sid,subject:sub,class:cls};if(!curr.marks)curr.marks=[];curr.marks.push(mk);}
  mk[f]=n;
  try{localStorage.setItem(_schoolKey,JSON.stringify(data));}catch(e){}
  _pendingDirty=true;
  localStorage.setItem(_schoolKey+'_localUpdatedAt', new Date().toISOString());
  clearTimeout(_pushTimer);
  _pushTimer=setTimeout(guardedPush, 600);

  if(!navigator.onLine||!sb||!window.currentSchool){
    idbPush({type:'mark',termKey:data.activeTerm||'',studentId:sid,subject:sub,fields:{[f]:n}});
    setSyncStatus('offline','⚡ Offline — mark saved locally');
    return;
  }

  const timerKey=sid+'::'+sub;
  clearTimeout(_autoSaveTimers[timerKey]);
  input.classList.add('mark-saving');

  _autoSaveTimers[timerKey]=setTimeout(async()=>{
    try{
      const localMk=(td().marks||[]).find(m=>m.studentId===sid&&m.subject===sub)||{};
      const row={
        school_id:window.currentSchool.id,
        term_key:data.activeTerm,
        student_id:sid,
        subject:sub,
        class:cls,
        updated_at:new Date().toISOString(),
        updated_by:window._authedEmail||'teacher'
      };
      getMarksSchema().forEach(c=>{ row[c.field]=localMk[c.field]??null; });

      const efRes=await fetch(`${window._sbUrl}/functions/v1/save-mark`,{
        method:'POST',
        headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':'Bearer '+(await sb.auth.getSession()).data?.session?.access_token},
        body:JSON.stringify({...row,school_id:window.currentSchool.id})
      });
      if(!efRes.ok){const ej=await efRes.json().catch(()=>({}));throw new Error(ej.error||'Save failed');}
      input.classList.remove('mark-saving');
      setMarkInputState(input);
      setSyncStatus('saved','✓ Mark saved');
    }catch(e){
      input.classList.remove('mark-saving');
      setSyncStatus('saved','⚠ Mark sync error — saved locally');
      idbPush({type:'mark',termKey:data.activeTerm||'',studentId:sid,subject:sub,fields:{[f]:n}});
    }
  },400);
}

async function recordDeviceSession(){
  if(!sb||!window._authedEmail||!window.currentSchool)return;
  try{
    const ua=navigator.userAgent;
    let dev='Unknown Device';
    if(/iPhone/i.test(ua))dev='iPhone';
    else if(/iPad/i.test(ua))dev='iPad';
    else if(/Android/i.test(ua))dev='Android '+(/Mobile/i.test(ua)?'Phone':'Tablet');
    else if(/Windows/i.test(ua))dev='Windows PC';
    else if(/Mac/i.test(ua))dev='Mac';
    else if(/Linux/i.test(ua))dev='Linux';
    const browsers=[['Edg/','Edge'],['OPR/','Opera'],['Chrome','Chrome'],['Firefox','Firefox'],['Safari','Safari']];
    for(const [k,n] of browsers){if(ua.includes(k)){dev+=' · '+n;break;}}
    const {data:sess}=await sb.auth.getSession();
    const jti=sess?.session?.access_token?btoa(sess.session.access_token.split('.')[1]||'').slice(0,16):String(Date.now());
    await sb.rpc('upsert_device_session',{_school_id:window.currentSchool.id,_email:window._authedEmail,_role:window._userRole||'teacher',_device_name:dev,_ip_hint:'',_session_token:jti});
  }catch(e){console.warn('recordDeviceSession:',e.message);}
}

async function hashPwd(pwd, salt){
  const enc=new TextEncoder().encode((salt||'jiib-salt-2026')+'|'+pwd);
  const buf=await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', enc);
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf)).map(b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
}
async function cacheOfflineCredentials(email, pwd){
  const h=await hashPwd(pwd, email);
  const cache=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('jiib_offline_creds')||'{}');
  cache[email.toLowerCase().trim()]={hash:h, savedAt:new Date().toISOString()};
  localStorage.setItem('jiib_offline_creds', JSON.stringify(cache));
}
async function verifyOfflineLogin(email, pwd){
  const cache=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('jiib_offline_creds')||'{}');
  const rec=cache[email.toLowerCase().trim()]; if(!rec) return false;
  const h=await hashPwd(pwd, email);
  return h===rec.hash;
}
function getCachedAllowedUsers(){return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('jiib_allowed_users')||'[]');}
function isEmailAllowedOffline(email){const e=email.toLowerCase().trim();return getCachedAllowedUsers().some(u=>(u.email||'').toLowerCase().trim()===e);}

function schedulePush(){
  if(!window._authedEmail) return;
  _pendingDirty=true;
  clearTimeout(_pushTimer);
  _pushTimer=setTimeout(guardedPush, 1500);
}

async function syncPushToCloud(){
  if(!sb||!window._authedEmail) return;
  if(!navigator.onLine){setNetStatus('offline','Offline · Pending'); return;}
  try{
    setNetStatus('syncing','Syncing...');
    const nowIso=new Date().toISOString();
    const {error}=await sb.from('school_data').upsert({school_id: window.currentSchool.id, data:data, updated_at:nowIso, updated_by:window._authedEmail}, {onConflict:'school_id'});
    if(error) throw error;
    localStorage.setItem('schoolDataCloudUpdatedAt', nowIso);
    _pendingDirty=false;
    setNetStatus('online','Online · Synced');
  }catch(e){console.warn('Push failed',e); setNetStatus('online','Online · Sync error'); if(!window._syncErrTimer){window._syncErrTimer=setTimeout(()=>{window._syncErrTimer=null;if(navigator.onLine)setNetStatus('online','Online · Synced');},5000);}}
}

async function syncPullFromCloud(){
  if(!sb||!navigator.onLine||!window._authedEmail||!window.currentSchool) return false;
  try{
    setNetStatus('syncing','Pulling…');
    const {data:row,error}=await sb.from('school_data')
      .select('data, updated_at')
      .eq('school_id',window.currentSchool.id)
      .maybeSingle();
    if(error) throw error;
    if(row&&row.data){
      const cloudTs=new Date(row.updated_at||0).getTime();
      _lastCloudUpdatedAt=cloudTs;
      const localTs=new Date(localStorage.getItem(_schoolKey+'_localUpdatedAt')||'0').getTime();
      if(cloudTs>localTs+500){
        let incoming=row.data;
        if(!incoming.terms) incoming=migrateOldData(incoming);
        if(!incoming.activeTerm||!incoming.terms[incoming.activeTerm]){
          const fk=Object.keys(incoming.terms||{})[0];
          incoming.activeTerm=fk||'2026-T1';
          if(!incoming.terms[incoming.activeTerm])
            incoming.terms[incoming.activeTerm]={label:'Term 1 — 2026',...createDefaultTermData()};
        }
        // ALWAYS merge — never raw-overwrite. _pendingDirty is in-memory
        // only and resets on every page reload, so it can't be trusted as
        // the sole signal that local has unsynced data. Merging is always
        // safe (it takes the higher mark value per field), so this never
        // loses data whether or not _pendingDirty was accurate.
        const wasPendingDirty=_pendingDirty;
        const merged=deepMergeSchoolData(incoming,data);
        const mergeChangedSomething=JSON.stringify(merged)!==JSON.stringify(incoming);
        data=merged;
        correctPassword=data.lockPassword||DEFAULT_PASSWORD;
        const updIso=row.updated_at||new Date().toISOString();
        localStorage.setItem(_schoolKey,JSON.stringify(data));
        localStorage.setItem(_schoolKey+'_localUpdatedAt',updIso);
        localStorage.setItem('schoolDataCloudUpdatedAt',updIso);
        if(wasPendingDirty||mergeChangedSomething){
          _pendingDirty=true;
          clearTimeout(_pushTimer);
          _pushTimer=setTimeout(guardedPush,500);
        } else {
          _pendingDirty=false;
        }
      } else if(_pendingDirty){
        await guardedPush();
      }
    } else {
      await guardedPush();
    }
    _lastPullAt=Date.now();
    setNetStatus('online','Online · Synced');
    return true;
  }catch(e){
    console.warn('[SYNC] Pull failed:',e.message);
    setNetStatus('online','Online · Sync error');
    return false;
  }
}

async function manualSync(){
  if(!navigator.onLine){showAppleNotif('📡 Offline','Connect to internet to sync','orange');return;}
  if(!window._authedEmail){showAppleNotif('🔒 Login required','','orange');return;}
  await syncPullFromCloud();
  refreshTermSelect();loadDashboard();
  showAppleNotif('🔄 Synced','Latest data loaded from cloud','green');
}

window.addEventListener('online', ()=>{setNetStatus('online','Online'); if(window._authedEmail){syncPullFromCloud();}});
window.addEventListener('offline',()=>setNetStatus('offline','Offline'));

// ===================== ALLOWED USERS PANEL =====================
async function loadAllowedUsers(){
  // Toggle admin-only visibility on the Manage Teachers form + render subject picker
  const form=document.getElementById('manageUsersForm');
  const lock=document.getElementById('manageUsersLock');
  if(form&&lock){
    if(isAdmin()){form.style.display='';lock.style.display='none';renderTeacherSubjectsPicker();}
    else{form.style.display='none';lock.style.display='';lock.textContent='🔒 Only the admin ('+ADMIN_EMAILS.join(', ')+') can add or remove teachers. You are signed in as '+(window._authedEmail||'unknown')+'.';}
  }
  let list=getCachedAllowedUsers();
  if(navigator.onLine && sb){
    try{
      const {data:rows, error}=await sb.from('allowed_users').select('*').eq('school_id', window.currentSchool.id).order('created_at',{ascending:true});
      if(!error && rows){list=rows; localStorage.setItem('jiib_allowed_users', JSON.stringify(rows));}
    }catch(e){}
  }
  const tbody=document.querySelector('#usersTable tbody'); if(!tbody) return;
  tbody.innerHTML='';
  if(!list.length){tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="5" class="users-table-empty">No teachers yet'+(isAdmin()?' — add one above.':'.')+'</td></tr>'; return;}
  list.forEach(u=>{
    const tr=document.createElement('tr');
    const isMe=window._authedEmail && u.email && u.email.toLowerCase()===window._authedEmail.toLowerCase();
    const isAdminRow=ADMIN_EMAILS.includes((u.email||'').toLowerCase());
    const role=isAdminRow?'admin':(u.role||'teacher');
    const youBadge=isMe?'<span class="badge badge-blue" style="font-size:.7rem;">You</span> ':'';
    const subs=(u.subjects||[]);
    const subSummary=subs.length?subs.map(s=>'<span class="badge badge-blue" style="font-size:.68rem;margin:1px;">'+s.subject+' · '+s.class+'</span>').join(''):'<span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.78rem;">—</span>';
    let actionCell;
    if(isMe||isAdminRow){actionCell='<span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.78rem;">—</span>';}
    else if(isAdmin()){actionCell=`<button class="btn-sm btn-danger" onclick="removeAllowedUser('${escapeJS(u.email)}')">🗑 Remove</button>`;}
    else{actionCell='<span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.78rem;">view only</span>';}
    tr.innerHTML=`<td><strong>${u.email}</strong> ${youBadge}</td><td>${u.display_name||'—'}</td><td><span class="role-badge ${role}">${role}</span></td><td>${subSummary}</td><td>${actionCell}</td>`;
    tbody.appendChild(tr);
  });
}
async function addAllowedUser(){
  if(!isAdmin()){showAppleNotif('🔒 Admin only','Only the admin can add teachers','red');return;}
  if(!navigator.onLine){showAppleNotif('📡 Offline','Connect to internet to add a teacher','orange');return;}
  const email=document.getElementById('newUserEmail').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const name=document.getElementById('newUserName').value.trim();
  const pwd=document.getElementById('newUserPassword').value;
  if(!email||!pwd){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Missing fields','Email + password required','orange');return;}
  if(pwd.length<6){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Weak password','Min 6 characters','orange');return;}
  const teacherSubs=getPickedTeacherSubjects();
  if(!teacherSubs.length){showAppleNotif('⚠️ No Subject Assigned','You must assign at least one subject to this teacher before adding them.','red',6000);SFX.error();return;}
  if(!sb){showAppleNotif('❌ Not configured','','red');return;}
  showCardOverlay('settings','Creating user...');
  try{
    // Attempt signup — silently ignore if user already exists in another school
    const {error:sErr}=await sb.auth.signUp({email, password:pwd, options:{data:{display_name:name}}});
    if(sErr && !/already registered|user already exists|email already/i.test(sErr.message)) throw sErr;
    // Always add/update this teacher in allowed_users for THIS school — different school = new entry
    const {error:iErr}=await sb.from('allowed_users').upsert({school_id: window.currentSchool.id, email, display_name:name||null, role:'teacher', subjects:teacherSubs}, {onConflict:'school_id,email'});
    if(iErr) throw iErr;
    document.getElementById('newUserEmail').value='';
    document.getElementById('newUserName').value='';
    document.getElementById('newUserPassword').value='';
    document.querySelectorAll('#teacherSubjectsPicker .ts-cb').forEach(cb=>cb.checked=false);
    showAppleNotif('✅ Teacher added',`${name||email} — ${teacherSubs.length} subject(s) assigned`,'green');
    SFX.success();
    await loadAllowedUsers();
  }catch(e){
    showAppleNotif('❌ Add failed', e.message||'Unknown error','red',8000);
  }finally{hideCardOverlay('settings');}
}
async function removeAllowedUser(email){
  if(!isAdmin()){showAppleNotif('🔒 Admin only','Only the admin can remove users','red');return;}
  if(ADMIN_EMAILS.includes((email||'').toLowerCase())){showAppleNotif('🔒 Cannot remove admin','','red');return;}
  const ok=await customConfirm(`Remove <strong>${email}</strong> from the allowed users list?<br>They will no longer be able to log in. (Their auth account is preserved — re-add to restore access.)`,'Remove User');
  if(!ok) return;
  if(!navigator.onLine){showAppleNotif('📡 Offline','Connect to internet to remove a user','orange');return;}
  try{
    const {error}=await sb.from('allowed_users').delete().eq('school_id', window.currentSchool.id).eq('email', email);
    if(error) throw error;
    // Also clear offline credentials
    const cache=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('jiib_offline_creds')||'{}');
    delete cache[email.toLowerCase()];
    localStorage.setItem('jiib_offline_creds', JSON.stringify(cache));
    showAppleNotif('🗑 Removed', email,'orange');
    await loadAllowedUsers();
  }catch(e){showAppleNotif('❌ Remove failed', e.message,'red');}
}

// ===================== YEAR ↔ TERM SYNC =====================
function syncReportYearFromTerm(){
  const m=(data.activeTerm||'').match(/^(\d{4})-T\d/);
  if(m){
    const inp=document.getElementById('reportYearInput');
    if(inp){inp.value=m[1]; appPrefs.reportYear=m[1]; savePrefs();}
  }
}
function getReportYear(){
  const inp=document.getElementById('reportYearInput');
  if(inp && /^\d{4}$/.test(inp.value.trim())) return inp.value.trim();
  return appPrefs.reportYear||'2026';
}
function onReportYearChanged(){
  const y=document.getElementById('reportYearInput').value.trim();
  if(!/^\d{4}$/.test(y)) return;
  appPrefs.reportYear=y; savePrefs();
  // If a term in this year exists, switch to it; otherwise just store the year for reports
  const candidate=Object.keys(data.terms).find(k=>k.startsWith(y+'-'));
  if(candidate && candidate!==data.activeTerm){
    data.activeTerm=candidate; saveData();
    refreshTermSelect(); loadDashboard();
    showAppleNotif('📅 Switched term',`Now viewing: ${data.terms[candidate].label}`,'blue');
  }
}

// CARD OVERLAY SPINNER
function showCardOverlay(id,msg='Loading...'){const o=document.getElementById(id+'Overlay');const m=document.getElementById(id+'OverlayMsg');if(o)o.classList.add('show');if(m)m.textContent=msg;}
function hideCardOverlay(id){const o=document.getElementById(id+'Overlay');if(o)o.classList.remove('show');}

// DASHBOARD HERO SPINNER
function showDashHeroSpinner(t='Loading...',s=''){const el=document.getElementById('dashHeroSpinner');const tt=document.getElementById('dashHeroSpinnerText');const ss=document.getElementById('dashHeroSpinnerSub');if(el)el.style.display='flex';if(tt)tt.textContent=t;if(ss)ss.textContent=s;}
function hideDashHeroSpinner(){const el=document.getElementById('dashHeroSpinner');if(el)el.style.display='none';}

// TERM MANAGEMENT
function buildTermKey(y,n){return`${y}-T${n}`;}
function refreshTermSelect() {
  const sel = document.getElementById('activeTermSelect');
  const wrap = sel ? sel.closest('.term-selector') : null;

  // Teachers: hide term selector entirely — they always use admin's active term
  if (isTeacher()) {
    if (wrap) wrap.style.display = 'none';
    return;
  }

  // Parents: also hide
  if (isParent()) {
    if (wrap) wrap.style.display = 'none';
    return;
  }

  // Admin: show only terms belonging to the current active year
  if (wrap) wrap.style.display = '';
  sel.innerHTML = '';

  const activeYear = (data.activeTerm || '').match(/^(\d{4})/)?.[1] || new Date().getFullYear().toString();

  Object.entries(data.terms).forEach(([k, t]) => {
    const termYear = k.match(/^(\d{4})/)?.[1];
    if (termYear !== activeYear) return; // skip other years
    const o = document.createElement('option');
    o.value = k;
    o.textContent = t.label;
    if (k === data.activeTerm) o.selected = true;
    sel.appendChild(o);
  });

  const b = document.getElementById('termStatusBadge');
  const curr = td();
  if (b) {
    b.textContent = curr.locked ? 'LOCKED' : 'ACTIVE';
    b.style.background = curr.locked ? 'rgba(231,76,60,0.7)' : 'rgba(39,174,94,0.5)';
  }
  updateTermDisplay();
}

function updateTermDisplay() {
  const term = td();
  const nameEl = document.getElementById('currentTermName');
  const displayWrap = document.getElementById('termNavDisplay');
  const role = window._userRole;

  if (displayWrap) {
    displayWrap.style.display = (role === 'teacher' || role === 'parent') ? 'flex' : 'none';
  }

  if (nameEl && term) nameEl.textContent = term.label || 'No Term';

  // Main selector badge (admin)
  const b1 = document.getElementById('termStatusBadge');
  // Display badge (teachers/parents)
  const b2 = document.getElementById('termStatusBadgeDisplay');

  [b1, b2].forEach(b => {
    if (!b) return;
    b.textContent = term?.locked ? 'LOCKED' : 'ACTIVE';
    b.style.background = term?.locked ? 'rgba(231,76,60,0.7)' : 'rgba(39,174,94,0.5)';
  });
}

function switchTerm(key){
  if(!data.terms[key])return;
  showCardOverlay('marks','Switching term...');
  setTimeout(()=>{data.activeTerm=key;saveData();refreshTermSelect();loadDashboard();hideCardOverlay('marks');showAppleNotif('📅 Term Switched',`Now viewing: ${data.terms[key].label}`,'blue');SFX.notify();syncReportYearFromTerm();updateTermDisplay();},800);
}
async function deleteVerificationCodesForTerm(termKey){
  if(!sb || !window.currentSchool || !termKey) return;
  try{
    await sb.from('report_verification_codes').delete().eq('school_id', window.currentSchool.id).eq('term_key', termKey);
  }catch(e){ console.warn('[Verify Codes] cleanup failed:', e.message); }
}
function openNewTerm(){
  const keys=Object.keys(data.terms),lk=keys[keys.length-1],m=lk.match(/(\d{4})-T(\d)/);
  let y=m?parseInt(m[1]):2026,n=m?parseInt(m[2]):1;n++;if(n>3){y++;n=1;}
  const nk=buildTermKey(y,n),names=['Term 1','Term 2','Term 3'],label=`${names[n-1]} — ${y}`;
  if(data.terms[nk]){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Term Exists',`${label} already exists`,'orange');return;}
  const prevTermKey=data.activeTerm;
  showCardOverlay('settings','Creating new term...');
  setTimeout(()=>{
    data.terms[nk]={label,...createDefaultTermData()};
    const prev=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(td().students));data.activeTerm=nk;td().students=prev;
    document.getElementById('reportYearInput').value=String(y);appPrefs.reportYear=String(y);savePrefs();
    saveData();refreshTermSelect();loadDashboard();loadTermMgmt();hideCardOverlay('settings');
    showAppleNotif('✅ New Term Opened',`${label} is now active`,'green');SFX.success();
    deleteVerificationCodesForTerm(prevTermKey);
  },800);
}
async function closeCurrentTerm(){
  const curr=td();if(curr.locked){showAppleNotif('ℹ️ Already Locked','','blue');return;}
  const ok=await customConfirm(`Lock <strong>${curr.label}</strong>?<br>Marks will become read-only.`,'Close Term');if(!ok)return;
  showCardOverlay('settings','Locking term...');
  setTimeout(()=>{curr.locked=true;curr.closedDate=new Date().toISOString();saveData();refreshTermSelect();loadTermMgmt();hideCardOverlay('settings');showAppleNotif('🔒 Term Closed',`${curr.label} locked`,'orange');SFX.lock();},600);
}
function loadTermMgmt(){
  const c=document.getElementById('termMgmtCard');if(!c)return;
  c.innerHTML=Object.entries(data.terms).map(([key,t])=>{
    const ia=key===data.activeTerm,dc=t.locked?'locked':(ia?'active':'open'),std=t.students?t.students.length:0,mks=t.marks?t.marks.length:0;
    return`<div class="term-item ${ia?'active-term':''} ${t.locked?'locked-term':''}"><div class="term-dot ${dc}"></div><div style="flex:1;"><div style="font-weight:700;font-size:.92rem;color:var(--text);">${t.label}</div><div style="font-size:.75rem;color:var(--text-muted);">${std} students · ${mks} mark entries${t.locked?' · 🔒 Locked':''}</div></div>${ia?'<span class="badge badge-green">ACTIVE</span>':''}${t.locked?'<span class="badge badge-red">LOCKED</span>':''}${!ia&&!t.locked?`<button onclick="switchTerm('${key}')" class="btn-sm" style="font-size:.75rem;">Switch</button>`:''}</div>`;
  }).join('');
}

// LOGIN
const MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS=3,LOCKOUT_SECS=30,SS_A='jiib_loginAttempts',SS_L='jiib_lockoutUntil';
function getAttempts(){return parseInt(sessionStorage.getItem(SS_A)||'0');}
function setAttempts(n){sessionStorage.setItem(SS_A,String(n));}
function getLockoutUntil(){return parseInt(sessionStorage.getItem(SS_L)||'0');}
function setLockoutUntil(t){sessionStorage.setItem(SS_L,String(t));}
function clearLoginSecurity(){sessionStorage.removeItem(SS_A);sessionStorage.removeItem(SS_L);}

async function attemptLogin(){
  const until=getLockoutUntil();if(until&&Date.now()<until){resumeCountdown(until);return;}
  const emailEl=document.getElementById('loginEmailInput');
  const input=document.getElementById('loginPasswordInput'),errEl=document.getElementById('loginError');
  const email=(emailEl.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const pwd=input.value;
  if(!email||!pwd){errEl.textContent='⚠️ Enter email and password.';return;}
  errEl.style.color='#f87171';
  errEl.textContent='Checking...';
  const proceed=async()=>{
    clearLoginSecurity();SFX.unlock();
    window._authedEmail=email;
    // Per-account notification scoping — wipe the previous user's notif inbox
    try{ if(typeof clearAllNotifications==='function')clearAllNotifications(); }catch(e){}
    // Fetch role + subjects + display_name from allowed_users
    let role='teacher',subs=[],dname=email;
    if(ADMIN_EMAILS.includes(email.toLowerCase())){role='admin';dname='Admin';}
    if(navigator.onLine && sb){
      try{
        const {data:row}=await sb.from('allowed_users').select('*').eq('school_id', window.currentSchool && window.currentSchool.id).eq('email',email).maybeSingle();
        if(row){role=row.role||role;subs=row.subjects||[];dname=row.display_name||dname;}
      }catch(e){}
    }else{
      const cached=getCachedAllowedUsers().find(u=>u.email===email);
      if(cached){role=cached.role||role;subs=cached.subjects||[];dname=cached.display_name||dname;}
    }
    if(ADMIN_EMAILS.includes(email.toLowerCase()))role='admin';
    window._userRole=role;window._userSubjects=subs;window._userDisplayName=dname;
    updateUserChip();
    applyRoleVisibility();
    const ov=document.getElementById('loginOverlay');ov.style.transition='opacity 0.5s ease';ov.style.opacity='0';document.body.classList.add('app-ready');
    setTimeout(()=>{ov.style.display='none'; setLoginLock(false);},500);
    input.value='';errEl.textContent='';resetAutoLockTimer();
    if(navigator.onLine){await syncPullFromCloud();refreshTermSelect();loadDashboard();loadAllowedUsers();}
    else{setNetStatus('offline','Offline · Local data');}
    // Persistent teacher login log — recorded even if admin is offline.
    // Teachers append a log entry; admins scan for unseen entries on every login.
    try{
      if(role==='teacher'){
        if(!Array.isArray(data.loginLog))data.loginLog=[];
        data.loginLog.push({email,name:dname,ts:Date.now()});
        if(data.loginLog.length>200)data.loginLog=data.loginLog.slice(-200);
        saveData();
      }else if(role==='admin'){
        scanTeacherLoginLogForAdmin();
      }
    }catch(e){}
    reloadPrefsForCurrentUser();
    startPresence();
    startRealtimeDataSync();
    try{ await recordDeviceSession(); }catch(e){}
    setTimeout(()=>showWelcomeMessage(),600);
    setTimeout(()=>jiibyProactiveNudge(),15000);
    setTimeout(()=>migrateExistingStudentsToTable(),5000);
    setTimeout(()=>checkAdminChatNotifications(),2000);
    setTimeout(()=>checkSuperAdminReplies(),3000);
  };
  const failOnce=(msg)=>{
    SFX.error();let a=getAttempts()+1;setAttempts(a);
    input.classList.remove('login-shake');void input.offsetWidth;input.classList.add('login-shake');
    setTimeout(()=>input.classList.remove('login-shake'),700);
    if(a>=MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS){setAttempts(0);const lu=Date.now()+LOCKOUT_SECS*1000;setLockoutUntil(lu);document.getElementById('loginOverlay').style.display='none';resumeCountdown(lu);return;}
    const left=MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS-a;errEl.textContent=msg+` (${left} attempt${left!==1?'s':''} left)`;
    input.style.borderColor='#ef4444';setTimeout(()=>{input.style.borderColor='rgba(255,255,255,0.18)';},1500);input.select();
  };
  // ONLINE PATH
  if(navigator.onLine && sb){
    try{
      let {data:auth, error}=await sb.auth.signInWithPassword({email, password:pwd});
      if(error){
        // If signInWithPassword fails, check whether the email exists in allowed_users
        // but the Supabase Auth account was never created (Edge Function not deployed during registration).
        // In that case, auto-create the auth account now using the supplied password.
        if(window.currentSchool && window.currentSchool.id){
          try{
            const {data:pendingUser}=await sb.from('allowed_users')
              .select('email,role,display_name')
              .eq('school_id',window.currentSchool.id)
              .eq('email',email)
              .maybeSingle();
            if(pendingUser){
              const {error:suErr}=await sb.auth.signUp({email,password:pwd,options:{data:{display_name:pendingUser.display_name||'School Admin'}}});
              if(!suErr || /already registered|already exists|user already/i.test(suErr?.message||'')){
                const {data:auth2,error:err2}=await sb.auth.signInWithPassword({email,password:pwd});
                if(!err2){auth=auth2;error=null;}
              }
            }
          }catch(autoErr){ /* ignore auto-create errors, fall through to normal error handling */ }
        }
        if(error){
          if(await verifyOfflineLogin(email, pwd) && isEmailAllowedOffline(email)){await proceed(); return;}
          failOnce('✗ Invalid email or password.'); return;
        }
      }
      // Verify email is in allowed_users gate
      const {data:authData2}=await sb.auth.getUser();
      if (authData2 && authData2.user) await loadUserRolesForSchool(authData2.user.id);
      // Check authorisation via user_roles table (multi-tenant)
      // Check authorisation via user_roles table (multi-tenant)

 if (!isSuperAdmin()) {
  const schoolId = window.currentSchool && window.currentSchool.id;
  if (!schoolId) { await sb.auth.signOut(); failOnce('✗ School not found.'); return; }

  // ALWAYS check allowed_users first — it's the source of truth for school access
  const {data:allowed} = await sb.from('allowed_users')
    .select('email, role, subjects, display_name')
    .eq('school_id', schoolId)
    .eq('email', email)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (!allowed) {
    // Not in allowed_users — check ADMIN_EMAILS as last resort
    if (!ADMIN_EMAILS.includes(email)) {
      await sb.auth.signOut();
      failOnce('✗ Not authorised for this school. Contact your admin.');
      return;
    }
    // ADMIN_EMAILS member — treat as admin
    window._schoolRoles = ['admin'];
  } else {
    window._schoolRoles = [allowed.role || 'teacher'];
    if (allowed.subjects) window._userSubjects = allowed.subjects;
    if (allowed.display_name) window._userDisplayName = allowed.display_name;
  }
}
  
  // Auto-seed user_roles if missing (fixes newly registered school admins)
if (!window._schoolRoles || window._schoolRoles.length === 0) {
  try {
    const schoolId = window.currentSchool && window.currentSchool.id;
    const isAdminEmail = ADMIN_EMAILS.includes(email);
    const roleToSet = isAdminEmail ? 'admin' : 'teacher';
    if (schoolId && authData2?.user?.id) {
      await sb.from('user_roles').upsert({
        user_id: authData2.user.id,
        school_id: schoolId,
        role: roleToSet,
        display_name: window._userDisplayName || email
      }, { onConflict: 'user_id,school_id,role' });
      window._schoolRoles = [roleToSet];
    }
  } catch(e) { console.warn('user_roles seed:', e.message); }
}
      await cacheOfflineCredentials(email, pwd);

      // Check if teacher is blocked
    const _blockedTeachers=data.blockedTeachers||JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('blockedTeachers_'+(window.currentSchool?.id||''))||'[]');
    if(window._userRole==='teacher'&&_blockedTeachers.includes(email.toLowerCase())){
      await sb.auth.signOut();
      errEl.style.color='#f87171';errEl.textContent='🚫 Your access has been restricted. Contact the school admin.';SFX.error();return;
    }

      // Refresh allowed users cache for offline use
      try{const {data:rows}=await sb.from('allowed_users').select('*').eq('school_id', window.currentSchool.id); if(rows) localStorage.setItem('jiib_allowed_users', JSON.stringify(rows));}catch(e){}
      await proceed();
    }catch(e){
      if(await verifyOfflineLogin(email, pwd) && isEmailAllowedOffline(email)){await proceed(); return;}
      failOnce('✗ '+(e.message||'Login failed.'));
    }
  }else{
    // OFFLINE PATH
    if(await verifyOfflineLogin(email, pwd) && isEmailAllowedOffline(email)){await proceed(); return;}
    failOnce('✗ No internet & no offline credentials for this user.');
  }
}
async function logoutAuthAndApp(){
  // Give a pending mark save a brief window to actually leave the device
  // BEFORE we null out the auth email — otherwise guardedPush() silently
  // no-ops (it requires window._authedEmail) and the mark is stranded.
  if(_pendingDirty && navigator.onLine){
    try{
      clearTimeout(_pushTimer);
      await Promise.race([guardedPush(), new Promise(res=>setTimeout(res,2500))]);
    }catch(e){}
  }
  // Non-blocking lock — never hangs the UI on slow networks
  try{stopPresence();}catch(e){}
  try{stopRealtimeDataSync();}catch(e){}
  // Fire and forget the network sign-out; don't await it
  try{ if(sb) sb.auth.signOut().catch(()=>{}); }catch(e){}
  window._authedEmail=null; window._userRole=null; window._userSubjects=null; window._userDisplayName=null;
  window._parentPhone=null; window._parentChildren=null;
  // Per-account notification scoping — wipe the inbox on sign-out
  try{ if(typeof clearAllNotifications==='function')clearAllNotifications(); }catch(e){}
  updateUserChip();
  setLoginMode('staff');
  // Re-apply role visibility so leftover role-only widgets reset
  try{ document.querySelectorAll('[data-roles]').forEach(el=>el.style.display=''); }catch(e){}
  logoutApp();
}
function resumeCountdown(lockUntil){
  const cd=document.getElementById('loginCountdown');cd.classList.add('show');
  function tick(){const r=Math.ceil((lockUntil-Date.now())/1000);if(r<=0){clearLoginSecurity();cd.classList.remove('show');const o=document.getElementById('loginOverlay');o.style.display='flex';o.style.opacity='1';document.getElementById('loginPasswordInput').value='';document.getElementById('loginError').textContent='';return;}const el=document.getElementById('countdownNumber');if(el)el.textContent=r;setTimeout(tick,400);}
  tick();
}
function toggleLoginPwd(){const i=document.getElementById('loginPasswordInput'),e=document.getElementById('loginEyeIcon');i.type=i.type==='password'?'text':'password';e.textContent=i.type==='password'?'👁':'🙈';}
function logoutApp(){const ov=document.getElementById('loginOverlay');ov.style.opacity='0';ov.style.display='flex'; setLoginLock(true); setTimeout(()=>{ov.style.transition='opacity 0.4s ease';ov.style.opacity='1';},10);document.getElementById('loginPasswordInput').value='';document.getElementById('loginError').textContent='';SFX.lock();}
let autoLockTimer=null;
function resetAutoLockTimer(){
  clearTimeout(autoLockTimer);
  // Only lock if user has been completely idle for 30 minutes AND is not a parent
  // (parents have no Supabase session so we lock them sooner at 20 min)
  const timeout=isParent()?20*60*1000:30*60*1000;
  autoLockTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
    // Before locking, try silently refreshing the session first
    if(sb&&navigator.onLine&&window._authedEmail){
      sb.auth.refreshSession().then(({error})=>{
        if(error){
          // Genuine expiry — lock
          logoutApp();
          document.getElementById('loginError').textContent='🔒 Session timed out. Please log in again.';
        }else{
          // Session refreshed silently — stay logged in, reschedule
          resetAutoLockTimer();
          if(_pendingDirty) guardedPush();
        }
      }).catch(()=>{
        logoutApp();
        document.getElementById('loginError').textContent='🔒 Session timed out.';
      });
    }else if(!navigator.onLine){
      // Offline — keep session alive, retry when back online
      resetAutoLockTimer();
    }else{
      logoutApp();
      document.getElementById('loginError').textContent='🔒 Session timed out.';
    }
  },timeout);
}
['click','keydown','mousemove','touchstart','scroll'].forEach(e=>{
  document.addEventListener(e,()=>{
    if(document.getElementById('loginOverlay').style.display==='none') resetAutoLockTimer();
  },{passive:true});
});

// ── Auto-refresh Supabase token every 10 minutes while logged in ──────────────
setInterval(async()=>{
  if(!sb||!window._authedEmail||!navigator.onLine)return;
  try{
    const {data:s}=await sb.auth.getSession();
    if(!s||!s.session)return;
    // Refresh if token expires within 15 minutes
    const exp=s.session.expires_at;
    if(exp&&(exp*1000-Date.now())<15*60*1000){
      await sb.auth.refreshSession();
    }
    // Also push any pending dirty data
    if(_pendingDirty) guardedPush();
    // Pull if nothing pushed in last 2 minutes
    else if(Date.now()-_lastPullAt>2*60*1000) syncPullFromCloud();
  }catch(e){console.warn('[AutoRefresh]',e.message);}
},10*60*1000);

function showForgotPassword(){
  const m=document.getElementById('forgotPasswordModal');
  document.getElementById('forgotStep1').style.display='block';document.getElementById('forgotStep2').style.display='none';
  ['forgotVerifyInput','forgotNewPwd','forgotConfirmPwd'].forEach(id=>document.getElementById(id).value='');
  ['forgotError1','forgotError2'].forEach(id=>document.getElementById(id).textContent='');
  m.style.display='flex';
}
function closeForgotPassword(){document.getElementById('forgotPasswordModal').style.display='none';}
function verifyForgotPassword(){
  const v=document.getElementById('forgotVerifyInput').value.trim();
  if(!v){document.getElementById('forgotError1').textContent='⚠️ Please enter the default password.';return;}
  if(v===DEFAULT_PASSWORD){document.getElementById('forgotStep1').style.display='none';document.getElementById('forgotStep2').style.display='block';}
  else{closeForgotPassword();document.getElementById('loginOverlay').style.display='none';showForgotTimeoutPage();}
}
function showForgotTimeoutPage(){const p=document.createElement('div');p.style.cssText='position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:99999999;background:#060a12;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-family:Segoe UI,sans-serif;text-align:center;pointer-events:all;';p.innerHTML='<div style="opacity:.18;font-size:3.5rem;margin-bottom:28px;">🔒</div><p style="color:rgba(255,255,255,0.18);font-size:1rem;letter-spacing:4px;text-transform:uppercase;">Session Timed Out</p>';document.body.appendChild(p);}
function saveForgotPassword(){
  const np=document.getElementById('forgotNewPwd').value,cp=document.getElementById('forgotConfirmPwd').value,e=document.getElementById('forgotError2');
  if(np.length<4){e.textContent='Minimum 4 characters.';return;}if(np!==cp){e.textContent='Passwords do not match.';return;}
  correctPassword=np;data.lockPassword=np;saveData();closeForgotPassword();
  document.getElementById('loginError').textContent='✅ Password reset! Log in with new password.';document.getElementById('loginError').style.color='#4ade80';
  setTimeout(()=>{document.getElementById('loginError').style.color='#f87171';document.getElementById('loginError').textContent='';},5000);
}

// WELCOME MESSAGE
function getTimeGreeting(){
  const h=new Date().getHours();
  if(h>=7&&h<12)return{greeting:'Good Morning!',emoji:'☀️',sub:'Start your day right with JIIB$CORE'};
  if(h>=12&&h<17)return{greeting:'Good Afternoon!',emoji:'😃',sub:'Keep up the great work today'};
  return{greeting:'Good Evening!',emoji:'🌇',sub:'Review today\'s academic progress'};
}
function showWelcomeMessage(){
  const {greeting,emoji,sub}=getTimeGreeting();
  const curr=td(),sc=(curr.students||[]).length,tc=Object.keys(data.terms).length;
  document.getElementById('welcomeEmoji').textContent=emoji;
  document.getElementById('welcomeGreeting').textContent=greeting;
  const nameEl=document.getElementById('welcomeUserName');
  if(nameEl){const nm=window._userDisplayName||window._authedEmail||'';nameEl.textContent=nm?nm:'';}
  document.getElementById('welcomeSub').textContent=sub;
  if(isParent()){
    const kids=window._parentChildren||[];
    if(kids.length>1){
      document.getElementById('welcomeJiiby').textContent='✦ You have '+kids.length+' children registered. Pick one from your dashboard to view their summary.';
      document.getElementById('welcomeStats').innerHTML=kids.map(k=>'<span class="badge badge-blue" style="margin:2px;">'+k.name.split(' ')[0]+' · '+k.class+'</span>').join('');
    }else{
      document.getElementById('welcomeJiiby').textContent='✦ Welcome — your child\'s summary is on the dashboard';
      document.getElementById('welcomeStats').textContent=kids.length+' child registered';
    }
  }else{
    document.getElementById('welcomeJiiby').textContent=`✦ JIIBY AI is online — monitoring ${sc} student${sc!==1?'s':''} across ${tc} term${tc!==1?'s':''}`;
    document.getElementById('welcomeStats').textContent=`Active Term: ${td().label}`;
  }
  document.getElementById('welcomeMessage').classList.add('show');
}
function dismissWelcome(){document.getElementById('welcomeMessage').classList.remove('show');SFX.success();}

// NOTIFICATIONS
const notifHistory=[];let notifUnreadCount=0;let bellShakeInterval=null;
function startBellShake(){if(bellShakeInterval)return;bellShakeInterval=setInterval(()=>{if(notifUnreadCount>0){const b=document.getElementById('notifBellBtn');if(b){b.classList.remove('bell-shaking');void b.offsetWidth;b.classList.add('bell-shaking');setTimeout(()=>b.classList.remove('bell-shaking'),700);}}},5000);}
function stopBellShake(){if(bellShakeInterval){clearInterval(bellShakeInterval);bellShakeInterval=null;}}
function showAppleNotif(title,subtitle,color='blue',duration=5000){
  SFX.notify();
  const stack=document.getElementById('notifStack'),id='notif_'+Date.now();
  const cMap={green:'#27ae60',red:'#e74c3c',blue:'#3b82f6',orange:'#e67e22',purple:'#7c3aed'};
  const iMap={green:'✅',red:'🔴',blue:'ℹ️',orange:'⚠️',purple:'✦'};
  const bMap={green:'rgba(39,174,96,0.12)',red:'rgba(231,76,60,0.12)',blue:'rgba(59,130,246,0.12)',orange:'rgba(230,126,34,0.12)',purple:'rgba(124,58,237,0.12)'};
  const toast=document.createElement('div');toast.className='notif-toast';toast.id=id;
  toast.innerHTML=`<div class="notif-left-bar" style="background:${cMap[color]||'#3b82f6'};"></div><div class="notif-icon-wrap" style="background:${bMap[color]||'rgba(59,130,246,0.12)'};">${iMap[color]||'ℹ️'}</div><div class="notif-body"><div class="notif-title">${title}</div><div class="notif-sub">${subtitle}</div><div class="notif-time">just now</div></div><button class="notif-close" onclick="dismissNotif('${id}')">✕</button><div class="notif-toast-bar ${color}" style="width:100%;transition:width ${duration/1000}s linear;"></div>`;
  stack.appendChild(toast);
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>toast.classList.add('show')));
  setTimeout(()=>{const bar=toast.querySelector('.notif-toast-bar');if(bar)bar.style.width='0';},100);
  setTimeout(()=>dismissNotif(id),duration);
  if (isParent() && color === 'purple') return;
  notifHistory.unshift({id,title,subtitle,color,time:new Date(),read:false});
  notifUnreadCount++;updateNotifBadge();refreshNotifDrawer();startBellShake();
}
function dismissNotif(id){const el=document.getElementById(id);if(!el)return;el.classList.remove('show');el.classList.add('dismiss');setTimeout(()=>el.remove(),400);}
function updateNotifBadge(){const b=document.getElementById('notifBadge');if(notifUnreadCount>0){b.classList.add('show');b.textContent=notifUnreadCount>9?'9+':notifUnreadCount;}else{b.classList.remove('show');stopBellShake();}}
function toggleNotifDrawer(){
  const d=document.getElementById('notifDrawer'),ov=document.getElementById('notifDrawerOverlay');
  d.classList.toggle('open');ov.classList.toggle('show');
  if(d.classList.contains('open')){notifUnreadCount=0;updateNotifBadge();notifHistory.forEach(n=>n.read=true);refreshNotifDrawer();}
}
function closeNotifDrawer(){document.getElementById('notifDrawer').classList.remove('open');document.getElementById('notifDrawerOverlay').classList.remove('show');}
function refreshNotifDrawer(){
  const body=document.getElementById('notifDrawerBody');if(!body)return;
  if(notifHistory.length===0){body.innerHTML='<p style="text-align:center;color:var(--text-muted);padding:30px 0;font-size:.9rem;">No notifications yet</p>';return;}
  const cMap={green:'#27ae60',red:'#e74c3c',blue:'#3b82f6',orange:'#e67e22',purple:'#7c3aed'};
  const unread=notifHistory.filter(n=>!n.read),read=notifHistory.filter(n=>n.read);
  let html='';
  if(unread.length>0){html+=`<div class="notif-section-label">Unread (${unread.length})</div>`+unread.map(n=>renderNI(n,cMap)).join('');}
  if(read.length>0){html+=`<div class="notif-section-label" style="margin-top:${unread.length>0?'12px':'0'};">Read</div>`+read.slice(0,20).map(n=>renderNI(n,cMap)).join('');}
  body.innerHTML=html;
}
function renderNI(n,cMap){const ago=getTimeAgo(n.time);const isJ=n.color==='purple';return`<div class="notif-drawer-item ${!n.read?'unread':''} ${isJ?'jiiby-nudge':''}" ${isJ?'onclick="openJiiby()"':''}><div style="font-size:1.2rem;flex-shrink:0;margin-top:2px;">${n.color==='green'?'✅':n.color==='red'?'🔴':n.color==='orange'?'⚠️':n.color==='purple'?'✦':'ℹ️'}</div><div style="flex:1;min-width:0;"><div style="font-weight:600;font-size:.88rem;color:var(--text);border-left:2px solid ${cMap[n.color]||'#3b82f6'};padding-left:6px;">${n.title}</div><div style="font-size:.78rem;color:var(--text-muted);margin-top:2px;">${n.subtitle}</div><div style="font-size:.7rem;color:var(--text-muted);margin-top:4px;">${ago}</div></div></div>`;}
function clearAllNotifications(){notifHistory.length=0;notifUnreadCount=0;updateNotifBadge();refreshNotifDrawer();stopBellShake();}
function getTimeAgo(date){const diff=Date.now()-date.getTime(),s=Math.floor(diff/1000),m=Math.floor(s/60),h=Math.floor(m/60);if(s<10)return'just now';if(s<60)return`${s}s ago`;if(m<60)return`${m}m ago`;if(h<24)return`${h}h ago`;return date.toLocaleDateString();}
setInterval(()=>{if(document.getElementById('notifDrawer').classList.contains('open'))refreshNotifDrawer();},30000);

// BACKGROUND WATCHER
let lastWatcher=0;
function runBackgroundWatcher(){
  const now=Date.now();if(now-lastWatcher<5*60*1000)return;lastWatcher=now;
  const curr=td();if(!curr||!curr.students)return;
  const ghosts=curr.students.filter(s=>{const sc=(s.subjects||[]).length,mc=(curr.marks||[]).filter(m=>m.studentId===s.id).length;return sc>0&&mc===0;});
  if(ghosts.length>5)showAppleNotif('👻 Missing Marks',`${ghosts.length} students have no marks yet`,'orange',8000);
  ['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4'].forEach(cls=>{const c=curr.students.filter(s=>s.class===cls).length;if(c>40)showAppleNotif('📢 Large Class',`${cls} now has ${c} students`,'blue',6000);});
}
setInterval(runBackgroundWatcher,5*60*1000);
