// ===================== JIIBY LIVE CHAT (LLM via Supabase Edge Function) =====================
function _jiibyChatKey(){
  const u=window._authedEmail||window._parentPhone||'anon';
  const s=(window.currentSchool&&window.currentSchool.id)||'root';
  return 'jiiby_chat::'+s+'::'+u;
}
let jiibyChatHistory = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(_jiibyChatKey())||'[]');
function renderJiibyChat(){
  if(!jiibyOpen) openJiiby();
  // Reload history scoped to current user+school each time chat opens
  jiibyChatHistory = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(_jiibyChatKey())||'[]');
  const body=document.getElementById('jiibyBody');
  body.innerHTML=`
    <div style="font-size:.75rem;color:var(--jiiby);font-weight:700;letter-spacing:1px;text-transform:uppercase;padding:0 0 8px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(124,58,237,0.2);margin-bottom:10px;">💬 Live Chat with JIIBY</div>
    <div id="jiibyChatLog" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px;margin-bottom:14px;"></div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:flex-end;">
      <textarea id="jiibyChatInput" rows="2" placeholder="Ask JIIBY anything about your school data..." style="flex:1;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:10px;font-family:inherit;font-size:.88rem;resize:vertical;background:var(--bg);color:var(--text);" onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'&&!event.shiftKey){event.preventDefault();jiibyChatSend();}"></textarea>
      <button onclick="jiibyChatSend()" style="background:var(--jiiby);color:white;border:none;padding:10px 14px;border-radius:10px;cursor:pointer;font-weight:600;">Send</button>
    </div>
    <div style="margin-top:8px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;">
      <span style="font-size:.7rem;color:var(--text-muted);">Press Enter to send · Shift+Enter for new line</span>
      <span onclick="jiibyChatClear()" style="font-size:.72rem;color:var(--danger);cursor:pointer;">🗑 Clear chat</span>
    </div>
  `;
  jiibyChatRedraw();
}
function jiibyChatRedraw(){
  const log=document.getElementById('jiibyChatLog'); if(!log) return;
  if(!jiibyChatHistory.length){
    log.innerHTML=`<div style="text-align:center;color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.85rem;padding:14px;">✦ Hi! I'm JIIBY. Ask me things like:<br><em>"Who are the weakest students in S.3 ICT?"</em><br><em>"Summarise this term's performance."</em></div>`;
    return;
  }
  log.innerHTML='';
  jiibyChatHistory.forEach(m=>{
    const isUser=m.role==='user';
    const div=document.createElement('div');
    div.style.cssText=`align-self:${isUser?'flex-end':'flex-start'};max-width:85%;padding:10px 13px;border-radius:14px;font-size:.88rem;line-height:1.45;background:${isUser?'rgba(124,58,237,0.15)':'var(--bg)'};border:1px solid ${isUser?'rgba(124,58,237,0.3)':'var(--border)'};`;
    div.innerHTML=`<div style="font-size:.7rem;font-weight:700;color:${isUser?'var(--jiiby)':'var(--text-muted)'};margin-bottom:4px;">${isUser?'You':'✦ JIIBY'}</div>${escapeHtml(m.content).replace(/\n/g,'<br>')}`;
    log.appendChild(div);
  });
  log.scrollTop=log.scrollHeight;
  log.parentElement.scrollTop=log.parentElement.scrollHeight;
}
function escapeHtml(s){return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));}
function jiibyChatClear(){jiibyChatHistory=[];localStorage.removeItem(_jiibyChatKey());jiibyChatRedraw();}
function buildJiibySchoolContext(){
  const curr=td()||{};const students=curr.students||[];const marks=curr.marks||[];
  const subjectsBy=data.subjects||{};
  const optCls=getOptionalSubjectsClass();
  const classOrder=['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4'];
  const summary={
    schoolName: window._schoolDisplayName || window.currentSchool?.name || data?.settings?.schoolName || 'Unknown School',
    schoolSlug: window.currentSchool?.slug || '',
    activeTerm:data.activeTerm,
    termLabel:curr.label,
    termLocked:!!curr.locked,
    totalStudents:students.length,
    totalMarkRecords:marks.length,
    classes:{},
    allTerms:Object.keys(data.terms||{}).map(k=>({key:k,label:data.terms[k].label,locked:!!data.terms[k].locked,students:(data.terms[k].students||[]).length}))
  };
  classOrder.forEach(cls=>{
    const cs=students.filter(s=>s.class===cls);
    const allSubs=(subjectsBy[cls]||[]);
    const needsOpt=classOrder.indexOf(cls)>=classOrder.indexOf(optCls);
    const _schema=getMarksSchema();
    // IMPORTANT: this must match getStudentPerf() exactly (used by Academic Giants/Dwarfs)
    // — average is over ALL subjects for the class, missing marks count as 0.
    // Previously this only averaged subjects that had marks entered, which gave
    // JIIBY a different "best performer" than the Performance page.
    const studentSummaries=cs.slice(0,80).map(s=>{
      const subs=needsOpt?allSubs.filter(sub=>s.subjects&&s.subjects.includes(sub.name)):allSubs;
      let tot=0;const subjScores={};
      subs.forEach(sub=>{
        const m=marks.find(x=>x.studentId===s.id&&x.subject===sub.name)||{};
        const t=_schema.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+(m[c.field]||0),0);
        subjScores[sub.name]=t;
        tot+=t;
      });
      const avg=subs.length?Number((tot/subs.length).toFixed(1)):0;
      return{name:s.name, avg, subjectsOffered:subs.length, subjects:subjScores};
    });
    const ranked=[...studentSummaries].sort((a,b)=>b.avg-a.avg);
    summary.classes[cls]={
      count:cs.length,
      subjects:allSubs.map(s=>s.name),
      students:studentSummaries,
      topPerformer: ranked.length?ranked[0].name:null,
      topPerformerAvg: ranked.length?ranked[0].avg:null,
      rankedByAverage: ranked.slice(0,10).map(r=>({name:r.name, avg:r.avg}))
    };
  });
  return summary;
}
async function jiibyChatSend(){
  const inp=document.getElementById('jiibyChatInput');
  const msg=(inp.value||'').trim(); if(!msg) return;
  if(!sb||!window._authedEmail){showAppleNotif('🔒 Login required','','orange');return;}
  jiibyChatHistory.push({role:'user',content:msg});
  inp.value='';
  jiibyChatRedraw();
  const log=document.getElementById('jiibyChatLog');
  const typing=document.createElement('div');
  typing.id='jiibyChatTyping';
  typing.style.cssText='align-self:flex-start;padding:10px 13px;color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.85rem;';
  typing.innerHTML='<span class="jiiby-typing"><span></span><span></span><span></span></span> JIIBY is thinking...';
  log.appendChild(typing);
  try{
    const ctx=buildJiibySchoolContext();
    const {data:resp, error}=await sb.functions.invoke('jiiby-chat',{
      body:{ messages: jiibyChatHistory.slice(-10), schoolContext: ctx }
    });
    if(error) throw error;
    const reply=(resp && (resp.reply||resp.message||resp.content))||'(No response)';
    jiibyChatHistory.push({role:'assistant',content:reply});
    if(jiibyChatHistory.length>40) jiibyChatHistory=jiibyChatHistory.slice(-40);
    localStorage.setItem(_jiibyChatKey(), JSON.stringify(jiibyChatHistory));
  }catch(e){
    jiibyChatHistory.push({role:'assistant',content:'⚠️ Chat unavailable: '+(e.message||'unknown error')+'\n\nMake sure the jiiby-chat Edge Function is deployed in Supabase and OPENAI_API_KEY is set. See SETUP.md → Stage 2.'});
  }finally{
    const t=document.getElementById('jiibyChatTyping'); if(t) t.remove();
    jiibyChatRedraw();
  }
}
function jiibyProactiveNudge(){
  if(isParent())return;
  if(appPrefs.jiibyEnabled===false)return;
  if(document.getElementById('loginOverlay').style.display!=='none'){setTimeout(()=>jiibyProactiveNudge(),2000);return;}
  const n=[{title:'✦ JIIBY has insights for you',subtitle:'Tap to see at-risk students →',color:'purple'},{title:'✦ Academic analysis ready',subtitle:'JIIBY found something interesting',color:'purple'}];
  const x=n[Math.floor(Math.random()*n.length)];showAppleNotif(x.title,x.subtitle,x.color,8000);
  document.getElementById('jiibFabBadge').textContent='!';document.getElementById('jiibFabBadge').classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(jiibyProactiveNudge,15*60*1000);
}
function jiibyAnalyse(type){
  if(!jiibyOpen)openJiiby();
  const body=document.getElementById('jiibyBody');
  const allMsgs={all:['Scanning all records...','Checking mark completeness...','Identifying patterns...','Building full report...'],risk:['Scanning marks...','Identifying struggling students...','Calculating thresholds...'],class:['Analysing class averages...','Comparing classes...'],subject:['Checking subject data...','Finding strongest & weakest...'],term:['Comparing term records...','Calculating trends...'],surprise:['Looking for surprises...']};
  const msgs=allMsgs[type]||['Processing...','Generating insights...'];
  body.innerHTML=`<div class="jiiby-typing"><span></span><span></span><span></span><div style="font-size:.8rem;color:var(--text-muted);margin-left:4px;">${msgs[0]}</div></div>`;
  let mi=0;const iv=setInterval(()=>{if(++mi>=msgs.length){clearInterval(iv);return;}body.innerHTML=`<div class="jiiby-typing"><span></span><span></span><span></span><div style="font-size:.8rem;color:var(--text-muted);margin-left:4px;">${msgs[mi]}</div></div>`;},600);
  setTimeout(()=>{clearInterval(iv);renderJiibyInsights(generateJiibyInsights(type),body);},msgs.length*600+400);
}
function generateJiibyInsights(type){
  const curr=td();if(!curr)return[];
  const students=curr.students||[],marks=curr.marks||[],ins=[],allKeys=Object.keys(data.terms);
  function gavg(s){const subs=(data.subjects[s.class]||[]).filter(sub=>!s.subjects||s.subjects.includes(sub.name));if(!subs.length)return 0;let t=0;subs.forEach(sub=>{const m=marks.find(x=>x.studentId===s.id&&x.subject===sub.name);t+=(m?(m.aoi||0)+(m.midterm||0)+(m.end||0):0);});return t/subs.length;}
  const h=new Date().getHours(),greet=h<12?'Good morning':'Good evening';
  if(students.length===0){ins.push({icon:'📭',color:'blue',title:'System is Empty — Ready to Begin',body:`I've scanned the entire system — it's completely empty. No students enrolled yet.<br><br>To get started:<br>• Go to <strong>Settings</strong> to add subjects<br>• Then go to <strong>Students</strong> to enrol students<br>• Then enter marks in <strong>Marks Entry</strong>`});return ins;}
  const withMarks=students.filter(s=>marks.some(m=>m.studentId===s.id));
  if(withMarks.length===0&&(type==='all'||type==='risk')){ins.push({icon:'📝',color:'orange',title:`${students.length} Students Enrolled — No Marks Yet`,body:`I can see <strong>${students.length} students</strong> enrolled but no marks have been entered yet for <strong>${curr.label}</strong>.<br><br>Please go to <strong>Marks Entry</strong> to begin entering marks. I'll analyse performance as soon as data is available.`});return ins;}
  if(type==='all'||type==='risk'){
    let te=0,tf=0;students.forEach(s=>{const subs=(data.subjects[s.class]||[]).filter(sub=>!s.subjects||s.subjects.includes(sub.name));subs.forEach(sub=>{te+=3;const m=marks.find(x=>x.studentId===s.id&&x.subject===sub.name);if(m){if(m.aoi!=null)tf++;if(m.midterm!=null)tf++;if(m.end!=null)tf++;}});});
    const pct=te>0?Math.round((tf/te)*100):0;
    const atRisk=students.filter(s=>gavg(s)<50&&gavg(s)>0).sort((a,b)=>gavg(a)-gavg(b));
    if(atRisk.length>0){ins.push({icon:'🔴',color:'red',title:`${atRisk.length} Student${atRisk.length!==1?'s':''} At Risk`,body:`Students averaging below 50%:<br><br>${atRisk.slice(0,5).map(s=>`<strong>${s.name.toUpperCase()}</strong> (${s.class}) — ${gavg(s).toFixed(1)} avg`).join('<br>')}${atRisk.length>5?`<br><em>...and ${atRisk.length-5} more</em>`:''}`});}
    else if(withMarks.length>0){ins.push({icon:'✅',color:'green',title:'No Students At Risk',body:`All ${withMarks.length} students with marks are above 50%. Excellent!`});}
    const ghosts=students.filter(s=>!marks.some(m=>m.studentId===s.id));
    if(ghosts.length>0){ins.push({icon:'👻',color:'orange',title:`${ghosts.length} Students With No Marks`,body:`${ghosts.slice(0,4).map(s=>`<strong>${s.name.toUpperCase()}</strong> (${s.class})`).join('<br>')}${ghosts.length>4?`<br>...and ${ghosts.length-4} more`:''}`});}
    ins.push({icon:'📊',color:'blue',title:`Overview — ${curr.label}`,body:`<strong>Students:</strong> ${students.length} enrolled<br><strong>With marks:</strong> ${withMarks.length}<br><strong>Data completion:</strong> ${pct}%<br><strong>Terms on record:</strong> ${allKeys.length}`,sparkData:{value:pct}});
  }
  if(type==='class'||type==='all'){
    ['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4'].forEach(cls=>{const cs=students.filter(s=>s.class===cls);if(!cs.length)return;const avgs=cs.map(s=>gavg(s)).filter(a=>a>0);if(!avgs.length){ins.push({icon:'📊',color:'blue',title:`${cls} — No Marks Yet`,body:`${cs.length} students enrolled but no marks entered yet.`});return;}const avg=(avgs.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/avgs.length).toFixed(1);const g=getGrade(parseFloat(avg));const top=cs.filter(s=>gavg(s)>=70).length,low=cs.filter(s=>gavg(s)<50&&gavg(s)>0).length;ins.push({icon:'📊',color:'blue',title:`${cls} — ${g.descriptor}`,body:`<strong>Class average:</strong> ${avg}/100<br><strong>Top performers (≥70):</strong> ${top}<br><strong>At-risk (<50):</strong> ${low}<br><strong>With marks:</strong> ${avgs.length}/${cs.length}`,sparkData:{value:parseFloat(avg)}});});
  }
  if(type==='subject'||type==='all'){
    let ss=[];Object.keys(data.subjects).forEach(cls=>{(data.subjects[cls]||[]).forEach(sub=>{const rel=students.filter(s=>s.class===cls&&(!s.subjects||s.subjects.includes(sub.name)));if(!rel.length)return;const tots=rel.map(s=>{const m=marks.find(x=>x.studentId===s.id&&x.subject===sub.name);return m?(m.aoi||0)+(m.midterm||0)+(m.end||0):null;}).filter(x=>x!==null);if(!tots.length)return;ss.push({name:sub.name,cls,avg:tots.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/tots.length,count:tots.length});});});
    ss.sort((a,b)=>b.avg-a.avg);
    if(ss.length>0){ins.push({icon:'⭐',color:'green',title:`Best: ${ss[0].name} (${ss[0].cls})`,body:`Average <strong>${ss[0].avg.toFixed(1)}/100</strong> across ${ss[0].count} students.`});ins.push({icon:'⚠️',color:'orange',title:`Needs Attention: ${ss[ss.length-1].name} (${ss[ss.length-1].cls})`,body:`Only <strong>${ss[ss.length-1].avg.toFixed(1)}/100</strong> average — consider extra sessions.`});}
    else{ins.push({icon:'📚',color:'blue',title:'No Subject Mark Data Yet',body:'Enter marks in Marks Entry to see subject analysis.'});}
  }
  if(type==='term'||type==='all'){
    if(allKeys.length>=2){
      const ck=data.activeTerm,pk=allKeys[allKeys.indexOf(ck)-1];
      if(pk&&data.terms[pk]){
        const pt=data.terms[pk],ps=pt.students||[],pm=pt.marks||[];
        const imps=[],decs=[];
        students.forEach(s=>{const pS=ps.find(x=>x.name.toLowerCase()===s.name.toLowerCase()&&x.class===s.class);if(!pS)return;const ca=gavg(s);function pa(st,xm){const sb=(data.subjects[st.class]||[]).filter(sub=>!st.subjects||st.subjects.includes(sub.name));if(!sb.length)return 0;let t=0;sb.forEach(sub=>{const m=xm.find(x=>x.studentId===st.id&&x.subject===sub.name);t+=(m?(m.aoi||0)+(m.midterm||0)+(m.end||0):0);});return t/sb.length;}const prA=pa(pS,pm);if(ca>0&&prA>0){const diff=ca-prA;if(diff>=10)imps.push({name:s.name,diff:diff.toFixed(1),cls:s.class});else if(diff<=-10)decs.push({name:s.name,diff:Math.abs(diff).toFixed(1),cls:s.class});}});
        if(imps.length>0)ins.push({icon:'📈',color:'green',title:`${imps.length} Students Improved`,body:imps.slice(0,4).map(s=>`<strong>${s.name.toUpperCase()}</strong> (${s.cls}) +${s.diff} pts`).join('<br>')});
        if(decs.length>0)ins.push({icon:'📉',color:'red',title:`${decs.length} Students Declined`,body:decs.slice(0,4).map(s=>`<strong>${s.name.toUpperCase()}</strong> (${s.cls}) −${s.diff} pts`).join('<br>')});
        if(!imps.length&&!decs.length&&withMarks.length>0)ins.push({icon:'📊',color:'blue',title:'Performance Stable',body:`Comparing ${data.terms[ck].label} vs ${data.terms[pk].label}. No major changes detected.`});
        if(withMarks.length===0)ins.push({icon:'📅',color:'blue',title:'Term Comparison Ready',body:`Previous term: <strong>${data.terms[pk].label}</strong> available. Enter current term marks to compare.`});
      }
    }else{ins.push({icon:'📅',color:'blue',title:'Single Term Active',body:'Open a second term in Settings to unlock cross-term trend analysis.'});}
  }
  if(type==='surprise'){const all=generateJiibyInsights('all');return all.length>0?[all[Math.floor(Math.random()*all.length)]]:[{icon:'✦',color:'purple',title:'All Good!',body:'JIIBY found nothing urgent. Well done!'}];}
  if(ins.length===0)ins.push({icon:'✦',color:'purple',title:'Add Data to Unlock Insights',body:`Tracking: <strong>${students.length}</strong> students · <strong>${marks.length}</strong> mark entries · <strong>${allKeys.length}</strong> terms`});
  return ins;
}
function renderJiibyInsights(insights,body){
  body.innerHTML='';
  const tc=document.createElement('div');tc.innerHTML=`<div style="font-size:.75rem;color:var(--jiiby);font-weight:700;letter-spacing:1px;text-transform:uppercase;padding:0 0 8px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(124,58,237,0.2);margin-bottom:4px;">✦ ${insights.length} Insight${insights.length!==1?'s':''} — ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}</div>`;
  body.appendChild(tc);
  insights.forEach((ins,i)=>{
    const div=document.createElement('div');div.className='jiiby-msg';div.style.cssText=`opacity:0;transform:translateY(10px);transition:opacity .3s ease ${i*0.1}s,transform .3s ease ${i*0.1}s`;
    const sp=ins.sparkData?`<div class="spark-row" style="margin-top:10px;"><div class="spark-label">Completion</div><div class="spark-bar-wrap"><div class="spark-bar" style="width:${ins.sparkData.value}%;background:${ins.sparkData.value>=70?'#27ae60':ins.sparkData.value>=50?'#e67e22':'#e74c3c'};"></div></div><div class="spark-val">${ins.sparkData.value.toFixed?ins.sparkData.value.toFixed(0):ins.sparkData.value}%</div></div>`:'';
    div.innerHTML=`<div class="jiiby-msg-header"><span style="font-size:1.1rem;">${ins.icon}</span><span>JIIBY AI</span><span class="jiiby-msg-time">${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}</span></div><div class="jiiby-msg-text">${ins.body}${sp}</div>`;
    body.appendChild(div);
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>{div.style.opacity='1';div.style.transform='translateY(0)';}));
  });
  SFX.jiiby();
}

// JIIBY FAB DRAG LOGIC
(function() {
  const fab = document.getElementById('jiibyFab');
  if (!fab) return;

  let isDragging = false;
  let dragMoved = false;
  let startX = 0, startY = 0;
  let initRight = 0, initBottom = 0;
  const DRAG_THRESHOLD = 10; // px moved before considered a drag

  function getFabRight() {
    return window.innerWidth - fab.getBoundingClientRect().right;
  }
  function getFabBottom() {
    return window.innerHeight - fab.getBoundingClientRect().bottom;
  }

  function pointerStart(clientX, clientY) {
    isDragging = true;
    dragMoved = false;
    startX = clientX;
    startY = clientY;
    initRight = getFabRight();
    initBottom = getFabBottom();
    fab.style.transition = 'none';
    fab.style.animation = 'none'; // pause pulse while dragging
  }

  function pointerMove(clientX, clientY) {
    if (!isDragging) return;
    const dx = clientX - startX;
    const dy = clientY - startY;

    if (!dragMoved && (Math.abs(dx) > DRAG_THRESHOLD || Math.abs(dy) > DRAG_THRESHOLD)) {
      dragMoved = true;
    }

    if (!dragMoved) return; // don't reposition until threshold exceeded

    let newRight = Math.max(8, Math.min(initRight - dx, window.innerWidth - 72));
    let newBottom = Math.max(8, Math.min(initBottom - dy, window.innerHeight - 72));

    fab.style.right = newRight + 'px';
    fab.style.bottom = newBottom + 'px';
    fab.style.left = 'auto';
    fab.style.top = 'auto';
  }

  function pointerEnd() {
    if (!isDragging) return;
    isDragging = false;
    fab.style.transition = 'box-shadow .3s, transform .3s';
    fab.style.animation = ''; // restore pulse

    if (!dragMoved) {
      // It was a genuine tap/click — toggle JIIBY
      toggleJiiby();
    }
    dragMoved = false;
  }

  // Mouse events
  fab.addEventListener('mousedown', e => {
    pointerStart(e.clientX, e.clientY);
    e.preventDefault();
  });
  window.addEventListener('mousemove', e => {
    if (isDragging) pointerMove(e.clientX, e.clientY);
  });
  window.addEventListener('mouseup', () => pointerEnd());

  // Touch events
  fab.addEventListener('touchstart', e => {
    const t = e.touches[0];
    pointerStart(t.clientX, t.clientY);
    e.preventDefault(); // prevents scroll-triggered false click
  }, { passive: false });

  window.addEventListener('touchmove', e => {
    if (!isDragging) return;
    const t = e.touches[0];
    pointerMove(t.clientX, t.clientY);
    if (dragMoved) e.preventDefault(); // only block scroll if actually dragging
  }, { passive: false });

  window.addEventListener('touchend', () => pointerEnd());
})();

// Remove old handleJiibyFabClick if it exists
function handleJiibyFabClick() {} // stub — click handled by IIFE above

// ===== JIIBY TOGGLE =====
function applyJiibyToggle(){
  const enabled = appPrefs.jiibyEnabled !== false;

  // FAB
  const fab = document.getElementById('jiibyFab');
  if (fab) fab.style.display = (enabled && !isParent()) ? 'flex' : 'none';

  // Settings page toggle (admin)
  const sw = document.getElementById('jiibyToggleSwitch');
  const slider = document.getElementById('jiibyToggleSlider');
  if (sw) sw.checked = enabled;
  if (slider) slider.style.background = enabled ? 'var(--jiiby)' : '#ccc';

  // Nav toggle (teacher)
  const navSw = document.getElementById('jiibyNavSwitch');
  const navSlider = document.getElementById('jiibyNavSlider');
  if (navSw) navSw.checked = enabled;
  if (navSlider) navSlider.style.background = enabled ? 'var(--jiiby)' : '#ccc';
}

function setJiibyEnabled(on){
  appPrefs.jiibyEnabled=on;
  savePrefs();
  applyJiibyToggle();
  showAppleNotif(on?'✦ JIIBY Enabled':'✦ JIIBY Disabled',on?'AI assistant is active':'AI assistant hidden','purple',4000);
}

// SPINNER
function showSpinner(title='Please Wait',msgs=[]){
  const sp=document.getElementById('fullSpinner');sp.classList.add('show');document.getElementById('spinnerTitle').textContent=title;
  const me=document.getElementById('spinnerMsgs');me.innerHTML='';
  msgs.forEach((m,i)=>{const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='spinner-msg';d.textContent=m;me.appendChild(d);setTimeout(()=>{d.classList.add('active');if(i>0){const p=me.children[i-1];if(p)p.classList.remove('active');}},i*700);});
}
function hideSpinner(){document.getElementById('fullSpinner').classList.remove('show');}

// UNDO
let undoStack=null,undoTimer=null;
function pushUndo(msg,fn){
  undoStack={msg,fn};const bar=document.getElementById('undoBar'),me=document.getElementById('undoMsg'),pr=document.getElementById('undoProgress');
  me.textContent=msg;pr.style.transition='none';pr.style.width='100%';bar.classList.add('show');clearTimeout(undoTimer);
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>{pr.style.transition='width 8s linear';pr.style.width='0';}));
  undoTimer=setTimeout(()=>{bar.classList.remove('show');undoStack=null;},8000);
}
function executeUndo(){if(!undoStack)return;clearTimeout(undoTimer);undoStack.fn();document.getElementById('undoBar').classList.remove('show');undoStack=null;showAppleNotif('↩ Undone','Action reversed','blue');SFX.undo();}

// NAVIGATION WITH SPINNER
const pageSpinMsgs={dashboard:['Loading overview...','Calculating stats...'],students:['Loading students...'],marks:['Loading marks entry...'],performance:['Analysing performance...'],reports:['Preparing reports...'],settings:['Loading settings...']};
function navigateTo(pageId){
  // If teacher taps marks, restore button visibility briefly then re-hide
  if(pageId==='marks' && window._userRole==='teacher'){
    const mb=document.getElementById('marksNavBtn');
    if(mb){
      mb.style.opacity='1';
      mb.style.pointerEvents='';
      clearTimeout(window._marksBtnTimer);
      window._marksBtnTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
        mb.style.opacity='0';
        mb.style.pointerEvents='none';
      },8000);
    }
  }
  showSpinner('Loading...',pageSpinMsgs[pageId]||['Loading...']);
  setTimeout(()=>{hideSpinner();showPage(pageId);},900);
}
function showPage(pageId){
  // Role guard — fall back to allowed default
  const role=window._userRole;
  const navBtn=document.querySelector(`nav button[data-page="${pageId}"]`);
  if(role && navBtn){
    const allowed=(navBtn.getAttribute('data-roles')||'').split(',').map(s=>s.trim());
    if(navBtn.getAttribute('data-roles') && !allowed.includes(role)){
      const fallback={admin:'dashboard',teacher:'dashboard',parent:'dashboard'}[role]||'dashboard';
      pageId=fallback;
    }
  }
  document.querySelectorAll('.page').forEach(p=>p.classList.remove('active'));document.getElementById(pageId).classList.add('active');
  document.querySelectorAll('nav button[data-page]').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));const ab=document.querySelector(`nav button[data-page="${pageId}"]`);if(ab)ab.classList.add('active');
  if(pageId==='parentView'){renderParentReport();}
  if(pageId==='parentsPortal'){loadParentsPortalPage();}
  if(pageId==='userManagement'){loadUserManagementPage();}
  if(pageId==='printMarksheets'){updatePmSubjectDropdown();}
  if(pageId==='termManagement'){loadTermManagementPage();}
  if(pageId==='manageSubjects'){loadManageSubjectsPage();loadMsSubjectTable();}
  if(pageId==='gradingSystem'){loadGsGradeTable();}
  if(pageId==='publishDeadline'){pdLoadPage(); ssRenderMarksColumns(); seLoadPage();}
  if(pageId==='schoolSettings'){ssLoadPage();}
  if(pageId==='systemSettings'){}
  if(pageId==='students'){loadStudents();document.getElementById('searchStudent').value='';toggleSubjectSelection();refreshHouseDropdown();}
  if(pageId==='overview'){loadOverview();loadSchoolNewsForm();}
  if(pageId==='settings'){loadSubjects();loadGrades();loadTermMgmt();loadAllowedUsers();}
  if(pageId==='performance'){loadPerformance();document.getElementById('searchPerfStudent').value='';}
  if(pageId==='marks'){
    updateSubjectDropdown();document.getElementById('searchMarksStudent').value='';
    // Show deadline banner for teachers
    if(isTeacher()&&data.marksDeadline&&data.marksDeadline.date){
      const banner=document.getElementById('teacherDeadlineBanner');
      const bannerText=document.getElementById('teacherDeadlineBannerText');
      if(banner&&bannerText){
        const d=new Date(data.marksDeadline.date);
        bannerText.textContent='Deadline: '+d.toLocaleString('en-GB',{weekday:'short',day:'numeric',month:'short',year:'numeric',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'})+(data.marksDeadline.message?' — '+data.marksDeadline.message:'');
        banner.style.display='flex';
      }
    }
  }

  if(pageId==='dashboard')loadDashboard();
}

// REPORT YEAR — syncs with term
function loadSavedReportYear(){syncReportYearFromTerm();}

// DASHBOARD
async function loadDashboard(){
  const welcomeP=document.getElementById('dashWelcomeText');
  if(welcomeP){
    const uname=window._userDisplayName||window._authedEmail||'';
    welcomeP.textContent = uname ? ('Welcome back, '+uname+'! Here\'s your school overview for today.') : 'Welcome back! Here\'s your school overview for today.';
  }
  // Parent — render their own simplified dashboard and skip admin/teacher metrics
  if(isParent()){
    hideDashHeroSpinner();
    const sg=document.getElementById('statGrid');if(sg){sg.style.display='none';sg.innerHTML='';}
    const dg=document.getElementById('dashGrid');if(dg){dg.style.display='none';dg.innerHTML='';}
    const pdc=document.getElementById('parentDashContent');if(pdc)pdc.style.display='';
    try{renderParentDashboard();}catch(e){}
    const t=document.getElementById('dashboardTitle');if(t){t.classList.remove('zoom');void t.offsetWidth;t.classList.add('zoom');}
    return;
  }
  // Make sure admin/teacher widgets are visible (they may have been hidden by a previous parent session)
  const sg=document.getElementById('statGrid');if(sg)sg.style.display='';
  const dg=document.getElementById('dashGrid');if(dg)dg.style.display='';
  const pdc=document.getElementById('parentDashContent');if(pdc){pdc.style.display='none';pdc.innerHTML='';}
  showDashHeroSpinner('Loading overview...','Scanning all data');
  const curr=td();if(!curr){hideDashHeroSpinner();return;}
  const students=curr.students||[],marks=curr.marks||[];
  const now=new Date();const ds=now.toLocaleDateString('en-GB',{weekday:'long',day:'numeric',month:'long',year:'numeric'});
  const hde=document.getElementById('dashHeroDateText');if(hde)hde.textContent=ds;
  await syncMarksFromTableToBlob();
  setTimeout(()=>{
    hideDashHeroSpinner();
    const ts=Object.values(data.subjects).reduce((a,b)=>a+b.length,0);
    const extraStatCards=isAdmin()?`<div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-icon orange">📝</div><div><div class="stat-value">${marks.length}</div><div class="stat-label">Marks Entries</div></div></div><div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-icon purple">🎓</div><div><div class="stat-value">${Object.keys(data.terms).length}</div><div class="stat-label">Terms on Record</div></div></div>`:'';
    document.getElementById('statGrid').innerHTML=`<div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-icon blue">👥</div><div><div class="stat-value">${students.length}</div><div class="stat-label">Total Students</div></div></div><div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-icon green">📚</div><div><div class="stat-value">${ts}</div><div class="stat-label">Total Subjects</div></div></div>${extraStatCards}`;
    const cc=students.reduce((acc,s)=>{acc[s.class]=(acc[s.class]||0)+1;return acc;},{});
    const cb=Object.entries(cc).sort().map(([cls,cnt])=>`<div class="class-row"><div class="class-row-name">${cls}</div><div class="class-row-count">${cnt} student${cnt!==1?'s':''}</div><span class="badge badge-blue">${(data.subjects[cls]||[]).length} subjects</span></div>`).join('')||'<p style="color:var(--text-muted);text-align:center;padding:20px;">No students yet</p>';
    let cr='';
    const optClsDash=getOptionalSubjectsClass();
    const classOrderDash=['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4'];
    ['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4'].forEach(cls=>{
      const subs=data.subjects[cls]||[],cs=students.filter(s=>s.class===cls);if(!subs.length||!cs.length)return;
      subs.forEach(sub=>{
        if(isTeacher()&&!canTeachSubject(cls,sub.name))return;
        const needsOptDash=classOrderDash.indexOf(cls)>=classOrderDash.indexOf(optClsDash);
        const rel=needsOptDash?cs.filter(s=>s.subjects&&s.subjects.includes(sub.name)):cs;if(!rel.length)return;
        const schema=getActiveMarksSchema();
        const fCounts=schema.map(c=>rel.filter(s=>{const m=marks.find(x=>x.studentId===s.id&&x.subject===sub.name);return m&&m[c.field]!=null;}).length);
        const tot=rel.length*schema.length,fil=fCounts.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0),pct=tot>0?Math.round((fil/tot)*100):0;
        const dc=pct===100?'done':pct>0?'partial':'empty',di=pct===100?'✓':pct>0?'~':'○',bc=pct===100?'#27ae60':pct>0?'#e67e22':'#cbd5e1';
        cr+=`<div class="completion-item"><div class="completion-dot ${dc}">${di}</div><div class="completion-name">${sub.name} <span class="badge badge-blue" style="font-size:.72rem;">${cls}</span></div><div style="font-size:.78rem;color:var(--text-muted);margin-right:8px;">${pct}%</div><div class="completion-bar-wrap"><div class="completion-bar" style="width:${pct}%;background:${bc};"></div></div></div>`;
      });
    });
    if(!cr)cr=isTeacher()?'<p style="color:var(--text-muted);text-align:center;padding:20px 0;">No subjects assigned to you yet</p>':'<p style="color:var(--text-muted);text-align:center;padding:20px 0;">No subjects configured yet</p>';
    // Marks completion visible to admin (all subjects) and teachers (their own subjects only)
   const completionCard=(isAdmin()||isTeacher())?`<div class="card" style="margin-bottom:0;"><div class="card-header"><div class="card-icon">📊</div><h2>${isTeacher()?'Your Marks Completion':'Marks Completion'}</h2></div><div style="max-height:340px;overflow-y:auto;">${cr}</div></div>`:'';
    const gridStyle=isAdmin()?'':'style="grid-template-columns:1fr;"';
    const eventsCard=`<div class="card" style="margin-bottom:0;"><div class="card-header"><div class="card-icon">🎉</div><h2>Upcoming Events</h2></div><div style="max-height:260px;overflow-y:auto;">${buildUpcomingEventsHTML()}</div></div>`;
    document.getElementById('dashGrid').innerHTML=`${completionCard}<div class="card" style="margin-bottom:0;"><div class="card-header"><div class="card-icon">🏫</div><h2>Class Breakdown</h2></div>${cb}</div>${eventsCard}`;
    document.getElementById('dashGrid').style.gridTemplateColumns='';
    const t=document.getElementById('dashboardTitle');t.classList.remove('zoom');void t.offsetWidth;t.classList.add('zoom');
    runBackgroundWatcher();
  },1200);
}

function buildUpcomingEventsHTML(){
  const todayStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
  const upcoming = (data.events||[]).filter(e=>e.date && e.date>=todayStr)
    .sort((a,b)=>(a.date+(a.time||'')).localeCompare(b.date+(b.time||''))).slice(0,5);
  if(!upcoming.length) return '<p style="color:var(--text-muted);text-align:center;padding:20px 0;">No upcoming events</p>';
  return upcoming.map(ev=>{
    const d = new Date(ev.date+'T'+(ev.time||'00:00'));
    const dateLabel = d.toLocaleDateString('en-GB',{weekday:'short',day:'numeric',month:'short'});
    return `<div class="completion-item"><div class="completion-dot" style="background:rgba(124,58,237,0.15);color:#7c3aed;">🎉</div>
      <div class="completion-name">${ev.title}<div style="font-size:.72rem;color:var(--text-muted);font-weight:500;">${dateLabel}${ev.time?' · '+ev.time:''}${ev.location?' · 📍'+ev.location:''}</div></div></div>`;
  }).join('');
}

// STUDENT BADGES
function computeStudentBadges(s){
  const curr=td();const mks=curr.marks||[];const subs=(data.subjects[s.class]||[]).filter(sub=>!s.subjects||s.subjects.includes(sub.name));if(!subs.length)return[];
 const _schemaCB=getMarksSchema();
  function avg(st){let t=0;subs.forEach(sub=>{const m=mks.find(x=>x.studentId===st.id&&x.subject===sub.name);t+=(m?_schemaCB.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+(m[c.field]||0),0):0);});return subs.length?t/subs.length:0;}
  const myA=avg(s),classmates=curr.students.filter(x=>x.class===s.class),avgs=classmates.map(x=>avg(x)).filter(a=>a>0).sort((a,b)=>b-a);
  const badges=[],top5=avgs[Math.floor(avgs.length*0.05)]||avgs[0];
  if(myA>0&&myA>=top5&&myA>=60)badges.push({cls:'sb-giant',label:'🏆 Giant'});
  const hasE=subs.some(sub=>{const m=mks.find(x=>x.studentId===s.id&&x.subject===sub.name);if(!m)return false;return getGrade(_schemaCB.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+(m[c.field]||0),0)).grade==='E';});
  return badges;
}
function renderStudentBadges(s){return computeStudentBadges(s).map(b=>`<span class="student-badge ${b.cls}">${b.label}</span>`).join(' ');}

// STUDENTS
function toggleSubjectSelection(){
  const cls=document.getElementById('studentClass').value,sel=document.getElementById('subjectSelection'),cb=document.getElementById('subjectCheckboxes');
  const optCls=getOptionalSubjectsClass(); // e.g. 'S.2' or 'S.3' from school settings
  // Classes that need optional selection: optCls and anything above it
  const classOrder=['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4'];
  const optIdx=classOrder.indexOf(optCls);
  const clsIdx=classOrder.indexOf(cls);
  const needsOptional=clsIdx>=optIdx&&optIdx>=0;
  if(!needsOptional){sel.style.display='none';cb.innerHTML='';return;}
  // For the exact optCls, pick from that class's optional subjects.
  // For classes above (e.g. S.4 when optCls=S.3), also pick from optCls pool.
  sel.style.display='block';cb.innerHTML='';
  const labelEl=document.getElementById('subjectSelection').querySelector('label');
  if(labelEl)labelEl.textContent='📚 Select exactly 2 optional subjects:';
  compulsorySubjects.forEach(n=>{const d=document.createElement('div');d.innerHTML=`<label><input type="checkbox" value="${n}" checked disabled> ${n}</label>`;cb.appendChild(d);});
  // Merge optional subjects from BOTH the optional-selection class (e.g. S.3) AND this student's own class
  // so newly added subjects show up no matter which class they were added under in Settings.
  const _mergedOptPool={};
  (data.subjects[optCls]||[]).forEach(s=>{_mergedOptPool[s.name]=s;});
  (data.subjects[cls]||[]).forEach(s=>{_mergedOptPool[s.name]=s;});
  Object.values(_mergedOptPool).filter(s=>!compulsorySubjects.includes(s.name)).forEach(s=>{const d=document.createElement('div');d.innerHTML=`<label><input type="checkbox" value="${s.name}"> ${s.name}</label>`;cb.appendChild(d);});
}
async function addStudent(){
  const name=document.getElementById('studentName').value.trim(),cls=document.getElementById('studentClass').value,house=document.getElementById('studentHouse').value.trim();
  const parentPhone=normalizePhone(document.getElementById('studentParentPhone').value);
  if(!name||!house){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Missing Fields','Fill name and house','orange');SFX.error();return;}
  if(!parentPhone||parentPhone.length<7){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Parent Phone Required','Needed for parent login','orange');SFX.error();return;}
  const curr=td();
  if(curr.students.some(s=>s.name===name&&s.class===cls)){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Duplicate','Student already exists','orange');SFX.error();return;}
  const student={id:`${cls.replace('.','')}_${Date.now()}`,name,class:cls,house,parentPhone};
  const optClsAdd=getOptionalSubjectsClass();
  const classOrderAdd=['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4'];
  const needsOptAdd=classOrderAdd.indexOf(cls)>=classOrderAdd.indexOf(optClsAdd)&&classOrderAdd.indexOf(optClsAdd)>=0;
  if(!needsOptAdd){student.subjects=(data.subjects[cls]||[]).map(s=>s.name);}
  else{const opts=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#subjectCheckboxes input:not([disabled]):checked')).map(c=>c.value);if(opts.length!==2){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Selection Error','Select exactly 2 optional subjects','orange');SFX.error();return;}student.subjects=[...compulsorySubjects,...opts];}
  const snap=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(curr.students));curr.students.push(student);saveData();
  showCardOverlay('students','Adding student...');
  try{
    const {data:sess}=await sb.auth.getSession();
    const token=sess?.session?.access_token;
    const efRes=await fetch(window._sbUrl+'/functions/v1/manage-student',{
      method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':'Bearer '+token},
      body:JSON.stringify({action:'add',student,school_id:window.currentSchool.id,term_key:data.activeTerm})
    });
    if(!efRes.ok){const ej=await efRes.json().catch(()=>({}));throw new Error(ej.error||'Save failed');}
  }catch(e){
    // Fallback to direct upsert if edge function unavailable
    sbUpsertStudent(student, data.activeTerm);
  }
  setTimeout(()=>{loadStudents();hideCardOverlay('students');},400);
  showAppleNotif('👤 Student Added',`${name.toUpperCase()} enrolled in ${cls}`,'green');SFX.success();
  pushUndo(`"${name}" added`,()=>{curr.students=snap;saveData();loadStudents();});
  document.getElementById('studentName').value='';document.getElementById('studentHouse').value='';document.getElementById('studentParentPhone').value='';toggleSubjectSelection();
}
let currentEditId=null;
function editStudent(id){
  const s=td().students.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!s)return;
  currentEditId=id;
  document.getElementById('currentStudentNameDisplay').textContent=s.name.toUpperCase()+' · '+s.class;
  document.getElementById('editStudentNewName').value=s.name;
  document.getElementById('editStudentNewHouse').value=s.house||'';
  document.getElementById('editStudentParentPhone').value=s.parentPhone||'';
  // Optional subjects picker — only S.3 / S.4
  const wrap=document.getElementById('editOptionalsWrap'),box=document.getElementById('editOptionalCheckboxes');
  const _optClsE=getOptionalSubjectsClass();
  const _classOrderE=['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4'];
  const optClsE=_classOrderE.indexOf(s.class)>=_classOrderE.indexOf(_optClsE)&&_classOrderE.indexOf(_optClsE)>=0;
  if(optClsE){
    // Merge optional subjects from BOTH the optional-selection class (e.g. S.3) AND this student's own class
    // so newly added subjects show up no matter which class they were added under in Settings.
    const _mergedEditOptPool={};
    (data.subjects[_optClsE]||[]).forEach(sub=>{_mergedEditOptPool[sub.name]=sub;});
    (data.subjects[s.class]||[]).forEach(sub=>{_mergedEditOptPool[sub.name]=sub;});
    const optionals=Object.values(_mergedEditOptPool).filter(sub=>!compulsorySubjects.includes(sub.name));
    const current=(s.subjects||[]).filter(n=>!compulsorySubjects.includes(n));
    box.innerHTML=optionals.map(sub=>{
      const checked=current.includes(sub.name)?'checked':'';
      return `<label class="subj-pill ${checked?'checked':''}"><input type="checkbox" value="${sub.name}" ${checked} onchange="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('checked',this.checked)"> ${sub.name}</label>`;
    }).join('')||'<span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.85rem;">No optional subjects configured for '+s.class+'.</span>';
    const editOptLabel=wrap.querySelector('label');
    if(editOptLabel) editOptLabel.textContent='📚 Optional subjects (pick 1 or more)';
    wrap.style.display='';
  }else{
    wrap.style.display='none';box.innerHTML='';
  }
  document.getElementById('editStudentModal').style.display='flex';
}
function closeEditStudentModal(){document.getElementById('editStudentModal').style.display='none';currentEditId=null;}
function saveEditedStudent(){
  if(!currentEditId)return;
  const nn=document.getElementById('editStudentNewName').value.trim();
  const nh=document.getElementById('editStudentNewHouse').value.trim();
  const np=normalizePhone(document.getElementById('editStudentParentPhone').value);
  if(!nn){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Error','Name cannot be empty','orange');return;}
  if(!np||np.length<7){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Parent phone required','Needed for parent login','orange');return;}
  const curr=td(),s=curr.students.find(x=>x.id===currentEditId);if(!s)return;
  if(curr.students.some(x=>x.id!==currentEditId&&x.name===nn&&x.class===s.class)){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Duplicate','Name already exists in '+s.class,'orange');return;}
  // Optional subjects (S.3 / S.4 only)
  let newSubjects=s.subjects||null;
  const _optClsSE=getOptionalSubjectsClass();
  const _coSE=['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4'];
  const optClsSE=_coSE.indexOf(s.class)>=_coSE.indexOf(_optClsSE)&&_coSE.indexOf(_optClsSE)>=0;
  if(optClsSE){
    const opts=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#editOptionalCheckboxes input:checked')).map(c=>c.value);
    if(opts.length<1){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Pick at least 1 optional subject','','orange');return;}
    newSubjects=[...compulsorySubjects,...opts];
  }
  s.name=nn;
  if(nh)s.house=nh;
  s.parentPhone=np;
  if(newSubjects)s.subjects=newSubjects;
  saveData();
  sbUpsertStudent(s, data.activeTerm);
  showCardOverlay('students','Saving...');
  setTimeout(()=>{loadStudents();hideCardOverlay('students');},400);
  showAppleNotif('✅ Updated','Student details saved','green');SFX.success();
  closeEditStudentModal();
}
function toggleSelectAll(cb){document.querySelectorAll('.student-row-cb').forEach(c=>c.checked=cb.checked);updateBulkToolbar();}
function updateBulkToolbar(){const ch=document.querySelectorAll('.student-row-cb:checked'),tb=document.getElementById('bulkToolbar');if(ch.length>0){tb.classList.add('visible');document.getElementById('bulkCount').textContent=`${ch.length} student${ch.length!==1?'s':''} selected`;}else tb.classList.remove('visible');document.getElementById('selectAllStudents').indeterminate=ch.length>0&&ch.length<document.querySelectorAll('.student-row-cb').length;}
function clearBulkSelection(){document.querySelectorAll('.student-row-cb').forEach(c=>c.checked=false);document.getElementById('selectAllStudents').checked=false;updateBulkToolbar();}
async function deleteSelectedStudents(){
  const ids=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.student-row-cb:checked')).map(c=>c.dataset.id);if(!ids.length)return;
  const ok=await customConfirm(`Delete <strong>${ids.length}</strong> student${ids.length!==1?'s':''}?<br>All marks will be deleted.`,'Delete Students');if(!ok)return;
  const curr=td(),snap=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify({students:curr.students,marks:curr.marks}));
  curr.students=curr.students.filter(s=>!ids.includes(s.id));curr.marks=curr.marks.filter(m=>!ids.includes(m.studentId));
  ids.forEach(id=>sbDeleteStudent(id, data.activeTerm));
  showCardOverlay('students','Deleting...');setTimeout(()=>{saveData();loadStudents();hideCardOverlay('students');},600);
  showAppleNotif('🗑 Deleted',`${ids.length} student${ids.length!==1?'s':''} removed`,'red');SFX.delete();
  pushUndo(`${ids.length} student(s) deleted`,()=>{curr.students=snap.students;curr.marks=snap.marks;saveData();loadStudents();});
}
async function loadStudents(){
  const curr=td();if(!curr)return;
  // Pull latest from Supabase table first (non-blocking)
  if(navigator.onLine&&sb&&window.currentSchool){
    await sbLoadStudentsIntoBlob(data.activeTerm);
  }
  const tbody=document.querySelector('#studentTable tbody'),fc=document.getElementById('filterClass').value,fh=document.getElementById('filterHouse').value,sq=document.getElementById('searchStudent').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const uh=[...new Set((curr.students||[]).map(s=>s.house.trim().toUpperCase()))].sort();
  const hs=document.getElementById('filterHouse'),ph=hs.value;hs.innerHTML='<option value="">All Houses</option>';
  uh.forEach(h=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=h;o.textContent=h;hs.appendChild(o);});if(ph&&uh.includes(ph))hs.value=ph;
  let fs=curr.students||[];if(fc)fs=fs.filter(s=>s.class===fc);if(fh)fs=fs.filter(s=>s.house.trim().toUpperCase()===fh);if(sq)fs=fs.filter(s=>s.name.toLowerCase().includes(sq));
  fs.sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name));tbody.innerHTML='';
  fs.forEach(s=>{const tr=document.createElement('tr');tr.innerHTML=`<td><input type="checkbox" class="student-row-cb" data-id="${s.id}" onchange="updateBulkToolbar()"></td><td onclick="showStudentSubjects('${s.id}')" style="cursor:pointer;font-weight:600;">${s.name.toUpperCase()}</td><td><span class="badge badge-blue">${s.class}</span></td><td><span class="badge badge-green">${s.house.toUpperCase()}</span></td><td>${renderStudentBadges(s)}</td><td><button class="btn-sm" onclick="event.stopPropagation();editStudent('${s.id}')">✏️</button> <button class="btn-sm btn-danger" onclick="event.stopPropagation();deleteStudent('${s.id}')">🗑</button></td>`;tbody.appendChild(tr);});
  updateBulkToolbar();
}

function printStudentContacts(){
  const curr=td();
  const fc=document.getElementById('filterClass').value;
  const sq=document.getElementById('searchStudent').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  let sts=(curr.students||[]).slice();
  if(fc) sts=sts.filter(s=>s.class===fc);
  if(sq) sts=sts.filter(s=>s.name.toLowerCase().includes(sq));
  sts.sort((a,b)=>a.class.localeCompare(b.class)||a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
  const schoolName=(window._schoolDisplayName||window.currentSchool?.name||'School').toUpperCase();
  const termLabel=td().label||'';
  const rows=sts.map((s,i)=>`<tr style="border-bottom:1px solid #ddd;"><td style="padding:6px 10px;font-size:10pt;">${i+1}</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;font-size:10pt;font-weight:600;">${s.name.toUpperCase()}</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;font-size:10pt;">${s.class}</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;font-size:10pt;">${(s.house||'').toUpperCase()}</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;font-size:10pt;color:#1e3a8a;">${s.parentPhone?'+256 '+s.parentPhone:'—'}</td></tr>`).join('');
  const win=window.open('','_blank');
  win.document.write(`<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><title>Student Contacts — ${schoolName}</title><style>body{font-family:Segoe UI,sans-serif;padding:20mm;color:#111;}h1{font-size:16pt;margin-bottom:4px;}p{font-size:9pt;color:#555;margin:0 0 12px;}table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;}th{background:#1e3a8a;color:white;padding:8px 10px;font-size:9.5pt;text-align:left;}tr:nth-child(even){background:#f4f7fb;}@page{size:A4;margin:15mm;}</style></head><body><h1>${schoolName}</h1><p>Student Names &amp; Parent Contacts &nbsp;|&nbsp; ${termLabel} &nbsp;|&nbsp; ${sts.length} student${sts.length!==1?'s':''} &nbsp;|&nbsp; Printed: ${new Date().toLocaleString('en-GB')}</p><table><thead><tr><th>#</th><th>Name</th><th>Class</th><th>House</th><th>Parent Phone</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table></body></html>`);
  win.document.close();
  win.focus();
  setTimeout(()=>win.print(),400);
}

function showStudentSubjects(id){const curr=td(),s=curr.students.find(x=>x.id===id);document.getElementById('modalStudentName').textContent=s?s.name.toUpperCase():'';const le=document.getElementById('modalSubjectList');if(s&&(s.class==='S.3'||s.class==='S.4')&&s.subjects?.length>0){le.innerHTML=s.subjects.map(sub=>`<li style="padding:8px 12px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);">${sub}</li>`).join('');}else{le.innerHTML='<li style="color:var(--text-muted);padding:14px;text-align:center;">Not applicable for S.1 & S.2</li>';}document.getElementById('subjectModal').style.display='block';}

async function deleteStudent(id){
  const curr=td(),s=curr.students.find(x=>x.id===id);
  const ok=await customConfirm(`Delete <strong>${s?s.name.toUpperCase():'this student'}</strong>?<br>All marks will be deleted.`,'Delete Student');if(!ok)return;
  const snap=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify({students:curr.students,marks:curr.marks}));
  curr.students=curr.students.filter(x=>x.id!==id);curr.marks=curr.marks.filter(m=>m.studentId!==id);
  if(!data.deletedStudentIds)data.deletedStudentIds=[];
  data.deletedStudentIds.push({id,ts:Date.now()});
  data.deletedStudentIds=data.deletedStudentIds.filter(d=>Date.now()-d.ts<14*24*60*60*1000);
  showCardOverlay('students','Removing...');
  try{
    const {data:sess}=await sb.auth.getSession();
    const token=sess?.session?.access_token;
    const efRes=await fetch(window._sbUrl+'/functions/v1/manage-student',{
      method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':'Bearer '+token},
      body:JSON.stringify({action:'delete',student_id:id,school_id:window.currentSchool.id,term_key:data.activeTerm})
    });
    if(!efRes.ok){const ej=await efRes.json().catch(()=>({}));throw new Error(ej.error||'Delete failed');}
  }catch(e){
    // Fallback to direct delete if edge function unavailable
    sbDeleteStudent(id, data.activeTerm);
  }
  setTimeout(()=>{saveData();loadStudents();hideCardOverlay('students');},500);
  showAppleNotif('🗑 Deleted',s?`${s.name.toUpperCase()} removed`:'Student removed','red');SFX.delete();
  pushUndo(`"${s?s.name:''}" deleted`,()=>{curr.students=snap.students;curr.marks=snap.marks;saveData();loadStudents();});
}
function isOptional(s){
  const optCls=getOptionalSubjectsClass();
  const classOrder=['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4'];
  return classOrder.indexOf(s.class)>=classOrder.indexOf(optCls)&&!compulsorySubjects.includes(s.name);
}
function showStudentsOfferingSubject(cls,sn){const curr=td(),list=curr.students.filter(s=>s.class===cls&&s.subjects&&s.subjects.includes(sn)).sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name));document.getElementById('modalStudentName').textContent=`${sn} (${cls}) — ${list.length} student${list.length!==1?'s':''}`;const le=document.getElementById('modalSubjectList');le.innerHTML=list.length===0?`<li style="color:var(--danger);text-align:center;padding:16px;">No students offering this subject.</li>`:list.map(s=>`<li style="padding:10px 14px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;justify-content:space-between;"><strong>${s.name.toUpperCase()}</strong><span style="color:var(--text-muted);">${s.house.toUpperCase()}</span></li>`).join('');document.getElementById('subjectModal').style.display='flex';}

  const schema=getActiveMarksSchema();
  const thead=document.getElementById('marksTableHead');
  if(thead){thead.innerHTML='<tr><th>Name</th>'+schema.map(c=>`<th>${c.name} (${c.max})</th>`).join('')+'</tr>';}
  const cls=document.getElementById('marksClass').value,sel=document.getElementById('marksSubject');sel.innerHTML='<option value="">Select Subject</option>';if(cls&&data.subjects[cls]){let subs=data.subjects[cls];if(isTeacher()){const allowed=(window._userSubjects||[]).filter(x=>x.class===cls).map(x=>x.subject);subs=subs.filter(s=>allowed.includes(s.name));if(!subs.length){const o=document.createElement('option');o.value='';o.textContent='— No subjects assigned to you in '+cls+' —';o.disabled=true;sel.appendChild(o);}}subs.forEach(s=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=s.name;o.textContent=s.name;sel.appendChild(o);});}document.querySelector('#marksTable tbody').innerHTML='';}
let marksLocked=true;
function loadMarksEntryWithSpinner(){const cls=document.getElementById('marksClass').value,sub=document.getElementById('marksSubject').value;if(!cls||!sub){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Selection Required','Select class and subject first','orange');return;}showCardOverlay('marks','Loading student marks...');setTimeout(()=>{loadMarksEntry();hideCardOverlay('marks');},700);}
function broadcastMarksPresence(cls,sub){
  if(!_presenceChannel||!window._authedEmail)return;
  try{_presenceChannel.track({email:window._authedEmail,name:window._userDisplayName||window._authedEmail,role:window._userRole||'teacher',ts:Date.now(),editingClass:cls,editingSubject:sub});}catch(e){}
}
async function loadMarksEntry(){
  const cls=document.getElementById('marksClass').value,sub=document.getElementById('marksSubject').value,sq=document.getElementById('searchMarksStudent').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  // Broadcast which subject this user is editing (presence conflict detection)
  if(cls&&sub) broadcastMarksPresence(cls,sub);
  const curr=td(),tbody=document.querySelector('#marksTable tbody');if(!cls||!sub)return;tbody.innerHTML='';
  // Teachers: validate subject assignment
  if(isTeacher()&&!canTeachSubject(cls,sub)){tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="4" style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--danger);">🔒 You are not assigned to teach '+sub+' in '+cls+'.</td></tr>';return;}
  // Teachers: always unlocked, no password gate
  const teacherMode=isTeacher();
  if(teacherMode)marksLocked=false;
  const optClsME=getOptionalSubjectsClass();
  const classOrderME=['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4'];
  const needsOptME=classOrderME.indexOf(cls)>=classOrderME.indexOf(optClsME);
  let students=(curr.students||[]).filter(s=>s.class===cls&&(needsOptME?(s.subjects&&s.subjects.includes(sub)):true));
  if(sq)students=students.filter(s=>s.name.toLowerCase().includes(sq));students.sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
  // Build dynamic header from schema
  const schema = getActiveMarksSchema();
  const thead = document.getElementById('marksTableHead');
  if(thead){
    thead.innerHTML = '<tr><th>Name</th>' + 
      schema.map(c=>`<th>${c.name} (${c.max})</th>`).join('') + 
    '</tr>';
  }

  // STEP 2 OF MIGRATION: the `marks` table is now the authoritative source
  // for what's displayed in Marks Entry (it was backfilled from the blob,
  // and every save already writes to it). The blob is now only a fallback
  // for values not yet synced — e.g. an offline entry still sitting in the
  // IndexedDB queue. This still writes back into the blob too, so
  // Dashboard/Report Studio (not yet migrated) keep working normally.
  let _cloudMarkMap={};
  if(navigator.onLine&&sb&&window.currentSchool){
    try{
      const {data:cloudRows}=await sb.from('marks')
        .select('*')
        .eq('school_id',window.currentSchool.id)
        .eq('term_key',data.activeTerm)
        .eq('subject',sub)
        .eq('class',cls);
      if(cloudRows&&cloudRows.length){
        cloudRows.forEach(r=>{
          _cloudMarkMap[r.student_id]=r;
          // Keep the blob updated too (fill gaps only — never overwrite a
          // blob value, in case it's a newer unsynced local edit).
          let lm=(curr.marks||[]).find(m=>m.studentId===r.student_id&&m.subject===r.subject);
          if(!lm){lm={studentId:r.student_id,subject:r.subject,class:r.class};if(!curr.marks)curr.marks=[];curr.marks.push(lm);}
          getMarksSchema().forEach(c=>{
            if(lm[c.field]==null && r[c.field]!=null) lm[c.field]=r[c.field];
          });
        });
      }
    }catch(e){ console.warn('[Marks] Cloud fetch failed, using local:',e.message); }
  }

  students.forEach(s=>{
    // TABLE WINS for display now. Local blob only fills a field the table
    // doesn't have yet — e.g. a mark just typed offline, still queued in
    // IndexedDB, not yet pushed to the table.
    const cloudRow=_cloudMarkMap[s.id]||null;
    const localMk=(curr.marks||[]).find(m=>m.studentId===s.id&&m.subject===sub)||{};
    let mk={};
    if(cloudRow){
      getMarksSchema().forEach(c=>{ mk[c.field]=cloudRow[c.field]; });
    }
    getMarksSchema().forEach(c=>{
      if(mk[c.field]==null && localMk[c.field]!=null) mk[c.field]=localMk[c.field];
    });
    const tr=document.createElement('tr');
    const dis=(!teacherMode&&marksLocked)?'disabled':'';
    if(!teacherMode&&marksLocked){
      tr.style.cursor='pointer';
      tr.onclick=e=>{if(e.target.tagName==='INPUT')return;showPasswordModal();};
    }else tr.onclick=null;

    const cellInput=(field,max,val)=>{
  const isEmpty=val==null||val==='';
  const cls2=teacherMode?(isEmpty?'mark-empty':'mark-filled'):'';
  const handler=teacherMode?'oninput="autoSaveMark(this)"':'';
  return `<td><input type="number" data-id="${s.id}" data-field="${field}" value="${val!=null?val:''}" min="0" max="${max}" style="width:70px;" class="${cls2}" ${dis} ${handler}></td>`;
};

    // Build cells dynamically from schema
    const cells = schema.map(c => cellInput(c.field, c.max, mk[c.field])).join('');
    tr.innerHTML = `<td><strong>${s.name.toUpperCase()}</strong></td>${cells}`;
    tbody.appendChild(tr);
  });
  if(!teacherMode){updateLockButton();updateClearMarksButton();}
}
function toggleLock(){if(marksLocked)showPasswordModal();else{marksLocked=true;disableMarksInputs();updateLockButton();updateClearMarksButton();SFX.lock();showAppleNotif('🔒 Marks Locked','','orange');}}
function showPasswordModal(){document.getElementById('passwordModal').style.display='flex';}
function closePasswordModal(){document.getElementById('passwordModal').style.display='none';document.getElementById('unlockPassword').value='';}
function unlockMarks(){const v=document.getElementById('unlockPassword').value;if(!v){document.getElementById('modalError').textContent='⚠️ Empty password';return;}if(v===correctPassword){marksLocked=false;enableMarksInputs();updateLockButton();updateClearMarksButton();showAppleNotif('🔓 Unlocked','You can now edit marks','green');SFX.unlock();closePasswordModal();loadMarksEntry();}else{document.getElementById('modalError').textContent='Incorrect password.';SFX.error();}}
function disableMarksInputs(){document.querySelectorAll('#marksTable input').forEach(i=>i.disabled=true);}
function enableMarksInputs(){document.querySelectorAll('#marksTable input').forEach(i=>i.disabled=false);}
function updateLockButton(){const lb=document.getElementById('lockButton');lb.textContent=marksLocked?'🔓 Unlock Marks':'🔒 Lock Marks';lb.style.background=marksLocked?'var(--danger)':'var(--success)';}
async function clearAllMarksInCurrentSubject(){
  const cls=document.getElementById('marksClass').value,sub=document.getElementById('marksSubject').value;if(!cls||!sub){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Select Class & Subject','','orange');return;}if(marksLocked){showAppleNotif('🔒 Unlock First','','orange');return;}
  const ok=await customConfirm(`Clear ALL marks for <strong>${sub}</strong> in ${cls}?`,'Clear Marks');if(!ok)return;
  const curr=td(),snap=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(curr.marks));curr.marks=curr.marks.filter(m=>!(m.class===cls&&m.subject===sub));
  showCardOverlay('marks','Clearing marks...');setTimeout(()=>{saveData();loadMarksEntry();hideCardOverlay('marks');},500);
  showAppleNotif('🗑 Cleared',`${sub} (${cls}) marks cleared`,'orange');SFX.delete();pushUndo(`Marks for "${sub}" cleared`,()=>{curr.marks=snap;saveData();loadMarksEntry();});
}
function updateClearMarksButton(){const cb=document.getElementById('clearMarksButton');if(marksLocked){cb.onclick=()=>{showAppleNotif('🔒 Unlock First','','orange');showPasswordModal();};cb.style.opacity='.6';}else{cb.onclick=clearAllMarksInCurrentSubject;cb.style.opacity='1';}}
