async function changePortalAccountPassword(){
  const np=(document.getElementById('portalNewPwd')?.value||'').trim();
  const nc=(document.getElementById('portalNewPwdConfirm')?.value||'').trim();
  const msgEl=document.getElementById('portalPwdMsg');
  if(!np||np.length<6){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--danger)';msgEl.textContent='⚠️ Password must be at least 6 characters.';}return;}
  if(np!==nc){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--danger)';msgEl.textContent='❌ Passwords do not match.';}return;}
  if(!sb){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--danger)';msgEl.textContent='❌ Not connected to server.';}return;}
  if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--text-muted)';msgEl.textContent='Updating…';}
  const {error}=await sb.auth.updateUser({password:np});
  if(error){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--danger)';msgEl.textContent='❌ Error: '+error.message;}return;}
  if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--success)';msgEl.textContent='✅ Password updated successfully.';}
  document.getElementById('portalNewPwd').value='';
  document.getElementById('portalNewPwdConfirm').value='';
  showAppleNotif('🔑 Password Updated','Your account password has been changed.','green');
}

function saveMarks(){
  const curr=td();if(marksLocked){showAppleNotif('🔒 Unlock First','','orange');return;}
  const cls=document.getElementById('marksClass').value,sub=document.getElementById('marksSubject').value;if(!cls||!sub){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Select Class & Subject','','orange');return;}
  const inputs=document.querySelectorAll('#marksTable input'),rows=document.querySelectorAll('#marksTable tbody tr');rows.forEach(r=>r.classList.remove('highlight-error'));
  let valid=true,inv=[];
const schema=getMarksSchema();
  const fieldMaxMap={};
  schema.forEach(c=>fieldMaxMap[c.field]=c.max);
  inputs.forEach(inp=>{
    const v=inp.value.trim(),f=inp.dataset.field,n=v===''?null:parseFloat(v);
    const maxVal=fieldMaxMap[f]||100;
    if(v!==''&&(isNaN(n)||n<0||n>maxVal)){valid=false;inv.push(inp.dataset.id);}
  });
 if(!valid){rows.forEach(r=>{if(inv.includes(r.querySelector('input').dataset.id))r.classList.add('highlight-error');});showAppleNotif('❌ Invalid Marks','AOI:0-10, Mid:0-10, End:0-80','red');SFX.error();return;}
  const snap=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(curr.marks));
  showCardOverlay('marks','Saving marks...');
  setTimeout(()=>{
    inputs.forEach(inp=>{const sid=inp.dataset.id,f=inp.dataset.field,v=inp.value.trim()===''?null:parseFloat(inp.value);let mk=curr.marks.find(m=>m.studentId===sid&&m.subject===sub);if(!mk){mk={studentId:sid,subject:sub,class:cls,aoi:null,midterm:null,end:null};curr.marks.push(mk);}mk[f]=v;});
    saveData();marksLocked=true;disableMarksInputs();updateLockButton();updateClearMarksButton();hideCardOverlay('marks');
    showAppleNotif('💾 Marks Saved',`${sub} (${cls}) updated`,'green');SFX.save();
    rows.forEach(r=>r.classList.add('highlight-success'));setTimeout(()=>rows.forEach(r=>r.classList.remove('highlight-success')),2000);
    loadMarksEntry();pushUndo(`Marks for "${sub}" saved`,()=>{curr.marks=snap;saveData();loadMarksEntry();marksLocked=false;enableMarksInputs();updateLockButton();updateClearMarksButton();});
    setTimeout(runBackgroundWatcher,3000);
  },700);
}

function getMarksSchema(){
  // Read from school settings if available, else fall back to default
  const schema = data?.settings?.marksSchema;
  if(Array.isArray(schema) && schema.length > 0) return schema;
  // Default AOI/Midterm/End structure
  return [
    {name:'AOI', max:10, field:'c1'},
    {name:'Midterm', max:10, field:'c2'},
    {name:'End of Term', max:80, field:'c3'}
  ];
}

function getActiveMarksSchema(){
  const schema=getMarksSchema();
  const active=data.settings?.activeMarkColumns;
  if(!Array.isArray(active)) return schema; // never configured — show all (back-compat)
  return schema.filter(c=>active.includes(c.field));
}

function ssRenderMarksColumns(){
  const el=document.getElementById('ssMarksColumnsList');if(!el)return;
  const schema=getMarksSchema();
  const active=data.settings?.activeMarkColumns;
  // Not configured yet → show all as checked (matches current back-compat behaviour)
  const activeSet = Array.isArray(active) ? new Set(active) : new Set(schema.map(c=>c.field));
  el.innerHTML = schema.map(c=>{
    const checked = activeSet.has(c.field);
    return `<label class="subj-pill ${checked?'checked':''}" style="cursor:pointer;">
      <input type="checkbox" class="ss-markcol-cb" value="${c.field}" ${checked?'checked':''} onchange="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('checked',this.checked)">
      <span>${c.name} (${c.max})</span>
    </label>`;
  }).join('') || '<span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.85rem;">No assessment components configured for this school.</span>';
}

function ssSaveMarksColumns(){
  const picked = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.ss-markcol-cb:checked')).map(cb=>cb.value);
  if(!data.settings) data.settings={};
  data.settings.activeMarkColumns = picked; // can be [] → names-only in Marks Entry
  saveData();
  const msgEl=document.getElementById('ssMarksColsMsg');
  const names=picked.map(f=>getMarksSchema().find(x=>x.field===f)?.name||f);
  if(msgEl){
    msgEl.style.color='var(--success)';
    msgEl.textContent = picked.length ? ('✅ Saved — visible: '+names.join(', ')) : '✅ Saved — only student names will show.';
  }
  showAppleNotif('📊 Marks Columns Updated', picked.length?(picked.length+' column(s) active'):'Only names will show','green');
  SFX.success();
  // Live-refresh if a teacher/admin currently has Marks Entry open
  if(document.getElementById('marks')?.classList.contains('active')){
    updateSubjectDropdown();
    loadMarksEntry();
  }
}

// PERFORMANCE
function getOptionalSubjectsClass(){
  return (data.settings && data.settings.optionalSubjectsClass) || 'S.3';
}
function getOptionalSubjectsPromotedClass(){
  return getOptionalSubjectsClass() === 'S.2' ? 'S.3' : 'S.4';
}
function getGrade(t){for(const g of data.grading)if(t>=g.min&&t<=g.max)return g;return{grade:'E',descriptor:'ELEMENTARY'};}
function getFullDescriptor(d){const m={'EXCEPTIONAL':'<p style="font-size:7.8pt">Applies innovatory and creativity of the acquired knowledge and skills.</p>','OUTSTANDING':'<p style="font-size:7.8pt">Applies high level of acquired knowledge & skills in life situations.</p>','SATISFACTORY':'<p style="font-size:7.8pt">Applies adequate level of acquired knowledge and skills in real life</p>','BASIC':'<p style="font-size:7.8pt">Demonstrates minimum level of acquired knowledge & skills in life</p>','ELEMENTARY':'<p style="font-size:7.8pt">Demonstrates basic level of acquired knowledge and skills in real life.</p>'};return m[d]||'';}
function getStudentPerf(s,cls){
  const curr=td(),mks=curr.marks||[];
 const optCls=getOptionalSubjectsClass();
  let subs=data.subjects[cls]||[];
  const _co=['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4'];
  if(_co.indexOf(cls)>=_co.indexOf(optCls)) subs=subs.filter(sub=>s.subjects&&s.subjects.includes(sub.name));
  const schema=getMarksSchema();
  let tot=0,nA=0,nE=0;
  subs.forEach(sub=>{
    const m=mks.find(x=>x.studentId===s.id&&x.subject===sub.name)||{};
    const t=schema.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+(m[c.field]||0),0);
    tot+=t;
    const g=getGrade(t);
    if(g.grade==='A')nA++;
    if(g.grade==='E')nE++;
  });
  return{avg:subs.length?tot/subs.length:0,numAs:nA,numEs:nE};
}

function getSubjectAvg(cls,name){
  const curr=td(),students=curr.students||[],marks=curr.marks||[];
  const schema=getMarksSchema();
  const optCls=getOptionalSubjectsClass();
 const _coA=['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4'];
  const _needsOpt=_coA.indexOf(cls)>=_coA.indexOf(optCls);
  const rel=students.filter(s=>s.class===cls&&(!_needsOpt||s.subjects&&s.subjects.includes(name)));
  const tots=rel.map(s=>{
    const m=marks.find(x=>x.studentId===s.id&&x.subject===name);
    return m?schema.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+(m[c.field]||0),0):0;
  });
  return{avg:rel.length?tots.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/rel.length:0,count:rel.length};
}

async function syncMarksFromTableToBlob(){
  if(!navigator.onLine||!sb||!window.currentSchool)return;
  try{
    const {data:rows}=await sb.from('marks')
      .select('*')
      .eq('school_id',window.currentSchool.id)
      .eq('term_key',data.activeTerm);
    if(!rows||!rows.length)return;
    const curr=td();
    if(!curr.marks)curr.marks=[];
    rows.forEach(r=>{
      let lm=curr.marks.find(m=>m.studentId===r.student_id&&m.subject===r.subject);
      if(!lm){lm={studentId:r.student_id,subject:r.subject,class:r.class};curr.marks.push(lm);}
      getMarksSchema().forEach(c=>{
        if(r[c.field]!=null) lm[c.field]=r[c.field];
      });
    });
    // Save merged state locally — no cloud push, just local cache update
    try{localStorage.setItem(_schoolKey,JSON.stringify(data));}catch(e){}
  }catch(e){console.warn('[Marks sync]',e.message);}
}

function printMarksheet(){
  const cls=document.getElementById('perfClass').value;
  const subjectFilter=(document.getElementById('perfMarksheetSubject')?.value||'').trim();
  if(!cls){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Select a Class first','','orange');return;}
  if(!subjectFilter){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Select a Subject to print','Use the subject dropdown next to the Print button','orange');return;}
  const curr=td();
  const schema=getMarksSchema();
  const schoolName=(window._schoolDisplayName||window.currentSchool?.name||'School').toUpperCase();
  const schoolAddr=data?.settings?.address||window.currentSchool?.address||'';
  const termLabel=curr.label||'';
  const printDate=new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-GB',{day:'numeric',month:'long',year:'numeric'});
  const optCls=getOptionalSubjectsClass();
  const classOrder=['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4'];
  const needsOpt=classOrder.indexOf(cls)>=classOrder.indexOf(optCls)&&classOrder.indexOf(optCls)>=0;
  const allSubs=data.subjects[cls]||[];
  let allStudents=(curr.students||[]).filter(s=>s.class===cls);
  allStudents.sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
  if(!allStudents.length){showAppleNotif('⚠️ No students in '+cls,'','orange');return;}

  // Only the subject the user selected
  const allSubsForClass=allSubs.filter(s=>{
    if(!needsOpt)return true;
    if(compulsorySubjects.includes(s.name))return true;
    return allStudents.some(st=>st.subjects&&st.subjects.includes(s.name));
  });
  const subjectsToShow=allSubsForClass.filter(s=>s.name===subjectFilter);
  if(!subjectsToShow.length){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Subject "'+subjectFilter+'" not found for '+cls,'','orange');return;}
  
  // How many student rows fit per landscape A4 page comfortably
  const ROWS_PER_PAGE=22;

  function buildHeader(sub,pageNum,totalPages){
    const pageLabel=totalPages>1?` &nbsp;·&nbsp; Page ${pageNum} of ${totalPages}`:'';
    return`
    <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#0f2d6b 0%,#1e3a8a 60%,#2563eb 100%);border-radius:10px;padding:14px 18px 10px;margin-bottom:12px;color:#fff;display:flex;align-items:flex-start;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;">
      <div style="flex:1;">
        <div style="font-size:14pt;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:.5px;line-height:1.1;">${schoolName}</div>
        ${schoolAddr?`<div style="font-size:7.5pt;opacity:.8;margin-top:2px;font-weight:600;">${schoolAddr}</div>`:''}
        <div style="margin-top:7px;display:flex;gap:0;flex-wrap:wrap;">
          <span style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.15);border-radius:5px;padding:3px 9px;font-size:7.5pt;font-weight:700;margin-right:6px;margin-bottom:4px;letter-spacing:.3px;">CLASS: ${cls}</span>
          <span style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.15);border-radius:5px;padding:3px 9px;font-size:7.5pt;font-weight:700;margin-right:6px;margin-bottom:4px;letter-spacing:.3px;">TERM: ${termLabel}</span>
          <span style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.15);border-radius:5px;padding:3px 9px;font-size:7.5pt;font-weight:700;margin-right:6px;margin-bottom:4px;letter-spacing:.3px;">DATE: ${printDate}</span>
          ${totalPages>1?`<span style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.2);border-radius:5px;padding:3px 9px;font-size:7.5pt;font-weight:700;margin-right:6px;margin-bottom:4px;color:#fde68a;">${pageLabel.trim()}</span>`:''}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="text-align:right;flex-shrink:0;">
        <div style="font-size:18pt;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:1px;line-height:1;">${sub.name}</div>
        ${sub.teacher?`<div style="font-size:8pt;opacity:.75;margin-top:2px;font-weight:600;">Teacher: ${sub.teacher}</div>`:''}
        <div style="font-size:7.5pt;opacity:.7;margin-top:4px;font-weight:600;">MARKSHEET</div>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }

  function buildTableForRows(rowsData,sub,startIdx){
    const schemaHeaders=schema.map(c=>`
      <th style="padding:7px 6px;background:#1e3a8a;color:#fff;font-size:8pt;font-weight:800;text-align:center;min-width:66px;border-bottom:2px solid #2563eb;">
        ${c.name}<br/><span style="font-size:6.5pt;font-weight:600;opacity:.85;">(${c.max} marks)</span>
      </th>`).join('');
    const totalCol=`<th style="padding:7px 6px;background:#0f2d6b;color:#fff;font-size:8pt;font-weight:800;text-align:center;min-width:58px;border-bottom:2px solid #2563eb;">TOTAL<br/><span style="font-size:6.5pt;font-weight:600;opacity:.85;">(100)</span></th>`;
    const studentRows=rowsData.map((s,i)=>{
      const globalIdx=startIdx+i;
      // Fetch actual marks for this student in this subject
      const _mk=(curr.marks||[]).find(m=>m.studentId===s.id&&m.subject===sub.name)||{};
      const _hasAnyMark=schema.some(c=>_mk[c.field]!=null&&_mk[c.field]!=='');
      const _total=_hasAnyMark?schema.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+(Number(_mk[c.field])||0),0):null;

      const markCells=schema.map(c=>{
        const val=(_mk[c.field]!=null&&_mk[c.field]!=='')?_mk[c.field]:null;
        const display=val!=null?`<span style="font-weight:700;font-size:9pt;color:#1e3a8a;">${val}</span>`:'';
        return `<td style="padding:4px 6px;border-bottom:1px solid #e2e8f0;text-align:center;">
          <div style="border:1.5px solid #cbd5e1;border-radius:6px;height:22px;width:54px;margin:0 auto;background:#fff;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;">${display}</div>
        </td>`;
      }).join('');
      const totalDisplay=_total!=null?`<span style="font-weight:800;font-size:9pt;color:#1e3a8a;">${_total}</span>`:'';
      const totalCell=`
        <td style="padding:4px 6px;border-bottom:1px solid #e2e8f0;text-align:center;">
          <div style="border:2px solid #1e3a8a;border-radius:6px;height:22px;width:46px;margin:0 auto;background:#eff6ff;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;">${totalDisplay}</div>
        </td>`;
      const rowBg=i%2===0?'#ffffff':'#f8fafc';
      return`<tr style="background:${rowBg};">
        <td style="padding:5px 10px;border-bottom:1px solid #e2e8f0;font-size:8.5pt;font-weight:700;color:#1e3a8a;white-space:nowrap;">${globalIdx+1}.&nbsp;${s.name.toUpperCase()}</td>
        ${markCells}
        ${totalCell}
      </tr>`;
    }).join('');
    return`
    <table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;border:1.5px solid #1e3a8a;border-radius:8px;overflow:hidden;">
      <thead>
        <tr>
          <th style="padding:8px 10px;background:#1e3a8a;color:#fff;font-size:9pt;font-weight:800;text-align:left;min-width:170px;border-bottom:2px solid #2563eb;">STUDENT NAME</th>
          ${schemaHeaders}
          ${totalCol}
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>${studentRows}</tbody>
    </table>`;
  }

  function buildFooter(sub,pageNum,totalPages){
    const pageInfo=totalPages>1?`Page ${pageNum} of ${totalPages} &nbsp;·&nbsp; ${cls} &nbsp;·&nbsp; ${sub.name}`:`${cls} &nbsp;·&nbsp; ${sub.name}`;
    return`
    <div style="margin-top:10px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-end;">
      <div style="font-size:8pt;color:#374151;font-weight:600;">
        Subject teacher: <span style="display:inline-block;width:200px;border-bottom:1.5px solid #374151;margin:0 8px;">&nbsp;</span> Signature: <span style="display:inline-block;width:130px;border-bottom:1.5px solid #374151;margin:0 8px;">&nbsp;</span>
      </div>
      <div style="text-align:right;">
        <div style="font-size:7pt;color:#94a3b8;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:.5px;">${pageInfo}</div>
        <div style="font-size:6.5pt;color:#cbd5e1;margin-top:2px;letter-spacing:.8px;">Printed from JIIBSCORE SYNC SYSTEM</div>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }

  // Build all pages across all subjects
  let allPages='';
  subjectsToShow.forEach(sub=>{
    // Filter students: for optional classes only those offering this subject
    let subStudents=allStudents;
    if(needsOpt&&!compulsorySubjects.includes(sub.name)){
      subStudents=allStudents.filter(s=>s.subjects&&s.subjects.includes(sub.name));
    }
    if(!subStudents.length)return;

    const totalPages=Math.ceil(subStudents.length/ROWS_PER_PAGE);
    for(let p=0;p<totalPages;p++){
      const chunk=subStudents.slice(p*ROWS_PER_PAGE,(p+1)*ROWS_PER_PAGE);
      const isLast=p===totalPages-1;
      allPages+=`
      <div style="padding:8mm 10mm;font-family:'Segoe UI',system-ui,Arial,sans-serif;color:#111;page-break-after:always;min-height:190mm;display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:space-between;">
        <div>
          ${buildHeader(sub,p+1,totalPages)}
          ${buildTableForRows(chunk,sub,p*ROWS_PER_PAGE)}
        </div>
        ${buildFooter(sub,p+1,totalPages)}
      </div>`;
    }
  });

  const win=window.open('','_blank');
  win.document.write(`<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><title>Marksheet — ${cls} — ${schoolName}</title>
  <style>
    *{box-sizing:border-box;margin:0;padding:0;}
    body{background:#fff;-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact;print-color-adjust:exact;}
    @page{size:A4 landscape;margin:0;}
    @media print{
      body{margin:0;}
      div[style*="page-break-after"]{page-break-after:always;}
    }
  </style>
  </head><body>${allPages}</body></html>`);
  win.document.close();
  win.focus();
  setTimeout(()=>win.print(),450);
}

function updatePerfMarksheetSubjectDropdown(){
  const cls=document.getElementById('perfClass').value;
  const sel=document.getElementById('perfMarksheetSubject');
  if(!sel)return;
  sel.innerHTML='<option value="">— Select Subject to Print —</option>';
  if(!cls)return;
  const subs=data.subjects[cls]||[];
  subs.forEach(s=>{
    const o=document.createElement('option');
    o.value=s.name;
    o.textContent=s.name+(s.teacher?' ('+s.teacher+')':'');
    sel.appendChild(o);
  });
}
function loadPerformanceWithSpinner(){showCardOverlay('perf','Analysing...');setTimeout(async()=>{await syncMarksFromTableToBlob();loadPerformance();updatePerfMarksheetSubjectDropdown();hideCardOverlay('perf');},600);}
function loadPerformance(){
  const cls=document.getElementById('perfClass').value,view=document.getElementById('perfView').value,sq=document.getElementById('searchPerfStudent').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const curr=td();if(!curr)return;const students=curr.students||[],marks=curr.marks||[];
  const tbody=document.querySelector('#perfTable tbody'),thead=document.querySelector('#perfTable thead');tbody.innerHTML='';thead.innerHTML='';if(!cls)return;
  if(view==='badges'){thead.innerHTML='<tr><th>Name</th><th>Class</th><th>Badges</th><th>Avg</th></tr>';let fs=students.filter(s=>s.class===cls);if(sq)fs=fs.filter(s=>s.name.toLowerCase().includes(sq));fs.forEach(s=>{const badges=computeStudentBadges(s),avg=getStudentPerf(s,cls).avg;const tr=document.createElement('tr');tr.innerHTML=`<td><strong>${s.name.toUpperCase()}</strong></td><td>${s.class}</td><td>${badges.map(b=>`<span class="student-badge ${b.cls}">${b.label}</span>`).join(' ')||'<span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.8rem;">No badges yet</span>'}</td><td>${avg.toFixed(1)}</td>`;tbody.appendChild(tr);});return;}
  if(view==='table'){let subs=data.subjects[cls]||[];thead.innerHTML='<tr><th>NAME</th>'+subs.map(s=>`<th>${s.name}</th>`).join('')+'<th>Avg</th></tr>';let fs=students.filter(s=>s.class===cls);if(sq)fs=fs.filter(s=>s.name.toLowerCase().includes(sq));fs.sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name));const _schema=getMarksSchema();const _optCls=getOptionalSubjectsClass();
    fs.forEach(s=>{
      let ss=subs;
      const _coPerf=['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4'];
      if(_coPerf.indexOf(cls)>=_coPerf.indexOf(_optCls)) ss=subs.filter(sub=>s.subjects&&s.subjects.includes(sub.name));
      let row=`<td><strong>${s.name.toUpperCase()}</strong></td>`,tot=0;
      subs.forEach(sub=>{
        if(!ss.includes(sub)){row+=`<td style="color:var(--text-muted);">—</td>`;return;}
        const m=marks.find(x=>x.studentId===s.id&&x.subject===sub.name)||{};
        const t=_schema.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+(m[c.field]||0),0);
        tot+=t;row+=`<td>${t}</td>`;
      });
      row+=`<td><strong>${ss.length?(tot/ss.length).toFixed(1):'0'}</strong></td>`;
      const tr=document.createElement('tr');tr.innerHTML=row;tbody.appendChild(tr);
    });}
  else if(view==='giants'||view==='dwarfs'){thead.innerHTML=`<tr><th>Rank</th><th>Name</th><th>Avg</th><th>${view==='giants'?"A's":"E's"}</th></tr>`;let fs=students.filter(s=>s.class===cls);if(sq)fs=fs.filter(s=>s.name.toLowerCase().includes(sq));fs=fs.map(s=>({...s,perf:getStudentPerf(s,cls)}));fs.sort((a,b)=>view==='giants'?b.perf.avg-a.perf.avg:a.perf.avg-b.perf.avg);fs.slice(0,10).forEach((s,i)=>{const tr=document.createElement('tr');tr.innerHTML=`<td>${i===0?'🥇':i===1?'🥈':i===2?'🥉':i+1}</td><td><strong>${s.name.toUpperCase()}</strong></td><td>${s.perf.avg.toFixed(1)}</td><td>${view==='giants'?s.perf.numAs:s.perf.numEs}</td>`;tbody.appendChild(tr);});}
  else{thead.innerHTML='<tr><th>Subject</th><th>Avg</th><th>Students</th></tr>';let subs=data.subjects[cls]||[];let avgs=subs.map(s=>({...s,...getSubjectAvg(cls,s.name)}));avgs.sort((a,b)=>view==='best-subjects'?b.avg-a.avg:a.avg-b.avg);avgs.forEach(s=>{const tr=document.createElement('tr');tr.innerHTML=`<td><strong>${s.name}</strong></td><td>${s.avg.toFixed(1)}</td><td>${s.count}</td>`;tbody.appendChild(tr);});}
}

// REPORTS
function genHTComment(d){const c={'EXCEPTIONAL':'Excellent performance! Keep it up','OUTSTANDING':'Very good performance across most subjects.','SATISFACTORY':'With consistent effort, you can achieve even better outcomes.','BASIC':'More effort needed to improve overall grades.','ELEMENTARY':'Significant improvement required.'};return c[d]||'Well done. Continue working hard.';}
function genCTComment(s,cls,subs){const curr=td(),mks=curr.marks||[];let low=Infinity,ls=[];subs.forEach(sub=>{const m=mks.find(x=>x.studentId===s.id&&x.subject===sub.name);if(!hasAnyMark(m))return;const t=(m.aoi||0)+(m.midterm||0)+(m.end||0);if(t<low){low=t;ls=[sub.name];}else if(t===low&&t>0)ls.push(sub.name);});if(!subs.length||low===Infinity)return'Encouraging effort shown.';if(low>=70)return'Well done. Strong performance!';if(ls.length===1)return`Good overall, but more effort needed in <b style="color:red;">${ls[0]}.</b>`;if(ls.length===2)return`Good results. More effort in <b style="color:red;">${ls[0]}</b> and <b style="color:red;">${ls[1]}.</b>`;return`Effort needed in <b style="color:red;">${ls.slice(0,-1).join(', ')}</b> and <b style="color:red;">${ls.slice(-1)[0]}.</b>`;}

function buildReportHTML(student,cls,year,curr,tableClass){
  let subs=data.subjects[cls]||[];
const _optClsRH=getOptionalSubjectsClass();
const _coRH=['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4'];
if(_coRH.indexOf(cls)>=_coRH.indexOf(_optClsRH))subs=subs.filter(s=>student.subjects&&student.subjects.includes(s.name));
  let rows='',tot=0,vc=0,dc={};
  subs.forEach(s=>{const mk=curr.marks.find(m=>m.studentId===student.id&&m.subject===s.name);const a=mk&&mk.aoi!=null?mk.aoi:'-',mid=mk&&mk.midterm!=null?mk.midterm:'-',e=mk&&mk.end!=null?mk.end:'-';let dT='-',dO='-',dG='-',dD='-';if(hasAnyMark(mk)){const t=(mk.aoi||0)+(mk.midterm||0)+(mk.end||0);dT=t.toFixed(1);const g=getGrade(t);dG=g.grade;dD=g.descriptor;dO=(t/33.33).toFixed(1);tot+=t;vc++;dc[g.descriptor]=(dc[g.descriptor]||0)+1;}rows+=`<tr><td>${s.name}</td><td style="color:blue;">${a}</td><td>${mid}</td><td style="color:green;">${e}</td><td>${dT}</td><td style="color:red;">${dO}</td><td>${dG}</td><td style="color:blue;">${dD}</td><td>${s.teacher||'N/A'}</td></tr>`;});
  const table=`<table class="${tableClass}" style="width:100%;font-size:7.5pt;border-collapse:collapse;"><tr><th>SUBJECT</th><th>AOI(10%)</th><th>MID(10%)</th><th>END(80%)</th><th>TOTAL</th><th>OUT OF 3</th><th>GRADE</th><th>DESCRIPTOR</th><th>TR</th></tr>${rows}</table>`;
  let gt=`<table class="grading-table" style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;"><thead><tr><th>GRADE</th><th>ACHIEVEMENT LEVEL</th><th>DESCRIPTOR</th></tr></thead><tbody>`;
  data.grading.forEach(g=>{gt+=`<tr><td>${g.grade}</td><td>${g.descriptor}</td><td>${getFullDescriptor(g.descriptor)}</td></tr>`;});gt+='</tbody></table>';
  const avg=vc?(tot/vc).toFixed(1):'-',aO=vc?(avg/33.33).toFixed(1):'-',mfd=vc?Object.keys(dc).reduce((a,b)=>dc[a]>dc[b]?a:b):'-',res=vc?(aO>=1.5?'RESULT 1':aO>=1?'RESULT 2':'RESULT 3'):'-',htc=vc?genHTComment(mfd):'No marks entered.',ctc=genCTComment(student,cls,subs);
  const _sn=(window.currentSchool?.name||window._schoolDisplayName||data?.settings?.schoolName||'SCHOOL').toUpperCase();
  const _sa=data?.settings?.address||window.currentSchool?.address||'';
  return`<div class="report"><div class="report-header"><img src="${window._schoolBadgeUrl||'badge.png'}" style="width:100px;display:block;margin:0 auto;" alt="Badge"><p style="line-height:1.0;text-align:center;font-size:24pt;font-family:Algerian;color:#1e3a8a;font-weight:bold;">${_sn}</p>${_sa?`<p style="font-size:10pt;color:black;word-spacing:7px;font-weight:bold;">${_sa}</p>`:''}<p style="line-height:1.0;font-size:12pt;text-align:center;word-spacing:10px;background-color:#1e3a8a;color:white;font-weight:bold;">END OF TERM LEARNER'S SUMMATIVE REPORT ${year}</p><h4></h4><h5>NAME: <span style="border:1px solid black;padding:0 2px;color:#1e3a8a;">${student.name.toUpperCase()}</span> &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;CLASS: <span style="border:1px solid black;padding:0 2px;color:#1e3a8a;">${student.class}</span> &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;HOUSE: <span style="border:1px solid black;padding:0 2px;color:#1e3a8a;">${student.house.toUpperCase()}</span></h5></div><p style="color:black;font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:1.8em;text-align:center;"><u><b>ASSESSMENT SCORE'S SUMMARY</u></p><b>${table}<p style="text-align:left;background-color:black;font-size:13.5px;"><strong style="color:white">OVERALL AVERAGE:</strong> <u style="color:rgb(228,174,74);"><b>${aO}</u>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<strong style="color:white">OVERALL PERFORMANCE:</strong> <u style="color:rgb(228,174,74);"><b>${mfd}</u>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<strong style="color:white">RESULT:</strong> <u style="color:rgb(228,174,74);"><b>${res}</u></p><p style="color:black;font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:1.8em;text-align:center;"><u>GRADING SYSTEM</u></p>${gt}<div class="comment-placeholder"><strong>Head Teacher's Comment: <i><b style="color:blue;"><u>${htc}</u></i></b> Sign: ..............</strong></div><div class="comment-placeholder"><strong>Class Teacher's Comment: <i><b style="color:blue;"><u>${ctc}</u></i></b> Sign: ................</strong></div><p style="border-bottom:2px solid #8B0000;"></p><p style="border-bottom:5px solid #8B0000;padding-bottom:3px;"></p><p style="color:black;font-size:9pt;"><i>Next Term Begins: .................................&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;NOT VALID WITHOUT STAMP</i></p><p style="color:black;font-size:9pt;"><i>Fees Balance: ............................................</i></p><p style="color:blue;font-size:9pt;text-align:center;"><i>In God We Trust</i></p></div>`;
}

subs=data.subjects[cls]||[];if(cls==='S.2'||cls==='S.3'||cls==='S.4')subs=subs.filter(sub=>s.subjects&&s.subjects.includes(sub.name));let rows='';subs.forEach(sub=>{const style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:9.5pt;"><tr><th style="background:#1e3a8a;color:white;padding:5px;">SUBJECT</th>${getMarksSchema().map(c=>`<th style="background:#1e3a8a;color:white;padding:5px;">${c.name}(${c.max}%)</th>`).join('')}</tr>${rows}</table><div style="border:1px dashed #1e3a8a;min-height:15mm;margin-top:8mm;padding:5px;"><strong style="font-size:9pt;">Class Teacher's Comment: ..................................................................................&nbsp;Signature: ______________</strong></div><p style="color:blue;font-size:10pt;text-align:center;margin-top:6mm;"><i>In God We Trust</i></p></div>`;});pages+=`<div style="width:297mm;height:210mm;page-break-after:always;">${slips}<div style="clear:both;"></div></div>`;}
  document.getElementById('reportPreview').innerHTML=pages;hideCardOverlay('reports');window.print();},700);
}

async function buildReportStudioSnapshot(){
  if(!window.currentSchool){
    throw new Error('School not selected');
  }

  // Load the latest school_data snapshot first.
  try{
    await syncPullFromCloud();
  }catch(e){
    console.warn('[Report] school_data sync failed:',e.message);
  }

  const reportData = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(data));
  const termKey = reportData.activeTerm;
  const term = reportData.terms && reportData.terms[termKey];

  if(!term){
    throw new Error('Active term data was not found');
  }

  if(!Array.isArray(term.students)) term.students = [];
  if(!Array.isArray(term.marks)) term.marks = [];
  if(!reportData.subjects) reportData.subjects = {};

  const schema = getMarksSchema();

  if(!sb){
    throw new Error('Supabase connection is not available');
  }

  const {data:cloudRows,error} = await sb
    .from('marks')
    .select('*')
    .eq('school_id',window.currentSchool.id)
    .eq('term_key',termKey);

  if(error) throw error;

  const studentsById = new Map(
    term.students.map(student => [
      String(student.id),
      student
    ])
  );

  const normaliseSubject = value =>
    String(value || '').trim().toUpperCase();

  // Index marks already present in school_data.
  const markMap = new Map();

  term.marks.forEach(mark => {
    if(!mark || !mark.studentId || !mark.subject) return;

    const key =
      String(mark.studentId) +
      '::' +
      normaliseSubject(mark.subject);

    markMap.set(key,mark);
  });

  let imported = 0;

  (cloudRows || []).forEach(row => {
    if(!row.student_id || !row.subject) return;

    const student = studentsById.get(String(row.student_id));

    // Use the student's current class first because the class on an
    // older marks row may be stale.
    const cls = student?.class || row.class || '';

    // Declare subjectName BEFORE using it.
    const incomingSubject = String(row.subject).trim();
    let subjectName = incomingSubject;

    // Restore or locate the subject in the student's current class.
    if(cls){
      if(!Array.isArray(reportData.subjects[cls])){
        reportData.subjects[cls] = [];
      }

      const existingSubject = reportData.subjects[cls].find(
        subject =>
          normaliseSubject(subject.name) ===
          normaliseSubject(incomingSubject)
      );

      if(existingSubject){
        subjectName = existingSubject.name;
      }else{
        // The subject exists in marks but is missing from school_data.
        // Add it to this temporary report snapshot.
        reportData.subjects[cls].push({
          name: incomingSubject,
          teacher: ''
        });
      }
    }

    const key =
      String(row.student_id) +
      '::' +
      normaliseSubject(subjectName);

    const mark = markMap.get(key) || {
      studentId: row.student_id,
      subject: subjectName,
      class: cls
    };

    mark.studentId = row.student_id;
    mark.subject = subjectName;

    if(cls){
      mark.class = cls;
    }

    // Copy the configured assessment fields from the dedicated marks row.
    // IMPORTANT: only fill fields that are missing in the snapshot — never
    // let a stale cloud "marks" table row overwrite a fresher value that
    // already made it into school_data. This was the cause of marks
    // entered on one device silently disappearing from Report Studio.
    schema.forEach(component => {
      const field = component.field;

      if(mark[field] === null || mark[field] === undefined){
        if(row[field] !== null && row[field] !== undefined){
          mark[field] = row[field];
        }
      }
    });

    markMap.set(key,mark);
    imported++;
    // NOTE: We intentionally do NOT auto-add subjectName to
    // student.subjects here. A mark existing for a subject does not mean
    // the student is assigned to it — report cards must strictly only
    // show subjects the student is actually enrolled in (compulsory +
    // assigned optionals). getStudentSubjects() in report_studio.html
    // enforces this filter.
  });

  // Replace the report snapshot's marks with the merged result.
  term.marks = Array.from(markMap.values());

  reportData.settings = {
    ...(reportData.settings || {}),
    schoolName:
      window.currentSchool?.name ||
      reportData.settings?.schoolName ||
      '',
    address:
      reportData.settings?.address ||
      '',
    motto:
      reportData.settings?.motto ||
      '',
    logoBase64:
      window._schoolBadgeUrl ||
      window.currentSchool?.logo_url ||
      reportData.settings?.logoBase64 ||
      '',
    marksSchema:
      reportData.settings?.marksSchema ||
      schema,
    schoolId:
      window.currentSchool?.id ||
      '',
    schoolSlug:
      window.currentSchool?.slug ||
      ''
  };

  console.log(
    '[Report] Prepared snapshot:',
    imported,
    'marks-table rows imported;',
    term.marks.length,
    'total marks in report snapshot'
  );

  return reportData;
}

async function openReportStudio(){
  if(!window.currentSchool){
    showAppleNotif('⚠ Select a school first','','orange');
    return;
  }

  // Open the tab SYNCHRONOUSLY, right now, before any await — this is what
  // stops browsers from treating it as a blocked popup. We fill it in once
  // the data is ready.
  const reportWindow = window.open('', '_blank');
  if(!reportWindow){
    showAppleNotif('❌ Report Studio blocked','Please allow pop-ups for this site in your browser settings, then try again','red',8000);
    return;
  }
  reportWindow.document.write(
    '<html><body style="font-family:sans-serif;background:#0b0f1a;color:#fff;'+
    'display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;height:100vh;margin:0;">'+
    '<div>🔄 Preparing your reports…</div></body></html>'
  );

  try{
    showAppleNotif(
      '🔄 Loading latest marks…',
      'Preparing reports from the teacher marks table',
      'blue',
      4000
    );

    const reportData = await buildReportStudioSnapshot();

    sessionStorage.removeItem('jiib_report_data');
    sessionStorage.setItem(
      'jiib_report_data',
      JSON.stringify(reportData)
    );

    reportWindow.location.href = '/report_studio.html';
  }catch(e){
    console.error('[Report] Failed to prepare report data:',e);
    reportWindow.close();

    showAppleNotif(
      '❌ Report data failed',
      e.message || 'Could not load the latest marks',
      'red',
      7000
    );
  }
}

// SETTINGS: SUBJECTS
function addSubject(){
  const name=document.getElementById('subjectName').value.trim().toUpperCase(),cls=document.getElementById('subjectClass').value,teacher=document.getElementById('subjectTeacher').value.trim().toUpperCase();
  if(!name||!teacher){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Fill All Fields','','orange');SFX.error();return;}
  if(data.subjects[cls].some(s=>s.name===name)){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Duplicate','Subject already exists','orange');SFX.error();return;}
  if((cls==='S.3'||cls==='S.4')&&compulsorySubjects.includes(name)){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Compulsory','Auto-added for S.3/S.4','orange');SFX.error();return;}
  const snap=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(data.subjects[cls]));data.subjects[cls].push({name,teacher});saveData();
  showCardOverlay('settings','Adding subject...');setTimeout(()=>{loadSubjects();hideCardOverlay('settings');},400);
  showAppleNotif('📚 Subject Added',`${name} added to ${cls}`,'green');SFX.success();
  pushUndo(`Subject "${name}" added`,()=>{data.subjects[cls]=snap;saveData();loadSubjects();});
  document.getElementById('subjectName').value='';document.getElementById('subjectTeacher').value='';
}
function loadSubjects(){
  const tbody=document.querySelector('#subjectTable tbody');
  const fcEl=document.getElementById('subjectFilterClass');
  const fc=fcEl?fcEl.value:'';
  if(tbody){
    tbody.innerHTML='';let list=[];
    Object.keys(data.subjects).forEach(cls=>{if(!fc||cls===fc)list=list.concat(data.subjects[cls].map(s=>({...s,class:cls})));});
    list.sort((a,b)=>a.class.localeCompare(b.class)||a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
    list.forEach(s=>{
      const isC=compulsorySubjects.includes(s.name);
      const db=isC?'<button disabled style="background:#ccc;" class="btn-sm">Delete</button>':`<button onclick="deleteSubject('${s.class}','${escapeJS(s.name)}')" class="btn-sm btn-danger">🗑</button>`;
      const tr=document.createElement('tr');tr.innerHTML=`<td>${isOptional(s)?`<span style="color:#2563eb;text-decoration:underline;cursor:pointer;" onclick="showStudentsOfferingSubject('${s.class}','${escapeJS(s.name)}')">${s.name}</span>`:s.name}</td><td><span class="badge badge-blue">${s.class}</span></td><td>${s.teacher||'N/A'}</td><td><button onclick="editSubject('${s.class}','${escapeJS(s.name)}')" class="btn-sm">✏️</button> ${db}</td>`;tbody.appendChild(tr);
    });
  }
  try{renderTeacherSubjectsPicker();}catch(e){}
  try{ if(typeof loadMsSubjectTable==='function') loadMsSubjectTable(); }catch(e){}
}
function editSubject(cls,name){const s=data.subjects[cls].find(x=>x.name===name);if(!s)return;document.getElementById('editSubjectName').value=s.name;document.getElementById('editSubjectTeacher').value=s.teacher||'';document.getElementById('editSubjectModal').style.display='flex';window.currentEditSubject={cls,name};}
function saveEditSubject(){if(!window.currentEditSubject)return;const t=document.getElementById('editSubjectTeacher').value.trim().toUpperCase();const s=data.subjects[window.currentEditSubject.cls].find(x=>x.name===window.currentEditSubject.name);if(s){s.teacher=t;saveData();loadSubjects();showAppleNotif('✅ Updated','','green');SFX.success();}closeEditSubjectModal();}
function closeEditSubjectModal(){document.getElementById('editSubjectModal').style.display='none';window.currentEditSubject=null;}
async function deleteSubject(cls,name){
  const ok=await customConfirm(`Delete <strong>${name}</strong> from ${cls}?`,'Delete Subject');if(!ok)return;
  const ss=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(data.subjects[cls])),curr=td(),sm=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(curr.marks));
  data.subjects[cls]=data.subjects[cls].filter(s=>s.name!==name);curr.marks=curr.marks.filter(m=>!(m.subject===name&&m.class===cls));
  showCardOverlay('settings','Removing subject...');setTimeout(()=>{saveData();loadSubjects();hideCardOverlay('settings');},400);
  showAppleNotif('🗑 Deleted',`${name} removed`,'red');SFX.delete();pushUndo(`"${name}" deleted`,()=>{data.subjects[cls]=ss;curr.marks=sm;saveData();loadSubjects();});
}

// GRADING
function addGrade(){
  const name=document.getElementById('gradeName').value.trim().toUpperCase(),min=parseInt(document.getElementById('gradeMin').value),max=parseInt(document.getElementById('gradeMax').value),desc=document.getElementById('gradeDescriptor').value.trim().toUpperCase();
  if(!name||isNaN(min)||isNaN(max)||!desc){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Fill All Fields','','orange');return;}if(min<0||max>100||min>max){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Invalid Range','','orange');return;}
  if(data.grading.some(g=>g.grade!==name&&((min>=g.min&&min<=g.max)||(max>=g.min&&max<=g.max)))){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Range Overlaps','','orange');return;}
  data.grading=data.grading.filter(g=>g.grade!==name);data.grading.push({grade:name,min,max,descriptor:desc});data.grading.sort((a,b)=>b.min-a.min);saveData();loadGrades();showAppleNotif('✅ Grade Added',`${name} added`,'green');SFX.success();
  ['gradeName','gradeMin','gradeMax','gradeDescriptor'].forEach(id=>document.getElementById(id).value='');
}
function loadGrades(){
  const tbody=document.querySelector('#gradeTable tbody');
  if(tbody){
    tbody.innerHTML='';
    data.grading.forEach(g=>{const tr=document.createElement('tr');tr.innerHTML=`<td><strong>${g.grade}</strong></td><td>${g.min}</td><td>${g.max}</td><td>${g.descriptor}</td><td><button onclick="deleteGrade('${g.grade}')" class="btn-sm btn-danger">🗑</button></td>`;tbody.appendChild(tr);});
  }
  try{ if(typeof loadGsGradeTable==='function') loadGsGradeTable(); }catch(e){}
}
async function deleteGrade(name){const ok=await customConfirm(`Delete grade <strong>${name}</strong>?`,'Delete');if(!ok)return;data.grading=data.grading.filter(g=>g.grade!==name);saveData();loadGrades();showAppleNotif('🗑 Deleted','','red');SFX.delete();}

// IMPORT / EXPORT
function exportData(){
  const schoolTag=(window.currentSchool?.name||window._schoolDisplayName||'').replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi,'_').slice(0,30)||'JIIBCORE';
  const dateTag=new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
  const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(data,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=url;a.download=schoolTag+'_data_'+dateTag+'.json';a.click();URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  showAppleNotif('⬇ Exported','','green');SFX.success();
}
function importData(){
  const fi=document.getElementById('importFile');if(!fi.files[0]){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Select File','','orange');return;}
  showSpinner('Importing',['Reading file...','Processing records...','Merging data...']);
  const reader=new FileReader();
  reader.onload=function(e){setTimeout(()=>{try{const imp=JSON.parse(e.target.result);if(imp.terms){Object.entries(imp.terms).forEach(([k,t])=>{if(!data.terms[k])data.terms[k]=t;});if(imp.subjects)Object.keys(imp.subjects).forEach(cls=>{data.subjects[cls]=[...new Set([...(data.subjects[cls]||[]).map(s=>s.name),...(imp.subjects[cls]||[]).map(s=>s.name)])].map(n=>{const ex=(data.subjects[cls]||[]).find(s=>s.name===n)||(imp.subjects[cls]||[]).find(s=>s.name===n);return{name:n,teacher:(ex&&ex.teacher)||''};});});if(imp.grading)data.grading=imp.grading;}else{const curr=td();(imp.students||[]).forEach(s=>{if(!curr.students.some(d=>d.id===s.id))curr.students.push(s);});(imp.marks||[]).forEach(m=>{const ex=curr.marks.find(d=>d.studentId===m.studentId&&d.subject===m.subject);if(!ex)curr.marks.push(m);else Object.assign(ex,m);});if(imp.grading)data.grading=imp.grading;}saveData();loadStudents();loadSubjects();loadGrades();hideSpinner();showAppleNotif('📥 Import Complete','','green');SFX.success();}catch(err){hideSpinner();showAppleNotif('❌ Import Failed','Invalid JSON','red');SFX.error();}},2000);};
  reader.readAsText(fi.files[0]);
}
function clearAllData(){document.getElementById('clearDataPasswordModal').style.display='flex';document.getElementById('clearDataPasswordInput').value='';document.getElementById('clearDataPwdError').textContent='';}
function closeClearDataPasswordModal(){document.getElementById('clearDataPasswordModal').style.display='none';}
async function verifyClearDataPassword(){
  const v=document.getElementById('clearDataPasswordInput').value.trim();
  if(!v){document.getElementById('clearDataPwdError').textContent='⚠️ Empty password';return;}
  // Use the admin's current login password stored in data.lockPassword (or correctPassword)
  if(v!==correctPassword){document.getElementById('clearDataPwdError').textContent='❌ Incorrect password';SFX.error();return;}
  closeClearDataPasswordModal();
  const ok=await customConfirm('Delete <strong>ALL system data</strong>?<br>Students, marks, subjects & terms will be wiped. Cannot be undone!','⚠️ Clear All Data');
  if(!ok)return;
  showCardOverlay('settings','Clearing all data...');
  setTimeout(async()=>{
    performClearAllData();
    // Also push the cleared state to cloud so all devices sync
    if(navigator.onLine && sb && window._authedEmail){
      try{await sb.from('school_data').upsert({
  school_id: window.currentSchool.id,
  data: data,
  updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  updated_by: window._authedEmail
}, {onConflict: 'school_id'});}catch(e){}
    }
    hideCardOverlay('settings');
    showAppleNotif('🗑 All Data Cleared','System has been reset','orange');SFX.delete();
  },800);
}function performClearAllData(){
  const d2=n=>compulsorySubjects.map(x=>({name:x,teacher:''}));
  data={activeTerm:'2026-T1',terms:{'2026-T1':{label:'Term 1 — 2026',...createDefaultTermData()}},subjects:{'S.1':d2(),'S.2':d2(),'S.3':d2(),'S.4':d2()},grading:[{grade:'A',min:80,max:100,descriptor:'EXCEPTIONAL'},{grade:'B',min:70,max:79,descriptor:'OUTSTANDING'},{grade:'C',min:60,max:69,descriptor:'SATISFACTORY'},{grade:'D',min:50,max:59,descriptor:'BASIC'},{grade:'E',min:0,max:49,descriptor:'ELEMENTARY'}],lockPassword:'jiib'};
  correctPassword='jiib';localStorage.removeItem(_schoolKey);refreshTermSelect();loadStudents();loadSubjects();loadGrades();loadDashboard();loadTermMgmt();showAppleNotif('🗑 Reset Complete','','orange');SFX.success();
}
async function mergeMultipleTeacherFiles(){
  const input=document.getElementById('mergeFilesInput'),files=input.files;if(files.length<2){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Select 2+ Files','','orange');return;}
  showSpinner('Merging',['Reading files...','Matching students...','Merging marks...','Syncing subjects...','Finalising...']);
  let master=null;const keyToId=new Map();
  try{
    for(let i=0;i<files.length;i++){const imp=JSON.parse(await files[i].text());const iS=imp.students||(imp.terms&&imp.terms[Object.keys(imp.terms)[0]]?.students)||[];const iM=imp.marks||(imp.terms&&imp.terms[Object.keys(imp.terms)[0]]?.marks)||[];if(i===0){master=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(imp));iS.forEach(s=>keyToId.set(`${s.name.toLowerCase().trim()}|${s.class}`,s.id));continue;}const mM=master.marks||(master.terms&&master.terms[Object.keys(master.terms)[0]]?.marks)||[];iM.forEach(mk=>{const is=iS.find(s=>s.id===mk.studentId);if(!is)return;const mid=keyToId.get(`${is.name.toLowerCase().trim()}|${is.class}`);if(!mid)return;const ei=mM.findIndex(m=>m.studentId===mid&&m.subject===mk.subject);if(ei===-1)mM.push({...mk,studentId:mid});else{const e=mM[ei];if(!e.aoi&&mk.aoi)e.aoi=mk.aoi;if(!e.midterm&&mk.midterm)e.midterm=mk.midterm;if(!e.end&&mk.end)e.end=mk.end;}});Object.keys(imp.subjects||{}).forEach(cls=>{if(!master.subjects)master.subjects={};if(!master.subjects[cls])master.subjects[cls]=[];(imp.subjects[cls]||[]).forEach(s=>{const ex=master.subjects[cls].some(x=>x.name===s.name);if(!ex)master.subjects[cls].push({...s});else{const e=master.subjects[cls].find(x=>x.name===s.name);if(s.teacher)e.teacher=s.teacher;}});});}
    setTimeout(()=>{data=master;saveData();refreshTermSelect();loadStudents();loadSubjects();loadDashboard();const curr=td();document.getElementById('mergeStatus').innerHTML=`<span style="color:var(--success);">✅ Merged ${files.length} files! Students: ${(curr.students||[]).length} | Marks: ${(curr.marks||[]).length}</span>`;hideSpinner();showAppleNotif('⚡ Merge Complete','','green');SFX.success();input.value='';},3500);
  }catch(e){hideSpinner();document.getElementById('mergeStatus').innerHTML=`<span style="color:var(--danger);">Error: ${e.message}</span>`;showAppleNotif('❌ Merge Failed',e.message,'red');SFX.error();}
}

// PROMOTION
async function promoteStudents(){
  const from=document.getElementById('promoteFromClass').value,sEl=document.getElementById('promotionStatus');if(!from){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Select Class','','orange');return;}
  const toMap={'S.1':'S.2','S.2':'S.3','S.3':'S.4'},to=toMap[from];const curr=td();const sts=curr.students.filter(s=>s.class===from);
  if(!sts.length){showAppleNotif(`⚠️ No Students in ${from}`,'','orange');return;}
  // Term 3 must be opened AND closed before any promotion is allowed
  const termKeys=Object.keys(data.terms).sort();
  const lastKey=termKeys[termKeys.length-1];
  const lastTerm=data.terms[lastKey]||{};
  const lastIsT3=/Term\s*3/i.test(lastTerm.label||'')||/-T3$/.test(lastKey);
  if(!lastIsT3){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Open Term 3 First','You must open Term 3 before promoting students.','red',8000);SFX.error();return;}
  if(!lastTerm.locked){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Close Term 3 First',`${lastTerm.label||'Term 3'} is still open. Close it from Settings → Term Management before promoting.`,'red',9000);SFX.error();return;}
  // Sanity: should also have at least some marks in T3
  const t3Marks=(lastTerm.marks||[]);
  if(!t3Marks.length){showAppleNotif('⚠️ No Term 3 Marks','Enter Term 3 marks before promoting.','red',8000);SFX.error();return;}
    const optClsP=getOptionalSubjectsClass();
  const classBelowOptP=optClsP==='S.3'?'S.2':'S.1';
  if(from!==classBelowOptP&&from!=='S.3'&&curr.students.some(s=>s.class===to)){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Class Not Empty',`Promote ${to} students first`,'orange');return;}
  if(from==='S.3'&&to==='S.4'){const s4=curr.students.filter(s=>s.class==='S.4');if(s4.length>0){const wipe=await customConfirm(`⚠️ <strong>${s4.length}</strong> students exist in S.4.<br>Promoting S.3 will <strong style="color:var(--danger);">permanently replace them</strong>.<br>Continue?`,'⚠️ Replace S.4');if(!wipe)return;const ids=s4.map(s=>s.id);curr.students=curr.students.filter(s=>s.class!=='S.4');curr.marks=curr.marks.filter(m=>!ids.includes(m.studentId));}}
  const ok=await customConfirm(`Promote <strong>${sts.length}</strong> students from <strong>${from}</strong> to <strong>${to}</strong>?`,'Promote');if(!ok)return;
    if(from===classBelowOptP&&to===optClsP){
    window.promotionTemp={};window.studentsBeingPromoted=[...sts];
    document.getElementById('promotionModal').classList.add('open');
    loadPromotionStudentList();
    const mInfo=document.getElementById('promotionModalInfo');
    if(mInfo)mInfo.textContent=`Assigning optional subjects for promotion to ${optClsP}`;
    const cBtn=document.getElementById('completePromotionBtn');
    if(cBtn)cBtn.textContent=`🎉 COMPLETE PROMOTION — Move All Students to ${optClsP}`;
    return;
  }
  showSpinner('Promoting',['Moving records...','Updating classes...','Clearing marks...','Done!']);
  let cnt=0;const aa=new Set();
  sts.forEach(s=>{
    const oldTermKey=data.activeTerm;
    sbDeleteStudent(s.id, oldTermKey);
    s.class=to;const optPromP=getOptionalSubjectsPromotedClass();
    if(from===optClsP&&to===optPromP){if(!s.subjects)s.subjects=[...compulsorySubjects];s.subjects.forEach(n=>{if(!compulsorySubjects.includes(n)&&!data.subjects[optPromP].some(x=>x.name===n)){data.subjects[optPromP].push({name:n,teacher:''});}});}cnt++;});
  const ids=sts.map(s=>s.id);curr.marks=curr.marks.filter(m=>!ids.includes(m.studentId));
    if(to===getOptionalSubjectsPromotedClass())data.subjects[to].sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
  setTimeout(()=>{saveData();loadStudents();loadDashboard();if(document.getElementById('settings').classList.contains('active'))loadSubjects();hideSpinner();showAppleNotif('🚀 Promotion Complete',`${cnt} students → ${to}`,'green');SFX.promote();sEl.style.color='var(--success)';sEl.textContent=`✅ ${cnt} students promoted to ${to}`;setTimeout(()=>sEl.textContent='',8000);},3000);
}
let currentPromoId=null;
function loadPromotionStudentList(){const tbody=document.querySelector('#promotionStudentsTable tbody');tbody.innerHTML='';document.getElementById('studentCount').textContent=window.studentsBeingPromoted.length;window.studentsBeingPromoted.forEach(s=>{const done=window.promotionTemp[s.id],tr=document.createElement('tr');tr.style.cursor='pointer';tr.innerHTML=`<td style="padding:12px;">${s.name.toUpperCase()}</td><td style="padding:12px;">${s.house.toUpperCase()}</td><td style="padding:12px;text-align:center;font-weight:700;color:${done?'var(--success)':'var(--accent)'};">${done?'✅ Assigned':'⏳ Pending'}</td>`;tr.onclick=()=>selectStudentForPromotion(s.id);tbody.appendChild(tr);});}
function selectStudentForPromotion(id){currentPromoId=id;const s=window.studentsBeingPromoted.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!s)return;document.getElementById('currentStudentTitle').innerHTML=`Assign 2 subjects for <strong>${s.name.toUpperCase()}</strong>`;const c=document.getElementById('promotionSubjectCheckboxes');c.innerHTML='';data.subjects[getOptionalSubjectsClass()].filter(x=>!compulsorySubjects.includes(x.name)).forEach(sub=>{const div=document.createElement('div');const ch=window.promotionTemp[id]&&window.promotionTemp[id].includes(sub.name)?'checked':'';div.innerHTML=`<label style="font-size:1rem;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:6px 12px;border-radius:20px;background:rgba(59,130,246,0.08);border:1px solid rgba(59,130,246,0.2);cursor:pointer;"><input type="checkbox" value="${sub.name}" ${ch}> ${sub.name}</label>`;c.appendChild(div);});document.getElementById('promotionSaveMessage').style.display='none';}
function savePromotionOptionals(){if(!currentPromoId)return;const sel=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#promotionSubjectCheckboxes input:checked')).map(c=>c.value);const msg=document.getElementById('promotionSaveMessage');if(sel.length!==2){msg.style.background='rgba(231,76,60,0.1)';msg.style.color='var(--danger)';msg.innerHTML='❌ Select EXACTLY 2';msg.style.display='block';return;}window.promotionTemp[currentPromoId]=sel;const s=window.studentsBeingPromoted.find(x=>x.id===currentPromoId);msg.style.background='rgba(39,174,96,0.1)';msg.style.color='var(--success)';msg.innerHTML=`✅ Saved for <strong>${s.name.toUpperCase()}</strong>`;msg.style.display='block';loadPromotionStudentList();const allDone=window.studentsBeingPromoted.every(x=>window.promotionTemp[x.id]);document.getElementById('completePromotionBtn').style.display=allDone?'block':'none';}
async function completeS2toS3Promotion(){
  const optClsC=getOptionalSubjectsClass();
  const ok=await customConfirm(`Promote <strong>${window.studentsBeingPromoted?.length||0}</strong> students to ${optClsC}?`,'Complete');
  if(!ok)return;
  const sts=[...(window.studentsBeingPromoted||[])],temps={...window.promotionTemp};
  closePromotionModal();
  showSpinner('Completing',['Assigning subjects...','Moving students...','Clearing marks...','Done!']);
  setTimeout(()=>{
    const curr=td();let cnt=0;
    sts.forEach(s=>{
      const opts=temps[s.id];if(!opts||opts.length!==2)return;
      s.class=optClsC;s.subjects=[...compulsorySubjects,...opts];cnt++;
    });
    const ids=sts.map(s=>s.id);
    curr.marks=curr.marks.filter(m=>!ids.includes(m.studentId));
    sts.forEach(s=>sbUpsertStudent(s, data.activeTerm));
    saveData();loadStudents();loadDashboard();hideSpinner();
    if(document.getElementById('settings').classList.contains('active'))loadSubjects();
    showAppleNotif('🎉 Promotion Complete',`${cnt} students → ${optClsC}`,'green');SFX.promote();
  },3000);
}
function closePromotionModal(){document.getElementById('promotionModal').classList.remove('open');currentPromoId=null;window.promotionTemp=null;window.studentsBeingPromoted=null;}

// CLOCK & DATE-TIME POPUP
const DAYS=['Sunday','Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday'];
const MONTHS=['January','February','March','April','May','June','July','August','September','October','November','December'];
let dtpOpen=false;
function updateClock(){const now=new Date();const t=`${String(now.getHours()).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(now.getSeconds()).padStart(2,'0')}`;const el=document.getElementById('navClock');if(el)el.textContent=t;if(dtpOpen){const d=document.getElementById('dtpTime');if(d)d.textContent=t;}}
function toggleDtpPopup(e){e.stopPropagation();if(dtpOpen){closeDtpPopup();return;}const popup=document.getElementById('dateTimePopup'),clock=document.getElementById('navClock');if(!popup||!clock)return;const now=new Date();document.getElementById('dtpTime').textContent=`${String(now.getHours()).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(now.getSeconds()).padStart(2,'0')}`;document.getElementById('dtpDay').textContent=DAYS[now.getDay()];document.getElementById('dtpDate').textContent=`${now.getDate()} ${MONTHS[now.getMonth()]} ${now.getFullYear()}`;const rect=clock.getBoundingClientRect();popup.style.top=(rect.bottom+8)+'px';popup.style.right=(window.innerWidth-rect.right)+'px';popup.style.left='auto';popup.classList.add('dtp-show');dtpOpen=true;setTimeout(()=>document.addEventListener('click',dtpOut),50);}
function dtpOut(e){if(!document.getElementById('dateTimePopup').contains(e.target))closeDtpPopup();}
function closeDtpPopup(){document.getElementById('dateTimePopup').classList.remove('dtp-show');dtpOpen=false;document.removeEventListener('click',dtpOut);}
updateClock();setInterval(updateClock,1000);

// Network status init — NO auto-login, always show login screen fresh
(async()=>{
  setNetStatus(navigator.onLine?'online':'offline');
  const slugCheck = getSchoolSlugFromURL();

  // ── ROOT URL → Marketing website ──────────────────────────────────────────
  if(slugCheck === ''){
    // Force-hide login overlay before anything else renders
    const _mktOv = document.getElementById('loginOverlay');
    if(_mktOv) _mktOv.style.cssText = 'display:none!important;';
    const _mktFab = document.getElementById('jiibyFab');
    if(_mktFab) _mktFab.style.display = 'none';
    setLoginLock(false);
    renderMarketingPage();
    return;
  }

  // ── /super_admin → Super Admin Portal ─────────────────────────────────────
  if(slugCheck === '__super_admin__'){
    document.getElementById('loginOverlay').style.display='none';
    document.body.classList.add('app-ready');
    setLoginLock(false);
    if(sb){
      try{
        const {data:existingSess}=await sb.auth.getSession();
        if(existingSess&&existingSess.session){
          await loadUserRolesForSchool(existingSess.session.user.id);
        }
      }catch(e){}
    }
    renderSuperAdminPortal();
    return;
  }

  // ── /slug → School Portal ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  await resolveSchoolFromURL();
  if(!window.currentSchool) return; // 404 already rendered
  if(sb){
    try{ await sb.auth.signOut(); }catch(e){}
  }
  const _ov = document.getElementById('loginOverlay');
  if(_ov){ _ov.style.display='flex'; _ov.style.opacity='1'; }
  setLoginLock(true);
})();

    else if(Date.now()-_lastPullAt>8000) syncPullFromCloud();
  }
}, 8000);

// ---- School News (admin writes, parents read) ----
function loadSchoolNewsForm(){
  const ti=document.getElementById('newsTitle');
  const bd=document.getElementById('newsBody');
  if(!ti||!bd)return;
  const n=(data.schoolNews||{});
  ti.value=n.title||'';
  bd.value=n.body||'';
  const meta=document.getElementById('newsMeta');
  if(meta){
    if(n.updatedAt){
      const d=new Date(n.updatedAt);
      meta.textContent='Last updated '+d.toLocaleString()+(n.updatedBy?(' by '+n.updatedBy):'');
    }else meta.textContent='No news posted yet.';
  }
}

async function saveSchoolNews() {
  const ti = (document.getElementById('newsTitle') ? document.getElementById('newsTitle').value :
               document.getElementById('schoolNewsTitle') ? document.getElementById('schoolNewsTitle').value : '').trim();
  const bd = (document.getElementById('newsBody') ? document.getElementById('newsBody').value :
               document.getElementById('schoolNewsBody') ? document.getElementById('schoolNewsBody').value : '').trim();
  if (!ti || !bd) { showAppleNotif('⚠️ Title & message required', '', 'orange'); return; }
  data.schoolNews = {
    title: ti, body: bd,
    updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    updatedBy: window._userDisplayName || window._authedEmail || 'Admin'
  };
  // Push to cloud immediately — don't wait for the 1.5s debounce timer
  if(sb && window.currentSchool && navigator.onLine){
    try{
      await sb.from('school_data').upsert(
        {school_id:window.currentSchool.id, data:data, updated_at:new Date().toISOString(), updated_by:window._authedEmail||'admin'},
        {onConflict:'school_id'}
      );
      _pendingDirty=false;
      setSyncStatus('saved','✓ News published');
    }catch(e){
      // Fall back to normal save queue
      saveData();
    }
  } else {
    saveData();
  }
  loadSchoolNewsForm();
  if (isParent()) try { renderParentDashboard(); } catch(e) {}
  showAppleNotif('📰 News Published', 'Parents notified instantly', 'green');
  SFX.success();
}

async function clearSchoolNews(){
  const ok=await customConfirm('Remove the current school news article? Parents will no longer see it.','Clear News');
  if(!ok)return;
  data.schoolNews=null;saveData();loadSchoolNewsForm();
  showAppleNotif('🗑 News Cleared','','red');SFX.delete();
}

// checkAndNotifyNewSchoolNews():
function checkAndNotifyNewSchoolNews() {
  if (!isParent()) return;
  const news = data.schoolNews;
  if (!news || !news.updatedAt || !news.title) return;

  const key = 'parent_last_news_seen_' + (window._parentPhone || 'anon');
  const lastSeen = localStorage.getItem(key) || '0';

  if (new Date(news.updatedAt) > new Date(lastSeen)) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      // Use 'blue' not 'purple' — purple is blocked for parents
      showAppleNotif(
        '📰 New from School',
        news.title,
        'blue',
        10000
      );
      localStorage.setItem(key, news.updatedAt);
    }, 1800);
  }
}

// ---- Admin Overview page (Students directory + News editor) ----
function loadOverview() {
  const curr = td();
  const tbody = document.querySelector('#overviewTable tbody');
  if (!tbody) return;

  const fc = (document.getElementById('overviewClassFilter') ? document.getElementById('overviewClassFilter').value : '');
  const sq = (document.getElementById('overviewSearch') ? document.getElementById('overviewSearch').value.trim().toLowerCase() : '');

  let sts = (curr.students || []).slice().sort((a, b) => a.class.localeCompare(b.class) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
  if (fc) sts = sts.filter(s => s.class === fc);
  if (sq) sts = sts.filter(s =>
    s.name.toLowerCase().includes(sq) ||
    (s.house || '').toLowerCase().includes(sq) ||
    (s.parentPhone || '').includes(sq)
  );

  const countEl = document.getElementById('overviewCount');
  if (countEl) countEl.textContent = sts.length + ' student' + (sts.length !== 1 ? 's' : '');

  tbody.innerHTML = sts.map((s, i) => {
    const ph = s.parentPhone
      ? `<a href="tel:+256${s.parentPhone}" style="color:#2563eb;font-weight:600;">+256 ${s.parentPhone}</a>`
      : '<span style="color:#e67e22;font-size:.8rem;">⚠️ Missing</span>';
    return `<tr>
      <td style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.82rem;">${i + 1}</td>
      <td><strong>${s.name.toUpperCase()}</strong></td>
      <td><span class="badge badge-blue">${s.class}</span></td>
      <td><span class="badge badge-green">${(s.house || '').toUpperCase()}</span></td>
      <td>${ph}</td>
    </tr>`;
  }).join('') || '<tr><td colspan="5" style="text-align:center;padding:18px;color:var(--text-muted);">No students found.</td></tr>';

  loadSchoolNewsForm();
}
function isOptionalClass(c){return c==='S.3'||c==='S.4';}

// ---- Notify admin about teacher logins they missed (offline-friendly) ----
function scanTeacherLoginLogForAdmin(){
  if(!isAdmin())return;
  const log=Array.isArray(data.loginLog)?data.loginLog:[];
  if(!log.length)return;
  const me=(window._authedEmail||'').toLowerCase();
  const key='lastLoginLogSeen::'+me;
  let lastSeen=parseInt(localStorage.getItem(key)||'0',10)||0;
  const fresh=log.filter(e=>(e.ts||0)>lastSeen).sort((a,b)=>(a.ts||0)-(b.ts||0));
  if(!fresh.length)return;
  // Cap to last 8 to avoid spamming when admin returns after a long break
  const show=fresh.slice(-8);
  show.forEach(e=>{
    const when=new Date(e.ts).toLocaleString();
    showAppleNotif('👨‍🏫 Teacher logged in', (e.name||e.email||'Teacher')+' • '+when, 'blue', 6000);
  });
  if(fresh.length>show.length){
    showAppleNotif('+'+(fresh.length-show.length)+' more teacher logins', 'while you were away', 'blue');
  }
  localStorage.setItem(key, String(fresh[fresh.length-1].ts||Date.now()));
}

function toggleNavGroup(groupId){
  const grp=document.getElementById(groupId);
  if(!grp)return;
  const isOpen=grp.classList.contains('open');
  closeNavGroups();
  if(!isOpen){
    grp.classList.add('open');
    // Use fixed positioning so dropdown never shifts page content
    const dropdown=grp.querySelector('.nav-group-dropdown');
    const header=grp.querySelector('.nav-group-header');
    if(dropdown&&header){
      const rect=header.getBoundingClientRect();
      dropdown.style.top=(rect.bottom+4)+'px';
      dropdown.style.left=rect.left+'px';
    }
  }
}
function closeNavGroups(){
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-group.open').forEach(g=>g.classList.remove('open'));
}
// Close dropdowns when clicking outside
document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
  if(!e.target.closest('.nav-group'))closeNavGroups();
});

// ---- Collapsible settings sections ----
// REPLACE the existing toggleSettingsSection function with this:
function toggleSettingsSection(sectionId, headerEl) {
  const sec = document.getElementById(sectionId);
  if (!sec) return;
  const isHidden = sec.style.display === 'none' || sec.style.display === '';
  sec.style.display = isHidden ? 'block' : 'none';
  if (headerEl) {
    const arr = headerEl.querySelector('.section-arrow');
    if (arr) arr.textContent = isHidden ? '▼' : '▶';
  }
}

// ---- Change Admin email/password ----
async function changeAdminCredentials(){
  if(!isAdmin()){showAppleNotif('⛔ Admin only','','red');return;}
  const newEmail=(document.getElementById('adminNewEmail').value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const newPass=(document.getElementById('adminNewPwd')||document.getElementById('adminNewPassword'))?.value||'';
  const confirmPass=(document.getElementById('adminConfirmPwd')||document.getElementById('adminConfirmPassword'))?.value||'';
  if(!newEmail&&!newPass){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Nothing to change','Enter a new email or password','orange');return;}
  if(newPass||confirmPass){
    if(newPass.length<6){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Password too short','Use at least 6 characters','orange');return;}
    if(newPass!==confirmPass){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Passwords do not match','','orange');return;}
  }
  if(newEmail&&!/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(newEmail)){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Invalid email','','orange');return;}
  const ok=await customConfirm('Update your admin login? You may be signed out and need to sign back in with the new details.','Update Admin Account');
  if(!ok)return;
  if(!sb){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Cloud unavailable','Connect to internet first','orange');return;}
  try{
    const upd={};if(newEmail)upd.email=newEmail;if(newPass)upd.password=newPass;
    const {error}=await sb.auth.updateUser(upd);
    if(error)throw error;
    showAppleNotif('✅ Account updated', newEmail?('New email: '+newEmail):'Password changed','green',6000);
    if(document.getElementById('adminNewEmail'))document.getElementById('adminNewEmail').value='';
    if(document.getElementById('adminNewPwd'))document.getElementById('adminNewPwd').value='';
    if(document.getElementById('adminNewPassword'))document.getElementById('adminNewPassword').value='';
    if(document.getElementById('adminConfirmPwd'))document.getElementById('adminConfirmPwd').value='';
    if(document.getElementById('adminConfirmPassword'))document.getElementById('adminConfirmPassword').value='';
    const statusEl=document.getElementById('adminCredsStatus');
    if(statusEl){statusEl.style.color='#16a34a';statusEl.textContent='✅ '+(newEmail?'Email updated — please log in again.':'Password changed successfully.');}
    if(newEmail){
      showAppleNotif('📧 Verify new email','Check your inbox to confirm','blue',8000);
    }
  }catch(e){
    showAppleNotif('❌ Could not update', e.message||'Try again','red');
    const statusEl=document.getElementById('adminCredsStatus');
    if(statusEl){statusEl.style.color='#dc2626';statusEl.textContent='❌ '+(e.message||'Update failed');}
  }
}
