  if(_pendingDirty && navigator.onLine && window._authedEmail){
    try{navigator.sendBeacon && fetch(SUPABASE_URL+'/rest/v1/school_data?on_conflict=id',{method:'POST',headers:{'apikey':SUPABASE_ANON_KEY,'Authorization':'Bearer '+SUPABASE_ANON_KEY,'Content-Type':'application/json','Prefer':'resolution=merge-duplicates'},body:JSON.stringify([{school_id:window.currentSchool && window.currentSchool.id, data:data, updated_at:new Date().toISOString(), updated_by:window._authedEmail}]),keepalive:true});}catch(e){}
  }
});

// ===================== PARENTS PORTAL PAGE =====================
function loadParentsPortalPage(){
  const curr=td();
  const sq=(document.getElementById('ppSearch')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const fc=document.getElementById('ppClassFilter')?.value||'';
  const blocked=(data.blockedParents||[]).concat(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('blockedParents_'+(window.currentSchool?.id||''))||'[]'));
  // Collect unique parents from all students
  const seen=new Set();const rows=[];
  (curr.students||[]).forEach(s=>{
    if(!s.parentPhone)return;
    if(fc&&s.class!==fc)return;
    const key=s.id+'::'+s.parentPhone;
    if(seen.has(key))return;seen.add(key);
    if(sq&&!s.name.toLowerCase().includes(sq)&&!s.parentPhone.includes(sq))return;
    rows.push({studentId:s.id,studentName:s.name,cls:s.class,phone:s.parentPhone,blocked:blocked.includes(s.parentPhone)});
  });
  // Stats
  const totalEl=document.getElementById('ppTotalCount');
  const activeEl=document.getElementById('ppActiveCount');
  const blockedEl=document.getElementById('ppBlockedCount');
  if(totalEl)totalEl.textContent=rows.length;
  if(activeEl)activeEl.textContent=rows.filter(r=>!r.blocked).length;
  if(blockedEl)blockedEl.textContent=rows.filter(r=>r.blocked).length;
  // Table
  const tbody=document.querySelector('#ppTable tbody');if(!tbody)return;
  tbody.innerHTML='';
  if(!rows.length){tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center;padding:18px;color:var(--text-muted);">No parent records found.</td></tr>';return;}
  rows.forEach((r,i)=>{
    const tr=document.createElement('tr');
    const statusBadge=r.blocked?'<span class="badge badge-red">Blocked</span>':'<span class="badge badge-green">Active</span>';
    const blockBtn=r.blocked
      ?`<button class="btn-sm btn-success" onclick="ppToggleBlock('${r.phone}',false)">✅ Unblock</button>`
      :`<button class="btn-sm btn-danger" onclick="ppToggleBlock('${r.phone}',true)">🚫 Block</button>`;
    const waBtn=`<button class="btn-sm" style="background:#25d366;color:white;" onclick="ppOpenWhatsApp('${r.phone}')">💬 WhatsApp</button>`;
    tr.innerHTML=`<td>${i+1}</td><td><strong>${r.studentName.toUpperCase()}</strong></td><td>${r.cls}</td><td>+256 ${r.phone}</td><td>${statusBadge}</td><td style="display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap;">${blockBtn}${waBtn}</td>`;
    tbody.appendChild(tr);
  });
}

function ppOpenWhatsApp(phone){
  // Normalize to international format — Uganda prefix 256
  const digits=phone.replace(/\D/g,'');
  const intl=digits.startsWith('256')?digits:'256'+digits;
  // wa.me checks if number exists on WhatsApp
  window.open('https://wa.me/'+intl,'_blank');
}

function ppToggleBlock(phone,block){
  const key='blockedParents_'+(window.currentSchool?.id||'');
  let list=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'[]');
  if(block){if(!list.includes(phone))list.push(phone);}
  else{list=list.filter(p=>p!==phone);}
  localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(list));
  // Also store in school data for cloud sync
  if(!data.blockedParents)data.blockedParents=[];
  if(block){if(!data.blockedParents.includes(phone))data.blockedParents.push(phone);}
  else{data.blockedParents=data.blockedParents.filter(p=>p!==phone);}
  saveData();
  showAppleNotif(block?'🚫 Parent Blocked':'✅ Parent Unblocked','+256 '+phone,block?'red':'green');
  loadParentsPortalPage();
}

// ===================== USER MANAGEMENT PAGE =====================
function loadUserManagementPage(){
  renderUmSubjectsPicker();
  loadUmTeachersList();
}
function renderUmSubjectsPicker(){
  const grid=document.getElementById('umSubjectsPicker');if(!grid)return;
  const owners=_existingSubjectAssignments();
  let html='';
  ['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4'].forEach(cls=>{
    const subs=(data.subjects&&data.subjects[cls])||[];
    if(!subs.length)return;
    html+=`<div class="subj-picker-class" data-class="${cls}">
      <div class="subj-picker-class-head" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('collapsed')">
        <span>${cls}</span><span style="font-size:.75rem;opacity:.6;">▼</span>
      </div>
      <div class="subj-picker-class-body">`;
    subs.forEach(s=>{
      const k=cls+'::'+s.name;const owner=owners[k];const isTaken=!!owner;
      html+=`<label class="subj-pill ${isTaken?'disabled checked':''}">
        <input type="checkbox" class="um-ts-cb" value="${k}" ${isTaken?'checked disabled':''} onchange="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('checked',this.checked)">
        <span>${s.name}</span>${isTaken?`<span class="owner-tag">${owner}</span>`:''}
      </label>`;
    });
    html+='</div></div>';
  });
  grid.innerHTML=html||'<p style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.85rem;">No subjects configured yet.</p>';
}
function getUmPickedSubjects(){
  return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#umSubjectsPicker .um-ts-cb:checked:not(:disabled)')).map(cb=>{const[c,s]=cb.value.split('::');return{class:c,subject:s};});
}
async function umAddTeacher(){
  const email=(document.getElementById('umNewEmail')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const name=(document.getElementById('umNewName')?.value||'').trim();
  const pwd=(document.getElementById('umNewPassword')?.value||'');
  const msgEl=document.getElementById('umAddMsg');
  if(!email||!pwd){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--danger)';msgEl.textContent='⚠️ Email and password required.';}return;}
  if(pwd.length<6){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--danger)';msgEl.textContent='⚠️ Password must be at least 6 characters.';}return;}
  const subs=getUmPickedSubjects();
  if(!subs.length){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--danger)';msgEl.textContent='⚠️ Assign at least one subject.';}return;}
  if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--text-muted)';msgEl.textContent='Adding teacher...';}
  if(!navigator.onLine){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--danger)';msgEl.textContent='⚠️ No internet connection.';}return;}
  try{
    const{error:sErr}=await sb.auth.signUp({email,password:pwd,options:{data:{display_name:name}}});
    if(sErr&&!/already registered|user already exists|email already/i.test(sErr.message))throw sErr;
    // Add teacher to THIS school's allowed_users regardless of other schools they belong to
    const{error:iErr}=await sb.from('allowed_users').upsert({school_id:window.currentSchool.id,email,display_name:name||null,role:'teacher',subjects:subs},{onConflict:'school_id,email'});
    if(iErr)throw iErr;
    if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--success)';msgEl.textContent='✅ Teacher added successfully.';}
    document.getElementById('umNewEmail').value='';
    document.getElementById('umNewName').value='';
    document.getElementById('umNewPassword').value='';
    document.querySelectorAll('#umSubjectsPicker .um-ts-cb').forEach(cb=>cb.checked=false);
    showAppleNotif('✅ Teacher Added',name||email,'green');SFX.success();
    await loadUmTeachersList();
  }catch(e){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--danger)';msgEl.textContent='❌ '+e.message;}}
}
async function loadUmTeachersList(){
  let list=getCachedAllowedUsers().filter(u=>u.role==='teacher');
  if(navigator.onLine&&sb){
    try{
      const{data:rows}=await sb.from('allowed_users').select('*').eq('school_id',window.currentSchool.id).eq('role','teacher').order('created_at',{ascending:true});
      if(rows){list=rows;localStorage.setItem('jiib_allowed_users',JSON.stringify([...getCachedAllowedUsers().filter(u=>u.role!=='teacher'),...rows]));}
    }catch(e){}
  }
  const blockedTeachers=(data.blockedTeachers||[]).concat(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('blockedTeachers_'+(window.currentSchool?.id||''))||'[]'));
  // Stats
  const totalEl=document.getElementById('umTotalCount');
  const activeEl=document.getElementById('umActiveCount');
  const blockedEl=document.getElementById('umBlockedCount');
  if(totalEl)totalEl.textContent=list.length;
  if(activeEl)activeEl.textContent=list.filter(u=>!blockedTeachers.includes(u.email)).length;
  if(blockedEl)blockedEl.textContent=list.filter(u=>blockedTeachers.includes(u.email)).length;
  // Table — store teachers list for button handlers
  window._umTeachersList=list;
  const tbody=document.querySelector('#umTable tbody');if(!tbody)return;
  tbody.innerHTML='';
  if(!list.length){tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="5" style="text-align:center;padding:18px;color:var(--text-muted);">No teachers added yet.</td></tr>';return;}
  list.forEach((u,idx)=>{
    const isBlocked=blockedTeachers.includes(u.email);
    const subs=(u.subjects||[]);
    const subSummary=subs.length?subs.map(s=>'<span class="badge badge-blue" style="font-size:.68rem;margin:1px;">'+s.subject+' · '+s.class+'</span>').join(''):'<span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.78rem;">—</span>';
    const tr=document.createElement('tr');
    tr.innerHTML=`<td><strong>${u.email}</strong></td>
      <td>${u.display_name||'—'}</td>
      <td>${subSummary}</td>
      <td>${isBlocked?'<span class="badge badge-red">Blocked</span>':'<span class="badge badge-green">Active</span>'}</td>
      <td style="display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap;">
        <button class="btn-sm" data-um-idx="${idx}" data-um-action="edit">✏️ Edit</button>
        <button class="btn-sm ${isBlocked?'btn-success':'btn-danger'}" data-um-idx="${idx}" data-um-action="${isBlocked?'unblock':'block'}">${isBlocked?'✅ Unblock':'🚫 Block'}</button>
        <button class="btn-sm btn-danger" data-um-idx="${idx}" data-um-action="remove">🗑 Remove</button>
      </td>`;
    tbody.appendChild(tr);
  });
  // Attach events after render — avoids inline onclick escaping issues
  tbody.querySelectorAll('button[data-um-idx]').forEach(btn=>{
    btn.addEventListener('click',function(){
      const idx=parseInt(this.dataset.umIdx);
      const action=this.dataset.umAction;
      const teacher=window._umTeachersList[idx];
      if(!teacher)return;
      if(action==='edit')umEditTeacherModal(teacher);
      else if(action==='block')umToggleBlock(teacher.email,true);
      else if(action==='unblock')umToggleBlock(teacher.email,false);
      else if(action==='remove')umRemoveTeacher(teacher.email);
    });
  });
}
function umToggleBlock(email,block){
  const key='blockedTeachers_'+(window.currentSchool?.id||'');
  let list=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'[]');
  if(block){if(!list.includes(email))list.push(email);}
  else{list=list.filter(e=>e!==email);}
  localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(list));
  if(!data.blockedTeachers)data.blockedTeachers=[];
  if(block){if(!data.blockedTeachers.includes(email))data.blockedTeachers.push(email);}
  else{data.blockedTeachers=data.blockedTeachers.filter(e=>e!==email);}
  saveData();
  showAppleNotif(block?'🚫 Teacher Blocked':'✅ Teacher Unblocked',email,block?'red':'green');
  loadUmTeachersList();
}
async function umRemoveTeacher(email){
  const ok=await customConfirm(`Remove <strong>${email}</strong> from allowed teachers?`,'Remove Teacher');
  if(!ok)return;
  if(!navigator.onLine){showAppleNotif('📡 Offline','Connect to internet','orange');return;}
  try{
    await sb.from('allowed_users').delete().eq('school_id',window.currentSchool.id).eq('email',email);
    showAppleNotif('🗑 Removed',email,'red');SFX.delete();
    await loadUmTeachersList();
  }catch(e){showAppleNotif('❌ Error',e.message,'red');}
}
function umEditTeacherModal(teacher){
  // Build subject picker for this teacher
  const currentSubs=(teacher.subjects||[]);
  let pickerHtml='';
  ['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4'].forEach(cls=>{
    const subs=(data.subjects[cls]||[]);if(!subs.length)return;
    pickerHtml+=`<div style="margin-bottom:10px;"><div style="font-weight:700;font-size:.82rem;color:var(--primary);margin-bottom:6px;">${cls}</div><div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;">`;
    subs.forEach(s=>{
      const checked=currentSubs.some(x=>x.class===cls&&x.subject===s.name)?'checked':'';
      pickerHtml+=`<label class="subj-pill ${checked?'checked':''}" style="cursor:pointer;">
        <input type="checkbox" class="um-edit-cb" value="${cls}::${s.name}" ${checked} onchange="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('checked',this.checked)"> ${s.name}
      </label>`;
    });
    pickerHtml+='</div></div>';
  });
  customConfirm(
    `<div style="text-align:left;">
      <p style="margin-bottom:6px;font-size:.85rem;color:var(--text-muted);">Editing subjects for: <strong>${teacher.email}</strong></p>
      <div style="max-height:300px;overflow-y:auto;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:10px;padding:12px;background:var(--bg);" id="umEditPickerWrap">${pickerHtml}</div>
    </div>`,
    '✏️ Edit Teacher Subjects'
  ).then(async ok=>{
    if(!ok)return;
    const picked=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.um-edit-cb:checked')).map(cb=>{const[c,s]=cb.value.split('::');return{class:c,subject:s};});
    if(!picked.length){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Select at least one subject','','orange');return;}
    if(!navigator.onLine){showAppleNotif('📡 Offline','Cannot save changes offline','orange');return;}
    try{
      const{error}=await sb.from('allowed_users').update({subjects:picked}).eq('school_id',window.currentSchool.id).eq('email',teacher.email);
      if(error)throw error;
      showAppleNotif('✅ Subjects Updated',teacher.email,'green');SFX.success();
      await loadUmTeachersList();
    }catch(e){showAppleNotif('❌ Error',e.message,'red');}
  });
}

// ===================== PRINT MARKSHEETS PAGE =====================
function updatePmSubjectDropdown(){
  const cls=document.getElementById('pmClass')?.value;
  const sel=document.getElementById('pmSubject');if(!sel)return;
  sel.innerHTML='<option value="">— All Subjects —</option>';
  if(!cls)return;
  (data.subjects[cls]||[]).forEach(s=>{
    const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=s.name;o.textContent=s.name+(s.teacher?' ('+s.teacher+')':'');sel.appendChild(o);
  });
  // For teachers: filter to only their subjects
  if(isTeacher()){
    const allowed=(window._userSubjects||[]).filter(x=>x.class===cls).map(x=>x.subject);
    Array.from(sel.options).forEach(o=>{if(o.value&&!allowed.includes(o.value))o.remove();});
  }
}
function printPmMarksheets(){
  const cls=document.getElementById('pmClass')?.value;
  const subjectFilter=document.getElementById('pmSubject')?.value||'';
  if(!cls){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Select a Class','','orange');return;}
  const curr=td();
  const schema=getMarksSchema();
  const schoolName=(window._schoolDisplayName||window.currentSchool?.name||'School').toUpperCase();
  const schoolSettings=data?.settings||{};
  const addr1=schoolSettings.addr1||schoolSettings.address||'';
  const addr2=schoolSettings.addr2||'';
  const addr3=schoolSettings.addr3||'';
  const termLabel=curr.label||'';
  const printDate=new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-GB',{day:'numeric',month:'long',year:'numeric'});
  const optCls=getOptionalSubjectsClass();
  const classOrder=['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4'];
  const needsOpt=classOrder.indexOf(cls)>=classOrder.indexOf(optCls)&&classOrder.indexOf(optCls)>=0;
  let allStudents=(curr.students||[]).filter(s=>s.class===cls);
  allStudents.sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
  if(!allStudents.length){showAppleNotif('⚠️ No students in '+cls,'','orange');return;}
  const allSubs=(data.subjects[cls]||[]).filter(s=>{
    if(!needsOpt)return true;
    if(compulsorySubjects.includes(s.name))return true;
    return allStudents.some(st=>st.subjects&&st.subjects.includes(s.name));
  });
  // Filter subjects
  let subjectsToShow=subjectFilter?allSubs.filter(s=>s.name===subjectFilter):allSubs;
  // Teacher restriction
  if(isTeacher()){
    const allowed=(window._userSubjects||[]).filter(x=>x.class===cls).map(x=>x.subject);
    subjectsToShow=subjectsToShow.filter(s=>allowed.includes(s.name));
  }
  if(!subjectsToShow.length){showAppleNotif('⚠️ No subjects available','','orange');return;}

  function buildHeader(sub){
    return`<div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#0f2d6b 0%,#1e3a8a 60%,#2563eb 100%);border-radius:10px;padding:14px 18px 10px;margin-bottom:12px;color:#fff;display:flex;align-items:flex-start;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;">
      <div style="flex:1;">
        <div style="font-size:14pt;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:.5px;line-height:1.1;">${schoolName}</div>
        ${addr1?`<div style="font-size:7.5pt;opacity:.8;margin-top:2px;">${addr1}</div>`:''}
        ${addr2?`<div style="font-size:7.5pt;opacity:.8;">${addr2}</div>`:''}
        ${addr3?`<div style="font-size:7.5pt;opacity:.8;">${addr3}</div>`:''}
        <div style="margin-top:7px;display:flex;gap:0;flex-wrap:wrap;">
          <span style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.15);border-radius:5px;padding:3px 9px;font-size:7.5pt;font-weight:700;margin-right:6px;margin-bottom:4px;">CLASS: ${cls}</span>
          <span style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.15);border-radius:5px;padding:3px 9px;font-size:7.5pt;font-weight:700;margin-right:6px;margin-bottom:4px;">TERM: ${termLabel}</span>
          <span style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.15);border-radius:5px;padding:3px 9px;font-size:7.5pt;font-weight:700;margin-right:6px;margin-bottom:4px;">DATE: ${printDate}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="text-align:right;flex-shrink:0;">
        <div style="font-size:18pt;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:1px;line-height:1;">${sub.name}</div>
        ${sub.teacher?`<div style="font-size:8pt;opacity:.75;margin-top:2px;">Teacher: ${sub.teacher}</div>`:''}
        <div style="font-size:7.5pt;opacity:.7;margin-top:4px;">MARKSHEET</div>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }

  function buildTable(students,sub){
    const schemaHeaders=schema.map(c=>`<th style="padding:7px 6px;background:#1e3a8a;color:#fff;font-size:8pt;font-weight:800;text-align:center;min-width:66px;border-bottom:2px solid #2563eb;">${c.name}<br/><span style="font-size:6.5pt;opacity:.85;">(${c.max} marks)</span></th>`).join('');
    const rows=students.map((s,i)=>{
      const mk=(curr.marks||[]).find(m=>m.studentId===s.id&&m.subject===sub.name)||{};
      const hasAny=schema.some(c=>mk[c.field]!=null&&mk[c.field]!=='');
      const total=hasAny?schema.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+(Number(mk[c.field])||0),0):null;
      const markCells=schema.map(c=>{const val=(mk[c.field]!=null&&mk[c.field]!=='')?mk[c.field]:null;return`<td style="padding:4px 6px;border-bottom:1px solid #e2e8f0;text-align:center;"><div style="border:1.5px solid #cbd5e1;border-radius:6px;height:22px;width:54px;margin:0 auto;background:#fff;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;">${val!=null?`<span style="font-weight:700;font-size:9pt;color:#1e3a8a;">${val}</span>`:''}</div></td>`;}).join('');
      const totalCell=`<td style="padding:4px 6px;border-bottom:1px solid #e2e8f0;text-align:center;"><div style="border:2px solid #1e3a8a;border-radius:6px;height:22px;width:46px;margin:0 auto;background:#eff6ff;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;">${total!=null?`<span style="font-weight:800;font-size:9pt;color:#1e3a8a;">${total}</span>`:''}</div></td>`;
      return`<tr style="background:${i%2===0?'#ffffff':'#f8fafc'};"><td style="padding:5px 10px;border-bottom:1px solid #e2e8f0;font-size:8.5pt;font-weight:700;color:#1e3a8a;white-space:nowrap;">${i+1}.&nbsp;${s.name.toUpperCase()}</td>${markCells}${totalCell}</tr>`;
    }).join('');
    return`<table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;border:1.5px solid #1e3a8a;border-radius:8px;overflow:hidden;">
      <thead><tr><th style="padding:8px 10px;background:#1e3a8a;color:#fff;font-size:9pt;font-weight:800;text-align:left;min-width:170px;border-bottom:2px solid #2563eb;">STUDENT NAME</th>${schemaHeaders}<th style="padding:8px 6px;background:#0f2d6b;color:#fff;font-size:8pt;font-weight:800;text-align:center;min-width:58px;border-bottom:2px solid #2563eb;">TOTAL<br/><span style="font-size:6.5pt;opacity:.85;">(100)</span></th></tr></thead>
      <tbody>${rows}</tbody>
    </table>`;
  }

  function buildFooter(sub){
    return`<div style="margin-top:10px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-end;">
      <div style="font-size:8pt;color:#374151;font-weight:600;">Subject teacher: <span style="display:inline-block;width:200px;border-bottom:1.5px solid #374151;margin:0 8px;">&nbsp;</span> Signature: <span style="display:inline-block;width:130px;border-bottom:1.5px solid #374151;margin:0 8px;">&nbsp;</span></div>
      <div style="font-size:6.5pt;color:#cbd5e1;letter-spacing:.8px;">Printed from JIIBSCORE SYNC SYSTEM</div>
    </div>`;
  }

  let allPages='';
  subjectsToShow.forEach(sub=>{
    let subStudents=allStudents;
    if(needsOpt&&!compulsorySubjects.includes(sub.name))subStudents=allStudents.filter(s=>s.subjects&&s.subjects.includes(sub.name));
    if(!subStudents.length)return;
    allPages+=`<div style="padding:8mm 10mm;font-family:'Segoe UI',system-ui,Arial,sans-serif;color:#111;page-break-after:always;min-height:190mm;display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:space-between;">
      <div>${buildHeader(sub)}${buildTable(subStudents,sub)}</div>
      ${buildFooter(sub)}
    </div>`;
  });

  if(!allPages){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Nothing to print','No data available','orange');return;}
  const win=window.open('','_blank');
  win.document.write(`<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><title>Marksheets — ${cls} — ${schoolName}</title><style>*{box-sizing:border-box;margin:0;padding:0;}body{background:#fff;-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact;print-color-adjust:exact;}@page{size:A4 landscape;margin:0;}@media print{body{margin:0;}div[style*="page-break-after"]{page-break-after:always;}}</style></head><body>${allPages}</body></html>`);
  win.document.close();win.focus();setTimeout(()=>win.print(),450);
}

// ===================== TERM MANAGEMENT PAGE =====================
function loadTermManagementPage(){
  loadTermMgmt2();
  loadTmDeleteTermSelect();
}
function loadTermMgmt2(){
  const c=document.getElementById('tmPageCard');if(!c)return;
  c.innerHTML=Object.entries(data.terms).map(([key,t])=>{
    const ia=key===data.activeTerm,dc=t.locked?'locked':(ia?'active':'open'),std=t.students?t.students.length:0,mks=t.marks?t.marks.length:0;
    return`<div class="term-item ${ia?'active-term':''} ${t.locked?'locked-term':''}"><div class="term-dot ${dc}"></div><div style="flex:1;"><div style="font-weight:700;font-size:.92rem;color:var(--text);">${t.label}</div><div style="font-size:.75rem;color:var(--text-muted);">${std} students · ${mks} mark entries${t.locked?' · 🔒 Locked':''}</div></div>${ia?'<span class="badge badge-green">ACTIVE</span>':''}${t.locked?'<span class="badge badge-red">LOCKED</span>':''}${!ia&&!t.locked?`<button onclick="switchTerm('${key}')" class="btn-sm" style="font-size:.75rem;">Switch</button>`:''}</div>`;
  }).join('');
}
function loadTmDeleteTermSelect(){
  const sel=document.getElementById('tmDeleteTermSel');if(!sel)return;
  sel.innerHTML='<option value="">— Select a closed term —</option>';
  Object.entries(data.terms).forEach(([key,t])=>{
    if(t.locked&&key!==data.activeTerm){
      const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=key;o.textContent=t.label||key;sel.appendChild(o);
    }
  });
}
async function tmDeleteClosedTermData(){
  const sel=document.getElementById('tmDeleteTermSel');
  const key=sel?.value;const msgEl=document.getElementById('tmDeleteMsg');
  if(!key){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--danger)';msgEl.textContent='Select a closed term first.';}return;}
  const term=data.terms[key];
  const ok=await customConfirm(`Permanently delete all student and marks data for <strong>${term?.label||key}</strong>?<br>The term record is kept but all data inside is wiped. Cannot be undone.`,'Delete Term Data');
  if(!ok)return;
  if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--text-muted)';msgEl.textContent='Deleting...';}
  data.terms[key].students=[];data.terms[key].marks=[];data.terms[key].closedDate=new Date().toISOString();
  saveData();loadTermManagementPage();
  if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--success)';msgEl.textContent='✅ Term data deleted.';}
  showAppleNotif('🗑 Term Data Deleted',term?.label||key,'red');SFX.delete();
}

// ===================== MANAGE SUBJECTS PAGE (wrapper) =====================
function loadManageSubjectsPage(){
  // Sync dropdowns from this page to the original functions
  const msFC=document.getElementById('msFilterClass');
  if(msFC)msFC.addEventListener('change',()=>{
    const orig=document.getElementById('subjectFilterClass');
    if(orig){orig.value=msFC.value;loadSubjects();}
    loadMsSubjectTable();
  });
  loadMsSubjectTable();
}
function msAddSubject(){
  const name=(document.getElementById('msSubjectName')?.value||'').trim().toUpperCase();
  const cls=document.getElementById('msSubjectClass')?.value;
  const teacher=(document.getElementById('msSubjectTeacher')?.value||'').trim().toUpperCase();
  if(!name||!teacher){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Fill all fields','','orange');return;}
  if(!data.subjects[cls])data.subjects[cls]=[];
  if(data.subjects[cls].some(s=>s.name===name)){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Duplicate','','orange');return;}
  data.subjects[cls].push({name,teacher});saveData();loadMsSubjectTable();
  showAppleNotif('📚 Subject Added',name+' → '+cls,'green');SFX.success();
  if(document.getElementById('msSubjectName'))document.getElementById('msSubjectName').value='';
  if(document.getElementById('msSubjectTeacher'))document.getElementById('msSubjectTeacher').value='';
}
function loadMsSubjectTable(){
  const tbody=document.querySelector('#msSubjectTable tbody');if(!tbody)return;
  const fc=document.getElementById('msFilterClass')?.value||'';
  let list=[];
  Object.keys(data.subjects).forEach(cls=>{if(!fc||cls===fc)list=list.concat(data.subjects[cls].map(s=>({...s,class:cls})));});
  list.sort((a,b)=>a.class.localeCompare(b.class)||a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
  tbody.innerHTML='';
  list.forEach(s=>{
    const isC=compulsorySubjects.includes(s.name);
    const db=isC?'<button disabled style="background:#ccc;" class="btn-sm">Delete</button>':`<button onclick="msDel('${s.class}','${escapeJS(s.name)}')" class="btn-sm btn-danger">🗑</button>`;
    const tr=document.createElement('tr');
    tr.innerHTML=`<td>${s.name}</td><td><span class="badge badge-blue">${s.class}</span></td><td>${s.teacher||'N/A'}</td><td><button onclick="editSubject('${s.class}','${escapeJS(s.name)}')" class="btn-sm">✏️</button> ${db}</td>`;
    tbody.appendChild(tr);
  });
}
async function msDel(cls,name){
  const ok=await customConfirm(`Delete <strong>${name}</strong> from ${cls}?`,'Delete Subject');if(!ok)return;
  data.subjects[cls]=data.subjects[cls].filter(s=>s.name!==name);
  const curr=td();curr.marks=curr.marks.filter(m=>!(m.subject===name&&m.class===cls));
  saveData();loadMsSubjectTable();showAppleNotif('🗑 Deleted',name,'red');SFX.delete();
}

// ===================== GRADING SYSTEM PAGE =====================
function gsAddGrade(){
  const name=(document.getElementById('gsGradeName')?.value||'').trim().toUpperCase();
  const min=parseInt(document.getElementById('gsGradeMin')?.value);
  const max=parseInt(document.getElementById('gsGradeMax')?.value);
  const desc=(document.getElementById('gsGradeDescriptor')?.value||'').trim().toUpperCase();
  if(!name||isNaN(min)||isNaN(max)||!desc){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Fill all fields','','orange');return;}
  if(min<0||max>100||min>max){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Invalid range','','orange');return;}
  data.grading=data.grading.filter(g=>g.grade!==name);data.grading.push({grade:name,min,max,descriptor:desc});data.grading.sort((a,b)=>b.min-a.min);
  saveData();loadGsGradeTable();showAppleNotif('✅ Grade Added',name,'green');SFX.success();
  ['gsGradeName','gsGradeMin','gsGradeMax','gsGradeDescriptor'].forEach(id=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.value='';});
}
function loadGsGradeTable(){
  const tbody=document.querySelector('#gsGradeTable tbody');if(!tbody)return;
  tbody.innerHTML='';
  data.grading.forEach(g=>{
    const tr=document.createElement('tr');
    tr.innerHTML=`<td><strong>${g.grade}</strong></td><td>${g.min}</td><td>${g.max}</td><td>${g.descriptor}</td><td><button onclick="gsDeleteGrade('${g.grade}')" class="btn-sm btn-danger">🗑</button></td>`;
    tbody.appendChild(tr);
  });
}
async function gsDeleteGrade(name){
  const ok=await customConfirm(`Delete grade <strong>${name}</strong>?`,'Delete');if(!ok)return;
  data.grading=data.grading.filter(g=>g.grade!==name);saveData();loadGsGradeTable();
  showAppleNotif('🗑 Deleted','','red');SFX.delete();
}

// ===================== PUBLISH DEADLINE =====================
function pdPublishDeadline(){
  const dt=document.getElementById('pdDeadlineInput')?.value;
  const msg=document.getElementById('pdDeadlineMsg')?.value||'';
  const statusEl=document.getElementById('pdStatus');
  if(!dt){if(statusEl){statusEl.style.color='var(--danger)';statusEl.textContent='⚠️ Select a deadline date and time.';}return;}
  data.marksDeadline={date:dt,message:msg,setAt:new Date().toISOString(),setBy:window._userDisplayName||window._authedEmail};
  saveData();
  if(statusEl){statusEl.style.color='var(--success)';statusEl.textContent='✅ Deadline published to all teachers.';}
  pdRenderCurrentDeadline();showAppleNotif('📢 Deadline Published',new Date(dt).toLocaleString(),'green');SFX.success();
}
async function pdClearDeadline(){
  const ok=await customConfirm('Remove the current marks deadline for all teachers?','Clear Deadline');if(!ok)return;
  data.marksDeadline=null;saveData();
  const statusEl=document.getElementById('pdStatus');
  if(statusEl){statusEl.style.color='var(--text-muted)';statusEl.textContent='Deadline cleared.';}
  pdRenderCurrentDeadline();showAppleNotif('🗑 Deadline Cleared','','orange');
}
function pdRenderCurrentDeadline(){
  const el=document.getElementById('pdCurrentDeadline');if(!el)return;
  const dl=data.marksDeadline;
  if(!dl||!dl.date){el.innerHTML='<p style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.85rem;">No deadline currently set.</p>';return;}
  const d=new Date(dl.date);
  el.innerHTML=`<div style="background:rgba(239,68,68,0.1);border:2px solid rgba(239,68,68,0.4);border-radius:12px;padding:16px 18px;">
    <div style="font-weight:700;color:var(--danger);font-size:.95rem;margin-bottom:4px;">⏰ Current Deadline</div>
    <div style="font-size:1.1rem;font-weight:800;color:var(--text);">${d.toLocaleString('en-GB',{weekday:'long',day:'numeric',month:'long',year:'numeric',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'})}</div>
    ${dl.message?`<div style="font-size:.88rem;color:var(--text-muted);margin-top:6px;">${dl.message}</div>`:''}
    <div style="font-size:.75rem;color:var(--text-muted);margin-top:8px;">Set by ${dl.setBy||'Admin'} on ${new Date(dl.setAt).toLocaleString('en-GB')}</div>
  </div>`;
}
function pdLoadPage(){
  pdRenderCurrentDeadline();
  const inp=document.getElementById('pdDeadlineInput');
  if(inp&&data.marksDeadline?.date)inp.value=data.marksDeadline.date;
  if(inp&&data.marksDeadline?.message){const m=document.getElementById('pdDeadlineMsg');if(m)m.value=data.marksDeadline.message;}
}

// ===================== SCHOOL SETTINGS PAGE =====================
function ssLoadPage(){
  const s=data?.settings||{};
  const setV=(id,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.value=v||'';};
  setV('ssSchoolName',s.schoolName||window._schoolDisplayName||window.currentSchool?.name||'');
  setV('ssMotto',s.motto||'');
  setV('ssAddr1',s.addr1||'');setV('ssAddr2',s.addr2||'');setV('ssAddr3',s.addr3||'');
  const badgeUrl=window._schoolBadgeUrl||window.currentSchool?.logo_url||'';
  const prev=document.getElementById('ssBadgePreview');
  if(prev){ if(badgeUrl){prev.src=badgeUrl;prev.style.display='block';} else prev.style.display='none'; }
  const fileInp=document.getElementById('ssBadgeFile'); if(fileInp) fileInp.value='';
  ssRenderHouseList();
  ssRenderMarksColumns(); // added in part 2 below
}
function ssRenderHouseList(){
  const el=document.getElementById('ssHouseList');if(!el)return;
  const houses=data?.settings?.houses||[];
  if(!houses.length){el.innerHTML='<span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.84rem;">No houses added yet.</span>';return;}
  el.innerHTML=houses.map((h,i)=>`
    <div style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;background:rgba(30,58,138,0.1);border:1.5px solid rgba(30,58,138,0.3);border-radius:20px;padding:6px 14px;">
      <span style="font-weight:700;color:var(--primary);font-size:.88rem;">🏠 ${h}</span>
      <button onclick="ssRemoveHouse(${i})" style="background:none;border:none;color:var(--danger);cursor:pointer;font-size:.9rem;padding:0;line-height:1;">✕</button>
    </div>`).join('');
}

function seLoadPage(){ seRenderEventList(); }

function seRenderEventList(){
  const el=document.getElementById('seEventList'); if(!el) return;
  const events=(data.events||[]).slice().sort((a,b)=>(a.date||'').localeCompare(b.date||''));
  if(!events.length){ el.innerHTML='<span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.84rem;">No events added yet.</span>'; return; }
  const todayStr=new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
  el.innerHTML=events.map(ev=>{
    const isPast = ev.date && ev.date < todayStr;
    const d = ev.date ? new Date(ev.date+'T'+(ev.time||'00:00')) : null;
    const dateLabel = d ? d.toLocaleDateString('en-GB',{weekday:'short',day:'numeric',month:'short',year:'numeric'}) : 'No date';
    return `<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start;gap:12px;padding:12px 14px;border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:12px;margin-bottom:10px;${isPast?'opacity:.55;':''}">
      <div>
        <div style="font-weight:700;color:var(--text);">${ev.title}</div>
        <div style="font-size:.8rem;color:var(--text-muted);margin-top:2px;">📅 ${dateLabel}${ev.time?' · '+ev.time:''}${ev.location?' · 📍 '+ev.location:''}</div>
        ${ev.description?`<div style="font-size:.82rem;color:var(--text-muted);margin-top:6px;">${ev.description}</div>`:''}
      </div>
      <button onclick="seDeleteEvent('${ev.id}')" style="background:none;border:none;color:var(--danger);cursor:pointer;font-size:.95rem;flex-shrink:0;">✕</button>
    </div>`;
  }).join('');
}

function seAddEvent(){
  const title=(document.getElementById('seTitle')?.value||'').trim();
  const date=document.getElementById('seDate')?.value||'';
  const time=document.getElementById('seTime')?.value||'';
  const location=(document.getElementById('seLocation')?.value||'').trim();
  const description=(document.getElementById('seDesc')?.value||'').trim();
  if(!title||!date){ showAppleNotif('⚠️ Title & date required','','orange'); return; }
  if(!data.events) data.events=[];
  data.events.push({
    id:'ev_'+Date.now()+'_'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7),
    title,date,time,location,description,
    createdAt:new Date().toISOString(),
    createdBy:window._userDisplayName||window._authedEmail||'Admin'
  });
  saveData();
  seRenderEventList();
  ['seTitle','seDate','seTime','seLocation','seDesc'].forEach(id=>{const el=document.getElementById(id); if(el) el.value='';});
  showAppleNotif('🎉 Event Added',title,'green'); SFX.success();
}

async function seDeleteEvent(id){
  const ev=(data.events||[]).find(e=>e.id===id); if(!ev) return;
  const ok=await customConfirm(`Remove event <strong>${ev.title}</strong>?`,'Remove Event'); if(!ok) return;
  data.events=(data.events||[]).filter(e=>e.id!==id);
  saveData();
  seRenderEventList();
  showAppleNotif('🗑 Event Removed',ev.title,'red'); SFX.delete();
}

function ssAddHouse(){
  const inp=document.getElementById('ssNewHouse');
  const name=(inp?.value||'').trim().toUpperCase();
  if(!name){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Enter a house name','','orange');return;}
  if(!data.settings)data.settings={};
  if(!data.settings.houses)data.settings.houses=[];
  if(data.settings.houses.includes(name)){showAppleNotif('⚠️ House already exists','','orange');return;}
  data.settings.houses.push(name);
  saveData();ssRenderHouseList();
  if(inp)inp.value='';
  showAppleNotif('🏠 House Added',name,'green');SFX.success();
  // Refresh student registration dropdown
  refreshHouseDropdown();
}
async function ssRemoveHouse(idx){
  const h=data?.settings?.houses?.[idx];
  if(!h)return;
  const ok=await customConfirm(`Remove house <strong>${h}</strong>?`,'Remove House');if(!ok)return;
  data.settings.houses.splice(idx,1);
  saveData();ssRenderHouseList();
  showAppleNotif('🗑 House Removed',h,'red');SFX.delete();
  refreshHouseDropdown();
}
function refreshHouseDropdown(){
  // Update the student registration house input to a dropdown
  const houses=data?.settings?.houses||[];
  const houseInp=document.getElementById('studentHouse');
  if(!houseInp)return;
  if(!houses.length){
    // Revert to text input if no houses
    if(houseInp.tagName==='SELECT'){
      const inp=document.createElement('input');
      inp.type='text';inp.id='studentHouse';inp.placeholder='House';inp.style.width='120px';
      houseInp.replaceWith(inp);
    }
    return;
  }
  // Convert to select dropdown
  const sel=document.createElement('select');
  sel.id='studentHouse';sel.style.minWidth='120px';
  sel.innerHTML='<option value="">Select House</option>'+houses.map(h=>`<option value="${h}">${h}</option>`).join('');
  if(houseInp.tagName==='SELECT'){
    // Already a select — just update options
    houseInp.innerHTML=sel.innerHTML;
  }else{
    houseInp.replaceWith(sel);
  }
}

function ssPreviewBadgeFile(input){
  const file=input.files[0];if(!file)return;
  const reader=new FileReader();
  reader.onload=e=>{
    const prev=document.getElementById('ssBadgePreview');
    if(prev){prev.src=e.target.result;prev.style.display='block';}
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
}

async function ssSchoolSave(){
  const g=(id)=>(document.getElementById(id)?.value||'').trim();
  const msgEl=document.getElementById('ssMsg');
  if(!data.settings)data.settings={};
  const newName=g('ssSchoolName');
  data.settings.schoolName=newName;
  data.settings.motto=g('ssMotto');
  data.settings.addr1=g('ssAddr1');
  data.settings.addr2=g('ssAddr2');
  data.settings.addr3=g('ssAddr3');

  let logoUrl=null, uploadErr=null;

  const badgeFileInput=document.getElementById('ssBadgeFile');
  if(badgeFileInput&&badgeFileInput.files&&badgeFileInput.files[0]&&sb&&window.currentSchool){
    if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--text-muted)';msgEl.textContent='Uploading badge...';}
    try{
      const badgeFile=badgeFileInput.files[0];
      const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
      const img=await new Promise((res,rej)=>{const i=new Image();const u=URL.createObjectURL(badgeFile);i.onload=()=>{URL.revokeObjectURL(u);res(i);};i.onerror=rej;i.src=u;});
      const ratio=Math.min(300/img.width,300/img.height,1);
      canvas.width=Math.round(img.width*ratio);canvas.height=Math.round(img.height*ratio);
      canvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(img,0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
      const blob=await new Promise(res=>canvas.toBlob(res,'image/png',0.92));
      const path='badges/'+window.currentSchool.slug+'_'+Date.now()+'.png';
      const {error:upErr}=await sb.storage.from('school-badges').upload(path,blob,{contentType:'image/png',upsert:true});
      if(upErr){ uploadErr=upErr.message; }
      else{
        const {data:ud}=sb.storage.from('school-badges').getPublicUrl(path);
        logoUrl=ud?.publicUrl||null;
        if(!logoUrl) uploadErr='Could not generate public URL for badge.';
      }
      badgeFileInput.value='';
    }catch(e){ uploadErr=e.message; }
  }

  let dbErr=null;
  if(sb&&window.currentSchool){
    const updates={};
    if(newName) updates.name=newName;
    if(logoUrl) updates.logo_url=logoUrl;
    if(Object.keys(updates).length>0){
      const {error}=await sb.from('schools').update(updates).eq('id',window.currentSchool.id);
      if(error){ dbErr=error.message; }
      else{
        if(newName){
          window._schoolDisplayName=newName;
          window.currentSchool.name=newName;
          const nb=document.querySelector('.nav-brand');if(nb)nb.innerHTML='⚡ '+newName;
          const dt=document.getElementById('dashboardTitle');if(dt)dt.textContent=newName+' 🗣️';
          const lt=document.querySelector('.login-title');if(lt)lt.textContent=newName;
          const ls=document.getElementById('loginSchoolSub');if(ls)ls.textContent=newName;
          document.title=newName+' — JIIB$CORE SYNC';
          document.querySelectorAll('[data-school-name]').forEach(el=>el.textContent=newName);
        }
        if(logoUrl){
          window.currentSchool.logo_url=logoUrl;
          window._schoolBadgeUrl=logoUrl;
          document.querySelectorAll('img[src="badge.png"],img[src*="badge.png"]').forEach(img=>img.src=logoUrl);
          const previewEl=document.getElementById('ssBadgePreview');if(previewEl){previewEl.src=logoUrl;previewEl.style.display='block';}
        }
        try{localStorage.setItem('school_cache:'+window.currentSchool.slug,JSON.stringify(window.currentSchool));}catch(e){}
      }
    }
  }

  saveData();

  if(uploadErr||dbErr){
    if(msgEl){
      msgEl.style.color='var(--danger)';
      msgEl.textContent='⚠️ '+(uploadErr?('Badge upload failed: '+uploadErr):'')+(uploadErr&&dbErr?' — ':'')+(dbErr?('Save failed: '+dbErr):'');
    }
    showAppleNotif('⚠️ Save Incomplete', dbErr||uploadErr, 'orange', 9000);
  }else{
    if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--success)';msgEl.textContent='✅ School settings saved.';}
    showAppleNotif('✅ Saved','School settings updated','green');SFX.success();
  }
}

// ===================== SYSTEM SETTINGS PAGE =====================
function sysImportData(){
  const fi=document.getElementById('sysImportFile');
  if(!fi?.files[0]){showAppleNotif('⚠️ Select a file','','orange');return;}
  // Reuse the existing importData logic but with new file input
  const reader=new FileReader();
  reader.onload=function(e){
    showSpinner('Importing',['Reading file...','Processing...','Merging...']);
    setTimeout(()=>{
      try{
        const imp=JSON.parse(e.target.result);
        if(imp.terms){Object.entries(imp.terms).forEach(([k,t])=>{if(!data.terms[k])data.terms[k]=t;});
          if(imp.subjects)Object.keys(imp.subjects).forEach(cls=>{if(!data.subjects[cls])data.subjects[cls]=imp.subjects[cls];});
          if(imp.grading)data.grading=imp.grading;
        }else{
          const curr=td();
          (imp.students||[]).forEach(s=>{if(!curr.students.some(d=>d.id===s.id))curr.students.push(s);});
          (imp.marks||[]).forEach(m=>{if(!curr.marks.find(d=>d.studentId===m.studentId&&d.subject===m.subject))curr.marks.push(m);});
          if(imp.grading)data.grading=imp.grading;
        }
        saveData();hideSpinner();showAppleNotif('📥 Import Complete','','green');SFX.success();
      }catch(err){hideSpinner();showAppleNotif('❌ Import Failed','Invalid JSON','red');SFX.error();}
    },2000);
  };
  reader.readAsText(fi.files[0]);
}
async function sysChangeAdminCredentials(){
  const newEmail=(document.getElementById('sysAdminNewEmail')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const newPwd=document.getElementById('sysAdminNewPwd')?.value||'';
  const newPwdC=document.getElementById('sysAdminNewPwdConfirm')?.value||'';
  const msgEl=document.getElementById('sysCredsMsg');
  if(!newEmail&&!newPwd){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--danger)';msgEl.textContent='⚠️ Enter a new email or password.';}return;}
  if(newPwd&&newPwd.length<6){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--danger)';msgEl.textContent='⚠️ Password too short (min 6).';}return;}
  if(newPwd&&newPwd!==newPwdC){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--danger)';msgEl.textContent='⚠️ Passwords do not match.';}return;}
  if(!sb){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--danger)';msgEl.textContent='⚠️ Not connected.';}return;}
  if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--text-muted)';msgEl.textContent='Updating...';}
  try{
    const {data:sess}=await sb.auth.getSession();
    const token=sess?.session?.access_token;
    const efRes=await fetch(window._sbUrl+'/functions/v1/change-admin-credentials',{
      method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':'Bearer '+token},
      body:JSON.stringify({new_email:newEmail||null,new_password:newPwd||null})
    });
    if(!efRes.ok){const ej=await efRes.json().catch(()=>({}));throw new Error(ej.error||'Update failed');}
    if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--success)';msgEl.textContent='✅ Credentials updated.';}
    if(document.getElementById('sysAdminNewEmail'))document.getElementById('sysAdminNewEmail').value='';
    if(document.getElementById('sysAdminNewPwd'))document.getElementById('sysAdminNewPwd').value='';
    if(document.getElementById('sysAdminNewPwdConfirm'))document.getElementById('sysAdminNewPwdConfirm').value='';
    showAppleNotif('✅ Credentials Updated','','green');SFX.success();
  }catch(e){
    if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='var(--danger)';msgEl.textContent='❌ '+e.message;}
    showAppleNotif('❌ Update Failed',e.message,'red');
  }
}

// ===================== PARENT-ADMIN REALTIME CHAT =====================
let _chatChannel = null;
let _currentChatPhone = null; // which parent phone the admin is chatting with
let _typingTimer = null;
let _adminTypingTimer = null;

// ---- SHARED: Build channel name from parent phone ----
function chatChannelName(phone) {
  return 'parent-chat-' + (phone || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
}

// ---- PARENT SIDE ----
function openParentChat() {
  if (!isParent() || !window._parentPhone) return;
  const panel = document.getElementById('chatPanel');
  panel.style.display = 'flex';
  document.getElementById('chatPanelName').textContent = (window.currentSchool?.name || 'School') + ' Admin';
  document.getElementById('chatPanelStatus').textContent = window.currentSchool?.name || 'School Administration';
  loadParentChatMessages();
  subscribeParentChat();
}

function closeChatPanel() {
  document.getElementById('chatPanel').style.display = 'none';
  if (_chatChannel) {
    try { _chatChannel.unsubscribe(); } catch(e) {}
    _chatChannel = null;
  }
}

function subscribeParentChat() {
  if (!sb || !window._parentPhone) return;
  const ch = chatChannelName(window._parentPhone);
  if (_chatChannel) { try { _chatChannel.unsubscribe(); } catch(e) {} }

  _chatChannel = sb.channel(ch, { config: { presence: { key: 'parent_' + window._parentPhone } } });

  // New messages
  _chatChannel.on('broadcast', { event: 'message' }, ({ payload }) => {
    appendChatMessage('chatMessages', payload, false);
  });

  // Typing
  _chatChannel.on('broadcast', { event: 'typing' }, ({ payload }) => {
    if (payload.sender === 'admin') {
      const el = document.getElementById('chatTypingIndicator');
      const txt = document.getElementById('chatTypingText');
      if (el && txt) { txt.textContent = 'Admin is typing...'; el.style.display = 'block'; }
      clearTimeout(_typingTimer);
      _typingTimer = setTimeout(() => { if (el) el.style.display = 'none'; }, 2500);
    }
  });

  _chatChannel.subscribe();
}

function broadcastTyping() {
  if (!_chatChannel) return;
  _chatChannel.send({ type: 'broadcast', event: 'typing', payload: { sender: 'parent', phone: window._parentPhone } });
}

async function sendChatMessage() {
  const inp = document.getElementById('chatInput');
  const text = (inp.value || '').trim();
  if (!text || !sb || !window._parentPhone) return;
  inp.value = ''; inp.style.height = 'auto';

  const parentName = window._userDisplayName || ('Parent · ' + window._parentPhone);
  const msg = {
    id: Date.now(),
    sender: 'parent',
    senderName: parentName,
    phone: window._parentPhone,
    text,
    ts: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  // Save to Supabase
  await sb.from('parent_chat').insert({ school_id: window.currentSchool.id, phone: window._parentPhone, sender: 'parent', sender_name: parentName, text, ts: msg.ts });

  // Broadcast to admin
  const ch = sb.channel(chatChannelName(window._parentPhone));
  ch.send({ type: 'broadcast', event: 'message', payload: msg });

  appendChatMessage('chatMessages', msg, true);
}

async function loadParentChatMessages() {
  if (!sb || !window._parentPhone) return;
  const { data: rows } = await sb.from('parent_chat').select('*').eq('school_id', window.currentSchool.id).eq('phone', window._parentPhone).order('ts', { ascending: true }).limit(80);
  const box = document.getElementById('chatMessages');
  if (!box) return;
  box.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;font-size:.75rem;color:var(--text-muted);padding:8px;">Messages visible to school admin only</div>';
  (rows || []).forEach(r => appendChatMessage('chatMessages', { sender: r.sender, senderName: r.sender_name, text: r.text, ts: r.ts }, r.sender === 'parent'));
  box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
}

// ---- ADMIN SIDE ----
async function openAdminChatInbox() {
  if (!isAdmin()) return;
  const panel = document.getElementById('adminChatInbox');
  panel.style.display = 'flex';
  loadAdminChatList();
  subscribeAdminAllChats();
}

function closeAdminChatInbox() {
  document.getElementById('adminChatInbox').style.display = 'none';
}

function closeAdminChatWindow() {
  document.getElementById('adminChatWindow').style.display = 'none';
  _currentChatPhone = null;
  if (_chatChannel) { try { _chatChannel.unsubscribe(); } catch(e) {} _chatChannel = null; }
}

async function deleteAdminChat() {
  if (!_currentChatPhone || !sb) return;
  const phone = _currentChatPhone;
  const name = document.getElementById('adminChatWindowName').textContent || ('Parent · ' + phone);
  const ok = await customConfirm(
    `Delete the entire conversation with <strong>${name}</strong>?<br>This cannot be undone and the parent will also lose the chat history.`,
    '🗑 Delete Conversation'
  );
  if (!ok) return;
  try {
    showCardOverlay('settings', 'Deleting conversation...');
    const { error } = await sb.from('parent_chat').delete().eq('school_id', window.currentSchool.id).eq('phone', phone);
    if (error) throw error;
    // Clear the message box immediately
    const box = document.getElementById('adminChatMessages');
    if (box) box.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.88rem;">Conversation deleted.</div>';
    hideCardOverlay('settings');
    showAppleNotif('🗑 Conversation Deleted', name, 'red', 5000);
    SFX.delete();
    // Refresh the inbox list and close the window after a short delay
    loadAdminChatList();
    setTimeout(() => closeAdminChatWindow(), 1200);
  } catch (e) {
    hideCardOverlay('settings');
    showAppleNotif('❌ Delete Failed', e.message || 'Try again', 'red');
  }
}

let _adminAllChatsChannel = null;
function subscribeAdminAllChats() {
  if (!sb) return;
  if (_adminAllChatsChannel) { try { _adminAllChatsChannel.unsubscribe(); } catch(e) {} }
  // Listen for new messages on a global admin channel
  _adminAllChatsChannel = sb.channel('admin-chat-notify');
  _adminAllChatsChannel.on('broadcast', { event: 'new_parent_message' }, ({ payload }) => {
    showAppleNotif('💬 New Message', 'From ' + (payload.senderName || 'a parent'), 'green', 8000);
    updateAdminChatBadge();
    loadAdminChatList();
  });
  _adminAllChatsChannel.subscribe();
}

async function loadAdminChatList() {
  if (!sb) return;
  // Get last message per phone number
  const { data: rows } = await sb.from('parent_chat').select('*').eq('school_id', window.currentSchool.id).order('ts', { ascending: false }).limit(200);
  const seen = new Set();
  const convos = [];
  (rows || []).forEach(r => {
    if (!seen.has(r.phone)) { seen.add(r.phone); convos.push(r); }
  });

  const list = document.getElementById('adminChatList');
  if (!list) return;
  if (!convos.length) { list.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.88rem;">No parent messages yet</div>'; return; }

  list.innerHTML = convos.map(c => {
    const time = c.ts ? new Date(c.ts).toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }) : '';
    const name = c.sender === 'parent' ? (c.sender_name || 'Parent · ' + c.phone) : 'You';
    const initials = (c.sender_name || 'P').split(' ').map(w => w[0]).join('').slice(0, 2).toUpperCase();
    return `<div onclick="openAdminChatWith('${c.phone}','${escapeJS(c.sender_name||'Parent')}')" 
      style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:14px 16px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);cursor:pointer;transition:background .15s;"
      onmouseover="this.style.background='rgba(37,211,102,0.06)'" onmouseout="this.style.background=''">
      <div style="width:46px;height:46px;border-radius:50%;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#25d366,#128c7e);color:white;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-weight:700;font-size:.95rem;flex-shrink:0;">${initials}</div>
      <div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">
        <div style="font-weight:700;font-size:.9rem;color:var(--text);">${c.sender_name || 'Parent · ' + c.phone}</div>
        <div style="font-size:.78rem;color:var(--text-muted);white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;">${c.sender==='parent'?'':' You: '}${c.text||''}</div>
      </div>
      <div style="font-size:.7rem;color:var(--text-muted);flex-shrink:0;">${time}</div>
    </div>`;
  }).join('');
}

async function openAdminChatWith(phone, parentName) {
  _currentChatPhone = phone;
  document.getElementById('adminChatWindowName').textContent = parentName || ('Parent · ' + phone);
  document.getElementById('adminChatWindowStatus').textContent = '+256 ' + phone;
  document.getElementById('adminChatWindow').style.display = 'flex';

  // Load messages
  const { data: rows } = await sb.from('parent_chat').select('*').eq('school_id', window.currentSchool.id).eq('phone', phone).order('ts', { ascending: true }).limit(100);
  const box = document.getElementById('adminChatMessages');
  box.innerHTML = '';
  (rows || []).forEach(r => appendChatMessage('adminChatMessages', { sender: r.sender, senderName: r.sender_name, text: r.text, ts: r.ts }, r.sender === 'admin'));
  box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;

  // Subscribe to this parent's channel
  if (_chatChannel) { try { _chatChannel.unsubscribe(); } catch(e) {} }
  _chatChannel = sb.channel(chatChannelName(phone));
  _chatChannel.on('broadcast', { event: 'message' }, ({ payload }) => {
    if (payload.sender === 'parent') {
      appendChatMessage('adminChatMessages', payload, false);
    }
  });
  _chatChannel.on('broadcast', { event: 'typing' }, ({ payload }) => {
    if (payload.sender === 'parent') {
      const el = document.getElementById('adminTypingIndicator');
      if (el) el.style.display = 'block';
      clearTimeout(_adminTypingTimer);
      _adminTypingTimer = setTimeout(() => { if (el) el.style.display = 'none'; }, 2500);
    }
  });
  _chatChannel.subscribe();
}

function adminBroadcastTyping() {
  if (!_chatChannel || !_currentChatPhone) return;
  _chatChannel.send({ type: 'broadcast', event: 'typing', payload: { sender: 'admin' } });
}

async function adminSendMessage() {
  const inp = document.getElementById('adminChatInput');
  const text = (inp.value || '').trim();
  if (!text || !sb || !_currentChatPhone) return;
  inp.value = ''; inp.style.height = 'auto';

  const adminName = 'School Admin';
  const msg = { sender: 'admin', senderName: adminName, phone: _currentChatPhone, text, ts: new Date().toISOString() };

  await sb.from('parent_chat').insert({ school_id: window.currentSchool.id, phone: _currentChatPhone, sender: 'admin', sender_name: adminName, text, ts: msg.ts });

  // Broadcast to parent
  if (_chatChannel) _chatChannel.send({ type: 'broadcast', event: 'message', payload: msg });

  appendChatMessage('adminChatMessages', msg, true);
}

function updateAdminChatBadge() {
  const b = document.getElementById('adminChatUnreadBadge');
  if (b) { b.style.display = 'flex'; b.textContent = '●'; }
}

// ---- SHARED: Render a chat bubble ----
function appendChatMessage(containerId, msg, isMine) {
  const box = document.getElementById(containerId);
  if (!box) return;
  const time = msg.ts ? new Date(msg.ts).toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }) : '';
  const div = document.createElement('div');
  div.style.cssText = `display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:${isMine ? 'flex-end' : 'flex-start'};`;
  div.innerHTML = `
    <div style="max-width:78%;padding:10px 14px;border-radius:${isMine ? '18px 18px 4px 18px' : '18px 18px 18px 4px'};
      background:${isMine ? 'linear-gradient(135deg,#25d366,#128c7e)' : 'var(--card)'};
      color:${isMine ? 'white' : 'var(--text)'};
      border:${isMine ? 'none' : '1px solid var(--border)'};
      font-size:.9rem;line-height:1.45;word-break:break-word;
      box-shadow:0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.1);">
      ${!isMine ? `<div style="font-size:.68rem;font-weight:700;color:#128c7e;margin-bottom:3px;">${msg.senderName || ''}</div>` : ''}
      ${escapeHtml(msg.text)}
    </div>
    <div style="font-size:.65rem;color:var(--text-muted);margin-top:3px;padding:0 4px;">${time}</div>`;
  box.appendChild(div);
  box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
}

// ---- Notify admin on login if new parent messages ----
async function checkAdminChatNotifications() {
  if (!isAdmin() || !sb) return;
  const key = 'admin_chat_last_seen';
  const lastSeen = localStorage.getItem(key) || '1970-01-01';
  try {
    const { data: rows } = await sb.from('parent_chat').select('sender_name, text, ts').eq('school_id', window.currentSchool.id).eq('sender', 'parent').gt('ts', lastSeen).order('ts', { ascending: false }).limit(5);
    if (rows && rows.length > 0) {
      showAppleNotif('💬 ' + rows.length + ' new parent message' + (rows.length > 1 ? 's' : ''), rows[0].sender_name + ': ' + (rows[0].text || '').slice(0, 60), 'green', 9000);
      updateAdminChatBadge();
      localStorage.setItem(key, new Date().toISOString());
    }
  } catch(e) {}
}

async function checkSuperAdminReplies(){
  if(!sb||!window.currentSchool||isParent()||isTeacher())return;
  const key='last_sa_reply_seen_'+window.currentSchool.id;
  // Use the timestamp of the newest message already acknowledged
  // Updated to current time as soon as admin opens the contact panel (see openContactSuperAdmin patch)
  const lastSeen=localStorage.getItem(key)||'1970-01-01';
  try{
    const {data:rows}=await sb.from('contact_messages')
      .select('id,message,created_at')
      .eq('school_id',window.currentSchool.id)
      .eq('sender','super_admin')
      .gt('created_at',lastSeen)
      .order('created_at',{ascending:false})
      .limit(5);
    if(rows&&rows.length>0){
      // Mark as seen immediately so it never fires again for these messages
      const newestTs=rows[0].created_at;
      localStorage.setItem(key,newestTs);
      showAppleNotif('⚡ Super Admin replied','Open Settings → Contact Super Admin to view','blue',10000);
      if(typeof addNotif==='function')addNotif('Super Admin Reply','New reply from JIIB$CORE Admin','blue');
    }
  }catch(e){}
}

// ===================== PWA REGISTRATION =====================
if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
  window.addEventListener('load', async () => {
    try {
        const reg = await navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js');
      console.log('[PWA] Service Worker registered:', reg.scope);

      // Check for updates every 30 minutes
      setInterval(() => reg.update(), 30 * 60 * 1000);

      // Notify user when new version is available
      reg.addEventListener('updatefound', () => {
        const newWorker = reg.installing;
        newWorker.addEventListener('statechange', () => {
          if (newWorker.state === 'installed' && navigator.serviceWorker.controller) {
            showAppleNotif(
              '🔄 Update Available',
              'New version ready — tap to refresh',
              'blue',
              0 // stays until dismissed
            );
          }
        });
      });
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('[PWA] Service Worker registration failed:', err);
    }
  });
}

// ===================== INSTALL PROMPT =====================
let _installPrompt = null;

window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', e => {
  e.preventDefault();
  _installPrompt = e;
  // Show install button in nav after login
  showInstallButton();
});

window.addEventListener('appinstalled', () => {
  _installPrompt = null;
  showAppleNotif('📱 App Installed!', 'JIIB$CORE is now on your home screen', 'green', 6000);
  hideInstallButton();
});

function showInstallButton() {
  // Only show if not already installed and user is logged in
  if (document.getElementById('loginOverlay').style.display !== 'none') return;
  let btn = document.getElementById('pwaInstallBtn');
  if (!btn) {
    btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.id = 'pwaInstallBtn';
    btn.className = 'nav-util-btn';
    btn.innerHTML = '📲 Install App';
    btn.style.cssText = 'background:rgba(39,174,96,0.2);border-color:rgba(39,174,96,0.4);color:#4ade80;animation:jiibPulse 2s infinite;';
    btn.onclick = triggerInstall;
    // Insert before the dark mode button
    const darkBtn = document.getElementById('toggle-button');
    if (darkBtn) darkBtn.parentNode.insertBefore(btn, darkBtn);
  }
  btn.style.display = '';
}

function hideInstallButton() {
  const btn = document.getElementById('pwaInstallBtn');
  if (btn) btn.style.display = 'none';
}

async function triggerInstall() {
  if (!_installPrompt) {
    showAppleNotif('ℹ️ Already Installed', 'Check your home screen', 'blue');
    return;
  }
  _installPrompt.prompt();
  const { outcome } = await _installPrompt.userChoice;
  if (outcome === 'accepted') {
    showAppleNotif('🎉 Installing...', 'Adding to your home screen', 'green');
  }
  _installPrompt = null;
  hideInstallButton();
}

// Lock body scroll when login overlay is showing
function setLoginLock(locked) {
  document.body.classList.toggle('login-active', locked);
}

// Call on page load — login is visible by default
setLoginLock(true);

// ─── SCHOOL ADMIN — Contact Super Admin ─────────────────────────────────────
window.openContactSuperAdmin = function(){
  const existing=document.getElementById('csaOverlay'); if(existing)existing.remove();
  const ov=document.createElement('div');
  ov.id='csaOverlay';
  ov.style.cssText='position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(0,0,0,0.7);backdrop-filter:blur(8px);z-index:6000;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:20px;';
  ov.innerHTML=`
    <div style="background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:20px;padding:24px;max-width:480px;width:100%;max-height:90vh;display:flex;flex-direction:column;box-shadow:0 24px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.4);">
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin-bottom:16px;flex-shrink:0;">
        <div style="width:40px;height:40px;border-radius:50%;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#3b82f6,#8b5cf6);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:1.1rem;flex-shrink:0;">⚡</div>
        <div style="flex:1;">
          <div style="font-size:1rem;font-weight:800;color:var(--text);">Super Admin</div>
          <div style="font-size:.75rem;color:var(--text-muted);">JIIB$CORE System Administrator</div>
        </div>
        <button onclick="document.getElementById('csaOverlay').remove();" style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.06);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:50%;width:32px;height:32px;color:var(--text-muted);cursor:pointer;font-size:.9rem;">✕</button>
      </div>
      <div id="csaChatLog" style="flex:1;overflow-y:auto;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px;padding:4px 0;min-height:120px;max-height:340px;background:var(--bg);border-radius:10px;padding:12px;margin-bottom:12px;">
        <div style="text-align:center;font-size:.75rem;color:var(--text-muted);">Loading messages…</div>
      </div>
      <div style="flex-shrink:0;">
        <div style="margin-bottom:10px;">
          <label style="font-size:.72rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--text-muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px;display:block;margin-bottom:6px;">New Message</label>
          <textarea id="csaMsg" rows="3" placeholder="Type your message…" style="width:100%;padding:11px 14px;background:var(--bg);border:1.5px solid var(--border);border-radius:10px;color:var(--text);font-size:.9rem;outline:none;resize:none;font-family:inherit;box-sizing:border-box;" onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'&&!event.shiftKey){event.preventDefault();csaSendMsg();}"></textarea>
        </div>
        <div id="csaStatus" style="font-size:.83rem;min-height:18px;margin-bottom:10px;"></div>
        <button onclick="csaSendMsg()" style="width:100%;padding:11px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#3b82f6,#1d4ed8);color:white;border:none;border-radius:10px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;font-size:.9rem;">📤 Send Message</button>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(ov);
  ov.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===ov)ov.remove();});
  csaLoadThread();
  csaSubscribeThread();
  // Mark all super admin replies as seen the moment admin opens this panel
  if(window.currentSchool){
    localStorage.setItem('last_sa_reply_seen_'+window.currentSchool.id, new Date().toISOString());
  }
};

async function csaLoadThread(){
  if(!sb||!window.currentSchool)return;
  const log=document.getElementById('csaChatLog');if(!log)return;
  try{
    const {data:rows}=await sb.from('contact_messages').select('*').eq('school_id',window.currentSchool.id).order('created_at',{ascending:true}).limit(100);
    log.innerHTML='';
    if(!rows||!rows.length){log.innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.84rem;padding:20px;">No messages yet. Send your first message below.</div>';return;}
    (rows||[]).forEach(m=>csaAppendBubble(m));
    log.scrollTop=log.scrollHeight;
  }catch(e){if(log)log.innerHTML='<div style="color:var(--danger);font-size:.84rem;">Could not load messages.</div>';}
}

function csaAppendBubble(m){
  const log=document.getElementById('csaChatLog');if(!log)return;
  const isMine=m.sender!=='super_admin';
  const timeStr=m.created_at?new Date(m.created_at).toLocaleTimeString('en-GB',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}):'';
  const senderLabel=isMine?(m.sender_name||window._userDisplayName||'You'):'Super Admin';
  const div=document.createElement('div');
  div.style.cssText=`display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:${isMine?'flex-end':'flex-start'};`;
  div.innerHTML=`
    <div style="max-width:82%;padding:10px 13px;border-radius:${isMine?'18px 18px 4px 18px':'18px 18px 18px 4px'};
      background:${isMine?'linear-gradient(135deg,#3b82f6,#1d4ed8)':'var(--card)'};
      color:${isMine?'white':'var(--text)'};
      border:${isMine?'none':'1px solid var(--border)'};
      font-size:.88rem;line-height:1.45;word-break:break-word;
      box-shadow:0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.1);">
      <div style="font-size:.68rem;font-weight:700;color:${isMine?'rgba(255,255,255,0.7)':'#3b82f6'};margin-bottom:3px;">${senderLabel}</div>
      ${(m.message||'').replace(/\n/g,'<br>')}
    </div>
    <div style="font-size:.65rem;color:var(--text-muted);margin-top:3px;padding:0 4px;">${timeStr}</div>`;
  log.appendChild(div);
  log.scrollTop=log.scrollHeight;
}

let _csaChannel=null;
function csaSubscribeThread(){
  if(!sb||!window.currentSchool)return;
  if(_csaChannel){try{_csaChannel.unsubscribe();}catch(e){}_csaChannel=null;}
  _csaChannel=sb.channel('csa-thread-'+window.currentSchool.id)
    .on('postgres_changes',{event:'INSERT',schema:'public',table:'contact_messages',filter:'school_id=eq.'+window.currentSchool.id},
      (payload)=>{
        csaAppendBubble(payload.new);
        const log=document.getElementById('csaChatLog');if(log)log.scrollTop=log.scrollHeight;
      })
    .subscribe();
}

async function csaSendMsg(){
  const msgEl=document.getElementById('csaMsg');
  const text=(msgEl?.value||'').trim();
  const status=document.getElementById('csaStatus');
  if(!text){if(status){status.style.color='#ef4444';status.textContent='Please enter a message.';}return;}
  if(!window.currentSchool){if(status){status.style.color='#ef4444';status.textContent='School not loaded.';}return;}
  if(status){status.style.color='#93c5fd';status.textContent='Sending…';}
  try{
    const {error}=await sb.from('contact_messages').insert({
      school_id:window.currentSchool.id,
      sender_name:window._userDisplayName||window._authedEmail||'Admin',
      sender_email:window._authedEmail||'',
      message:text,
      read:false,
      sender:'school_admin',
      direction:'in',
      created_at:new Date().toISOString()
    });
    if(error)throw new Error(error.message);
    if(msgEl)msgEl.value='';
    if(status)status.textContent='';
  }catch(e){if(status){status.style.color='#ef4444';status.textContent='Error: '+e.message;}}
}

window.doSendContactMessage = async function(btn){
  const name=(document.getElementById('csaName')?.value||'').trim();
  const msg=(document.getElementById('csaMsg')?.value||'').trim();
  const status=document.getElementById('csaStatus');
  if(!msg){status.style.color='#ef4444';status.textContent='Please enter a message.';return;}
  btn.disabled=true;status.style.color='#93c5fd';status.textContent='Sending\u2026';
  try{
    if(!window.currentSchool)throw new Error('School not loaded.');
    const {data:sd,error:fe}=await sb.from('school_data').select('data').eq('school_id',window.currentSchool.id).maybeSingle();
    if(fe)throw new Error(fe.message);
    const nd=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(sd?.data||{}));
    if(!nd.contactMessages)nd.contactMessages=[];
    nd.contactMessages.push({ts:new Date().toISOString(),senderName:name||window._authedEmail,senderEmail:window._authedEmail||'',message:msg,schoolName:window.currentSchool.name||''});
    if(nd.contactMessages.length>20)nd.contactMessages=nd.contactMessages.slice(-20);
    const {error}=await sb.from('school_data').update({data:nd,updated_at:new Date().toISOString()}).eq('school_id',window.currentSchool.id);
    if(error)throw new Error(error.message);
    status.style.color='#34d399';status.textContent='\u2705 Message sent to Super Admin!';
    btn.disabled=false;
    setTimeout(()=>{document.querySelector('div[style*="inset:0"]')?.remove();},2000);
  }catch(e){status.style.color='#ef4444';status.textContent='Error: '+e.message;btn.disabled=false;}
};

// ─── School portal auto-refresh (3 min) ──────────────────────────────────────
setInterval(async()=>{
  try{
    if(!window._authedEmail||!sb||!window.currentSchool||!navigator.onLine)return;
    await syncPullFromCloud();
    checkBlobSize();
    flushOfflineQueue();
  }catch(e){}
},3*60*1000);

<!-- FAQ -->
<section class="mkt-features" style="background:#fff;">
  <div class="mkt-section-label">Common Questions</div>
  <h2 class="mkt-section-title">Frequently Asked Questions</h2>
  <div style="max-width:720px;margin:0 auto;">
    <div class="mkt-faq-item">
      <div class="mkt-faq-q" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('open')">
        <span>Does JIIB$CORE work without internet?</span><span class="mkt-faq-arrow">▾</span>
      </div>
      <div class="mkt-faq-a">Yes. Marks entry, viewing student records, and printing reports all work offline. Data syncs to the cloud automatically the next time the device connects to the internet.</div>
    </div>
    <div class="mkt-faq-item">
      <div class="mkt-faq-q" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('open')">
        <span>How long does setup take?</span><span class="mkt-faq-arrow">▾</span>
      </div>
      <div class="mkt-faq-a">Most schools are fully set up — with students, subjects, and staff accounts — within a single day of receiving their portal link.</div>
    </div>
    <div class="mkt-faq-item">
      <div class="mkt-faq-q" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('open')">
        <span>Can parents see other students' results?</span><span class="mkt-faq-arrow">▾</span>
      </div>
      <div class="mkt-faq-a">No. Parents log in using their registered phone number and can only ever view their own children's performance summaries — data is fully isolated per family.</div>
    </div>
    <div class="mkt-faq-item">
      <div class="mkt-faq-q" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('open')">
        <span>Is our school's data shared with other schools?</span><span class="mkt-faq-arrow">▾</span>
      </div>
      <div class="mkt-faq-a">Never. Each school gets a fully isolated portal and database. No student, staff, or academic data is ever visible to or shared with another school on the platform.</div>
    </div>
    <div class="mkt-faq-item">
      <div class="mkt-faq-q" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('open')">
        <span>What does JIIBY AI actually do?</span><span class="mkt-faq-arrow">▾</span>
      </div>
      <div class="mkt-faq-a">JIIBY scans your school's academic data and surfaces things a busy administrator might miss — students trending downward, subjects underperforming across a class, or students close to failing — so you can intervene early.</div>
    </div>
  </div>
