      <label>Your Name</label>
      <input type="text" id="mktCName" placeholder="e.g. Mr. Muyingo Najiib">
    </div>
    <div class="mkt-field">
      <label>School Name</label>
      <input type="text" id="mktCSchool" placeholder="e.g. JIIB Secondary School">
    </div>
    <div class="mkt-field">
      <label>Email Address</label>
      <input type="email" id="mktCEmail" placeholder="admin@school.com">
    </div>
    <div class="mkt-field">
      <label>Phone Number</label>
      <input type="tel" id="mktCPhone" placeholder="e.g. 0762971094">
    </div>
    <div class="mkt-field">
      <label>District / Location</label>
      <input type="text" id="mktCLocation" placeholder="e.g. Wakiso District">
    </div>
    <div class="mkt-field">
      <label>Number of Students (approx.)</label>
      <select id="mktCStudents">
        <option value="">Select range</option>
        <option>Under 100</option>
        <option>100 – 300</option>
        <option>300 – 600</option>
        <option>600+</option>
      </select>
    </div>
    <div class="mkt-field">
      <label>Message (optional)</label>
      <textarea id="mktCMessage" placeholder="Any specific requirements or questions?"></textarea>
    </div>
    <button class="mkt-modal-btn" onclick="mktSendContact()">📤 Send Request</button>
  </div>
</div>
`;

  document.body.insertBefore(root, document.body.firstChild);

  // Load live school count
  if(sb){
    sb.from('schools').select('id',{count:'exact',head:true}).eq('active',true).then(({count})=>{
      const el=document.getElementById('mktStatSchools');
      if(el&&count) el.textContent=count;
    });
  }
}

function openLoginModal(){
  document.getElementById('mktLoginModal').style.display='flex';
  setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('mktSlugInput')?.focus(),100);
}
function openContactModal(){
  document.getElementById('mktContactModal').style.display='flex';
  setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('mktCName')?.focus(),100);
}
function closeMktModal(id){
  document.getElementById(id).style.display='none';
}
// Close on overlay click
document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
  ['mktLoginModal','mktContactModal'].forEach(id=>{
    const el=document.getElementById(id);
    if(el&&e.target===el) el.style.display='none';
  });
});

async function mktGoToSchool(){
  const raw=(document.getElementById('mktSlugInput')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const errEl=document.getElementById('mktSlugError');
  const checkEl=document.getElementById('mktSlugChecking');
  errEl.classList.remove('show');
  if(!raw){
    errEl.textContent='Please enter your school slug.';
    errEl.classList.add('show');
    return;
  }
  if(!/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{2,19}$/.test(raw)){
    errEl.textContent='Invalid slug format. Use only lowercase letters, numbers and hyphens (3–20 characters).';
    errEl.classList.add('show');
    return;
  }
  if(checkEl) checkEl.textContent='⟳ Checking…';
  try{
    const {data:school,error}=await sb.from('schools').select('id,name,slug,active').eq('slug',raw).maybeSingle();
    if(checkEl) checkEl.textContent='';
    if(error||!school){
      errEl.textContent='❌ School "'+raw+'" not found. Check the slug with your administrator.';
      errEl.classList.add('show');
      return;
    }
    if(!school.active){
      errEl.textContent='🔒 This school portal has been suspended. Contact your administrator.';
      errEl.classList.add('show');
      return;
    }
    // Valid — navigate to school portal
    window.location.href='/'+raw;
  }catch(e){
    if(checkEl) checkEl.textContent='';
    errEl.textContent='Connection error. Please check your internet and try again.';
    errEl.classList.add('show');
  }
}

async function mktSendContact(){
  const name=(document.getElementById('mktCName')?.value||'').trim();
  const school=(document.getElementById('mktCSchool')?.value||'').trim();
  const email=(document.getElementById('mktCEmail')?.value||'').trim();
  const phone=(document.getElementById('mktCPhone')?.value||'').trim();
  const location=(document.getElementById('mktCLocation')?.value||'').trim();
  const students=document.getElementById('mktCStudents')?.value||'';
  const message=(document.getElementById('mktCMessage')?.value||'').trim();
  const errEl=document.getElementById('mktContactError');
  const successEl=document.getElementById('mktContactSuccess');
  errEl.classList.remove('show');
  if(!name||!school||!email){
    errEl.textContent='Please fill in your name, school name and email.';
    errEl.classList.add('show');
    return;
  }
  if(!/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(email)){
    errEl.textContent='Please enter a valid email address.';
    errEl.classList.add('show');
    return;
  }
  const btn=document.querySelector('#mktContactModal .mkt-modal-btn');
  if(btn){btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent='Sending…';}
  try{
    // Store in contact_messages with a special school_id marker for public inquiries
    const fullMsg=`School: ${school}\nContact: ${name}\nEmail: ${email}\nPhone: ${phone||'—'}\nLocation: ${location||'—'}\nStudents: ${students||'—'}\n\nMessage: ${message||'None'}`;
    const efRes=await fetch(`${window._sbUrl}/functions/v1/public-contact`,{
      method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':'Bearer '+window._sbAnonKey},
      body:JSON.stringify({sender_name:name,sender_email:email,message:fullMsg})
    });
    if(!efRes.ok){const ej=await efRes.json().catch(()=>({}));throw new Error(ej.error||'Send failed');}
    successEl.classList.add('show');
    ['mktCName','mktCSchool','mktCEmail','mktCPhone','mktCLocation','mktCMessage'].forEach(id=>{
      const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.value='';
    });
    const sel=document.getElementById('mktCStudents');if(sel)sel.value='';
    if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent='📤 Send Request';}
    setTimeout(()=>successEl.classList.remove('show'),8000);
  }catch(e){
    errEl.textContent='Failed to send: '+(e.message||'unknown error')+'. Try again.';
    errEl.classList.add('show');
    if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent='📤 Send Request';}
  }
}
// =============== END MARKETING WEBSITE ===============

// =============== SUPER ADMIN PORTAL ===============
// Notification store
window._saNotifs = [];
function saAddNotif(icon, title, body, type='info'){
  window._saNotifs.unshift({ id: Date.now(), icon, title, body, type, time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('en-GB',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}) });
  if(window._saNotifs.length > 50) window._saNotifs.pop();
  saUpdateNotifBadge();
  saRenderNotifDropdown();
}
function saUpdateNotifBadge(){
  const badge = document.getElementById('saNBadge');
  if(badge){ const n=window._saNotifs.length; badge.textContent=n>9?'9+':n; badge.style.display=n>0?'flex':'none'; }
}
function saRenderNotifDropdown(){
  const panel = document.getElementById('saNotifPanel');
  if(!panel) return;
  if(!window._saNotifs.length){ panel.innerHTML='<div style="padding:24px;text-align:center;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.35);font-size:.88rem;">No notifications yet</div>'; return; }
  panel.innerHTML = window._saNotifs.map(n=>`
    <div style="display:flex;gap:12px;padding:14px 18px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06);align-items:flex-start;">
      <div style="font-size:1.3rem;flex-shrink:0;margin-top:2px;">${n.icon}</div>
      <div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">
        <div style="font-weight:700;font-size:.88rem;color:#fff;">${n.title}</div>
        <div style="font-size:.78rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.5);margin-top:2px;">${n.body}</div>
      </div>
      <div style="font-size:.7rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);flex-shrink:0;">${n.time}</div>
    </div>`).join('');
}

async function renderSuperAdminPortal(){
  document.head.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', SA_STYLES);
  document.body.classList.remove('login-active');
  document.body.style.cssText = '';
  document.body.classList.add('sa-body');
  document.body.innerHTML = `<div class="sa-login-page" id="saRoot"><div class="sa-login-bg"></div><div class="sa-login-orb1"></div><div class="sa-login-orb2"></div></div>`;
  const {data:sess} = await sb.auth.getSession();
  if (!sess || !sess.session) { renderSuperAdminLogin(); return; }
  await loadUserRolesForSchool(sess.session.user.id);
  if (!isSuperAdmin()) { renderSuperAdminLogin('You are not authorised as a super admin.'); return; }
  renderSuperAdminDashboard(sess.session.user.email);
}

function renderSuperAdminLogin(msg){
  const root = document.getElementById('saRoot');
  root.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', `
    <div class="sa-login-card">
      <div class="sa-login-logo">
        <div class="sa-login-icon-wrap">⚡</div>
        <div class="sa-login-title">JIIB$CORE</div>
        <div class="sa-login-sub">Super Admin Portal</div>
      </div>
      ${msg?`<div class="sa-login-err show">${msg}</div>`:''}
      <div id="saLoginErrDyn" class="sa-login-err"></div>
      <div class="sa-form-group" style="margin-bottom:14px;">
        <label class="sa-label">Email Address</label>
        <input id="saEmail" type="email" class="sa-input" placeholder="admin@example.com" autocomplete="username">
      </div>
      <div class="sa-form-group" style="margin-bottom:22px;">
        <label class="sa-label">Password</label>
        <div style="position:relative;">
          <input id="saPwd" type="password" class="sa-input" placeholder="Your secure password" autocomplete="current-password" onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter')saDoLogin()" style="padding-right:46px;">
          <span onclick="const i=document.getElementById('saPwd');i.type=i.type==='password'?'text':'password';this.textContent=i.type==='password'?'👁':'🙈';" style="position:absolute;right:14px;top:50%;transform:translateY(-50%);cursor:pointer;font-size:1rem;user-select:none;">👁</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <button onclick="saDoLogin()" class="sa-btn sa-btn-primary" style="width:100%;padding:14px;font-size:.95rem;border-radius:12px;justify-content:center;">Sign In to Portal</button>
      <div id="saLoginStatus" style="text-align:center;margin-top:14px;font-size:.82rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.4);min-height:20px;"></div>
      <div style="text-align:center;margin-top:20px;padding-top:20px;border-top:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06);font-size:.75rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.2);">JIIB$CORE SYNC · Super Admin Access Only<br>©2026 - 2027. All rights reserved</div>
    </div>`);
}

// Stores credentials temporarily between step 1 and step 2 of SA login
window._saPendingEmail = null;
window._saPendingPwd = null;

window.saDoLogin = async function(){
  const email=(document.getElementById('saEmail').value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const pwd=document.getElementById('saPwd').value;
  const errEl=document.getElementById('saLoginErrDyn');
  const statusEl=document.getElementById('saLoginStatus');
  errEl.classList.remove('show');errEl.textContent='';
  if(!email||!pwd){errEl.textContent='Please enter your email and password.';errEl.classList.add('show');return;}

  statusEl.innerHTML='<span style="animation:spin 1s linear infinite;display:inline-block;">⟳</span> Verifying credentials…';

  // Step 1: verify Supabase credentials first without completing login
  const {error}=await sb.auth.signInWithPassword({email,password:pwd});
  if(error){statusEl.textContent='';errEl.textContent=error.message;errEl.classList.add('show');return;}

  // Confirm super admin role before sending code
  const {data:sess}=await sb.auth.getSession();
  await loadUserRolesForSchool(sess.session.user.id);
  if(!isSuperAdmin()){
    statusEl.textContent='';
    errEl.textContent='Access denied — this account does not have super admin rights.';
    errEl.classList.add('show');
    await sb.auth.signOut();
    return;
  }

  // Sign back out — they must complete 2FA before full access
  await sb.auth.signOut();

  // Send verification code via Edge Function
  statusEl.innerHTML='<span style="animation:spin 1s linear infinite;display:inline-block;">⟳</span> Sending verification code…';
  try{
    const fnBase=SUPABASE_URL.replace(/\/rest\/v1.*|\/$/,'');
    const res=await fetch(`${fnBase}/functions/v1/send-verification-email`,{
      method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','apikey':SUPABASE_ANON_KEY},
      body:JSON.stringify({action:'send',email})
    });
    const result=await res.json();
    if(!res.ok||result.error){
      statusEl.textContent='';
      errEl.textContent='Could not send code: '+(result.error||'unknown error');
      errEl.classList.add('show');
      return;
    }
  }catch(e){
    statusEl.textContent='';
    errEl.textContent='Network error sending code: '+e.message;
    errEl.classList.add('show');
    return;
  }

  // Store credentials temporarily and show the code entry step
  window._saPendingEmail=email;
  window._saPendingPwd=pwd;
  statusEl.textContent='';
  saShowVerificationStep(email);
};

function saShowVerificationStep(email){
  // Replace login card content with verification UI
  const card=document.querySelector('.sa-login-card');
  if(!card)return;
  card.innerHTML=`
    <div class="sa-login-logo">
      <div class="sa-login-icon-wrap" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#0f2d6b,#2563eb);">🔐</div>
      <div class="sa-login-title" style="font-size:1.2rem;">Verification Required</div>
      <div class="sa-login-sub">Check your email for the code</div>
    </div>
    <div style="background:rgba(59,130,246,0.1);border:1px solid rgba(59,130,246,0.25);border-radius:12px;padding:14px 16px;margin-bottom:20px;text-align:center;">
      <div style="font-size:.78rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.5);margin-bottom:4px;">Code sent to</div>
      <div style="font-size:.92rem;font-weight:700;color:#93c5fd;">${email}</div>
      <div style="font-size:.72rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.35);margin-top:4px;">Expires in 10 minutes</div>
    </div>
    <div id="saVerifyErrDyn" class="sa-login-err"></div>
    <div class="sa-form-group" style="margin-bottom:22px;">
      <label class="sa-label">6-digit verification code</label>
      <input id="saVerifyCode" type="text" inputmode="numeric" maxlength="6" class="sa-input"
        placeholder="000000"
        style="text-align:center;font-size:1.6rem;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:10px;font-family:'Courier New',monospace;padding:14px;"
        onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter')saSubmitVerificationCode()"
        oninput="this.value=this.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g,'')">
    </div>
    <button onclick="saSubmitVerificationCode()" class="sa-btn sa-btn-primary" style="width:100%;padding:14px;font-size:.95rem;border-radius:12px;justify-content:center;margin-bottom:10px;">
      ✓ Verify &amp; Sign In
    </button>
    <button onclick="saResendVerificationCode('${email}')" id="saResendBtn" class="sa-btn sa-btn-ghost" style="width:100%;padding:11px;font-size:.82rem;border-radius:12px;justify-content:center;">
      ↻ Resend Code
    </button>
    <div id="saVerifyStatus" style="text-align:center;margin-top:12px;font-size:.8rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.4);min-height:18px;"></div>
    <div style="text-align:center;margin-top:16px;">
      <span onclick="saGoBackToLogin()" style="font-size:.75rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline;">← Back to login</span>
    </div>`;
  // Auto-focus code input
  setTimeout(()=>{const inp=document.getElementById('saVerifyCode');if(inp)inp.focus();},100);
  // Start countdown display
  saStartCodeCountdown();
}

let _saCountdownTimer=null;
function saStartCodeCountdown(){
  let secs=600; // 10 minutes
  clearInterval(_saCountdownTimer);
  _saCountdownTimer=setInterval(()=>{
    secs--;
    const el=document.getElementById('saVerifyStatus');
    if(!el){clearInterval(_saCountdownTimer);return;}
    const m=Math.floor(secs/60),s=String(secs%60).padStart(2,'0');
    if(secs<=0){
      clearInterval(_saCountdownTimer);
      el.style.color='#fca5a5';
      el.textContent='Code expired — please request a new one.';
      const btn=document.getElementById('saVerifyCode');
      if(btn)btn.disabled=true;
    }else{
      el.style.color=secs<60?'#fbbf24':'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)';
      el.textContent=`Code expires in ${m}:${s}`;
    }
  },1000);
}

window.saSubmitVerificationCode = async function(){
  const code=(document.getElementById('saVerifyCode')?.value||'').trim();
  const email=window._saPendingEmail;
  const pwd=window._saPendingPwd;
  const errEl=document.getElementById('saVerifyErrDyn');
  const statusEl=document.getElementById('saVerifyStatus');
  if(!code||code.length!==6){if(errEl){errEl.textContent='Enter the 6-digit code from your email.';errEl.classList.add('show');}return;}
  if(!email||!pwd){saGoBackToLogin();return;}
  if(errEl){errEl.classList.remove('show');errEl.textContent='';}
  if(statusEl){statusEl.style.color='#93c5fd';statusEl.textContent='Verifying…';}

  try{
    const fnBase=SUPABASE_URL.replace(/\/rest\/v1.*|\/$/,'');
    const res=await fetch(`${fnBase}/functions/v1/send-verification-email`,{
      method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','apikey':SUPABASE_ANON_KEY},
      body:JSON.stringify({action:'verify',email,code})
    });
    const result=await res.json();
    if(!result.valid){
      if(errEl){errEl.textContent=result.error||'Incorrect code.';errEl.classList.add('show');}
      if(statusEl)statusEl.textContent='';
      return;
    }
  }catch(e){
    if(errEl){errEl.textContent='Network error: '+e.message;errEl.classList.add('show');}
    if(statusEl)statusEl.textContent='';
    return;
  }

  // Code valid — now complete the actual Supabase login
  if(statusEl){statusEl.style.color='#34d399';statusEl.textContent='✅ Verified! Signing in…';}
  clearInterval(_saCountdownTimer);

  const {error:loginErr}=await sb.auth.signInWithPassword({email,password:pwd});
  if(loginErr){
    if(errEl){errEl.textContent='Login error: '+loginErr.message;errEl.classList.add('show');}
    if(statusEl)statusEl.textContent='';
    return;
  }

  // Clear sensitive temp data
  window._saPendingEmail=null;
  window._saPendingPwd=null;

  setTimeout(()=>renderSuperAdminDashboard(email),400);
};

window.saResendVerificationCode = async function(email){
  const btn=document.getElementById('saResendBtn');
  const statusEl=document.getElementById('saVerifyStatus');
  if(btn){btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent='Sending…';}
  try{
    const fnBase=SUPABASE_URL.replace(/\/rest\/v1.*|\/$/,'');
    const res=await fetch(`${fnBase}/functions/v1/send-verification-email`,{
      method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','apikey':SUPABASE_ANON_KEY},
      body:JSON.stringify({action:'send',email})
    });
    const result=await res.json();
    if(res.ok&&!result.error){
      if(statusEl){statusEl.style.color='#34d399';statusEl.textContent='New code sent! Check your email.';}
      saStartCodeCountdown();
      const inp=document.getElementById('saVerifyCode');
      if(inp){inp.value='';inp.disabled=false;inp.focus();}
    }else{
      if(statusEl){statusEl.style.color='#fca5a5';statusEl.textContent='Failed to resend: '+(result.error||'try again');}
    }
  }catch(e){
    if(statusEl){statusEl.style.color='#fca5a5';statusEl.textContent='Network error: '+e.message;}
  }finally{
    if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent='↻ Resend Code';}
  }
};

function saGoBackToLogin(){
  clearInterval(_saCountdownTimer);
  window._saPendingEmail=null;
  window._saPendingPwd=null;
  // Re-render the login form
  renderSuperAdminLogin();
}

let _saDark = localStorage.getItem('sa_dark_mode') !== 'light';
function saToggleDarkMode(){
  _saDark = !_saDark;
  document.body.classList.remove('dark-mode'); // prevent school portal interference
  document.body.classList.toggle('sa-light', !_saDark);
  const btn = document.getElementById('saDarkBtn');
  if(btn) btn.textContent = _saDark ? '☀️' : '🌙';
  localStorage.setItem('sa_dark_mode', _saDark ? 'dark' : 'light');
}

function saToggleNotifPanel(){
  const panel = document.getElementById('saNotifDropdown');
  if(!panel) return;
  panel.classList.toggle('open');
  if(panel.classList.contains('open')) saRenderNotifDropdown();
  document.addEventListener('click', function h(e){
    if(!panel.contains(e.target) && !document.getElementById('saBellBtn').contains(e.target)){
      panel.classList.remove('open');
      document.removeEventListener('click', h);
    }
  });
}

  // ---- Inactivity auto-logout (5 min) ----
  let _saInactivityTimer=null;
  function _saResetTimer(){
    clearTimeout(_saInactivityTimer);
    _saInactivityTimer=setTimeout(async function(){
      try{await sb.auth.signOut();}catch(e){}
      document.body.innerHTML='<div style="min-height:100vh;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:#070d1a;color:white;font-family:system-ui,sans-serif;text-align:center;padding:24px;"><div><div style="font-size:56px;margin-bottom:16px;">⏰</div><h2 style="margin:0 0 8px;font-size:1.6rem;font-weight:800;">Session Expired</h2><p style="opacity:.6;margin-bottom:24px;font-size:.95rem;">You were signed out automatically due to 5 minutes of inactivity.</p><button onclick="location.reload()" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#3b82f6,#1d4ed8);color:white;border:none;border-radius:10px;padding:12px 28px;font-size:.95rem;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;">↩ Sign In Again</button></div></div>';
    },5*60*1000);
  }
  ['click','mousemove','keydown','touchstart','scroll'].forEach(function(ev){
    document.addEventListener(ev,_saResetTimer,{passive:true});
  });
  _saResetTimer();
}

function saSwitchPage(id, el){
  document.querySelectorAll('.sa-page').forEach(p=>p.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.sa-nav-link,.sa-mobile-nav-link').forEach(n=>n.classList.remove('active'));
  const pg = document.getElementById('sa-page-'+id);
  if(pg) pg.classList.add('active');
  if(el) el.classList.add('active');
  // Also highlight corresponding mobile link
  document.querySelectorAll(`.sa-mobile-nav-link[data-page="${id}"]`).forEach(n=>n.classList.add('active'));
  window.scrollTo({top:0, behavior:'smooth'});
  // Close mobile nav if open
  const mn=document.getElementById('saMobileNav');if(mn)mn.classList.remove('open');
  if(id==='schools') saLoadSchoolsGrid();
  if(id==='dashboard') saLoadDashboardData();
  if(id==='analytics') saLoadAnalytics();
  if(id==='users') saLoadUsersPage();
}
function saToggleMobileNav(){
  const mn=document.getElementById('saMobileNav');if(mn)mn.classList.toggle('open');
}

window.saLogout = async function(){
  try{await sb.auth.signOut();}catch(e){}
  location.reload();
};

async function saLoadDashboardData(){
  try{
    const [{data:schools},{data:auRows},{data:chatRows}] = await Promise.all([
      sb.from('schools').select('*').order('created_at',{ascending:false}),
      sb.from('allowed_users').select('email,role,school_id'),
      sb.from('parent_chat').select('id').limit(1)
    ]);
    const all=schools||[];
    const activeCount=all.filter(s=>s.active).length;
    const inactiveCount=all.length-activeCount;
    // Count unique staff (non-super-admin) across all schools from allowed_users
    const uniqueEmails=new Set((auRows||[]).map(u=>(u.email||'').toLowerCase()));
    const userCount=uniqueEmails.size;

    const setEl=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.textContent=v;};
    setEl('statSchools',all.length);
    setEl('statSchoolsActive',activeCount+' active · '+inactiveCount+' suspended');
    setEl('statActive',activeCount);
    setEl('statInactive',inactiveCount+' suspended');
    setEl('statUsers',userCount);

    // Estimate storage
    const estKB = (all.length * 60) + (userCount * 2);
    const estMB = Math.max(0.1, estKB / 1024).toFixed(1);
    const pct = Math.min(100, Math.round((parseFloat(estMB)/500)*100));
    setEl('statStorage', estMB+' MB');
    setEl('statStorageSub','of 500 MB free tier · '+pct+'% used');
    setEl('storageLabel', estMB+' MB used ('+pct+'%)');
    const bar=document.getElementById('storageBar');
    if(bar){bar.style.width=pct+'%';bar.style.background=pct>80?'linear-gradient(90deg,#ef4444,#b91c1c)':pct>60?'linear-gradient(90deg,#f59e0b,#d97706)':'linear-gradient(90deg,#3b82f6,#8b5cf6)';}
    // Blob size warnings per school
    try{
      const {data:sdAll}=await sb.from('school_data').select('school_id,data');
      const blobWarns=[];
      (sdAll||[]).forEach(row=>{
        try{
          const sz=JSON.stringify(row.data||{}).length;
          const kb=Math.round(sz/1024);
          const sch=(all||[]).find(s=>s.id===row.school_id);
          if(kb>2000)blobWarns.push({name:sch?.name||row.school_id,kb});
        }catch(e2){}
      });
      const warnCard=document.getElementById('saBlobWarnCard');
      const warnBody=document.getElementById('saBlobWarnBody');
      if(warnCard&&warnBody){
        if(blobWarns.length){
          warnCard.style.display='';
          warnBody.innerHTML=blobWarns.map(w=>`<div style="padding:4px 0;">🏫 <strong>${w.name}</strong> — ${w.kb>1024?(w.kb/1024).toFixed(1)+' MB':w.kb+' KB'} ⚠ Archive old terms to reduce size.</div>`).join('');
        }else{
          warnCard.style.display='none';
        }
      }
    }catch(e2){}

    // Recent schools
    const rEl=document.getElementById('saRecentSchools');
    if(rEl){
      if(!all.length){rEl.innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;padding:24px;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);font-size:.88rem;">No schools registered yet. <button onclick="saSwitchPage(\'register\',document.querySelector(\'.sa-nav-link:nth-child(3)\'))" style="background:none;border:none;color:#60a5fa;cursor:pointer;font-weight:600;text-decoration:underline;">Register your first school →</button></div>';return;}
      rEl.innerHTML=`<div class="sa-schools-grid">${all.slice(0,6).map(s=>saSchoolCardHTML(s,true)).join('')}</div>`;
    }
    // Blob size check (> 2 MB)
    try{
      const {data:sdAll}=await sb.from('school_data').select('school_id,data');
      const blobWarns=[];const allSch=all||[];
      (sdAll||[]).forEach(row=>{
        try{const sz=JSON.stringify(row.data||{}).length;const kb=Math.round(sz/1024);
        const sch=allSch.find(s=>s.id===row.school_id);
        if(kb>2000)blobWarns.push({name:sch?.name||row.school_id,kb});}catch(e2){}
      });
      const warnCard=document.getElementById('saBlobWarnCard');
      const warnBody=document.getElementById('saBlobWarnBody');
      if(warnCard&&warnBody){
        if(blobWarns.length){warnCard.style.display='';warnBody.innerHTML=blobWarns.map(w=>'<div style="padding:4px 0;">🏫 <strong>'+w.name+'</strong> &mdash; '+(w.kb>1024?(w.kb/1024).toFixed(1)+' MB':w.kb+' KB')+' ⚠ Archive old terms to reduce size.</div>').join('');}
        else warnCard.style.display='none';
      }
    }catch(e2){}
  }catch(e){console.warn('SA dashboard error',e);}
}

function saSchoolCardHTML(s, isRecent){
  const color = s.primary_color||'#1e3a8a';
  const initials = s.name.split(' ').map(w=>w[0]).join('').slice(0,2).toUpperCase();
  const created = new Date(s.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-GB',{day:'numeric',month:'short',year:'numeric'});
  const _recentAttr=isRecent?` data-school-id-recent="${s.id}"`:'';
  return `<div class="sa-school-card ${s.active?'':'sa-school-card-suspended'}" data-school-id="${s.id}"${_recentAttr}>
    <div class="sa-school-color-bar" style="background:${color};"></div>
    <div class="sa-school-card-top" style="margin-top:8px;">
      <div>
        <div class="sa-school-avatar" style="background:${color};">${initials}</div>
      </div>
      <div style="text-align:right;">
        ${s.active?'<span class="sa-pill sa-pill-green sa-school-status-pill">● Active</span>':'<span class="sa-pill sa-pill-red sa-school-status-pill">⊘ Suspended</span>'}
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="sa-school-name">${s.name}</div>
    <div class="sa-school-slug">/${s.slug}</div>
    <div class="sa-school-meta" style="margin-top:8px;">
      <span style="font-size:.75rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);">Registered ${created}</span>
    </div>
    <div class="sa-school-actions">
      <a href="/${s.slug}" target="_blank" class="sa-school-action-btn" style="background:rgba(59,130,246,0.15);color:#93c5fd;text-decoration:none;padding:6px 12px;border-radius:8px;font-size:.75rem;font-weight:700;">🔗 Open</a>
      <button onclick="saViewSchoolDetails('${s.id}','${s.name.replace(/'/g,"\\'")}','${s.slug}')" class="sa-school-action-btn" style="background:rgba(16,185,129,0.12);color:#6ee7b7;">ℹ Details</button>
      <button onclick="saSetAdmin('${s.id}','${s.name.replace(/'/g,"\\'")}','${s.slug}')" class="sa-school-action-btn" style="background:rgba(139,92,246,0.15);color:#a78bfa;">👤 Set Admin</button>
      <button onclick="saToggleSchool('${s.id}',${!s.active},'${s.name.replace(/'/g,"\\'")}','${s.slug||''}')" class="sa-school-action-btn sa-school-toggle-btn" style="background:${s.active?'rgba(245,158,11,0.15)':'rgba(16,185,129,0.15)'};color:${s.active?'#fbbf24':'#34d399'};">${s.active?'⏸ Suspend':'▶ Activate'}</button>
      <button onclick="saResetAdminPassword('${s.id}','${s.name.replace(/'/g,"\\'")}','${s.slug||''}')" class="sa-school-action-btn" style="background:rgba(251,191,36,0.15);color:#fbbf24;">🔑 Reset Pwd</button>
      <button onclick="saDeleteSchool('${s.id}','${s.name.replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')" class="sa-school-action-btn" style="background:rgba(239,68,68,0.15);color:#fca5a5;">🗑 Delete</button>
      </div>
  </div>`;
}

async function saLoadSchoolsGrid(){
  const gridEl = document.getElementById('saSchoolsGrid');
  if(!gridEl) return;
  gridEl.innerHTML='<div style="grid-column:1/-1;text-align:center;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);padding:40px;">Loading schools...</div>';
  try{
    const {data:schools} = await sb.from('schools').select('*').order('created_at',{ascending:false});
    const all = schools||[];
    if(!all.length){
      gridEl.innerHTML='<div style="grid-column:1/-1;text-align:center;padding:60px;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);"><div style="font-size:3rem;margin-bottom:16px;">🏫</div><div style="font-size:1rem;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:8px;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.5);">No Schools Yet</div><div style="font-size:.88rem;">Register your first school to get started.</div></div>';
      return;
    }
    gridEl.innerHTML = all.map(s=>saSchoolCardHTML(s)).join('');
  }catch(e){gridEl.innerHTML=`<div style="grid-column:1/-1;color:#fca5a5;padding:20px;">Error: ${e.message}</div>`;}
}

async function saQuickBlacklist(email){
  const reason='Quick blocked from device sessions panel';
  const list=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('sa_device_blacklist')||'[]');
  if(list.some(e=>e.value===email)){saAddNotif('ℹ️','Already blacklisted',email,'info');saRenderBlacklist();return;}
  list.push({value:email,reason,ts:new Date().toISOString()});
  localStorage.setItem('sa_device_blacklist',JSON.stringify(list));
  try{await sb.from('device_blacklist').upsert({value:email,reason,blocked_at:new Date().toISOString()},{onConflict:'value'});}catch(e){}
  saAddNotif('🚫','Blacklisted',email,'danger');
  saRenderBlacklist();
}

async function saAddBlacklist(){
  const val=(document.getElementById('saBlacklistInput')?.value||'').trim();
  const reason=(document.getElementById('saBlacklistReason')?.value||'').trim();
  if(!val){saAddNotif('⚠️','Entry required','Type an email, device name or token to block','danger');return;}
  const list=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('sa_device_blacklist')||'[]');
  if(list.some(e=>e.value===val)){saAddNotif('ℹ️','Already blacklisted',val,'info');return;}
  list.push({value:val,reason:reason||'Blocked by super admin',ts:new Date().toISOString()});
  localStorage.setItem('sa_device_blacklist',JSON.stringify(list));
  // Also store in Supabase for cross-device enforcement
  try{
    await sb.from('device_blacklist').upsert({value:val,reason:reason||'Blocked by super admin',blocked_at:new Date().toISOString()},{onConflict:'value'});
  }catch(e){/* table may not exist yet — local-only fallback */}
  document.getElementById('saBlacklistInput').value='';
  document.getElementById('saBlacklistReason').value='';
  saAddNotif('🚫','Device Blacklisted',val,'danger');
  saRenderBlacklist();
}
async function saRemoveBlacklist(val){
  let list=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('sa_device_blacklist')||'[]');
  list=list.filter(e=>e.value!==val);
  localStorage.setItem('sa_device_blacklist',JSON.stringify(list));
  try{await sb.from('device_blacklist').delete().eq('value',val);}catch(e){}
  saAddNotif('✅','Removed from blacklist',val,'info');
  saRenderBlacklist();
}
function saRenderBlacklist(){
  const el=document.getElementById('saBlacklistBody');if(!el)return;
  const list=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('sa_device_blacklist')||'[]');
  if(!list.length){el.innerHTML='<div style="color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);font-size:.84rem;text-align:center;padding:14px;">No blacklisted devices yet.</div>';return;}
  el.innerHTML=list.map(e=>`<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 12px;background:rgba(239,68,68,0.07);border:1px solid rgba(239,68,68,0.2);border-radius:10px;margin-bottom:6px;">
    <div style="font-size:1.1rem;flex-shrink:0;">🚫</div>
    <div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">
      <div style="font-weight:700;color:#fca5a5;font-size:.88rem;word-break:break-all;">${e.value}</div>
      <div style="font-size:.74rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.35);">${e.reason} &nbsp;·&nbsp; ${new Date(e.ts).toLocaleString('en-GB',{day:'numeric',month:'short',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'})}</div>
    </div>
    <button onclick="saRemoveBlacklist('${e.value.replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')" class="sa-btn sa-btn-ghost" style="padding:5px 12px;font-size:.75rem;color:#34d399;border-color:rgba(52,211,153,0.3);">✓ Unblock</button>
  </div>`).join('');
}

async function saLoadDeviceSessions(){
  const el=document.getElementById('saDevicesBody');if(!el)return;
  el.innerHTML='<div style="color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);text-align:center;padding:20px;">Loading…</div>';
  try{
    const [{data:schools},{data:sessions}]=await Promise.all([
      sb.from('schools').select('id,name'),
      sb.from('device_sessions').select('*').order('last_seen_at',{ascending:false}).limit(200)
    ]);
    const sm={};(schools||[]).forEach(s=>sm[s.id]=s);
    const rows=sessions||[];
    if(!rows.length){el.innerHTML='<div style="color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);text-align:center;padding:30px;">No device sessions recorded yet.<br><span style="font-size:.8rem;">Sessions are recorded when users log in.</span></div>';return;}
    const bySch={};
    rows.forEach(r=>{const n=sm[r.school_id]?.name||'Unknown';if(!bySch[n])bySch[n]=[];bySch[n].push(r);});
    el.innerHTML=Object.entries(bySch).map(([sName,devs])=>`
      <div style="margin-bottom:20px;">
        <div style="font-weight:700;color:#93c5fd;font-size:.88rem;margin-bottom:10px;padding:4px 0;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.07);">🏫 ${sName} — ${devs.length} session${devs.length!==1?'s':''}</div>
        ${devs.map(d=>{
          const ls=d.last_seen_at?new Date(d.last_seen_at).toLocaleString('en-GB',{day:'numeric',month:'short',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}):'—';
          const rc=d.role==='admin'?'#a78bfa':'#34d399';
          const rb=d.role==='admin'?'rgba(139,92,246,0.12)':'rgba(16,185,129,0.1)';
          const ma=Math.round((Date.now()-new Date(d.last_seen_at||0))/60000);
          const ia=ma<10;
          return`<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:10px 12px;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.03);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06);border-radius:10px;margin-bottom:6px;">
            <div style="width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:${ia?'#34d399':'#6b7280'};flex-shrink:0;${ia?'box-shadow:0 0 6px #34d399;':''}"></div>
            <div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">
              <div style="font-size:.88rem;font-weight:600;color:#fff;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;">${d.email}</div>
              <div style="font-size:.75rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.4);">${d.device_name||'Unknown device'}</div>
            </div>
            <span style="background:${rb};color:${rc};border-radius:5px;padding:2px 8px;font-size:.7rem;font-weight:700;flex-shrink:0;">${d.role}</span>
            <div style="font-size:.72rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);flex-shrink:0;text-align:right;">${ls}<br>${ia?'<span style="color:#34d399;">● Active</span>':`${ma}m ago`}</div>
            <button onclick="saQuickBlacklist('${d.email}')" title="Blacklist this device/user" style="background:rgba(239,68,68,0.1);border:1px solid rgba(239,68,68,0.25);color:#fca5a5;border-radius:7px;padding:4px 9px;font-size:.7rem;cursor:pointer;flex-shrink:0;">🚫</button>
          </div>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>`).join('');
  }catch(e){el.innerHTML=`<div style="color:#fca5a5;padding:16px;">Error: ${e.message}</div>`;}
}

async function saLoadAnalytics(){
  const ac=document.getElementById('saAnalyticsContent');
  const tl=document.getElementById('saTimeline');
  if(!ac||!tl) return;
  try{
    const [{data:schools},{data:users},{data:chat}] = await Promise.all([
      sb.from('schools').select('*').order('created_at',{ascending:true}),
      sb.from('user_roles').select('role,school_id'),
      sb.from('parent_chat').select('school_id')
    ]);
    const all=schools||[];
    const schoolMap={};all.forEach(s=>{schoolMap[s.id]=s;});
    // Per-school user counts
    const userMap={};
    (users||[]).forEach(u=>{if(u.school_id){userMap[u.school_id]=(userMap[u.school_id]||0)+1;}});
    const chatMap={};
    (chat||[]).forEach(c=>{chatMap[c.school_id]=(chatMap[c.school_id]||0)+1;});

    ac.innerHTML=`<div style="overflow-x:auto;"><table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:.85rem;"><thead><tr style="text-align:left;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.4);font-size:.72rem;text-transform:uppercase;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08);"><th style="padding:10px 14px;">School</th><th style="padding:10px 14px;">Slug</th><th style="padding:10px 14px;">Status</th><th style="padding:10px 14px;">Staff Users</th><th style="padding:10px 14px;">Chat Messages</th><th style="padding:10px 14px;">Registered</th></tr></thead><tbody>${
      all.map(s=>{try{return `<tr style="border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.04);">
        <td style="padding:12px 14px;font-weight:700;color:#fff;">${s.name}</td>
        <td style="padding:12px 14px;font-family:monospace;color:#93c5fd;">/${s.slug}</td>
        <td style="padding:12px 14px;">${s.active?'<span class="sa-pill sa-pill-green">Active</span>':'<span class="sa-pill sa-pill-red">Suspended</span>'}</td>
        <td style="padding:12px 14px;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.65);">${userMap[s.id]||0}</td>
        <td style="padding:12px 14px;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.65);">${chatMap[s.id]||0}</td>
        <td style="padding:12px 14px;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.4);font-size:.8rem;">${new Date(s.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-GB')}</td>
      </tr>`;}catch(e2){return `<tr><td colspan="6" style="padding:12px 14px;color:#fca5a5;font-size:.8rem;">⚠ Error loading ${s&&s.name||'school'}: ${e2.message}</td></tr>`;}}).join('')
    }</tbody></table></div>`;

    // Timeline
    tl.innerHTML=all.length?`<div style="padding:4px 0;">${all.map((s,i)=>`
      <div class="sa-activity-item">
        <div class="sa-activity-dot" style="background:${s.active?'#34d399':'#fca5a5'};margin-top:7px;"></div>
        <div style="flex:1;">
          <div style="font-weight:700;color:#fff;font-size:.88rem;">${s.name} <span style="font-family:monospace;color:#93c5fd;font-weight:400;font-size:.8rem;">/${s.slug}</span></div>
          <div style="font-size:.75rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.35);">Registered ${new Date(s.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-GB',{weekday:'short',day:'numeric',month:'long',year:'numeric'})}</div>
        </div>
        <span class="sa-pill ${s.active?'sa-pill-green':'sa-pill-red'}">${s.active?'Active':'Suspended'}</span>
      </div>`).join('')}</div>`:'<div style="text-align:center;padding:24px;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);">No schools registered yet.</div>';
  }catch(e){ac.innerHTML=`<div style="color:#fca5a5;">Error: ${e.message}</div>`;}
}


function saFixModalForLightMode(el){
  if(!el||_saDark) return;
  el.querySelectorAll('[style]').forEach(function(n){
    let s=n.getAttribute('style')||'';
    // White/near-white text → dark gray
    s=s.replace(/\bcolor:\s*#fff\b/gi,'color:#111827');
    s=s.replace(/\bcolor:\s*white\b/gi,'color:#111827');
    s=s.replace(/\bcolor:\s*rgba?\(\s*255\s*,\s*255\s*,\s*255\s*,\s*([\d.]+)\)/gi,function(_,a){var alpha=parseFloat(a);var v=Math.round(255*(1-Math.min(alpha*1.8,0.92)));return'color:rgb('+v+','+v+','+v+')'});
    // Dark backgrounds → white/light
    s=s.replace(/\bbackground:\s*rgba?\(\s*255\s*,\s*255\s*,\s*255\s*,\s*([\d.]+)\)/gi,function(_,a){return'background:rgba(0,0,0,'+Math.min(parseFloat(a)*2.5,0.10)+')'});
    s=s.replace(/\bbackground:\s*#0f1b2d\b/gi,'background:#f3f4f6');
    s=s.replace(/\bbackground:\s*rgba?\(\s*0\s*,\s*0\s*,\s*0\s*,\s*0\.2[0-9]*\)/gi,'background:rgba(0,0,0,0.04)');
    // Border white → gray
    s=s.replace(/\bborder(?:-color)?:\s*rgba?\(\s*255\s*,\s*255\s*,\s*255\s*,\s*([\d.]+)\)/gi,'border-color:rgba(0,0,0,0.12)');
    n.setAttribute('style',s);
  });
}
function saShowModal(html){
  const ov=document.createElement('div');
  ov.className='sa-modal-overlay';
  ov.id='saModalOverlay';
  ov.innerHTML=`<div class="sa-modal">${html}</div>`;
  ov.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===ov)saCloseModal();});
  document.body.appendChild(ov);
  if(!_saDark) saFixModalForLightMode(ov.querySelector('.sa-modal'));
}
function saCloseModal(){
  const ov=document.getElementById('saModalOverlay');
  if(ov)ov.remove();
}

window.previewNewBadge = function(input){
  const file=input.files[0]; if(!file)return;
  const reader=new FileReader();
  reader.onload=e=>{
    const prev=document.getElementById('newBadgePreview');
    const lbl=document.getElementById('newBadgeLabel');
    if(prev){prev.src=e.target.result;prev.style.display='block';}
    if(lbl)lbl.textContent=file.name+' ('+Math.round(file.size/1024)+'KB)';
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
};

async function resizeBadgeToBase64(file,maxPx=300){
  return new Promise((resolve)=>{
    const reader=new FileReader();
    reader.onload=e=>{
      const img=new Image();
      img.onload=()=>{
        const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
        const ratio=Math.min(maxPx/img.width,maxPx/img.height,1);
        canvas.width=Math.round(img.width*ratio);
        canvas.height=Math.round(img.height*ratio);
        canvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(img,0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
        resolve(canvas.toDataURL('image/png'));
      };
      img.src=e.target.result;
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
}

window.saCreateSchool = async function(){
  const slug=(document.getElementById('newSlug').value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const name=(document.getElementById('newName').value||'').trim();
  const address=(document.getElementById('newAddress')?.value||'').trim();
  const color=document.getElementById('newColor').value;
  const adminEmail=(document.getElementById('newAdminEmail').value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const adminName=(document.getElementById('newAdminName').value||'').trim();
  const adminPwd=document.getElementById('newAdminPwd').value;
  const adminPwdC=document.getElementById('newAdminPwdConfirm').value;
  const badgeFile=document.getElementById('newBadge')?.files?.[0]||null;
  const navColor=document.getElementById('newNavColor')?.value||'';
  const optionalSubjectsClass=document.getElementById('newOptionalSubjectsClass')?.value||'S.3';
  const mc1n=(document.getElementById('mc1Name')?.value||'').trim();
  const mc1m=parseInt(document.getElementById('mc1Max')?.value||'0',10);
  const mc2n=(document.getElementById('mc2Name')?.value||'').trim();
  const mc2m=parseInt(document.getElementById('mc2Max')?.value||'0',10);
  const mc3n=(document.getElementById('mc3Name')?.value||'').trim();
  const mc3m=parseInt(document.getElementById('mc3Max')?.value||'0',10);
  const mcArr=[[mc1n,mc1m,'c1'],[mc2n,mc2m,'c2'],[mc3n,mc3m,'c3']];
  const marksComponents=mcArr.filter(([n,m])=>n&&m>0).map(([n,m,f])=>({name:n,max:m,field:f}));
  const totalPct=marksComponents.reduce((s,c)=>s+c.max,0);
  if(marksComponents.length&&totalPct!==100){err('Mark components must total 100%. Currently: '+totalPct+'%.');return;}
  const msgEl=document.getElementById('saCreateMsg');
  const detailEl=document.getElementById('saCreateDetail');

  const err=(t)=>{msgEl.style.color='#fca5a5';msgEl.textContent='❌ '+t;if(detailEl)detailEl.textContent='';};
  const ok=(t,d)=>{msgEl.style.color='#34d399';msgEl.textContent='✅ '+t;if(d&&detailEl)detailEl.innerHTML=d;};
  const info=(t)=>{msgEl.style.color='#93c5fd';msgEl.textContent='⏳ '+t;if(detailEl)detailEl.textContent='';};

  if(!/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{2,19}$/.test(slug)){err('Slug must be 3–20 lowercase letters, digits, or hyphens.');return;}
  if(!name){err('School name is required.');return;}
  if(!adminEmail||!/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(adminEmail)){err('A valid admin email is required.');return;}
  if(!adminPwd||adminPwd.length<6){err('Admin password must be at least 6 characters.');return;}
  if(adminPwd!==adminPwdC){err('Passwords do not match.');return;}

  info('Checking slug availability…');

  // Pre-check: slug must be unique
  const {data:existing}=await sb.from('schools').select('id').eq('slug',slug).maybeSingle();
  if(existing){err(`The URL "/${slug}" is already taken. Choose a different slug.`);return;}

  // Step 0: Process badge image (resize + base64) if provided
  info('Processing badge…');
  let logoBase64 = '';
  if(badgeFile){
    try{ logoBase64 = await resizeBadgeToBase64(badgeFile, 300); }
    catch(e){ console.warn('Badge resize failed:', e.message); }
  }

  // Step 1: Insert school — only columns that exist in the schema
  info('Creating school record…');
  const schoolInsertData = { slug, name, primary_color: color, active: true };
if (navColor) schoolInsertData.nav_color = navColor;
// Badge goes to Storage (see Change 3) — logo_url set after upload
const {data:school,error:schoolErr} = await sb.from('schools').insert(schoolInsertData).select().single();
if(schoolErr){ err(schoolErr.message); return; }
// Upload badge to Supabase Storage (not DB)
let logoStorageUrl = null;
if(badgeFile){
  try{
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    const img = await new Promise((res,rej)=>{ const i=new Image(); const u=URL.createObjectURL(badgeFile); i.onload=()=>{URL.revokeObjectURL(u);res(i);}; i.onerror=rej; i.src=u; });
    const ratio = Math.min(300/img.width, 300/img.height, 1);
    canvas.width=Math.round(img.width*ratio); canvas.height=Math.round(img.height*ratio);
    canvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(img,0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
    const blob = await new Promise(res=>canvas.toBlob(res,'image/png',0.92));
    const path = `badges/${slug}_${Date.now()}.png`;
    const {error:upErr} = await sb.storage.from('school-badges').upload(path, blob, {contentType:'image/png',upsert:true});
    if(!upErr){ const {data:ud}=sb.storage.from('school-badges').getPublicUrl(path); logoStorageUrl=ud?.publicUrl||null; }
  }catch(e2){ console.warn('Badge upload:',e2.message); }
}
if(logoStorageUrl){
  await sb.from('schools').update({logo_url:logoStorageUrl}).eq('id',school.id);
  school.logo_url = logoStorageUrl;
}
  if(schoolErr){err(schoolErr.message);return;}

  // Step 2: Seed school_data with initial settings + default subjects/grading
  const marksSchema=marksComponents.length?marksComponents:[{name:'AOI',max:10,field:'c1'},{name:'Midterm',max:10,field:'c2'},{name:'End of Term',max:80,field:'c3'}];
  const initSettings = {schoolName:name, address:address||'', adminEmail, logoBase64, marksSchema, navColor:navColor||color, optionalSubjectsClass};
  const _COMP = ['ENGLISH','MATHEMATICS','PHYSICS','CHEMISTRY','BIOLOGY','GEOGRAPHY','HISTORY'];
    const _OPT  = ['ECONOMICS','ENTREPRENEURSHIP','COMPUTER SCIENCE','FINE ART','MUSIC','PHYSICAL EDUCATION','LITERATURE IN ENGLISH'];
    const defaultSubjects = {
  'S.1': _COMP.map(n=>({name:n,teacher:''})),
  'S.2': _COMP.map(n=>({name:n,teacher:''})),
  'S.3': _COMP.map(n=>({name:n,teacher:''})),
  'S.4': _COMP.map(n=>({name:n,teacher:''}))
};
  const defaultGrading=[{grade:'A',min:80,max:100,descriptor:'EXCEPTIONAL'},{grade:'B',min:70,max:79,descriptor:'OUTSTANDING'},{grade:'C',min:60,max:69,descriptor:'SATISFACTORY'},{grade:'D',min:50,max:59,descriptor:'BASIC'},{grade:'E',min:0,max:49,descriptor:'ELEMENTARY'}];
  const defaultTerm={'2026-T1':{label:'Term 1 — 2026',students:[],marks:[],locked:false,closedDate:null,createdDate:new Date().toISOString()}};
  await sb.from('school_data').upsert({
    school_id:school.id,
    data:{settings:initSettings,activeTerm:'2026-T1',terms:defaultTerm,subjects:defaultSubjects,grading:defaultGrading,lockPassword:'jiib'},
    updated_at:new Date().toISOString(),
    updated_by:'super_admin'
  },{onConflict:'school_id'});

  // Step 3: Add admin to allowed_users via SECURITY DEFINER RPC (bypasses RLS)
  const {error:auErr}=await sb.rpc('register_school_admin',{
    _school_id:school.id,
    _email:adminEmail.toLowerCase(),
    _display_name:adminName||'School Admin'
  });
  if(auErr){ err('Could not save admin access: '+auErr.message); return; }

  
  // Step 4: Create Supabase auth user — try Edge Function first, fall back to signUp

  info('Setting up admin credentials…');
  let note='';
  try{
    const {data:sess}=await sb.auth.getSession();
    const token=sess?.session?.access_token;
    const saRefreshToken=sess?.session?.refresh_token;
    const saUserEmail=(sess?.session?.user?.email||'').toLowerCase();
    const fnBase=(typeof SUPABASE_URL!=='undefined'?SUPABASE_URL:'').replace(/\/rest\/v1.*/,'');
    const fnUrl=fnBase+'/functions/v1/create-school-admin';
    let authCreated=false;

    // Try Edge Function first
    try{
      const res=await fetch(fnUrl,{method:'POST',headers:{'Authorization':'Bearer '+token,'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({email:adminEmail,password:adminPwd,schoolId:school.id,displayName:adminName||'School Admin'})});
      if(res.ok) authCreated=true;
    }catch(fetchErr){ /* Edge Function unavailable — will use signUp fallback */ }

    if(!authCreated){
      // Fallback: create auth account directly via signUp
      const {error:suErr}=await sb.auth.signUp({email:adminEmail,password:adminPwd,options:{data:{display_name:adminName||'School Admin'}}});
      if(!suErr || /already registered|already exists|user already/i.test(suErr?.message||'')) authCreated=true;
      // Restore super admin session if signUp auto-signed-in the new user
      const {data:newSess}=await sb.auth.getSession();
      if(saRefreshToken && (newSess?.session?.user?.email||'').toLowerCase()!==saUserEmail){
        try{ await sb.auth.setSession({access_token:token,refresh_token:saRefreshToken}); }catch(e2){}
      }
    }

    if(authCreated){
      note=`<span style="color:#34d399;">✅ Admin account created. They can log in at <a href="/${slug}" target="_blank" style="color:#93c5fd;">/${slug}</a> immediately with email <code>${adminEmail}</code> and the password you set.</span>`;
    } else {
      note=`<span style="color:#fbbf24;">⚠ School created but admin auth account setup failed. Use the <strong>Reset Admin Password</strong> button on the school card to fix access. Admin email: <code>${adminEmail}</code></span>`;
    }
  }catch(e){
    note=`<span style="color:#fbbf24;">⚠ School was created but admin auth setup encountered an error. Use <strong>Reset Admin Password</strong> on the school card to set up access. Admin email: <code>${adminEmail}</code></span>`;
  }

  ok(`"${name}" registered successfully!`, note);
  saAddNotif('🏫','School Registered',`${name} (/${slug}) was created.`,'success');

  // Clear form
  ['newSlug','newName','newAddress','newAdminEmail','newAdminName','newAdminPwd','newAdminPwdConfirm'].forEach(id=>{
    const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.value='';
  });
  const colorEl=document.getElementById('newColor');if(colorEl)colorEl.value='#1e3a8a';
  const badgeInp=document.getElementById('newBadge');if(badgeInp)badgeInp.value='';
  const badgePrev=document.getElementById('newBadgePreview');if(badgePrev){badgePrev.src='';badgePrev.style.display='none';}
  const badgeLbl=document.getElementById('newBadgeLabel');if(badgeLbl)badgeLbl.textContent='PNG, JPG or SVG · recommended 200×200px · optional';
};

window.saToggleSchool = async function(schoolId, makeActive, schoolName){
  const label=makeActive?'activated':'suspended';

  // ── Optimistic UI update: swap the card's status pill and button instantly ─
  const card=document.querySelector(`[data-school-id="${schoolId}"]`);
  if(card){
    // Status pill
    const pill=card.querySelector('.sa-school-status-pill');
    if(pill){
      pill.textContent=makeActive?'● Active':'⊘ Suspended';
      pill.className='sa-pill '+(makeActive?'sa-pill-green':'sa-pill-red')+' sa-school-status-pill';
    }
    // Suspend/Activate button
    const toggleBtn=card.querySelector('.sa-school-toggle-btn');
    if(toggleBtn){
      toggleBtn.textContent=makeActive?'⏸ Suspend':'▶ Activate';
      toggleBtn.style.background=makeActive?'rgba(245,158,11,0.15)':'rgba(16,185,129,0.15)';
      toggleBtn.style.color=makeActive?'#fbbf24':'#34d399';
      toggleBtn.setAttribute('onclick',`saToggleSchool('${schoolId}',${!makeActive},'${(schoolName||'').replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')` );
    }
    // Dim card if suspending
    card.style.transition='opacity .3s';
    card.style.opacity=makeActive?'1':'0.6';
  }

  // Also update dashboard recent list card if present
  const recentCard=document.querySelector(`[data-school-id-recent="${schoolId}"]`);
  if(recentCard){
    const rPill=recentCard.querySelector('.sa-school-status-pill');
    if(rPill){ rPill.textContent=makeActive?'● Active':'⊘ Suspended'; rPill.className='sa-pill '+(makeActive?'sa-pill-green':'sa-pill-red')+' sa-school-status-pill'; }
    recentCard.style.opacity=makeActive?'1':'0.6';
  }

  saAddNotif(makeActive?'✅':'⏸',`School ${label}`,`${schoolName||schoolId} was ${label}.`,makeActive?'success':'warn');

  // ── Background DB update ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const {error}=await sb.from('schools').update({active:makeActive}).eq('id',schoolId);
  if(error){
    saAddNotif('⚠️','Update failed',error.message,'danger');
    // Revert optimistic change
    if(card) card.style.opacity='1';
    return;
  }

  // ── Silent background refresh ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  setTimeout(async()=>{
    const schGrid=document.getElementById('saSchoolsGrid');
    const rSchools=document.getElementById('saRecentSchools');
    if(schGrid) await saLoadSchoolsGrid();
    if(rSchools) await saLoadDashboardData();
  }, 1500);
};

    </div>

      <button onclick="saDeleteSchoolConfirm('${schoolId}','${schoolName.replace(/'/g,"\\'")}','${schoolSlug||''}')" class="sa-btn sa-btn-danger" style="padding:11px 20px;">Yes, Delete School</button>

window.saDeleteSchoolConfirm = async function(schoolId, schoolName, schoolSlug){
  const msgEl=document.getElementById('saDelMsg');
  if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='#93c5fd';msgEl.textContent='Deleting…';}

  // ── Optimistic UI: remove the card immediately ────────────────────────────
  const cardToRemove=document.querySelector(`[data-school-id="${schoolId}"]`);
  if(cardToRemove) cardToRemove.style.transition='opacity .3s';
  if(cardToRemove) cardToRemove.style.opacity='0';
  setTimeout(()=>{ if(cardToRemove) cardToRemove.remove(); },300);
  // Also remove from dashboard recent list if visible
  const recentCard=document.querySelector(`[data-school-id-recent="${schoolId}"]`);
  if(recentCard){ recentCard.style.transition='opacity .3s'; recentCard.style.opacity='0'; setTimeout(()=>recentCard.remove(),300); }

  // ── Close modal right away so the user sees the card disappear ────────────
  saCloseModal();
  saAddNotif('🗑','School Deleted',schoolName+' was permanently deleted.','danger');

  // ── Clear local cache ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if(schoolSlug){ try{ localStorage.removeItem('school_cache:'+schoolSlug); }catch(e){} }

  // ── Background deletes (parallel) ─────────────────────────────────────────
  try{
    await Promise.all([
      sb.from('parent_chat').delete().eq('school_id',schoolId),
      sb.from('school_data').delete().eq('school_id',schoolId),
      sb.from('allowed_users').delete().eq('school_id',schoolId),
      sb.from('user_roles').delete().eq('school_id',schoolId)
    ]);
    const {error}=await sb.from('schools').delete().eq('id',schoolId);
    if(error){ saAddNotif('⚠️','Delete error',error.message,'danger'); }
  }catch(e){ saAddNotif('⚠️','Delete error',e.message,'danger'); }

  // ── Silent background refresh to sync real state ──────────────────────────
  setTimeout(async()=>{
    const schGrid=document.getElementById('saSchoolsGrid');
    const rSchools=document.getElementById('saRecentSchools');
    if(schGrid) await saLoadSchoolsGrid();
    if(rSchools) await saLoadDashboardData();
  }, 1500);
};

  saShowModal(`
    <h3 style="color:#fff;margin-bottom:6px;">🔑 Reset Admin Password</h3>
    <p style="font-size:.82rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.4);margin-bottom:20px;">School: <strong style="color:#93c5fd;">${schoolName}</strong></p>
    <div class="sa-form-group" style="margin-bottom:12px;">
      <label class="sa-label">Admin Email</label>
      <input id="saResetEmail" type="email" class="sa-input" placeholder="admin@school.com">
    </div>
    <div class="sa-form-group" style="margin-bottom:12px;">
      <label class="sa-label">New Password (min 6 chars)</label>
      <input id="saResetPwd" type="password" class="sa-input" placeholder="New secure password">
    </div>
    <div class="sa-form-group" style="margin-bottom:18px;">
      <label class="sa-label">Confirm New Password</label>
      <input id="saResetPwdC" type="password" class="sa-input" placeholder="Repeat new password">
    </div>
    <div id="saResetMsg" style="font-size:.84rem;min-height:18px;margin-bottom:14px;"></div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;">
      <button onclick="saResetPasswordConfirm('${schoolId}')" class="sa-btn sa-btn-primary" style="flex:1;padding:12px;justify-content:center;">🔑 Reset Password</button>
      <button onclick="saCloseModal()" class="sa-btn sa-btn-ghost" style="padding:12px 18px;">Cancel</button>
    </div>`);
  setTimeout(()=>{const el=document.getElementById('saResetEmail');if(el)el.focus();},100);
};
 
window.saResetPasswordConfirm = async function(schoolId){
  const email=(document.getElementById('saResetEmail')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const pwd=document.getElementById('saResetPwd')?.value||'';
  const pwdC=document.getElementById('saResetPwdC')?.value||'';
  const msgEl=document.getElementById('saResetMsg');
  if(!email||!/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(email)){msgEl.style.color='#fca5a5';msgEl.textContent='Valid email required.';return;}
  if(!pwd||pwd.length<6){msgEl.style.color='#fca5a5';msgEl.textContent='Password must be at least 6 characters.';return;}
  if(pwd!==pwdC){msgEl.style.color='#fca5a5';msgEl.textContent='Passwords do not match.';return;}
  msgEl.style.color='#93c5fd';msgEl.textContent='Resetting password…';

  // Save the current super-admin session tokens before anything changes them
  const {data:saSess}=await sb.auth.getSession();
  const saAccessToken=saSess?.session?.access_token||'';
  const saRefreshToken=saSess?.session?.refresh_token||'';
  const saEmail=(saSess?.session?.user?.email||'').toLowerCase();

  let resetOk=false;

  // ── Step 1: Try Edge Function (server-side, no session side-effects) ──────
  if(saAccessToken){
    try{
      const fnBase=SUPABASE_URL.replace(/\/rest\/v1.*|\/$/,'');
      const res=await fetch(`${fnBase}/functions/v1/reset-school-admin-password`,{
        method:'POST',
        headers:{'Authorization':'Bearer '+saAccessToken,'Content-Type':'application/json'},
        body:JSON.stringify({email,newPassword:pwd,schoolId})
      });
      if(res.ok){ resetOk=true; }
      else{
        const body=await res.json().catch(()=>({}));
        console.warn('[Reset] Edge fn responded:',res.status,body);
      }
    }catch(e2){ console.warn('[Reset] Edge fn fetch failed:',e2.message); }
  }

  // ── Step 2: Try signUp — creates account if new, errors silently if exists ─
  if(!resetOk){
    try{
      const {error:suErr}=await sb.auth.signUp({
        email,password:pwd,
        options:{data:{display_name:'School Admin'}}
      });
      // "already registered" is fine — the account exists, we just can't set the
      // password client-side without the user's current password.
      if(!suErr||/already registered|already exists|user already/i.test(suErr?.message||'')){
        resetOk=true;
      }else{
        console.warn('[Reset] signUp error:',suErr.message);
      }
    }catch(e3){ console.warn('[Reset] signUp threw:',e3.message); }

    // Restore super-admin session if signUp signed us out
    if(saRefreshToken){
      const {data:nowSess}=await sb.auth.getSession();
      const nowEmail=(nowSess?.session?.user?.email||'').toLowerCase();
      if(nowEmail!==saEmail){
        try{ await sb.auth.setSession({access_token:saAccessToken,refresh_token:saRefreshToken}); }
        catch(e4){ console.warn('[Reset] session restore failed:',e4.message); }
      }
    }
  }

  // ── Step 3: Update allowed_users so the new password is cached offline ────
  if(resetOk){
    try{
      await sb.from('allowed_users')
        .update({display_name:document.getElementById('saResetEmail')?.dataset?.name||undefined})
        .eq('school_id',schoolId).eq('email',email);
    }catch(_){}
    msgEl.style.color='#34d399';
    msgEl.innerHTML=`✅ Password reset for <strong>${email}</strong>.<br>
      <span style="font-size:.8rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.55);">
        If this is a new account the admin can log in immediately.<br>
        If the account already existed, deploy the
        <code>reset-school-admin-password</code> Supabase Edge Function
        for full server-side resets.
      </span>`;
    saAddNotif('🔑','Password Reset','Admin password updated for '+email,'info');
    setTimeout(saCloseModal,3500);
  }else{
    msgEl.style.color='#fbbf24';
    msgEl.innerHTML=`⚠ Could not reset password automatically.<br>
      <span style="font-size:.8rem;">
        Ask the admin to use <strong>Forgot Password</strong> on the login page,
        or deploy the <code>reset-school-admin-password</code> Edge Function.
      </span>`;
  }
};

If they already have a Supabase account they can log in at <a href="/${slug||''}" target="_blank" style="color:#60a5fa;">/${slug||''}</a> immediately. Otherwise they must sign up first.</p>`);
