window.saSetAdminConfirm = async function(schoolId, schoolName){
  const email=(document.getElementById('saSetEmail')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const name=(document.getElementById('saSetName')?.value||'').trim();
  const msgEl=document.getElementById('saSetMsg');
  if(!email||!/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(email)){msgEl.style.color='#fca5a5';msgEl.textContent='A valid email is required.';return;}
  msgEl.style.color='#93c5fd';msgEl.textContent='Saving…';
  const {error}=await sb.rpc('register_school_admin',{
    _school_id:schoolId,
    _email:email.toLowerCase(),
    _display_name:name||'School Admin'
  });
  if(error){msgEl.style.color='#fca5a5';msgEl.textContent='Error: '+error.message;return;}
  // 2. Also upsert user_roles so login check passes
  try{
    const {data:authList}=await sb.auth.admin.listUsers();
    const found=authList?.users?.find(u=>u.email?.toLowerCase()===email.toLowerCase());
    if(found){
      await sb.from('user_roles').upsert({
        user_id:found.id, school_id:schoolId, role:'admin',
        display_name:name||'School Admin'
      },{onConflict:'user_id,school_id,role'});
    }
  }catch(e){console.warn('[SA] user_roles sync skipped (user not yet in auth):', e.message);}
  msgEl.style.color='#34d399';msgEl.textContent='✅ Admin access granted for '+email;
  saAddNotif('👤','Admin Set',`${email} was set as admin for ${schoolName}.`,'info');
  setTimeout(saCloseModal, 1800);
};

window.saChangePwd = async function(){
  const np=document.getElementById('saNp').value;
  const npc=document.getElementById('saNpC').value;
  const msgEl=document.getElementById('saAccMsg');
  if(!np||np.length<6){msgEl.style.color='#fca5a5';msgEl.textContent='Password must be at least 6 characters.';return;}
  if(np!==npc){msgEl.style.color='#fca5a5';msgEl.textContent='Passwords do not match.';return;}
  msgEl.style.color='#93c5fd';msgEl.textContent='Updating…';
  const {error}=await sb.auth.updateUser({password:np});
  if(error){msgEl.style.color='#fca5a5';msgEl.textContent='Error: '+error.message;return;}
  msgEl.style.color='#34d399';msgEl.textContent='✅ Password updated successfully.';
  document.getElementById('saNp').value='';
  document.getElementById('saNpC').value='';
  saAddNotif('🔑','Password Changed','Super admin password was updated.','info');
};

window.saClearDataPrompt = function(){
  saShowModal(`
    <div style="text-align:center;margin-bottom:20px;">
      <div style="font-size:3rem;margin-bottom:12px;">☠️</div>
      <h3 style="color:#fca5a5;margin-bottom:8px;">Wipe All System Data?</h3>
      <p style="font-size:.88rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.55);line-height:1.6;">This will permanently erase <strong style="color:#fff;">ALL</strong> schools, users, student data, marks, and chat messages from the database. The system will be reset to zero.</p>
      <p style="font-size:.82rem;color:#fbbf24;margin-top:10px;">⚠ This <strong>cannot be undone</strong>.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="sa-form-group" style="margin-bottom:14px;">
      <label class="sa-label">Confirm: Type <strong style="color:#fca5a5;">DELETE ALL</strong> to proceed</label>
      <input id="saWipeConfirmText" type="text" class="sa-input" placeholder="DELETE ALL" autocomplete="off">
    </div>
    <div style="background:rgba(59,130,246,0.08);border:1px solid rgba(59,130,246,0.2);border-radius:10px;padding:10px 14px;font-size:.8rem;color:#93c5fd;margin-bottom:14px;">
      📥 A full JSON backup will be automatically downloaded before any data is deleted.
    </div>
    <div id="saWipeMsg" style="font-size:.85rem;min-height:18px;margin-bottom:14px;text-align:center;"></div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;justify-content:center;">
      <button onclick="saClearDataExecute()" class="sa-btn sa-btn-danger" style="padding:12px 20px;">🗑 Export Backup &amp; Wipe</button>
      <button onclick="saCloseModal()" class="sa-btn sa-btn-ghost" style="padding:12px 20px;">Cancel</button>
    </div>`);
};

window.saClearDataExecute = async function(){
  const confirmText=(document.getElementById('saWipeConfirmText')?.value||'').trim();
  const msgEl=document.getElementById('saWipeMsg');
  if(confirmText!=='DELETE ALL'){msgEl.style.color='#fca5a5';msgEl.textContent='Please type DELETE ALL exactly.';return;}
  const {data:sess}=await sb.auth.getSession();
  if(!sess?.session){msgEl.style.color='#fca5a5';msgEl.textContent='Session expired. Please reload and try again.';return;}
  msgEl.style.color='#93c5fd';msgEl.textContent='Exporting backup…';
  try{
    const [{data:allSchools},{data:allSD},{data:allAU},{data:allRoles},{data:allChat}]=await Promise.all([
      sb.from('schools').select('*'),sb.from('school_data').select('*'),
      sb.from('allowed_users').select('*'),sb.from('user_roles').select('*'),
      sb.from('parent_chat').select('*')
    ]);
    const backup={exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),version:'JIIB$CORE v8 wipe backup',schools:allSchools||[],school_data:allSD||[],allowed_users:allAU||[],user_roles:allRoles||[],parent_chat:allChat||[]};
    const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(backup,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
    const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='jiibcore_backup_'+new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)+'.json';a.click();URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    msgEl.style.color='#34d399';msgEl.textContent='✅ Backup downloaded. Proceeding with wipe…';
    await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,1500));
  }catch(ex){msgEl.style.color='#fbbf24';msgEl.textContent='⚠ Backup failed ('+ex.message+'). Wipe aborted for safety.';return;}
  msgEl.style.color='#fbbf24';msgEl.textContent='⚠ Deleting all data…';
  // Try RPC first (SECURITY DEFINER bypasses RLS)
  const {error:rpcErr}=await sb.rpc('wipe_all_data').catch(e=>({error:e}));
  if(!rpcErr){
    msgEl.style.color='#34d399';msgEl.textContent='✅ All data wiped via RPC. The system is now empty.';
    saAddNotif('☠️','Data Wiped','All system data was permanently deleted by super admin.','danger');
    setTimeout(()=>{saCloseModal();saLoadDashboardData();},2500);
    return;
  }
  // Fallback: direct table deletes (may be blocked by RLS
  const errs=[];
  const tryWipe=async(table,filter)=>{
    try{
      let q=sb.from(table).delete();
      if(filter)q=filter(q);
      const{error:e}=await q;
      if(e)errs.push(table+': '+e.message);
    }catch(ex){errs.push(table+': '+ex.message);}
  };
  await tryWipe('parent_chat',q=>q.not('id','is',null));
  await tryWipe('school_data',q=>q.not('school_id','is',null));
  await tryWipe('allowed_users',q=>q.not('email','is',null));
  await tryWipe('user_roles',q=>q.not('user_id','is',null));
  await tryWipe('schools',q=>q.not('id','is',null));
  if(errs.length){
    msgEl.style.color='#fbbf24';
    msgEl.innerHTML='⚠ Partial wipe. Run <code>05_wipe_all_data_rpc.sql</code> in Supabase to fix RLS: '+errs.slice(0,2).join('; ');
  }else{
    msgEl.style.color='#34d399';msgEl.textContent='✅ All data wiped. The system is now empty.';
    saAddNotif('☠️','Data Wiped','All system data was permanently deleted by super admin.','danger');
  }
  setTimeout(()=>{saCloseModal();saLoadDashboardData();},3000);
};
// ---- Live slug availability check ----
let _slugCheckTimer = null;
window.saCheckSlugAvailability = function(raw){
  const slug = (raw||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const ind = document.getElementById('slugIndicator');
  if(!ind) return;
  if(!slug || !/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{2,19}$/.test(slug)){
    ind.className='slug-indicator';ind.textContent='';return;
  }
  ind.className='slug-indicator checking';ind.textContent='⟳ Checking…';
  clearTimeout(_slugCheckTimer);
  _slugCheckTimer = setTimeout(async()=>{
    try{
      const {data}=await sb.from('schools').select('id').eq('slug',slug).maybeSingle();
      if(data){ind.className='slug-indicator taken';ind.textContent='✗ Already taken';}
      else{ind.className='slug-indicator available';ind.textContent='✓ Available';}
    }catch(e){ind.className='slug-indicator';ind.textContent='';}
  }, 500);
};

// ---- View School Details modal ----
window.saViewSchoolDetails = async function(schoolId, schoolName, slug){
  saShowModal(`
    <h3 style="color:#fff;margin-bottom:4px;">ℹ School Details</h3>
    <p style="font-size:.8rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.35);margin-bottom:18px;font-family:monospace;">/${slug}</p>
    <div id="saDetailsBody" style="font-size:.88rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.7);line-height:1.8;">Loading…</div>
    <div style="margin-top:20px;text-align:right;">
      <button onclick="saCloseModal()" class="sa-btn sa-btn-ghost" style="padding:10px 20px;">Close</button>
    </div>`);
  try{
    const [{data:au},{data:sd}] = await Promise.all([
      sb.from('allowed_users').select('email,role,display_name').eq('school_id',schoolId).order('role'),
      sb.from('school_data').select('updated_at,updated_by').eq('school_id',schoolId).maybeSingle()
    ]);
    const users = au||[];
    const admins = users.filter(u=>u.role==='admin');
    const teachers = users.filter(u=>u.role==='teacher');
    const lastSync = sd?.updated_at ? new Date(sd.updated_at).toLocaleString('en-GB') : 'No data yet';
    const el = document.getElementById('saDetailsBody');
    if(el) el.innerHTML = `
      <div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.04);border-radius:10px;padding:14px;margin-bottom:12px;">
        <div style="font-size:.7rem;font-weight:700;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.8px;margin-bottom:8px;">Administrators (${admins.length})</div>
        ${admins.length ? admins.map(u=>`<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:4px 0;"><span style="background:rgba(139,92,246,0.2);border-radius:4px;padding:1px 6px;font-size:.7rem;color:#a78bfa;">admin</span><span style="color:#fff;">${u.email}</span>${u.display_name?`<span style="color:rgba(255,255,255,0.4);font-size:.78rem;">(${u.display_name})</span>`:''}</div>`).join('') : '<div style="color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);font-size:.82rem;font-style:italic;">No admins set yet</div>'}
      </div>
      <div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.04);border-radius:10px;padding:14px;margin-bottom:12px;">
        <div style="font-size:.7rem;font-weight:700;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.8px;margin-bottom:8px;">Teachers (${teachers.length})</div>
        ${teachers.length ? teachers.map(u=>`<div style="padding:3px 0;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.7);">${u.email}${u.display_name?` — <span style="color:rgba(255,255,255,0.4);">${u.display_name}</span>`:''}</div>`).join('') : '<div style="color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);font-size:.82rem;font-style:italic;">No teachers added yet</div>'}
      </div>
      <div style="font-size:.78rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);">Last cloud sync: ${lastSync}</div>`;
    if(!_saDark) saFixModalForLightMode(document.getElementById('saModalOverlay')?.querySelector('.sa-modal'));
  }catch(e){
    const el=document.getElementById('saDetailsBody');
    if(el) el.innerHTML=`<span style="color:#fca5a5;">Error: ${e.message}</span>`;
  }
};

// ---- Users page ----
window._saAllUsers = [];
window._saUsersSchools = [];
async function saLoadUsersPage(){
  const tableEl = document.getElementById('saUsersTable');
  const filterEl = document.getElementById('saUsersSchoolFilter');
  if(!tableEl) return;
  tableEl.innerHTML='<div style="color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);text-align:center;padding:30px;">Loading…</div>';
  try{
    const [{data:au},{data:schools}] = await Promise.all([
      sb.from('allowed_users').select('email,role,display_name,school_id').order('email'),
      sb.from('schools').select('id,name,slug').order('name')
    ]);
    window._saAllUsers = au||[];
    window._saUsersSchools = schools||[];
    const _sEl=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.textContent=v;};
    _sEl('statTotalUsers',(au||[]).length);
    _sEl('statTotalTeachers',(au||[]).filter(u=>u.role==='teacher').length);
    _sEl('statTotalAdmins',(au||[]).filter(u=>u.role==='admin').length);
    try{const {data:lr}=await sb.from('user_roles').select('updated_at').order('updated_at',{ascending:false}).limit(1);if(lr&&lr[0]){const d=new Date(lr[0].updated_at);_sEl('statLastSeen',d.toLocaleDateString('en-GB',{day:'numeric',month:'short',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}));}}catch(e){}

    // Populate school filter
    if(filterEl){
      filterEl.innerHTML='<option value="">All schools</option>'+(schools||[]).map(s=>`<option value="${s.id}">${s.name}</option>`).join('');
    }
    saFilterUsers();
  }catch(e){tableEl.innerHTML=`<div style="color:#fca5a5;padding:20px;">Error: ${e.message}</div>`;}
}
window.saFilterUsers = function(){
  const q=(document.getElementById('saUsersSearch')?.value||'').toLowerCase();
  const sid=document.getElementById('saUsersSchoolFilter')?.value||'';
  const tableEl=document.getElementById('saUsersTable');
  if(!tableEl) return;
  const schoolMap={};(window._saUsersSchools||[]).forEach(s=>schoolMap[s.id]=s);
  let rows=(window._saAllUsers||[]).filter(u=>{
    const matchQ = !q || (u.email||'').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (u.display_name||'').toLowerCase().includes(q);
    const matchS = !sid || u.school_id===sid;
    return matchQ && matchS;
  });
  if(!rows.length){tableEl.innerHTML='<div style="color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);text-align:center;padding:30px;font-size:.88rem;">No users found.</div>';return;}
  tableEl.innerHTML=`<div style="overflow-x:auto;"><table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:.85rem;"><thead><tr style="text-align:left;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.35);font-size:.72rem;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08);"><th style="padding:10px 14px;">Email</th><th style="padding:10px 14px;">Display Name</th><th style="padding:10px 14px;">Role</th><th style="padding:10px 14px;">School</th></tr></thead><tbody>${
    rows.map(u=>{
      const sch=schoolMap[u.school_id];
      const roleColor=u.role==='admin'?'#a78bfa':u.role==='teacher'?'#34d399':'#93c5fd';
      const roleBg=u.role==='admin'?'rgba(139,92,246,0.15)':u.role==='teacher'?'rgba(16,185,129,0.12)':'rgba(59,130,246,0.1)';
      return`<tr style="border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.04);">
        <td style="padding:11px 14px;color:#fff;">${u.email||'—'}</td>
        <td style="padding:11px 14px;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.6);">${u.display_name||'—'}</td>
        <td style="padding:11px 14px;"><span style="background:${roleBg};color:${roleColor};border-radius:5px;padding:2px 8px;font-size:.72rem;font-weight:700;">${u.role||'—'}</span></td>
        <td style="padding:11px 14px;color:#93c5fd;">${sch?`<a href="/${sch.slug}" target="_blank" style="color:#93c5fd;text-decoration:none;">${sch.name}</a>`:'<span style="color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);">Unknown</span>'}</td>
      </tr>`;
    }).join('')
  }</tbody></table></div><div style="font-size:.75rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.25);margin-top:10px;padding:0 4px;">${rows.length} user${rows.length!==1?'s':''} shown</div>`;
};


// ─── Marks Template Helper ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
window.saApplyMarksTemplate = function(val){
  const el=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=v;};
  const show=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.closest('.sa-form-group').style.display=v?'':'none';};
  if(val==='aoi-mid-end'){el('mc1Name','AOI');el('mc1Max','10');el('mc2Name','Midterm');el('mc2Max','10');el('mc3Name','End of Term');el('mc3Max','80');['mcRow1a','mcRow1b','mcRow2a','mcRow2b'].forEach(id=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.style.display='';});}
  else if(val==='mid-end'){el('mc1Name','');el('mc1Max','0');el('mc2Name','Midterm');el('mc2Max','20');el('mc3Name','End of Term');el('mc3Max','80');['mcRow1a','mcRow1b'].forEach(id=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.style.display='none';});['mcRow2a','mcRow2b'].forEach(id=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.style.display='';});}
  else if(val==='end-only'){el('mc1Name','');el('mc1Max','0');el('mc2Name','');el('mc2Max','0');el('mc3Name','End of Term');el('mc3Max','100');['mcRow1a','mcRow1b','mcRow2a','mcRow2b'].forEach(id=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.style.display='none';});}
  else{['mcRow1a','mcRow1b','mcRow2a','mcRow2b'].forEach(id=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.style.display='';});}
};

// ─── Auto-refresh ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
let _saAutoRefreshInt=null;
function saToggleAutoRefresh(on){
  clearInterval(_saAutoRefreshInt);if(!on)return;
  _saAutoRefreshInt=setInterval(()=>{
    const pg=document.querySelector('.sa-page.active');if(!pg)return;
    const id=pg.id.replace('sa-page-','');
    if(id==='dashboard')saLoadDashboardData();
    else if(id==='schools')saLoadSchoolsGrid();
    else if(id==='analytics')saLoadAnalytics();
    else if(id==='users')saLoadUsersPage();
    else if(id==='settings'){saLoadTermManagerSchools();saLoadAdminMessages();}
  },5*60*1000);
}

async function saCheckNewContactMessages(){
  if(!sb)return;
  try{
    // Fetch ALL unread messages — no time filter so nothing is missed on login
    const {data:rows}=await sb.from('contact_messages')
      .select('id,sender_name,message,created_at,school_id')
      .eq('read',false)
      .order('created_at',{ascending:false})
      .limit(20);
    if(rows&&rows.length>0){
      // Fetch school names for context
      const schoolIds=[...new Set(rows.map(r=>r.school_id).filter(Boolean))];
      let schoolMap={};
      if(schoolIds.length){
        const {data:schools}=await sb.from('schools').select('id,name').in('id',schoolIds);
        (schools||[]).forEach(s=>schoolMap[s.id]=s.name);
      }
      rows.forEach(m=>{
        const schoolName=!m.school_id?'🌐 Public Inquiry':schoolMap[m.school_id]||'A school';
        const preview=(m.message||'').slice(0,70);
        saShowMsgPopup({schoolId:m.school_id, schoolName, senderName:m.sender_name||'Admin', preview:(m.message||'').slice(0,80), ts:m.created_at});
        saAddNotif('📬',`${schoolName} sent a message`,`${m.sender_name||'Admin'}: ${preview}`,'info');
      });
      // Show a summary toast if more than 3
      if(rows.length>3){
        saAddNotif('🔔',`${rows.length} unread school messages`,'Open Settings → School Admin Messages to view all','info');
      }
      // Subscribe to real-time new messages while super admin is logged in
      saSubscribeToContactMessages(schoolMap);
    } else {
      // Still subscribe even if no backlog
      saSubscribeToContactMessages({});
    }
  }catch(e){console.warn('saCheckNewContactMessages:',e.message);}
}

// Real-time subscription so new messages pop up immediately without refresh
let _saContactChannel=null;
async function saSubscribeToContactMessages(schoolMap){
  if(!sb||_saContactChannel)return;
  _saContactChannel=sb.channel('sa-contact-messages')
    .on('postgres_changes',{event:'INSERT',schema:'public',table:'contact_messages'},async(payload)=>{
      const m=payload.new;
      // Fetch school name if not cached
      let schoolName=schoolMap[m.school_id]||'';
      if(!schoolName&&m.school_id){
        try{const {data:s}=await sb.from('schools').select('name').eq('id',m.school_id).maybeSingle();schoolName=s?.name||'A school';}catch(e){}
      }
      saAddNotif('📬',`${schoolName||'A school'} sent a message`,`${m.sender_name||'Admin'}: ${(m.message||'').slice(0,70)}`,'info');
      // Also refresh the inbox if settings page is open
      const settingsPg=document.getElementById('sa-page-settings');
      if(settingsPg&&settingsPg.classList.contains('active'))saLoadAdminMessages();
    })
    .subscribe();
}

// ─── Term Manager ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function saLoadTermManagerSchools(){
  const sel=document.getElementById('tmSchoolSel');if(!sel)return;
  try{
    const {data:schools}=await sb.from('schools').select('id,name').order('name');
    sel.innerHTML='<option value="">\u2014 Select School \u2014</option>'+(schools||[]).map(s=>'<option value="'+s.id+'">'+s.name+'</option>').join('');
  }catch(e){}
}
window.tmLoadTerms=async function(){
  const schoolId=document.getElementById('tmSchoolSel')?.value;
  const termSel=document.getElementById('tmTermSel');if(!termSel)return;
  if(!schoolId){termSel.innerHTML='<option value="">\u2014 Select Term \u2014</option>';return;}
  try{
    const {data:sd}=await sb.from('school_data').select('data').eq('school_id',schoolId).maybeSingle();
    const terms=sd?.data?.terms||{};
    termSel.innerHTML='<option value="">\u2014 Select Term \u2014</option>'+Object.entries(terms).map(([k,t])=>'<option value="'+k+'">'+(t.label||k)+' ('+((t.students||[]).length)+' students, '+((t.marks||[]).length)+' marks)</option>').join('');
  }catch(e){termSel.innerHTML='<option value="">Error loading terms</option>';}
};
window.tmExportTerm=async function(){
  const schoolId=document.getElementById('tmSchoolSel')?.value;
  const termKey=document.getElementById('tmTermSel')?.value;
  const msgEl=document.getElementById('tmMsg');
  if(!schoolId||!termKey){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='#fca5a5';msgEl.textContent='Select a school and term first.';}return;}
  if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='#93c5fd';msgEl.textContent='Loading term data\u2026';}
  try{
    const {data:sd}=await sb.from('school_data').select('data').eq('school_id',schoolId).maybeSingle();
    const term=sd?.data?.terms?.[termKey];
    if(!term){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='#fca5a5';msgEl.textContent='Term not found.';}return;}
    const exp={exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),schoolId,termKey,term};
    const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(exp,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
    const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='term_'+termKey+'_'+new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)+'.json';a.click();URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='#34d399';msgEl.textContent='\u2705 Term data exported.';}
  }catch(e){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='#fca5a5';msgEl.textContent='Error: '+e.message;}}
};
window.tmClearTerm=function(){
  const schoolId=document.getElementById('tmSchoolSel')?.value;
  const termKey=document.getElementById('tmTermSel')?.value;
  const msgEl=document.getElementById('tmMsg');
  if(!schoolId||!termKey){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='#fca5a5';msgEl.textContent='Select a school and term first.';}return;}
  saShowModal('<div style="text-align:center;margin-bottom:18px;"><div style="font-size:2.5rem;margin-bottom:10px;">\uD83D\uDDD1</div><h3 style="color:#fca5a5;margin-bottom:8px;">Clear Term Data?</h3><p style="font-size:.87rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.55);line-height:1.6;">Permanently erase all marks for <strong style="color:#fbbf24;">'+termKey+'</strong>.<br>Export a backup first!</p></div><div id="tmClearMsg" style="font-size:.84rem;text-align:center;min-height:18px;margin-bottom:14px;"></div><div style="display:flex;gap:10px;justify-content:center;"><button onclick="tmClearTermConfirm(\''+schoolId+'\',\''+termKey+'\')" class="sa-btn sa-btn-danger" style="padding:11px 20px;">Yes, Clear</button><button onclick="saCloseModal()" class="sa-btn sa-btn-ghost" style="padding:11px 20px;">Cancel</button></div>');
};
window.tmClearTermConfirm=async function(schoolId,termKey){
  const msgEl=document.getElementById('tmClearMsg');
  if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='#93c5fd';msgEl.textContent='Clearing\u2026';}
  try{
    const {data:sd,error:fe}=await sb.from('school_data').select('data').eq('school_id',schoolId).maybeSingle();
    if(fe||!sd){if(msgEl)msgEl.textContent='Error: '+(fe?.message||'not found');return;}
    const nd=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(sd.data));
    if(!nd.terms||!nd.terms[termKey]){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='#fca5a5';msgEl.textContent='Term not found.';}return;}
    nd.terms[termKey].students=[];nd.terms[termKey].marks=[];nd.terms[termKey].closedAt=new Date().toISOString();
    const {error}=await sb.from('school_data').update({data:nd,updated_at:new Date().toISOString(),updated_by:'super_admin'}).eq('school_id',schoolId);
    if(error){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='#fca5a5';msgEl.textContent='Error: '+error.message;}return;}
    saAddNotif('\uD83D\uDDD1','Term Cleared',termKey+' data was cleared.','info');
    if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='#34d399';msgEl.textContent='\u2705 Term data cleared.';}
    setTimeout(saCloseModal,1800);
  }catch(e){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='#fca5a5';msgEl.textContent='Error: '+e.message;}}
};

// ─── School Admin Messages Inbox ──────────────────────────────────────────────
// ─── MESSAGES PAGE ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
window._saMsgConvos = [];      // [{phone, senderName, schoolName, schoolId, messages:[]}]
window._saActiveMsgPhone = null;
window._saMsgUnreadCount = 0;

async function saLoadAdminMessages(){
  // Called from settings page (legacy stub — redirect to messages page)
  const el=document.getElementById('saAdminMsgsBody');
  if(el) el.innerHTML='<div style="padding:14px;font-size:.85rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.5);">Open the <strong style="color:#93c5fd;">💬 Messages</strong> tab in the nav to view school admin messages.</div>';
}

async function saLoadMessagesPage(){
  const listEl = document.getElementById('saMsgConvoList');
  if(!listEl) return;
  listEl.innerHTML = '<div style="padding:24px;text-align:center;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);font-size:.84rem;">Loading…</div>';
  try{
    const [{data:msgs},{data:schools}] = await Promise.all([
      sb.from('contact_messages').select('*').order('created_at',{ascending:true}),
      sb.from('schools').select('id,name')
    ]);
    const sm={};(schools||[]).forEach(s=>sm[s.id]=s);
    // Group by school_id (one conversation per school)
    const convMap={};
    (msgs||[]).forEach(m=>{
      const key = m.school_id||'unknown';
      if(!convMap[key]) convMap[key]={
        schoolId:key,
        schoolName:!key||key==='unknown'?'🌐 Public Inquiry':sm[key]?.name||'Unknown School',
        senderName:m.sender_name||'Admin',
        senderEmail:m.sender_email||'',
        messages:[],
        unread:0,
        lastTs:null
      };
      convMap[key].messages.push(m);
      if(!m.read) convMap[key].unread++;
      if(!convMap[key].lastTs||m.created_at>convMap[key].lastTs) convMap[key].lastTs=m.created_at;
      if(m.sender_name) convMap[key].senderName=m.sender_name;
      if(m.sender_email) convMap[key].senderEmail=m.sender_email;
    });
    window._saMsgConvos = Object.values(convMap).sort((a,b)=>(b.lastTs||'').localeCompare(a.lastTs||''));
    window._saMsgUnreadCount = window._saMsgConvos.reduce((s,c)=>s+c.unread,0);
    saRenderMsgList();
    saUpdateMsgNavBadge();
    // Re-open active conversation if any
    if(window._saActiveMsgPhone) saOpenMsgConvo(window._saActiveMsgPhone);
  }catch(e){
    if(listEl) listEl.innerHTML=`<div style="color:#fca5a5;padding:16px;font-size:.84rem;">Error: ${e.message}</div>`;
  }
}

function saRenderMsgList(filter=''){
  const listEl=document.getElementById('saMsgConvoList');if(!listEl)return;
  const convos=(window._saMsgConvos||[]).filter(c=>
    !filter || c.schoolName.toLowerCase().includes(filter) || c.senderName.toLowerCase().includes(filter)
  );
  if(!convos.length){
    listEl.innerHTML='<div style="padding:24px;text-align:center;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.25);font-size:.84rem;">'+(filter?'No conversations match.':'No messages from school admins yet.')+'</div>';
    return;
  }
  listEl.innerHTML=convos.map(c=>{
    const initials=(c.senderName||c.schoolName).split(' ').map(w=>w[0]).join('').slice(0,2).toUpperCase();
    const last=c.messages[c.messages.length-1];
    const preview=(last?.message||'').slice(0,48)+(last?.message?.length>48?'…':'');
    const time=c.lastTs?new Date(c.lastTs).toLocaleTimeString('en-GB',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}):'';
    const isActive=window._saActiveMsgPhone===c.schoolId;
    return`<div class="sa-msg-convo-item${isActive?' active':''}" onclick="saOpenMsgConvo('${c.schoolId}')">
      <div style="width:44px;height:44px;border-radius:50%;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#25d366,#128c7e);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-weight:700;font-size:.88rem;color:white;flex-shrink:0;">${initials}</div>
      <div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">
        <div style="font-weight:700;font-size:.88rem;color:#fff;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;">${c.schoolName}</div>
        <div style="font-size:.76rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.4);white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;margin-top:2px;">${preview||'No messages'}</div>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-end;gap:4px;flex-shrink:0;">
        <div style="font-size:.68rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);">${time}</div>
        ${c.unread>0?`<div class="sa-msg-badge">${c.unread}</div>`:''}
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }).join('');
}

function saFilterMsgList(){
  const q=(document.getElementById('saMsgSearch')?.value||'').toLowerCase();
  saRenderMsgList(q);
}

function saOpenMsgConvo(schoolId){
  window._saActiveMsgPhone=schoolId;
  const convo=(window._saMsgConvos||[]).find(c=>c.schoolId===schoolId);
  if(!convo)return;
  // Mark as read in DB (fire and forget)
  const unreadIds=convo.messages.filter(m=>!m.read).map(m=>m.id);
  if(unreadIds.length){
    sb.from('contact_messages').update({read:true}).in('id',unreadIds).then(()=>{});
    convo.messages.forEach(m=>m.read=true);
    convo.unread=0;
    window._saMsgUnreadCount=Math.max(0,window._saMsgUnreadCount-unreadIds.length);
    saUpdateMsgNavBadge();
  }
  // Update header
  const initials=(convo.senderName||convo.schoolName).split(' ').map(w=>w[0]).join('').slice(0,2).toUpperCase();
  const avatarEl=document.getElementById('saMsgChatAvatar');
  const nameEl=document.getElementById('saMsgChatName');
  const schoolEl=document.getElementById('saMsgChatSchool');
  if(avatarEl) avatarEl.textContent=initials;
  if(nameEl) nameEl.textContent=convo.senderName+(convo.senderEmail?' · '+convo.senderEmail:'');
  if(schoolEl) schoolEl.textContent='🏫 '+convo.schoolName;
  // Show chat view
  const emptyEl=document.getElementById('saMsgEmpty');
  const chatView=document.getElementById('saMsgChatView');
  if(emptyEl) emptyEl.style.display='none';
  if(chatView) chatView.style.display='flex';
  // Render messages
  saRenderMsgThread(convo.messages);
  // Highlight active in list
  saRenderMsgList((document.getElementById('saMsgSearch')?.value||'').toLowerCase());
}

function saRenderMsgThread(messages){
  const body=document.getElementById('saMsgChatBody');if(!body)return;
  if(!messages||!messages.length){
    body.innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.2);padding:40px;font-size:.85rem;">No messages</div>';
    return;
  }
  // Group by date
  let lastDate='';
  body.innerHTML='';
  messages.forEach(m=>{
    const msgDate=m.created_at?new Date(m.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-GB',{weekday:'long',day:'numeric',month:'long'}):'';
    if(msgDate!==lastDate){
      lastDate=msgDate;
      const sep=document.createElement('div');
      sep.style.cssText='text-align:center;font-size:.72rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.25);margin:8px 0;';
      sep.textContent=msgDate;
      body.appendChild(sep);
    }
    const isOutgoing=m.sender==='admin'||m.direction==='out';
    const timeStr=m.created_at?new Date(m.created_at).toLocaleTimeString('en-GB',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}):'';
    const bubble=document.createElement('div');
    bubble.dataset.msgId=m.id;
    bubble.style.cssText='display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:6px;'+(isOutgoing?'flex-direction:row-reverse;':'');
    bubble.innerHTML=`
      <div class="${isOutgoing?'sa-chat-bubble-out':'sa-chat-bubble-in'}" style="position:relative;">
        ${!isOutgoing?`<div style="font-size:.7rem;font-weight:700;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.5);margin-bottom:4px;">${m.sender_name||'Admin'}</div>`:''}
        <div style="word-break:break-word;">${(m.message||'').replace(/\n/g,'<br>')}</div>
        <span class="${isOutgoing?'sa-chat-time-out':'sa-chat-time'}">${timeStr}</span>
        <button class="sa-chat-delete-btn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();saDeleteSingleMsg('${m.id}')" title="Delete message">✕</button>
      </div>`;
    body.appendChild(bubble);
  });
  body.scrollTop=body.scrollHeight;
}

async function saDeleteSingleMsg(msgId){
  if(!msgId) return;
  try{
    await sb.from('contact_messages').delete().eq('id',msgId);
    // Remove from local state
    const convo=(window._saMsgConvos||[]).find(c=>c.schoolId===window._saActiveMsgPhone);
    if(convo){convo.messages=convo.messages.filter(m=>String(m.id)!==String(msgId));}
    // Re-render thread
    const convoFresh=(window._saMsgConvos||[]).find(c=>c.schoolId===window._saActiveMsgPhone);
    if(convoFresh) saRenderMsgThread(convoFresh.messages);
    saRenderMsgList((document.getElementById('saMsgSearch')?.value||'').toLowerCase());
  }catch(e){ saAddNotif('❌','Delete failed',e.message,'danger'); }
}

window.saDeleteConversation = async function(){
  const schoolId=window._saActiveMsgPhone;if(!schoolId)return;
  const convo=(window._saMsgConvos||[]).find(c=>c.schoolId===schoolId);
  const name=convo?.schoolName||'this school';
  saShowModal(`
    <div style="text-align:center;margin-bottom:18px;">
      <div style="font-size:2.5rem;margin-bottom:10px;">🗑</div>
      <h3 style="color:#fca5a5;margin-bottom:8px;">Delete Conversation?</h3>
      <p style="font-size:.86rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.55);line-height:1.6;">All messages from <strong style="color:#fff;">${name}</strong> will be permanently deleted. This cannot be undone.</p>
    </div>
    <div id="saDelConvoMsg" style="font-size:.84rem;min-height:16px;margin-bottom:14px;text-align:center;"></div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;justify-content:center;">
      <button onclick="saDeleteConversationConfirm('${schoolId}')" class="sa-btn sa-btn-danger" style="padding:11px 22px;">Delete</button>
      <button onclick="saCloseModal()" class="sa-btn sa-btn-ghost" style="padding:11px 22px;">Cancel</button>
    </div>`);
};

window.saDeleteConversationConfirm = async function(schoolId){
  const msgEl=document.getElementById('saDelConvoMsg');
  if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='#93c5fd';msgEl.textContent='Deleting…';}
  try{
    const {error}=await sb.from('contact_messages').delete().eq('school_id',schoolId);
    if(error) throw error;
    // Clear from local state
    window._saMsgConvos=(window._saMsgConvos||[]).filter(c=>c.schoolId!==schoolId);
    window._saActiveMsgPhone=null;
    // Reset chat view
    const emptyEl=document.getElementById('saMsgEmpty');
    const chatView=document.getElementById('saMsgChatView');
    if(emptyEl) emptyEl.style.display='flex';
    if(chatView) chatView.style.display='none';
    saRenderMsgList('');
    saCloseModal();
    saAddNotif('🗑','Conversation deleted','','info');
    saUpdateMsgNavBadge();
  }catch(e){
    if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='#fca5a5';msgEl.textContent='Error: '+e.message;}
  }
};

async function saSendReplyToAdmin(){
  const inp=document.getElementById('saMsgReplyInput');
  const text=(inp?.value||'').trim();
  if(!text||!window._saActiveMsgPhone) return;
  inp.value=''; inp.style.height='auto';
  const schoolId=window._saActiveMsgPhone;
  try{
    const now=new Date().toISOString();
    const {data:inserted,error}=await sb.from('contact_messages').insert({
      school_id:schoolId,
      sender_name:'Super Admin',
      sender_email:'',
      message:text,
      read:false,
      sender:'super_admin',
      direction:'out',
      created_at:now
    }).select().single();
    if(error) throw error;
    const convo=(window._saMsgConvos||[]).find(c=>c.schoolId===schoolId);
    if(convo){convo.messages.push(inserted||{id:Date.now(),message:text,sender:'admin',sender_name:'Super Admin',created_at:now,direction:'out'});convo.lastTs=now;saRenderMsgThread(convo.messages);}
    saRenderMsgList('');
  }catch(e){saAddNotif('❌','Reply failed',e.message,'danger');}
}

async function saMarkAllMessagesRead(){
  try{
    await sb.from('contact_messages').update({read:true}).eq('read',false);
    (window._saMsgConvos||[]).forEach(c=>{c.messages.forEach(m=>m.read=true);c.unread=0;});
    window._saMsgUnreadCount=0;
    saUpdateMsgNavBadge();
    saRenderMsgList('');
    saAddNotif('✓','All messages marked read','','info');
  }catch(e){}
}

function saUpdateMsgNavBadge(){
  // Update Messages nav link with unread count badge
  document.querySelectorAll('.sa-nav-link,.sa-mobile-nav-link').forEach(btn=>{
    if(btn.textContent.includes('Messages')){
      const n=window._saMsgUnreadCount||0;
      if(n>0&&!btn.querySelector('.sa-msg-nav-badge')){
        const sp=document.createElement('span');
        sp.className='sa-msg-nav-badge';
        sp.style.cssText='background:#ef4444;color:white;border-radius:20px;min-width:17px;height:17px;font-size:.62rem;font-weight:800;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:0 4px;margin-left:5px;';
        sp.textContent=n>9?'9+':n;
        btn.appendChild(sp);
      }else if(btn.querySelector('.sa-msg-nav-badge')){
        const badge=btn.querySelector('.sa-msg-nav-badge');
        if(n>0){badge.textContent=n>9?'9+':n;}else{badge.remove();}
      }
    }
  });
}

// ─── Real-time popup notification for new messages ────────────────────────────
let _saMsgRealtimeChannel=null;
function saSubscribeMessagesRealtime(){
  if(!sb||_saMsgRealtimeChannel)return;
  _saMsgRealtimeChannel=sb.channel('sa-contact-messages-rt')
    .on('postgres_changes',{event:'INSERT',schema:'public',table:'contact_messages'},async(payload)=>{
      const m=payload.new;
      if(!m||m.direction==='out')return; // ignore replies we sent
      // Fetch school name
      let schoolName='A school';
      try{const {data:s}=await sb.from('schools').select('name').eq('id',m.school_id).maybeSingle();schoolName=s?.name||'A school';}catch(e){}
      // WhatsApp-style popup
      saShowMsgPopup({
        schoolId:m.school_id,
        schoolName,
        senderName:m.sender_name||'Admin',
        preview:(m.message||'').slice(0,80),
        ts:m.created_at
      });
      // Add to notif panel too
      saAddNotif('💬',`${schoolName}`,`${m.sender_name||'Admin'}: ${(m.message||'').slice(0,60)}`,'info');
      // Refresh message list if open
      await saLoadMessagesPage();
    })
    .subscribe();
}

let _saPopupQueue=[],_saPopupShowing=false;
function saShowMsgPopup(opts){
  _saPopupQueue.push(opts);
  if(!_saPopupShowing) saNextPopup();
}
function saNextPopup(){
  if(!_saPopupQueue.length){_saPopupShowing=false;return;}
  _saPopupShowing=true;
  const opts=_saPopupQueue.shift();
  const initials=(opts.senderName||opts.schoolName).split(' ').map(w=>w[0]).join('').slice(0,2).toUpperCase();
  const timeStr=opts.ts?new Date(opts.ts).toLocaleTimeString('en-GB',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}):'now';
  const id='popup_'+Date.now();
  const div=document.createElement('div');
  div.className='sa-popup-notif';
  div.id=id;
  div.title='Click to open conversation';
  div.innerHTML=`
    <div class="sa-popup-avatar">${initials}</div>
    <div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">
      <div class="sa-popup-title">📬 ${opts.schoolName}</div>
      <div class="sa-popup-preview">${opts.senderName}: ${opts.preview}</div>
      <div class="sa-popup-time">${timeStr}</div>
    </div>
    <button class="sa-popup-close" onclick="event.stopPropagation();saPopupDismiss('${id}')">✕</button>`;
  div.onclick=()=>{
    saPopupDismiss(id);
    saSwitchPage('messages',null);
    setTimeout(()=>saOpenMsgConvo(opts.schoolId),300);
  };
  document.body.appendChild(div);
  // Auto-dismiss after 7 seconds
  setTimeout(()=>saPopupDismiss(id),7000);
}
function saPopupDismiss(id){
  const el=document.getElementById(id);if(!el)return;
  el.classList.add('leaving');
  setTimeout(()=>{el.remove();setTimeout(saNextPopup,200);},300);
}

// ─── Patch saSwitchPage for settings sub-sections ────────────────────────────
const _origSaSwitchFn=typeof saSwitchPage!=='undefined'?saSwitchPage:null;
if(_origSaSwitchFn){
  saSwitchPage=function(id,elArg){
    _origSaSwitchFn(id,elArg);
    if(id==='settings'){saLoadTermManagerSchools();saLoadAdminMessages();}
    if(id==='devices'){saLoadDeviceSessions();saRenderBlacklist();}
    if(id==='messages') saLoadMessagesPage();
  };
}

// =============== END SUPER ADMIN PORTAL ===============

</script>

<div id="conflictWarning"></div>
</body>
</html>
